			mūvā yiratteḻu nūṟiṟ ṟuvakkiya
		25.	kaṭaikkaḻa kattuk kaviṉpari pāṭal
			āṟṟaṟ pēricai kūttuc ciṟṟicai
			nimirvari muṉṉaiya vamiḻteṉat tōṉṟiṉa.
āyntu kāṇal
			mutaliṭaik kaḻaka muṉṉīr koḷḷap
			paṭutali ṉintiyap paṭiyiṉ vaḷamvavi
		30.	ayaṉāṭ ṭavarī rāyira māṇṭāp
			paṭaiyeṭut tuttuṉ puṟuttiya paṭiyāl
			nūlkaḷu mavaṟṟi nuṇṇiya vaḻakkum
			aruki maṟaintaṉa vātali ṉavvaḻi
			tirumuṟai kaṇṭa perumaic cōḻaṉ
		35.	nīla kaṇṭayāḻp pāṇar marapil
			vantapeṇ vaḻiyā yaṟintu parappiṉaṉ
			nantuta lillā vanta muṟaiyil
			tēvā rappēr mūvāt tamiḻmaṟai
			nacaiyoḻi mēlō ricaitiru vicaippāp
		40.	paṇṇoṭu paṭikku maṇṇiṉ vaḻakkum
			icainūṉ muṟaitē riḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ
			cilappati kārac cevviya moḻiyum,
			paṭimēṟ pulava raṭiyārkku nallār
			uraiyiṟ kūṟiya vuyarmēṟ kōḷum,
		45.	paripā ṭalpayil paṇṇiṉ muṟaiyum,
			maṟṟuḷa paṉṉū ṉuṭpamum, āyntu;
			ciṟpanūl vallār ciṟitō ruṟuppuk
			kaippeṟiṉ maṟṟelāṅ kaṇṭu kaṇakkāṟ
			ṟērtal pōlat teyva nallaruḷ
		50.	kāṭṭap poruḷelāṅ karutti lōrntē;
tamiḻicai nūl
			āṭu mutalām paṉṉiru vīṭṭil
			kuraliḷik koṉṟoṉṟu koṭuttain tiṉukkum
			iraṇṭiraṇ ṭāka vīyiṟ paṉṉiraṇ
			ṭilaniṟai veytu; mituvē yāḻiṟ
		55. 	paṉṉiru vīṭṭiṟ payilicai yākum;
			ataiyiraṭ ṭikka virupattu nāṉku
			kēḷvi varunilai mūṉṟiṉuṅ keḻumum;
			otta vaḷaviṟ pāttaṟ kovvum;
			iṇaikiḷai naṭpup pakaimuṟaik kiyalum;
		60.	paḻantami ḻicainūṟ pālaika ṇāṉkiṟ
			paṉṉī rilliṟ payilva tāyam;
			āya miraṭṭikki lākum vaṭṭam;
			vaṭṭa miraṭṭikkil varuntiri kōṇam;
			kōṇa miraṭṭikkiṟ kulavuñ caturam;
		65.	aṟunāṉ kalakuka ḷilaiyē loṉṟumūṉ
			ṟaintē ḻalaki loṉṟā kēḷvi;
			kōṇañ catura milaiyēṟ kēḷvik
			kālarai mukkāṟ kaṇakkuk kiṭamilai;
			ātali ṉuḷki yaṟimi ṉeṉṟum;
		70.	vaṭṭap pālaiyil yāḻvakai nāṉkum
			paṉṉiru pālaiyum pālaiyē ḻēḻum
			perumpaṇ ṇāṉkum cāti nāṉkum
			uḷavatil viḷari kaikkiḷaik kovvōr
			alaku kuṟaittiru pattiraṇ ṭākki
		75.	icaikka nūlka ḷicaittaṉa veṉṟum;
			pālai nāṉkiṟ paṉṉī rāyiram
			paṇkaḷ tamiḻiṟ palkiṉa veṉṟum;
			teṉṉaṉ civaṉē maṉṉevai kaṭku
			muṉṉiṟai yākko ḷiṉṟamiḻ vaḻakkāl,
		80.	tamiḻkkaḻa kaṅkaḷ taḻaikkac ceyta
			teṉṉavar marapiṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟik
			kaṭalkōṭ paṭṭa kālait tamiḻakam
			marakkalat tuyntu maṟuttu mūrceyta
			catyavi rataṉeṉac catapatap pirāmaṇam
		85.	eḻiṟpā kavata mēṉaip purāṇam
			palavum pakarvai vaccuta maṉuvum
			avarvaḻi vanta pāṇṭiya raṉaivarum
			tamiḻicai vaḷartta talaiva rātaliṉ
			teṉṉā teṉāveṉu māḷatti yituvarai
		90.	maṉṉi vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkuḷa teṉṟum;
			irukku muṉṉaiya veḻiṉmaṟai mūṉṟum
			nālicai yōṭu naṇṇip piṉṉart
			tamiḻa ṉirāvaṇa ṉamarē ḻicaiyoṭu
			cāmam pāṭit taṇṇaruḷ peṟṟaṉaṉ
		95.	atumuta lammaṟai yēḻicai yōṭu
			vaḻaṅkal kēṭṭaliṟ paḻampurai yēḻicai
			tamiḻark kuritteṉal cālpām eṉṟum;
			tamiḻicai nūliṉ ṟakaimai viḷakki
vaṭamoḻi yicainūl
			āyirattu nāṉū ṟāṇṭiṉ muṉṉart
		100.	tōṉṟiya paratar colvaṭa nūlil
			pañcama mintaḷam pāṇaṟ kaucikam
			vēḷā vaḷicī rāka muṉṉaiya
			tamiḻppaṇ kaḷumaṟu nūṟṟi yeṇpa
			tāṇṭumuṟ ṟōṉṟiya cāraṅka tēvar
		105.	iyaṟṟiya ratnā karatti lintaḷam
			kāntāram pañcamam kāntāra pañcamam
			cātāri kaucikañ cutta cātāri
			kuṟiñciyoṇ takka rākaṅ kuccari
			naṭṭapāṭai mēka rāka muṉṉaiya
		110.	tamiḻicai nūlkaḷ cāṟṟiya paṇkaḷum
			eṭuttuk kūṟali ṉivaitē vārat
			tuḷaveṉac cāraṅka tēvar kuṟittaliṉ
			aṉṉavar tamiḻiṟ paṇkaḷai yāyntu
			vaṭamoḻi yiṉiṉūl vakutta kālai
		115.	īrala kukuṟait ticaikkap peṟṟa
			vaṭṭap pālaiyi liḷikura lāṉa
			neytal yāḻkkuriya nāṉmūṉ ṟiraṇṭu
			nāṉāṉ mūṉṟiraṇ ṭākiya kēḷvi
			irupat tiraṇṭiṉai yorunilaik kuriya
		120.	alakeṉa mayaṅki yākkam pēci
			yuraittaṉa rammuṟai yotta pakuppiṟ
			kovvā taṉṟik kōṇañ caturap
			pālaiyi ṉāṟpat teṭṭun toṇtoṇṇūṟ
			ṟāṟumāp pakuttaṟ kiyalā tammuṟai
		125. 	muṉṉark kūṟi nāṉū ṟāṇṭu
			munti yakōpilar pārijā tattiṟ
			piṉṉar yāḻiṟ paṉṉī ricaiyē
			nāratar vaḻiyeṉ ṟirākam vakuttaṉar
			muṉṉūṟ ṟaṟupa tāṇṭu muṉṉar
		130.	naṉittamiḻ veṟutta rāmā māttiyar
			taṉiccura mēḷa kaḷāniti cāṟṟiṉar
			muṉṉū ṟāṇṭu muṉcutā nitinūl
			kōvinta tīkṣitar kuyiṟṟiṉa rataṉpiṉ
			irunūṟ ṟaimpati yāṇṭu muṉṉar
		135.	viḷaṅkiya vēṅkaṭa makippēr mikkōṉ
			pēṇcaturt taṇṭip pirakā cikaiyil
			ēḻupā laiyilvaru paṉṉiru vaṭṭat
			tiyalu meḻupat tiraṇṭu mēḷam
			vakuttavai kaṭku vaṭamoḻip putuppeyar
		140.	vaḻaṅkiṉa ṉaṉṉavar muṉṉaiya pallōr
			icaikkuḷa kēḷvi yirupat tiraṇṭeṉ
			ṟuraittamai muṟṟu movvā teṉṟum,
			aḷantō rāma lavvaḻi nampip
			paṟpala rinnāṭ pāṭakar coṟṟa
		145.	kaṇakku kaḷiluḷa kaṟaiyivai yeṉṟum
			vaṭanūṟ kaṇṇuḷa vaḻuveṭut tōti,
mēṟkōḷ
			āṅkila vallā raḷaviṭ ṭaṟintu
			kēḷvi yalaiyiṟ kiḷakkuṅ kaṇakkum
			pirama mēḷat tirupā ṉāṉku
		150.	kēḷvi muṟaiyiṟ kiḷanta nalamum
			icainūṟ ṟoṉmai yiṉitu viḷaṅkak
			kiṟittu maṟainūl kiḷanta palavum
			paṟpala vaṟiñar pakarmēṟ kōḷum
			eṭuttuk kūṟi yiyaipukaḷ kāṭṭi
		155.	īrala kukuṟait ticaikkum vaṭṭap
			pālaik kuriya virupā ṉiraṇṭē
			nilaiyoṉ ṟukkiyal kēḷviyā meṉṉal
			ceḻumpaṇ ṇiyaltiṟan tiṟattiṟaṅ kaṭkuṟu
			mirupatu patiṉā ṟīṟāṟu kēḷvi
		160.	kaḷiloṉṟu kūṟal kaṭukku mataṉāl
			kēḷvi yorunilaik keṇmūṉ ṟēyām
			iṉṟēl naṭaipeṟu mirākaṅ kaḷiluḷa
			kālarai mukkā lalaku kaḷukkōr
			iṭamilai yinnāṭ pāṭaka rataṟkuk
		165.	kamaka meṉappēr kaṇṭuraik kiṉṟaṉar
			cāraṅkar kamaka micaiyacai veṉṟaṉar
			icaiyoṉṟu piṟiti ṉeḻilaic cārtal
			kamaka meṉavēṅ kaṭamaki cāṟṟiṉar
			icaiviṉai yāku meṉṟu miṉpam
		170.	payakkak kuṟaittup pāṭutal pāṭa
			lamuta meṉṟu maṟaintaṉar tamiḻar
			icainuṇ ṇalakum palavakai viṉaiyum
			vevvē ṟeṉpatu veḷippaṭai yataṉāl
			alaki ṉuṭpa maṭaitaṟ kuriya
		175.	vaṭṭaṅ kōṇañ caturap pālaik
			kēḷvi yilaiyēṟ kiḷakkak kaṇakkilai
			nilaikkaṟu nāṉku kēḷvi nikaḻtticai
			marapunūl kaṇṭār mayakkaṅ kaṇṭilar
			eṉṟu pakaru meṉvaḻak kuvantu
nūṟpayaṉ
		180.	iruntamiḻp paṇkava riruḷpō yakala
			arumpoṟ paṇṇamu tamarntiṉi tarunti
			aṉpi ṉulakō riṉpan tiḷaikka
			ulakelān tamiḻi ṉuyarvun toṉmaiyum
			kaṇṭuḷa naṉmalar viṇṭu kaḷikka
		185.	paṇkaḷuk kuriya payaṉuṭaṉ palikkat
			teyvan tāḻpavar meyyaṉpu perukik
			karutip pāṭik kaṉintuḷa murukap
			putuvaḻi vāṭṭi mututamiḻ neṟiyaik
			kāṭṭi nilaikkuk kēḷvi yeṇmūṉ
		190.	ṟēyeṉat tīṭṭi yuṇmaiyai nāṭṭi
			yāvaru muṇara naṟṟamiḻ naṭaiyil
			karuṇā miruta cākara meṉṉum
			nūloṉ ṟiyaṟṟi ñālam pukaḻatai
araṅkēṟṟal
			añcu pakuppiṉu mañcā tāṭci
		195.	naṇṇic celuttu maṇṇaṟ kōmāṉ
			aintām jārjeṉu maintār perumai
			maṉṉiṟai yaṉpu tuṉṉic cāṉṟa
			kalvi poruḷeṉuñ celva nirampiya
			marāṭa mākiya parōṭā nāṭṭai
		200.	tuykkanaṉ ṟāḷuṅ keykkavā riṟaivaṉ
			kiṟittā yirattut toḷḷā yirappati
			ṉāṟā māṇṭi laṉaivarum vēṇṭa
			iṉitu naṭāttiya vicaippē ravaiyil
			paṟpala koḷkaiya roppi makiḻa
		205.	naṉṟaraṅ kēṟṟi maṉṟiṉi luḷḷār
			aiyan tīra meyyelām virittu
			ellām valla viṟaivaṉ mecciya
			vaṇmaip pāṇar marapiṟ ṟōṉṟit
			tamiḻmaṟaip paṇkaḷ taḻaikkac cōḻaṉ
		210.	avaiyiṟ pāṭiya varuṭpeṇ maṇiyeṉat
			tōṉṟi yaṟivu cāṉṟu teyva
			aṉpuṅ kalvi vaṉpuṅ kaṟpum
			tēṉiṉu miṉimai yāṉanaṟ pāṭṭum
			niṟaintu paṇpiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum
		215.	marakata valli kaṉakavalli yeṉṟa
			taṉpeṇ maṇikaḷ yāḻoṭu pāṭi
			nūṟpaṭi vaḻaṅkum nuṭpaṅ kāṭṭac
			ceytu teḷittu meymmaik kaṭavuḷ
			aruḷuñ celvamu malakil nallōr
		220.	naṉku pōṟṟu nalamuṅ kaḷippum
			aṟamum pukaḻu maṭaintu ciṟantaṉaṉ
ākkiyōṉ
			maṉuviṉ kulattu vanta pāṇṭiyar
			āṇṭanaṉ ṉāṭṭi lamaintu ciṟanta
			cāmpūr vaṭakaraic cāmpuva ṉōṭaiyiṟ
		225.	cāṉṟā riṉattiṟ ṟalaimaipūṇ ṭōṅkiya
			pallak kūrnta celvar vaḻivaru
			mettunaṟ cīrtti muttuc cāmivēḷ
			naṉmaṉai yāntiru vaṉṉammai yiṉpāl
			aṉpuṅ koṭaiyum aṟamum aruḷum
		230.	oruṅkuru veṭuttiv virumpuvi vanteṉat
			tōṉṟiya viṉco lāṉṟanaṟ ṟōṉṟal
			meypporu ḷiṉpā luytta maṉattaṉ
			kalviyuñ celvamu malkiya nallōṉ
			nattumoṇ perumaic cittar kuḻumiya
		235.	curuḷi malaiyiṉ maruvi yuṟaiyum
			karuṇā nantat teruḷcāṉ muṉivaṟ
			kaṇṭupē raṉpu koṇṭava raruḷāl
			vātam maruttuva mātinūṉ muṟaikaḷ
			aṇṇaru ñāṉa vuṇmai yuṇarntōṉ
		240.	ulaka muḻuvatum nilavat taṉmarun
			tūkka moṭumuyaṉ ṟākkam peṟṟōṉ
			ceṉṉaik kavarṉar taṉṉilam vantu
			viruntuṇ kiṉṟa peruntakai yuṭaiyōṉ
			tañcaic caṅkīta vityā makājaṉa
		245.	caṅkam nāṭṭiya maṅkāp pukaḻōṉ
			kiṟittu maṟainū ṉiṟaitta vuḷattaṉ
			āṅkila vāṭciya raṟintuyar vaṇmai
			paṇittarāv cāhip paṭṭam puṉaintōṉ
			ākinaṟ ṟañcaiyi lamuteṉa vōṅkum
		250.	āpira kāmeṉu maruntavat tōṉē.

taṉiyaṉ.
	kāmaṇakkun tañcaivaḷa rāpirakām paṇṭitaṉaṟ kalaika ḷāyntu
	pāmaṇakka viyaṟṟukaru ṇāmiruta cākaranūṟ paṭikki liṉpa
	nāmaṇakkuṅ kēṭpavartañ cevimaṇakkum piḻaikkoḷkai naviyu muḷḷiṟ
	ṟēmaṇakku micainūliṉ ṟēṇmaṇakku maṇakkumiṟai tirunā raṉṟē.
poruḷ muṭimuṟai.
	

tañcai kalyāṇa cuntaram aiskūl tamiḻt talaimaip paṇṭitar,
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi  L.ulakanātapiḷḷai avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
nēricai yāciriyappā.
			pūviṉ maṇameṉa veḷḷiṉu ṇeypōl
			iyaṅkuva nilaippaveṉ ṟirupāṟ ṟiṇaiyilum
			uḷḷum puṟamum veḷḷiṭai yiṉṟi
			nīkkaṟa niṟainta pōkkaṟu moruvaṉ
		5.	oppuyar vikanta varuḷā lolikeḻum
			āḻi māṉilam vāḻiya veṉṉap
			pakaikkaḷi ṟuḷḷam pataipatait toṭuṅka
			maṭaṅkaṟ paṭāmeṇ ṭicaiyiṉu nuṭaṅka
			urukeḻu mērit tirumaka ṭaṉṉōṭu
		10.	nantā veṉṟi aintām jārjumaṉ
			ariyaṇai mēviya yāṇṭē ḻataṉil
			varamiku kiṟittup paramaṉ piṟantapiṉ
			pattoṉpa taṭukkiya nūṟṟup patiṉā
			ṟāṭṭaiyi lirunāṉ kāan tiṅkaḷil
		15.	oṉṟoḻi yirupati lirupatiṟ kūṭiya
			mañciṉanta tavaḻu miñcicūḻ tañcai
			caṅkīta vittiyā capaikkaḷa mataṉil
			vayavā ḷuḻavaṉ cayamā kīrttiyaṉ
			nilattaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiya ṉirīiya
		20.	paṭutirai vāykkoḷu naṭuva ṇavaikkaṇ
			akattiya ṉōṭirun taruntami ḻāynta
			tavalaruṅ kēḷvit tuvaraik kōmāṉ
			ticaimayak kaṟāta vicaippula vōrkaṭam
			piṇakkanta tīrtticai nuṇukkaṅ kāṭṭuvāṉ
		25.	mēviṉaṉ pōṉmeṉa nāṉila micaippat
			tiruvuṅ kalviyu maruvati kāramum
			oruṅku paṭaitta peruntakai vicumpiṉ
			vāṉavar kōmāṉ maṇikkalap pēḻaiyiṉ
			vāytiṟan teṉṉa vayaṅkiṭu parōṭā
		30.	māṭcimai mikka cūḻccicā lamaiccar
			vī.pi. mātava rāv. si.i.i.
			akkirā caṉatti lamarntiṉi tiruppa
			iyalu micaiyuṅ kaṇitamu meṉṉu
			muttuṟai pōkiya vittakar kuḻīi
		35.	nuṇṇitiṟ ṟerintu paṉmuṟai viyappak
			kaḻaka miraṇṭuṅ kārkōḷ koṇṭapiṉ
			aruntami ḻicainūl peruvaḻak koḻiya
			mūṉṟāṅ kaḻakamu muṭinta piṉṟai
			iḷaṅkō vaṭika ḷuḷaṅkaṉin turaitta
		40.	tēṉpaṭu cuvaiyiṉ mūṉṟutamiḻ virāya
			uraiyiṭai yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷil
			ilaimaṟai kāyeṉa maṟaipaṭak kiṭanta
			icainuṇuk kaṅka ḷacaivi lūkkamuñ
			curuḷiye maṭikaḷ karuṇā ṉantar
		
45. 	kāṉmalar poḻinta karuṇaiyuṅ kāṭṭa
			yāṇṭupala kaḻiyat turīik kaṇṭu
			centamiḻ nilattoṭu muntuṟu makkaḷ
			vaḻivaḻi yāka muṟaiyiṉiṟ payiṉṟa
			palliyan taḻuum pāṭali ṉiyalpum
		50.	nāṟperum paṇṇeṉu nālvakai yāḻataṉ
			pāṟpaṭu nāṉmaic cātiyiṉ ṟiṟaṉum
			paṇṇoṭu paṇṇiyam paṇṭiṟan tiṟattiṟam
			eṉṉum peṟṟiyiṟ paṇṇi ṉīrmaiyum
			ciṟumai perumaik kiruvaram peyti
		55.	aintiṉu mēḻiṉum vantiṭu neṟiyiṟ
			paṉṉiru vakaippaṭūum pālaiyi ṉērmaiyum
			munnāṉ kiraṭṭi kēḷviyu mavaṟṟiṉu
			nuṇṇiya vāya curutiyum viḷakkum
			āya mutalā vaṭṭamī ṟākap
		60.	pālaikaḷ varaitaru pāṉmaiyum piṟavum
			evva nīṅki yettakai yōruṅ
			kaippaṭu nelliyiṟ kāṇat tokuttu
			mēṟkōṇ mutalā vēṟpaṉa tōṟṟip
			piṟarmataṅ kaḷaiit taṉkō ṇirīip
		65.	parīik kaḻaka mirīiyaraṅ kēṟiya
			pāṇṭiya reṉṉa mēvi yīṇṭavai
			iṉṉicai māṭciyi ṉiyalpuṇa rāru
			mayalaṟa nāṭi māṇporu ṭēṟak
			karuṇā ṉanta cākaram eṉumicai
		70.	yuraicā ṉūlaraṅ kēṟṟiṉaṉ puraitapu
			teṉṉavar nāṭu ceytamā tavattāṉ
			muraṇpakai paṉikku maraṇṭaku kāppi
			ṉāvaloṭu peyariya vūrvaṭa karaikkaṇ
			nalaṉoruṅ kuṟṟeṉa naṇṇiya cīlaṉ
		75.	tamiḻmoḻi yēmoḻi tamiḻicai yicaiyeṉat
			tāviṉṟi niṟuvun tamiḻnar kōmāṉ
			tāḻicai vaṇṭun tamiḻyāḻ muralum
			ēḻicaic cūḻa liṭameṉa vāḻvōṉ
			mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟirintu
		80.	pālai yāmeṉum pāṉmai vaṟitākap
			payiṉiṉ murampuṭaip pālai tirittu
			vaṇṭu varipāṭat taṇpō talarntu
			tātun taḷiru mētakat tuvaṉṟiya
			palpūñ cōlaiyum payaṉṟaru maraṉum
		85.	nāṇmalark koṭikaḷu naṉantalai mayaṅka
			mayiloṭu kuyilkūuṅ kuralum payilap
			paṉṉiṟaṅ kañaliya viṉcuvaik karumpum
			kaḷiṟu māykkuṅ kaḻaṉiyuñ ceṟitara
			mullaiyu marutamu meṉṉac colliyal
		90.	karuṇā ṉanta puraṅkā ṇuravōṉ
			orukuṭai niḻaṟṟi yirunilam purakku
			maṉṉavar maṉaṅkoḷu naṉmatip puṭaiyōṉ
			payaṉmaram paḻutteṉa vūruṇi niṟainteṉa
			nayaṉuṭaic celvam piṟarkkeṉa vāḻvōṉ
		95.	illa mutalā vellāp poruḷkaḷum
			aruṭkuru nātaṟ kākkiyoṉ ṟēṉun
			taṉakkeṉa vāḻāt takaimai yāḷaṉ
			tiruttaku marapiṉ maruttunū laṉṟi
			yellām valla paṇṭita ṉivaṉeṉa
		100.	mīppuka ḻulakelā mōṅkum
			āppira kāmpeya raṟiñarperu māṉē.

maturaic caṅka vitvāṉum, cīkāḻi luttaraṉ haiskūl tamiḻppōtakāciriyarumākiya, paṟaṅkippēṭṭai,
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi muttuttāṇṭavarāyapiḷḷaiyavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyavai.
		matikku micaippāṇa mānilat tempōṟ
		katikkum varaiyatariṟ kāṉpōyt - tutittakkāṟ
		ṟañcai nakarāḷ taruvaṉā ṉāpirakām
		tañca muṉakkut tarum.	
pāṭāṇṭiṇai, pāṇāṟṟuppaṭai. (1)
		malaicēr matiya vataṉattar kūttiṟ
		ṟalaicēr talaivanī cāriṉ-nilaicēruṅ
		kāraṉṉa tañcaiyar kāvala ṉāpirakām
		tāraṉṉa vākkan tarum.	..., 
kūttarāṟṟuppaṭai. (2)
		taraikoḷ peruvaḷat tañcaittēñ cāriṉ
		maraikoḷ tamiḻaṇaṅkiṉ māṇa - viraikoḷ
		aruḷīn taḷikku mavaṉā pirakām
		poruḷīn tuvakkum pukaḻ.	..., 
pulavarāṟṟuppaṭai. (3)
		matiyam vataṉan tāṉā matiyak
		kayalpāy taṭattuk kuvaḷai kaṇṇā
		viyalara viṉpaṭa makalviḷak kākap
		parampara ṉaḷippap paṟṟiṉai yiyalaṇi
	5.	yeṉiṉu miṉippaṇ viṟalī yiyamputu
		naṉimakiḻ tuṭavai naṟumatu tuḷippak
		kaḷivaṇ ṭāṭuṅ kāmar tañcai
		mānakarc celīi yāṇṭuḷa kōmā
		ṉāṭalcā ṉāyakaṉ kūṭalam patikka
	10.	ṇaracu purīip paracumut tamiḻaic
		caṅkat tirīi yiṅkitap paṭuttiya
		vaḻutiyar ceḻiyar vaḻivarun tōṉṟa
		liyalē yicaiyē yiyalunā ṭakamē
		paṉṉaru mavaṟṟiṉ paṉṉiru pālē
	15.	yakattiya ṉikarppac cakattiṭai yāyupu
		muppā ṉūṟku mippār paravu
		miyalpiṉ karaiyaip payilvuṟuṅ kalaiñaṉ
		āpirā muyariya vappa ṉeṉṟum
		āpira kāmpal liṉattiṉark kappa
	20.	ṉeṉṟum vallavar coṉṉavap paricē
		naṟṟamiḻ nayattai yuṟṟanam marapō
		ruṇartara vuraittaliṟ kuṇañceṟi periyō
		ṉiruṅkaṭa luṭīiya perumpuvi vakuttup
		purantaruḷ tūyōṉ varantaru pōḻti
	25.	ṉuṉṉaip periya vuṟaviṉa ṉākkuva
		luṉṉiṟ puṭavi yuyartavar yāvarum
		naṉṉaya muṟuva reṉṟavam māṇpē
		nammavark kittamiḻ nalamelām pakarvō
		ṉiruvē ṟulakat tiyaṟkai tiruvēṟu
	30.	teḷḷiya rātalum vēṟeṉat tēva
		rōtiya māṟṟa muvappa nītiyi
		ṉiruporuṭ ceṟivum viraviya cemmal
		tampiyar tanayar taṉṉil lattuṇai
		tumpuru viyakkun tuppuṟu maṭantaiyar
	35.	marakata maṉṉa marakata valliyuṅ
		kaṉakanēr ciṟappiṉ kaṉaka valliyuñ
		cevviti ṉillaṟañ ceḻippa vuṟuvōṉ
		pāvu mirāca pattiyiṉ mutirntu
		rāvu cāhipu mēviya paṭṭaṉ
	40.	icaiyi ṉuṇukka micaikeḻīip payiṉṟu
		peruṅkāp piyameṉa varuṅkalai vāṇar
		cāṟṟukar ṇāmirta cākaran taṉṉai
		mātava ṉaṇaiyacīr mātava rāya
		makipaṉ parōṭā maṉṉavaṉ pāṅka
	45.	ṉavaikkaḷat tiruntiṅ karaṅkēṟṟu nipuṇaṉ
		viruntōm puntava ṉaṟuntēṉ ṟārāṉ
		palvaḷa nilaṉuñ colvaḷat toḻilu
		māṭa māḷikai kūṭakō puramu
		mamaitarat tañcaiyiṟ kamaipeṟu kuṇattu
	50.	nāṟkavi rāyarai mēṟpaṭap purappōṉ
		āpira kāmeṉa mēṟpula meṅku
		mīta licaiyi ṉicaintōṉ kīrtti
		yāṭi yuḻuvaliṟ pāṭip paṭartiyēṟ
		kaḻakañ cātta viḻaiceya laṇiyuṅ
	55.	koṭuppa milaintu viṭuppa vuvantu
		pūtta koṭiyiṟ polintiṅ
		kiṉṉē varuti yiṟaimaka ḷeṉṉavē.	
..., viṟaliyāṟṟuppaṭai (4)
tirunelvēli, makā vitvāṉ
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi kavirāja nellaiyappapiḷḷai avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
cāṟṟukavi.
cīrārun tañcainaka rāpirakām paṇṭitaṉaṟ celvaṉ caṅkat
tārāṟu tikaḻcaṭaiya ṉoṭutērnta vicainūlait takaiyi ṉāyntē
yīrāṟō ṭīraintāñ curutiyeṉa vicaippavarcol līrntu pōkki
nērāruñ curutiyeṇmūṉ ṟeṉattamiḻnūṉ muṟaiviḷakki nilaikka nāṭṭi (1)
paṇṇiyalpun taṇṭamiḻiṉ maṟaiyilavai yaṇṇiyalpum palavu maṟṟār
eṇṇariya nuṇmaikaḷu miṉitamuta meṉaviyaintu cālum
viṇṇavarum pukaḻkaruṇā mirutacā karanūlai viḷampi yinta
maṇṇulakil naṇṇumuyar pulavartiṉam pōṟṟunalam vāyttāṉ maṉṉō. (2)
ampācamuttiram, tīrttapati aiskūl tamiḻppaṇṭitar,
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi arikarapāratiyār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
taravu koccakak kalippā.
	1.	kārparavu kaṉamparavak karuṅkōṭṭi liḻitaruveṇ
		ṇīrparavu nilamparavu nīḷporunai natiyōṭuñ
		cīrparavun tamiḻoṭumat tiṟamparavuñ cittacaṉār
		tērparavu mērparavu teṉpāṇṭi naṉṉāṭṭil
	2.	tāṟṟōṭai vaṉamaruntu taṭappuḻakkaip puḻutiyaḷāyt
		tūṟṟōṭaik kaḷiṟukurān toṭarmalaiyak kīḻcāral
		mēṟṟōṭaic cuḷaipiḷappa viyaṉpalavi ṉiḻitēṟal
		āṟṟōṭai niṟaipeṟucām pavaṉōṭai yāmpatiyil
	3.	īṉṟōriṟ puvipuranta eḻilvēmpaṉ paḻaṅkiḷaiyāy
		ēṉṟōrī ḻattaraciṉ muṭipaṟippa itaṉimittam
		vāṉṟōyuñ cōlainakar nāṭāṇṭa vamcameṉac
		cāṉṟōrkaḷ palaviḷakkuñ cāṉṟārtam perumarapil.
vēṟu
	4.	puvicērap pukaḻantac cāṉṟārta māṉṟakulam poruvil cīrtti
		cevicērap peruṅkēḷvi pālacuppi ramaṇiyappērc celva ṉuṇmaic
		cavicēru mariyāḷtaṉ taṉayaṉiru tāṇmalarkkē tāca ṉākic
		cuvicēṭa mutteṉumpē reytiṉaṉṟaṉ pōṉeṉac ceppu vōṉum.
	5.	coṟṟakavi cēṭamuttu palanāḷu maṉuṭṭitta tūyta vattāṟ
		peṟṟacuku māraṉmuttu cāmiyeṉpā ṉillaṟattiṉ perumai yeytak
		kaṟṟakaṟpi ṉāluyarnta aṉanaṭaiyaṉ ṉammāḷaik kaṭima ṇattil
		uṟṟaṉaṉīṇ ṭavarkkutitta makkaḷeṇmar tamiṉmutalā yutittō ṉeṉṟum.
	6.	kalaikūrnta lārṭupeṇṭu laṇṭārtar lāliyeṉuṅ kavarṉar kaṭkun
		talaikūrnta ārpiṭaṭṭu peṭpōrṭṭāṅ kalaikṭarukkuñ cakāya māṉa
		nilaikūrnta nitittalaimai eytiyacā mikkaṇṇu nipuṇa rukkum
		alaikūrnta talampukaḻum āṉarapiḷ civacāmi aiya rukkum.
	7.	caṇmukamey ñāṉacivā cāriyarāñ cuvāmikaṭkum cārntārk kellān
		taṇmukamē tarumoruti vāṉmāta varāvavarkkum taṉṉē ruṟṟār
		uṇmukanī raṟiyūki kamiṣaṉarām parāṅkucanā yuṭukā rukkum‘
		maṇmukamē luṇmaiyiṉiṉ ṟeṇmaiyunīṅ kātuneṟi yaccel vōrkkum.
	8.	iṉṉicainūṉ muṟaiterintu maṉṉicaiyai evarcevikku miṉitu peytu
		taṉṉicaiyai vaḷarttiṭumut taiyaṉeṉum pākavatar talaiva ṉukkum
		miṉṉicaimāṟ ṟāyirañcē raparañci yoḻukkameṉa miṭaṟṟiṉ cevvit
		teṉṉicaiyai yicaikkumakā liṅkaṉeṉap peyarpūṇṭa cīrā ḷarkkum.
	9.	orutaivañ civamateṉa orumoḻiyun tamiḻateṉa vulaku ṟutti
		virutainilai nāṭṭiyacīr virutaiciva ñāṉiyarkkum vitta kattōr
		irutayamān tamiḻāyntu vacaṉāti nāṟkaviyi ṉiṉiya nūlkaḷ
		porutalaṟap palavicaitta arikarapā ratiyeṉṉap pukalu meṟkum
	10.	paruttaparum pātakattiṉ uyirnīttup piṟakeḻunta parama ṉārkkē
		karuttutavu paṭivarukkuṅ kāyakaṟpa nilaiyirunta kaṉayō kaṟku
		maruttuvarkkuñ, cōtiṭarkkum iyalicainā ṭakattavarkkum vaṟumai yōrpoṉ
		peruttavarkku muḷaṅkaṉintu nēcikku nēcaṉeṉap pēcu vōṉum.
	11.	cakamēya taṇmaiyiṉā luṇmaiyiṉāl vaṇmaiyiṉāl taṉattā liṉpam
		akamēva vacaṉikku māṟṟaloṭu kalvimati atinuṭ pattāl
		ikamēvu nāṭṭārām nāṭārtam vaṅkicamā miraṇi yaccīr
		makamēruk kōṭṭilvaitta maṅkāta cuṭarviḷakkiṉ vayaṅku vōṉum.
	12.	centamiḻan teḷḷicaiyun tēvarpirāṉ kātaikaḷuñ ceviyē kēṭpa
		kantamuṟu pōṉakamuṅ kaṉakamuṭaṉ pulavarukkuk kaiyē nalka
		vantavartam moḻikēṭṭu vēṇṭuvaṉa vucitampōl vāyva ḻaṅka
		antaraṅka muḻutoruvark kālayamē yākanita mākki ṉōṉum.
	13.	iruḷiyalu mikavāḻkkai yeṉaveṟuttē vaikainati yeḻuntu lāvum
		curuḷiyilē ceṉṟukaru ṇānitiyām muṉivaraṉaic cūḻntu pōṟṟa
		aruḷiyalu mavaṉaḷitta varaṉmuṟaiyē maruttuvaṅka ḷaṉaittun tērntu
		teruḷiyalum vaittiyattāl maṉṉuyiriṉ nōypalavun tīrkkiṟ pōṉum.
	14.	pollāta viṉaipurintiṅ kuyirvāḻtal pāvamelām poruntu meṉṟē
		nallāṉa vuvāttimaiyān toḻilataṉai maṉaiviyuṭa ṉaṉimēṟ koṇṭu
		pallāṉa nakaruṟaintu cōḻarkaṭan talainakarāp pakarā niṉṟa
		allāṉa cōlaiyuṭai cūḻtañcai māṉakarai aṭuttō ṉeṉṟum.
	15.	aṭuttiṭṭa tañcaiyilē eṭuttiṭṭa muṉviṉaiyiṉ āṟṟāṟ cennel
		maṭuttiṭṭa pūmpaḻaṉap parappiṉoṭuṅ kaṉikaḷellām malkit tūṅkat
		toṭuttiṭṭa cōlaikaḷu mirunitiyu maṇimāṭat toṭarpuñ cūḻal
		uṭuttiṭṭa tōṟṟamuṭaṉ appatikkō rēṟṟamuṭaṉ uyarntō ṉeṉṟum.
	16.	uraitaruvōṉ apitāṉam āpirakām paṇṭitaṉeṉ ṟulakaṅ kūṟum					taraitarumōr tarumameṉu mavaṉappāṟ ṟaraṇiyelān taṉikkō lōccu
		varaitarutōḷ āṅkilacak rātipati yaruṇōkka mariti ṉōkkit
		tiraitarumā ṉilattaḷitta rāvucā yapuppaṭṭañ ciṟappap peṟṟōṉ.
	17.	māṇmikka aṇikalaṉām paṭṭappēr taṉaivaṉaiyav vaḷḷal peṟṟa
		āṇmakka ṇālvarukkum peṇmakka ḷaṟuvarukku marumai yāṉa
		pūṇmikka vaṇitaralpō lāṅkilamum paintamiḻum poliyac ceytu
		pāṇmikka vūṭṭiṭappeṇ makkaḷukkō ricaivalaṉaip pārtta maittāṉ.
	18.	pārttamaitta kāyakaru makārkkaruttu caraḷimutaṟ palavi yīṟā
		ārttamuṟai taṉaikkaṭantaṅ kicaittalaiyu micaiyaṟiyā ranēkar kūṭi
		nīrttavicai nūṟpiṟappu vaṭamoḻikka ṇallātu nilattōr pāṅkar
		pōrttatami ḻiṭamileṉap pukaltalaiyuṅ kēṭṭuḷattiṟ puṇpā ṭuṟṟē.
	19.	paccaṅkat tariyaṟiyāp paraṉaṭatti ṉiṭaimuḷaittup paranta kālam
		muccaṅkat tiṭaik kiṭantu tēcamelān tavaḻntēṟi muḻaṅki mēṉmēl
		uccaṅko ḷicaiyiṉiya muttamiḻi ṉorutamiḻā yulāvak kaṇṭum
		accaṅko ḷātiṅka ṉavaricaittal viyappeṉatta ṉakatti luṉṉi.
	20.	pāṇpiṟanta neṟiyiteṉa atuvaḷarnta viṭamiteṉap paripā litta
		māṇpiṉaru mivareṉṉa maṟṟataṉaik kaiyāṇṭār vakuppī teṉṉac
		cēṇpiṟaṅku navanavamā yirākaṅkaḷ kītāti ceytaṟ kāṉa
		vēṇpiṟaṅkil vaḻiyiteṉac curutikaḷit tuṇaiyeṉṉa virippa vēṇṭi.
	21.	patiṉāṉki yāṇṭaḷavāy manumutalōr vakuttaḷitta paṇṭai nūlum
		vitinūleṇ paratavayit tiyanūluñ cittarpalar viritta nūlun
		tutimēvu mitikāca purāṇamutaṉ matanūliṉ ṟokaiyum vēṇṭum
		nitimēvu mēṉāṭṭār nūlpalavu māyntataṉṟi nirampak kēṭL.
	22.	cāraṅkar uyarpāri jātaroṭu paratarivar tanta nūliṉ
		cāraṅkaḷ paḻaiyatamiḻk kāviyamō ṭakampuṟamāc cāṟṟu nūliṉ
		cāraṅkaḷ tamiḻmaṟaiyi ṉamiḻtiṉeḻu tarumiṉiya caṅkī tattiṉ
		cāraṅkaḷ cattiyavē tatticaiyiṉ virikiṉṟa cārām caṅkaḷ.
	23.	iṉṉavicai nuṇukkamelāñ cenneṟiyiṟ ṟeratteḷintu cintai yellām
		teṉṉicaiyiṉ mayamāka vaṉupavittav vaṉupavattiṉ cīrmai yellām
		ennilamun teḷintuyvāṉ uṇmainilai kaṭaippiṭittē iyampa lāṉāṉ
		muṉṉanaviṉ ṟiṭṭamuṟai viṉākkaḷukkiṅ kēṟṟaviṭai moḻiyak kēṇmiṉ.
	24.	mutaṉaṭippak kōcikatti ṉiṭaiyeḻunta ticaiyeṉṟu muṉṉai mūṉṟu
		vitamaruvu tamiḻkkaḻaka matuvaḷarppa vaḷarntateṉṟum virumpi nāḷum
		pataṉamuṟak kāttavarkaḷ tiraviṭamū vēntareṉṟum parāvu maṉṉōrk
		kitaṉamarnta tamiḻarkaḷē kaiyāṇṭār annāḷtoṭ ṭiṉṟu meṉṟum.
	25.	carimutalā meḻucuraṅkaḷ nilaimāṟa irākaṅkaḷ caṉikku manta
		varimuṟaiyiṟ cilavicaiyē īṇṭuvaḻak kattuḷatu maṟṟu māṟṟal
		terimuṟaiyi lirākaṅkaḷ pallāyi rattokaiyiṟ ceṟiva veṉṟum
		uriyavanta muṟainavamē yāyiṭiṉum paḻamaimuṟaik kuṟṟa teṉṟum
	26.	avvaḻiyē kītaṅkaḷ kīrttaṉaikaḷ irākaṅkaḷ āḷat tikkāñ
		cevvaḻikaṭ kutāraṇamāyp palaviyaṟṟit tamiḻicaiyiṉ ciṟappī teṉṉa
		evvaḻiyun talaituḷakka vēṟṟaneṟi taṉaippukaṉṟu micaikkōr tāyiṉ
		ovvariya curutikaḷai oruvāṟu kaṇittaṟivā ṉuḷatti luṉṉi
	27.	eḻumūṉṟō ṭoṉṟeṉṟum oṉṟeṉṟum palaveṉṟu moḻuṅkē tiṉṟi
		vaḻumūṇṭa mayakkattāl vakuttavaṭa nūlkaḷiṉai vakaiyiṉ māṟṟit
		toḻumūvil tamiḻmaṟaiyāl paramparaiyāl aṉupavattāl curuti taṉṉāl
		eḻumūtu curutiiru patiṉāṉkē yāmeṉavu miyampuṅ kālai
	28.	kaṭuttacilar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukaṉṟatuvē cālumeṉak kaḻaṟi vātan
		toṭuttiṭalu maṅkavaraic curutiyutti yaṉupavattiṉ ṟuṟaika ḷālē
		maṭuttapiṭi viṭappurintāṉ maṟṟeṉakku mimmuṟaiyiṉ mayakkameytal
		aṭuttaṟintuṅ katainīppa āppirakām paṇṭitaṉā mariya cīlaṉ.
	29.	tāṉīṉṟa taṉayaimara katavalli kaṉakavalli tampāṟ kūṟa
		mīṉīṉṟa viḻicciyarac caṅkīta viṟpaṉikaḷ vīṇai taṉṉāl
		tēṉīṉṟa miṭaṟṟicaiyāl curutiiru patiṉāṉkāyt teḷiyac ceytār
		āṉīṉṟa kaṉṟeṉavē aṟiyātu tuḷḷukiṟpārk kaṟaiva teṉṉē.
	30.	muṉpanta muṟainikaḻnta vicainuṇukka mutaliyaṉa muṭintu pōkap
		piṉpanta vicainuṇukkam puntivaḻi evvaḻiyō peritu tōṉṟa
		aṉpanta muṟākkaḷippi ṉampuviyōr aṉupavikku māṟu ceytāṉ
		iṉpanta muḷamperukku mittalañcey kaimmāṟiṅ keṉṉē yeṉṉē.
	31.	innūṟkiṅ kēṟṟakaru ṇāmiruta cākarappē riyaiya vaittē
		ennūṟkun talaiparōṭā maṉṉamaiccar mātavarāv iṟaimai mēya
		toṉṉūṟka ṇaṉipayiṉṟār niṟaiyavaikka ṇaraṅkēṟṟal ceytāṉ kāṉap
		paṉṉūṟku minnūṉmēm paṭṭateṉat tutimoḻikaḷ pārit tārāl.
	32.	malaiviḷaṅku maṇimāṭat tañcaiyilum tamiḻicaiyil vaṭāta tāṉa
		kalaiviḷaṅku pulavarpalar cirantuḷakka araṅkēṟṟal kaviṉac ceytē
		alaiviḷaṅku kaṭalpuṭaicūḻ avaṉiyelām pukaḻnilavā lalaṅka rittāṉ
		talaiviḷaṅka vutittavari livaṉṟuṇaiyā rirumainayan taṉaippeṟ ṟōrē.
	33.	ilakkaṇamē ilakkiyamē kāviyamē purāṇāti itikā cammē
		talakkaṇuyar tamiḻnāṭṭuc carittiramē vaittiyamē camāti yōkan
		tulakkumoru nūlāti yākamamē cōtiṭamē curuti kīta
		valakkaṇatē yeṉpakaru ṇāmiruta cākaramā vaḻaṅkinnūlē.
maturai
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu.rā.kantacāmik kavirāyaravarkaḷiyaṟṟiya
ciṟappuppāyiram.
	1.	pūmiyaiyōr curapiyeṉap paratamatiṉ maṭiyeṉṉap politeṉ ṉāṭu
		tēmalipāṟ curaiyeṉṉa vaṟiñarpukal vaḷañciṟanta ceḻiyar nāṭṭiṉ
		māmalipūn taṭaccāmpūr vaṭakaraiyān tirunakariṉ māṭci mēvum
		pāmalicīr peṟucāṉṟōr palaruḷḷuñ cāṉṟōṉām pāṉmai mikkōṉ.
	2.	tavamaruvum pālacuppi ramaṇiyamā līṉṟamuttu cāmi vēḷu
		muvamaiyilā vaṉṉammā ḷumpurinta nalantiraṇṭō ruruva mākik
		kuvalayatti lutitteṉṉa vantamutaṟ kumaraṉuyar kuṇaṅkaḷ yāvu
		mivaṉiṭattu vaḷaravaḷarn teṇṇilkalai niṟaivuṟappeṟ ṟilaṅkuñ cīlaṉ.
	3.	meṉmatura vākkuṭaiyāṉ miḷirumuḻu matikkuṭaiyāṉ mēva lārcey
		vaṉmaimiku ceyaṟkuṭaiyāṉ māḻainū laraikkuṭaiyāṉ varaiyā teṉṟum
		naṉmaiceyat tāmatiyāṉ nalamilarai yuḷamatiyā ṉaṉṉūl kūṟun
		taṉmaiyelā niṟaimatiyāṉ ṟakavilviṉai cammatiyāṉ ṟayavāṉ mikkāṉ.
	4.	aintaruvō virunitiyō māmaṇiyō karumukilō vāvō yāvu
		mintavula kiṉiloṉṟā yaṭuttālu miṇaiyākā vīkai mēlōṉ
		centamiḻāṅ kilamāti palapāṣā paṇṭita vicēṭa cīlaṉ
		cantatamum piṟarkkutavi ceyvatilē maṉañceluttun taruma vaḷḷal.
	5.	kaṉaviṉilu maṟavātu kaṭavuḷaṭi malarpēṇuṅ karutta ṉiṉcol
		maṉavamaiti poṟumaiyaṉpu vāymaiviṭā muyaṟcimaṉa makiḻcci nallō
		riṉamaruvi yiruttaleḷi yavariṭatti lirakkamivai yellāṅ koṇṭa	
		taṉataṉikar āpirakām paṇṭita kuṇākara vutāra tīraṉ.
	6.	pūruvattup puṇṇiyattāṟ ṟāṉiṉaitta naṉmaiyelām poruntac ceyya
		nēraṭutta tuṇaiviyāyk kaṟpiṉukkō raṇiyāki nilaṅkoṇ ṭāṭum
		pērpaṭaittu miḷirñāṉa vaṭivupoṉṉam māḷuṭaṉē peritāṅ kalvi
		cīrpeṟappō taṉaipuriyun toḻilēṟṟut tañcainakar ciṟappa vantāṉ.
	7.	taṇṭamiḻi ṉilakkiyamu milakkaṇamum piṟanūlun takavi ṉāyntu
		koṇṭucevi vaḻippukuntu maṉattuṟaintu karumpukaṉi kōṟṟē ṉaṟkaṟ
		kaṇṭamuta meṉaviṉikkuñ caṅkītat tuṟaitērntu kaḷippu mēvi
		yeṇṭicaiyu micaipaṭaittāṉ āpirakām paṇṭitappē ricaival lōṉē.
	8.	nīraritāyk kallumuḷḷu niṟaivatalāṟ pullumilā nilaṅkaḷ vāṅki
		yārumika vaticayippak kiṇaṟukaḷvā vikaḷtōṇṭi yakilat teṅkum
		pērirukka māpalā teṉṉaimutaṟ cōlaivaḷam perukac ceytu
		vāramikka vaḷamaruta vaippākkic ciṟantatoḻil valimīk koṇṭōṉ.
	9.	karuṇaimika niṟaipiriṭṭiṣ makarājar pukaḻōṅkak kaviṉcēr ceṉṉai
		varukavarṉar sarārtar lālimahāp pirapuvivaṉ māṭci yellām
		perituṇarntu rāvcāhip eṉṟakavu ravappaṭṭam piṟaṅkat tantā
		rirunilattil rāvcāhip āpirakām paṇṭitamāṟ kiṇaiyā rammā.
	10.	vātamvayit tiyamyōkam ñāṉamivai kaḷaitteḷivāy maṉatiṟ ṟēṟuṅ
		kātaluṟṟā yiratteṇtoṇṇūṟ ṟeḻupattē ḻāṇṭiluyar karuṇā nantar
		pātatari caṉaicuruḷi malaiyileyti yavaṉaruḷāṟ palavun tērntu
		pōtamikkī rāṟāṇṭukaḷ vayittiyattā lulakamelām pōṟṟap peṟṟāṉ.
	11.	tāṉpeṟṟa piḷḷaikaḷuṅ kuṭumpattār piḷḷaikaḷun tamiḻā rāyntu
		tēṉpeṟṟa cuvaimaliyuñ caṅkīta cāttiraṅka ṭeḷivi ṉōrntu
		vāṉpeṟṟa teḷḷamutan tuḷippavicai pāṭavumyāḻ vakaikaik koṇṭu
		kāṉpeṟṟa nalantikaḻa vicaimaḻaipey yavumpurintu kaḷikkum cemmal.
	12.	uttiyō kampuriya vantupala perunalaṅka ḷuñaṟṟic cīrāl
		nittiyā tipaṉivaṉē yeṉavāḻun tañcaiyilē nikaḻcaṅ kīta
		vittiyā mahācaṉaca paiyeṉupē ravaiyoṉṟu mēvat tāpit
		tetticā mukattiluntaṉ ṉicaivaḷarum paṭiceyumpē rēṟṟa muḷḷōṉ.
vēṟu.
	13.	tittikkuñ centēṉō tēmpākō teḷḷamutō
		puttikku ḷiṇaiyākap poruntumeṉuñ caṅkīta
		muttikku vaḻikāṭṭu muṟaiyaṉaittun teḷivittē
		yettikkuṅ koṇṭāṭu mēṟṟamiku tōṟṟamuḷōṉ.
	14.	kaṇṇaḷavuk kaṭaṅkātu kaṟpakakkāk kaḷaimēvi
		yuṇṇamutac cuvaikkaṉika ḷutavumivaṉ cōlaivaḷa
		meṇṇaḷaviṟ poruntuvatō vicaivēntar kavarṉar muṉṉōr
		naṇṇimika makiḻpūttu nāviṉikkap pukaḻvārāl.
	15.	uṇṇīrō veṉappalarai yoruṅkaḻaittiṉ ṉuṇavaḷippōṉ
		maṇṇīrum viṇṇīrum vaṟṟukiṉum vaṟṟāta
		teṇṇīrāṅ kiṇaṟukallit tēkacukam peṟavintat
		taṇṇīrē pōtumeṉat takkavarcol coṟpeṟuvōṉ.
	16.	illārkkup poṉṉaḷippā ṉiraṅkivantārk kuṇavaḷippāṉ
		pollānō yuṭaiyavarkkup poruntumuyar maruntaḷippāṉ
		pallārkkum vēṇṭuvaṉa pārttuppārt tiṉitaḷippāṉ
		ellārkku mupakāri mivaṉpōlvā rilaiyulakil.
	17.	uṇṭikoṭup pavartamaiyu muyirkoṭuppō reṉparpiṇi
		miṇṭiyiṟak kuntitiyiṉ melipavaruk karumaruntu
		koṇṭutavi yuyiraiyē koṭukkuminta āpirakām
		paṇṭitamāl ceyaṉōkkiṟ pakarvateṉṉō vaṟikilamāl.
	18.	karuṇānantap periyōṉ karuṇaipurin tataimaṉatti
		lorunāḷu maṟavāmai yulakaṟiyap palamaruntu
		nirumāṇam purikaruṇā nitivayitya cālaivaitta
		perumāṉṟāy tantainikar peṟṟiyaṉpal luyirkaḷukkē.
	19.	taṉṟiruppē ruṭaṉērāv cāypeṉṉum paṭṭamoṉṟu
		naṉṟutavuṅ kavarṉarsrī lāliyaṇṇal peyarviḷaṅka
		veṉṟumuyar tarulāli elekṭrikpiras ēṟpaṭuttit
		teṉṟamiḻnā ṭiṉitēttac ciṟappitta peruntakaiyōṉ.
vēṟu
	20.	makkaḷelāṅ kītamuṟaip paṭipayilac curutinilai vakuttuk kūṟat
		tokkavaṇup piramāṇac curutikaḷai yunteḷivāc colanan nāṭṭup
		pukkakī tattiṉilē kītaṅkīrt taṉamrākam poruntap paṇṇun
		takkamuṟai yillaiyeṉuṅ kuṟaiyaṟavē ḻiraṇṭāṇṭu takamu yaṉṟāṉ.
	21.	ōraṟivē mutalāka vāṟaṟivu varaikoḷḷu muyirka ḷellān
		tāraṇiyiṉ makiḻntupara vacamēvak kaṭavuḷaruñ cantō ṣikkac
		cīratikam peṟukīta muttiyaiyuṅ koṭukkumeṉuñ ciṟappait tērntē
		yārumiṉi titaiyuṇara veḷiyiṭuvā ṉoruperunū lākka nērntāṉ.
	22.	annūṟkup peyarkaruṇā mirutacā karameṉavē yamaittā ṉanta
		naṉṉūliṟ ṟamiḻttoṉmai tamiḻuyarvu tamiḻoṉṟē naṟṟāyp pāṣai
		yeṉṉumuṟai maṟukkamuṭi yātaniyā yaṅkaḷuṭa ṉeṭuttuk kāṭṭip 
		paṉṉuperum pulavaruḷaṅ kutukulippa viḷakkiyirup patuṭaṉ piṉṉar.
	23.	teṉṉintiya caṅkītat toṭakkamata ṉuyarvupaṇṭē cekattai yāṇṭa
		maṉṉavarā tarittamuṟai kōyilkaḷiṟ kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ vakutta māṭci
		tuṉṉucilā cācaṉaṅka ḷivaiyivaiyeṉ ṟeṭutteṭuttuc coṟka ṭōṟuṅ 
		kaṉṉalamu tantuḷippak karatalā malakameṉak kaviṉak kāṭṭi.
	24.	parataruṭaṉ cāraṅka tēvarmuṉṉōr nūlkaḷiru pāṉi raṇṭē
		curutiyeṉal tavaṟeṉṉac caṅkanū leṉacciṟantu toṉmai mēvum
		paripāṭal cilappatikā rampiṅka lantaimutaṟ palavaṟ ṟāluṅ
		karutuyukti yālumanu pavattālum viḷakkamuṟak kāṭṭi vaittē.
	25.	aṉaiyapala pirapalani yāyaṅka ḷāṟcuruti yaṟunāṉ keṉṟē
		niṉaivukoḷak kaṇakkukaḷā tāraṅka ḷoṭukāṭṭi nilaikkac ceytum
		iṉaiyavakai yicainalaṅkaḷ paṟpalavum viḷakkiyunal licaival lōrkaḷ
		puṉaiyumapi tāṉamelāṅ kāṭṭiyumiṉ ṉumpalacīr poruntac cērttē.
	26.	coṟceṟivāṟ poruṭpolivāṟ paṟpalavi cēṭamali tokaiyā liṅṅaṉ
		naṟperunū liḵtoṉṟē yeṉavulakat tellavaru nayantu kūṟap
		poṟpumika niṟaikaruṇā mirutacā karanūlaip puṉaintu tantāṉ
		aṟputamāṅ kītavallōṉ āpirakām paṇṭitaṉpō lārval lārē.
	27.	maṅkāta pukaḻmēvum parōṭāmaṉ ṉivvāṇṭu mārccu mātam
		iṅkārum piramikkat taṉmuṉṉark kūṭṭuvitta ellā intya
		caṅkīta capaiyiṉilē tivāṉākum mātavarāv takkō ṉāti
		poṅkārvaṅ kalviyicaip pulamaipaṭait tavarpalarum poruntum pōtu.
	28.	intavuya rāpirakām paṇṭitamāṟaṉ ṟavattā līṉṟe ṭukka
		vantamara katavalli kaṉakavalli yeṉumpeyarppeṇ maṇika ḷōṭu
		muntavuṟṟa peṇmaṇikaḷ vāyālum vīṇaikoṇṭu muṟaiva ḻātu
		cantavitip paṭiyicaittēm poḻiyaccey tavaimakiḻntu cāṟṟap peṟṟē.
	29.	curutiyaṟu nāṉkeṉaṟkuk kaṇakkuniyā yamumeṭuttuc colli yāruñ
		caricariyeṉ ṟiṭappeṟṟut tāṉceykaru ṇāmiruta cāra nūliṉ
		maruvupala viṣayamum viyācamā vācittam maṉṉaṉ muṉṉē
		piriyamuṟa varaṅkēṟṟap peṟṟateṉiṉ maṟṟitaṉcīr pēcaṟ pāṟṟē.
	30.	iṭamutavip poruḷutavi yēvalā ḷaraiyutavi yiṉiya kūṟi
		yuṭaliluḷa piṇiyakala maruntutavi yupakarippā ruṇṭō vuṇṭō
		kaṭalulaki livaiyaṉaittum āpirakām paṇṭitaṉpāṟ kaṇṭōṅ kaṇṭōn
		tiṭacukaca rīracampat tātiyuṭa ṉivaṉvāḻka cekattiṉ maṉṉō.
ceṉṉai veslikālēj talaimaittamiḻppulavar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tiru. vi. kaliyāṇacuntara mutaliyār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
nēricaiyāciriyappā.
			icaivaṭi vāku macaivilāp poruḷil

aṟucīrkkaḻiṉeṭilāciriya viruttam.
	1.	cīrpūtta vimayamalai yeṉappoliyum potiyattiṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟip
		pērpūtta tamiḻamutat toṭumpaḻakik kaṅkaiyilum perumai vāynta
		nīrpūtta porunainati pāytaliṉāṟ palavaḷaṅka ṇiṟaiyap peṟṟu
		nārpūtta periyōrkaḷ vāḻvataṟku nalliṭamām pāṇṭi nāṭṭil,
	2.	pāṟkaṭaliṉ mītukaruṅ kārkkaṭalpōṟ kaṇvaḷarum paramaṉ mārpiṟ
		cēṟkaruṅkaṭ ṭirumakaḷun ticaimukaṉā vamarntaruḷpā makaḷuñ cērntu
		nāṟkaṭalcū ḻavaṉiyuḷōr koṇṭāṭa vīṟṟirukku nalama tāki
		mēṟkaviñar vāḻtaluṟu cāmpavā ṉōṭaiyeṉum patiyiṉ mēvi,
	3.	taṅkupuka ḻuṭaiccāṉṟōr kulattalaimai peṟumuttu cāmi vēḷum
		maṅkaiyaruk karaciyā maṉṉammā ḷeṉumpuṉita mātu muṉcey
		poṅkutavap payaṉākip piṟantamutaṟ kumaraṉeṉap poliyuñ cīrāṉ
		caṅkaniti yeṉappulava ruḷaṅkaḷikkat tarupukaḻcēr takaimai yuḷḷōṉ,
	4.	aṉṉaiyiṉu maṉpuṭaiyā ṉamaitarumiṉ colluṭaiyā ṉaṟivo ḻukkan
		tuṉṉuperum pukaḻuṭaiyāṉ colluṟuti māṟāmai yuṭaiya tūyōṉ
		taṉṉikari lātakaru ṇāṉanta cittaṉaruḷ cārta lālē
		maṉṉupati ṉeṇcitta nūlvakaiyu mataṉporuḷu maṉatti ṉōrntōṉ,
	5.	avartalaimā nilattilaruk kaṉaikkaṇṭa paṉiyeṉṉa vaṭaintōr nōykaḷ
		vilakiyiṭa naṉmaruntu mētakucā laiyumvaḷañcēr cōlai tāṉum
		kulavukaru ṇāṉantaṉ peyarālē kuyiṟṟiyaṟaṅ kuṟaivi lātu
		nilaipeṟupoṉ māṭamali tañcainakar taṉileṉṟu niṟuvi yuḷḷōṉ,
	6.	iyalicainā ṭakamūṉṟu miyaintilaku tamiḻkkaṭalai yiṉiti ṉōrntu
		mayalaṟumam mūṉṟiṉicai nūṟperumai yituveṉṉa makita lattil
		nayamoḻicē ricaippulava ravaikkaḷatti ṉālāṟu curuti nāṭṭi
		viyaluṟuyāḻk karuviyiṭai yuḷḷaṅkai nelliyeṉa viḷakkip piṉṉum,
	7.	muṉṉāḷiṟ ṟamiḻnāṭṭiṉ perumaiyuman nāṭṭiṉiṉmut tamiḻōr vāḻntu
		paṉṉāḷu mattamiḻai yiyalicainā ṭakameṉṉap pakuttuk kāṭṭic
		coṉṉārka ḷeṉṉavataṉ perumaiyumat tūyatamiḻp pāṭaik kellām
		ennāḷun tāyāku meṉṟumataṟ kicaiyapala vētuk kāṭṭi,
	8.	aintuvakai nilappaṇṇu mataṟkuriya yāḻiṉvakai yamaippuṅ kūṟi 
		maintuṭaiya veḻuvakaippā laiyumavaṟṟiṉ vakainalamum vakuttu māntar
		cintaimakiḻn tiṭaviṉikkun tēvārap paṇvakaiyuñ cīrtti mikka
		centamiḻiṟ poruṇṭaicoṉ ṉaṭaicēra vacaṉattiṟ ciṟappa tāka,
	9.	eṉṟumaḻi yātukaru ṇāmiruta cākarameṉ ṟiṉiya pērāṟ
		ṟuṉṟucuvai nūliyaṟṟic curutivallōrk kiṉitākat tokuttu raittāṉ
		naṉṟunama taracarmakiḻn tutaviyarāv cākēppe ṉalamun tāṅkic 
		ceṉṟupukaḻ ticaitōṟuñ ciṟakkumā pirakāmeṉ cīmāṉ ṟāṉē.

ciṟappuppāyiram.
	1.	iṟaivaṉaru ḷilaṅkavemai yāṇṭaruḷu mammaiyappa ṉiyalva lattāl
		maṟaiyuṇarnta mōṉartiṟa lilaṅkumara ṇakaḻimatil vaḷañcēr tañcai
		kaṟaitapunaṟ patiyiltamiḻ ceḻittōṅkap potikaimuṉi kaviṉkoṇ ṭārun
		tiṟaikoḷmaṉa ṉāpirakāñ caumiyaṉcī raṟintapaṭi ciṟitu colvām.
	2.	miṉpūtta makarantam putunaṟavat tuṇcōlai vekuvā yōṅkik
		koṉpūtta vamutameṉap puṭaiparanta poṉṉinati kulavu kaṅkiṟ
		poṉpūtta ceṉṉalviḷai marutanilam polintilaku cōḻa nāṭṭiṟ
		ṟeṉpūtta varacarkaḷāṟ ceḻittāṇṭa tañcainakar ciṟakkuñ srīmāṉ.
	3.	pūmātu malarmātu pukaḻmātu polintilaku poṟpiṉ mikkāṉ
		nāmātu nēmātu nakaimukattuñ colliṭattum nakāri yāṉōṉ
		tūmātu viḷaṅkumaṉat tūymaiyoṭu vāymaiyuḷan tuḷaṅkuñ cīrtti
		tēmātu kāvalpurin teḻiṉmātu viḷaṅkucem piyaṉā maṉṉō.
	4.	āṅkāṅku māṭaniṟai māḷikaiyiṉ ṟokaiyu
			mamaippamaippā muṟaviṉarka ḷamaiyillat tokaiyum
		tūṅkuperuñ cālaicuṟṟil nuṭpamoṭu cuḻalum
			nūtaṉamār calayanti raṅkaṇmāru tattāl
		pāṅkupeṟa viyaṅkaḻakum palamarutak kaṅkiṟ
			paiṅkarumpu vāḻaicuvai yoṭṭumāṅ kaṉikaḷ
		ōṅkuteṅku vakaikaḷuṭa ṉuyarmarutaṅ kaḷilē
			yuyarcālip peruṅkuviya luṉatavarai yāmāl.
	5.	vāṉulakak kaṇkāṭci yintiraṉpōl vāḻum
			vaḷaṅkulavu māḷikaiyil maññaikaḷit takavum
		mīṉulavu cuṉaikaḷilē miñiṟukaḷit tōṭa
			viḷaiyāṭip pacumpulliṉ mītutuḷḷuṅ kāli
		āṉulavuṅ kaṉṟuṭaṉē yantiyelvai yiṉilē
			yaḻakākac cērntoḻuku maṭukkaṇi cevviyiṉaik
		kōṉulavuñ cēṭaṉatu kulicanā viruntāṟ
			kuṟitturaikka lākumeṉuṅ koḷkaiyilnā ṇiṉaṉē.
	6.	varakuṇapāṇ ṭiyaṉṟoṭarpi lapirāma pāṇṭiyaṉ
			vaḻivanta vēṉāti nāyaṉār marapiṟ
		cikaramuḷa vaḻutimuttuc cāmiyaṉṉam māḷcey
			tivyatavaṅ kalināṉkoṉ paḵtaṟupa tiraṇṭil
		virakamaṟu teyvaravut tiriyutti rāṭam
			viyaṉcōma pūrvapakka mīroṉpāṉ nāḷiṟ
		kirakavamaip patilācā ṉucca yōkaṅ
			kiḷarccimaṅ kaḷamataṉiṟ * keḻumiyutit taṉaṉē
	7.	araciyalā rarivaimukurt tañcilaiyi lāral
			amaicciyā makaratti lirākukaṭa kattiṟ
		paracukuru putaṉkētu ravicuṅka ṉariyiṟ

		caracakuṇa campaṉṉaṉ ṟavayōki cīlaṉ

		muracamoṭu yāḻkīta vakaimuḻuṅkun tivviya
			muṉṟilāṉ ṟaṉvantiri mutalvaḷḷa lāmāl
	8.	teyvamuṟaip paṇkaṭeri māvaracaṉ pāliṟ
			ṟikaḻrāṇi raipatūrmā tavarāvti vāṉuṅ
		kaivanta kaṉiyeṉanā ṭakavicaiyiṟ ṟērntē

		meyvanta pallavikaḷ viyaṉpāṭal patti

		poyvanta pulavarpuṟap pōkkiloḷit tēṅkap
			pukaḻmaṅka ḷamvāḻttup pulamaimuḻaṅ kiṉavē
	9.	pantaṉañcey yākaṅkaḷ palavirata māṟṟum pakkuvattiṟ kēṟṟapala caṉaṉaṅkaḷ toṭarpāl
		intaṉañcēr tavantēruṅ karuṇā ṉanta viruṭitava rājayō kiyarpāli liḻuttuc
		cantamā rutamvīcuñ curuḷimalaic celluñ camayattiṟ cārntāryā varuntaṉittu mayaṅka
		vantaṉaicey muṟaimaiyoṭu mātavarka ḷuṭaṉē makimaiyaṇi makiḻvāka vaṉacurattē kiṉarē
	10.	ēkiyavat tavattarpā liḷakumaṉa tuṭaṉē yīṟilāṉ pōloḷiru miruṭiyarpāl viṭukka
		yōkiyav virācamuṉi yuvantivaṉai nōkki yuṇmaineṟi tavaṟiṭā tuyarnilaiyaik kaṇṭuñ
		cēkarañcēr kulamuṟaiyiṟ kuṇaccelva ṉeṉṟun teyvavaḻi pāṭuṭaiya tīravā ṉeṉṟuñ
		cākarañcū ḻulakilivaṉ ṟaṉmaipolin tōṅkat tavānilaitēr mūlikaic caraḷaṅkāṭ ṭiṉarē.
	11.	ivvitamāy maṉumuṟaiyiṟ pirapalamā māṟaṅ kiyaṟṟiyiru ḷakaṟṟiyapiṉ paḵtāṇṭu kaḻittu
		evvamelān tolaiyappiṉ pivaṉulakiṟ kāmā ṟiyaṟṟiviṉait toṭararuppan tolaiyavaruḷ 													purivāṉ
		cevvitēr cikiccaiyaṇi maruntucin tūran teḷipaṟpa mutalāya vilēkiyaṅkaḷ kuḷikai
		avviyami lāmaṟṟan tammarapu viḷaṅka vamaimakaṉā kaccitta roruvaṉaittan taṉarē.
	12.	maruttuvantēr toḻilivaṉāl vaḻivaḻiyēyulakar varuntutuṉpa makaṉṟumaṅ kaḷameyti makiḻap
		poruttamuṟuñ cikiccaipalap purintivaṇē ruṟṟa puṇṇiyaṅkaḷ tiraṇṭupala vūḻimikap peruka
		aruttamiṭa mēvalā tiyavellā mamaintiṅ kaṉpelā maṇimaṇiyā yaḻakamaintu peruka
		tiruttamiñcuñ ceykaiyelān teyvaruṭaḻaikkat tēcavara cāḷmaṉṉar cevviyilvāḻ 													pavaṉē.
vēṟu
	13.	aḷitūṅkuñ curumpārkku muyyā ṉatti laḻakuyarun teṅkupuḷi kōṅku vāḻai
		kaḷitūṅku macōkatti yāccā vātti kāṉmara milantaipaṉai kaṉakaṟ cūram
		muḷitūṅku koṉṟainelli nāval canta muruṅkaicaṇ pakamīntu puṉṉai nārttai
		kiḷitūṅku māppalā vilvam pālai kēḻpuṉṉai pātiriyun tamālamā palavē.
vēṟu
	14.	makara murukkum paṭrōjā koṭicam paṅki vaḷarmalli
		cikara kucumam maṉaracitan tērpam pilmās mātuḷaiyum
		pakara vilañci maraiyāmpaṟ pakarum nīlōṟ palampicaṅkan
		takarak kariya kuḻalārkaḷ taṅkuñ cōlai kaḷiṟcamaiyum.
	15.	kāṇu meḻutā veḻuttataṉiṟ kalanta vaṉēka piriveḻuttun
		tōṇuñ ceṭipūṅ koṭiyuruvan tulaṅku viḷimpiṉ pūṅkoṭiyun
		tāṇu meḻuttiṉ vakaiyaṟaikaḷ camaiyum vaḷiyen tiracālai
		pūṇu māṭi paṅkaḷā pukaḻkal mējai pīrōkkaḷ.
	16.	karumpo ṉaḻakār kēṭṭamaittuk kaitē rūliṅ paiṇṭiṅkum
		arumpu māṭpiras kiḷēsrīmkaḷ alamār maika ḷamaiyuruḷum
		virumpu mulakar kaṭkiṉitāy viḷaṅku nōṭīs pukpāraṅ
		karutum ṭaipskaḷ vārppiṭaṅkaḷ kāṭcik kiyalu maticayamē.
	17.	kāla tēya nikaḻvataṉāṟ kalanta vulakar tamaivāṭṭak
		kōla viṉaiyiṉ toṭarpu toṭṭuk kūṟṟāyvanta nōykaḷvakai
		mūlaṅ kālan terintuṇarntu mutirun tēkak kūṟuṇarntu
		ñāla mataṉil mūlikkai nāṭṭan terinta nayacukuṇaṉ.
vēṟu
	18.	tēṉoḻuku milēkiyamāt tiraikaṭṭuvāti ciṟantaracā yaṉaṅkaḷcin tūranaṟ paṟpam
		vāṉoḻuku mamutaṣar pattumutalāya māmaruntu vakaikaḷvaṭa kaṅkaḷkuḻit tailam
		kāṉoḻuku puṭpavakai paṉṉī rattar kāṇumnō yāḷikaḷkai kaṇṭapari kāram
		mīṉoḻuku vāṉulaka vintiraṉaippōla mēṉmaiyil laṟamnaṭattum vivēkakaṇ ṇiyaṉē
vēṟu
	19.	pūrkku mulaka pāṣaikaḷiṟ polivāy viḷaṅkip pukaḻmalintiṅ
		kārkkum nalantē rarumaiyuya raraṅkat tirunta mūvēntar
		cērkkun teyvac cuvaipaḻuttut teḷinta vamiḻtān tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ
		ṟūrkku maṉātit toṉmaiyiṉait tulaṅka viḷakkuñ cuyātipati.
	20.	naṟavu tuḷikkun tuṇarmaliyu napacayat tōṅkum paṇaiyillil
		uṟavō ruṭaṉē vuṇṭukaḷit tuvakkuñ cīla ṉuḷappuṉitaṉ
		tuṟavōr kāṇiṟ cūḻntaṉṉōr tuṇaiyai nāṭun tōṉṟalaṉāṉ
		puṟavaik kātta puravalaṉpōṟ pukaḻtēr nilaiyiṟ puṟañcellāṉ.
	21.	aṉpa ritaya vuḷakkōyi lārnta paramā ṉantaveḷḷaṉ
		tuṉpa maṉatiṟ ṟaṭṭāta tuṅkat tuṇaiyō ravaiyakalāṉ
		iṉpa niṟaivān tulākkōli liruntiv vulakōr iṭartīrppāṉ
		muṉapi ṉuṇarnta nēyampōl mukavi lāca muṟuvaluḷāṉ.
vēṟu
	22.	kalaipayiṉṟa vaṭivaḻakaṉ kaliyāṇa mukavaḻakaṉ kaṉinta viṉcoṉ
		malaipayiṉṟa peruṅkuṇattaṉ varampayiṉṟa vaḷḷalaṉā uḷamā ṟātu
		nilaipayiṉṟa neṟiyaḻakaṉ nicampayiṉṟa collaḻakaṉ niṉaiviṉ mēlān
		talaipayiṉṟa tulaiyaḻakaṉ tāratam miyamilāc caturaṉ maṉṉō.
vēṟu
	23.	kaṇṇār nutalā ṉaruḷcuranta karuṇai vilācan tērtamiḻiṉ
		eṇṇārn toḷiru miyaltērntiṅ kēkat tuvatti ṉilaiterintu
		paṇṇārn toḷirpāṭ ṭiyaltērntu payiluntāḷa catilayaicīr
		viṇṇārn tiyalkiṉ ṉararāti mēṉmai viḷaṅkac ceyumvituraṉ.
	24.	ulakat taṉāti nilaikaḷilē yōṅki yoḷirun tamiḻmoḻiyi
		vilaki yoḷiru meyññāṉat tiyaltē ricainā ṭakattamiḻiṉ
		ṟilakam pōlu marumaiyiṉaic celuttun teyvac curutilayai
		kalakaṅ kāṇā virākattiṉ kaṇakkait terikkuṅ kaṇkāṇi.
	25.	ūṟu tōṉṟā nilaiyuṇarntaṅ kutikku mēlā mupacāntan
		tēṟu nilainiṉ ṟaṟiviṉmayat tiyāṉan tērciṉ mayamuṟṟu
		vīṟu mirāja yōkiyarkaḷ mēlām nilaitēr vivēkattaṉ
		āṟu terintō ramutūṟum vaḷavāṇ ṭakaiyā riyaṉāvāṉ.
vēṟu
	26.	iyalicainā ṭakattiyalai yakattiyarā ṟiraṇṭākku mērpō rāmal
		ayalāṉā riṭumpaipala vāṟṟimiñcu macaṭṭaikuṇa vakantai māḷu
		mayalāṉār kaṭkelā maṉṉumeḻu tāveḻuttil makiḻnta ḷittāṉ
		kayalpuraḷuñ cuṉaicōlai tañcaivā ḻāpirakāṅ kaṇyaṉ maṉṉō
vēṟu
	27.	poṉṉā lilaku maṉaiyāḷum puviyā lilaku makaṇmārun
		teṉṉā teriya licaināṭa kantēr ceḻippiṉ ciṟappōcai
		muṉṉē viḷaṅku māṭakattiṉ mutita mōṅka muḻakkumeṉil
		eṉṉē teyva mātarkaḷi ṉiyalpai viḷakku meṇṭikkum.
	28.	vāṇi nikartta maṉaiyāḷum valattiṟ kiyainta makaṇmāruñ
		cāṇai piṭitta virattiṉampōṟ ṟakunta muṟaiyiṟ cuticērttu
		vīṇai mutalām vāttiyattil viḷaṅkak kaitēr vititavaṟā
		tāṇai yuṭaṉvī cattaraikkā laraimuk kāliṟ curavamaippai.
	29.	palañcē raṉēka kālattiṟ palitta tavattiṉ paṇpataṉāṟ
		kulañcē rariya māṉuṭattiṟ kuṟikkun teyvak kaṇavakaiyiṟ
		palañcē rīcaṉ pattiyupā caṉaiyō ṭicaiyā ṉantattil
		alañcēr pākki yantaḻaikku marumai yataṉai yacaipparitē.
vēṟu
	30.	caṅkīta rattiṉā kararstāyi yoṉṟiṟ
			ṟakuñcuruti yirupatti raṇṭeṉanic cayittār
		aṅkatamā yamaintapala ruṭkaruttai yakaṟṟi
			yataṟkiyainta kaṇakkālaṅ kamaimēṟkōḷ kāṭṭi
		caṅkatamā yirupatti nāṉkucuru tikaḷē
			cācuvata meṉṉanic cayattakaimai yuṟṟāṉ
		iṅkitamā kiyacukuṇa ṉēntaleṉu māprakām
			iṉṉamuta curutiyiṟṟē riruṭiyeṉa lāmē.
	31.	mutaṟcaṅka muṉpallā yiravaruṭa kāla
			muṉmuḻaṅku māyppālai vaṭṭap pālaiyaiyu
		mataṟkiṇaiyān tirikōṇañ caturappā laikaṭ
			kannāḷi lakattiyaṉār ciṟuperunū lamaittār
		kutarkkamiṟol kāppiyaṉār cārpaṇiyiṟ ciṟanta
			koḷkaiyiyaṟ paṉṉiraṇṭāyk kuṟittaṇinūṟ koṭuttār
		putalvarkuru muṉivarvaḻi paṉṉiruvar ceyunūṟ
			puṇarpaṟiyā viḷampulavar puṟampōntā roḷittē.
	32.	āyappā laiyiluḷḷa veḻupālai kaḷaiyum
			āmvaṭṭap pālaiyī riraṇṭeṉumyāḻ kaḷaiyum
		nēyamuṭa ṉatilnāṉku cātikaḷiṉ vakaiyil
			nēruriya virākaṅka ḷattaṉaiyun teḷivāy
		āyakalai karnāṭa pūrvacaṅkī tatti
			lamaiyicaippā vaḻaṅkunuṭpa māñcuruti vakaiyiṟ
		ṟūyavaṟu patumēlu tāraṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟac
			colliyeḻu tāveḻuttiṟ ṟulaṅkakkāṭ ṭiṉaṉē.
	33.	virumpukarnā ṭakaccuruti viḷainuṭpak karuttil
			mēṉāṭṭār kaḷiṉkaṇakkai mēṉiṟutti yotukki
		arumpumuṟaip paṭinuṭpa māṅkaṇakkiṉ ṟokaiyai
			aṟintukoḷḷum paṭiteḷivāyk kāṭṭiṉatu maṉṟi
		tirumpuñ cā pāmūṉṟi loṉṟukālmā kāṇi
			tērkāṇi yaraikkāṇi kīḻaraiyē yaraikkāl
		karumpāmuk kāṇimun tiriyimmi yēḻu
			kaṇakkiṭumpō tiṟpalariṉ muṟaikaḷaik kaṇṭittē.
	34.	pūrvatamiḻ muṟaiyāku micaiyilā ḷatti
			poruntaccey muṟaikaḷilum naṭañceymuṟai kaḷilum
		ārvavapi nayañceymuṟai koṭṭuṅkaru vikaḷi
			laṇitāṉaṅ kāṭṭiyāḻ mīṭṭumuṟai kaḷiluñ
		cārvētaṅ kaṇitacō tiṭavaittiya caranūṟ
			ṟakumoḻikaḷ vakaicaṅkī tattiluḷa teṉṉap
		pārvaipeṟuṅ kaṇakkukaḷiṟ kaitērnta nipuṇaṉ
			pākkiyaṉāp pirāmpaṇṭi taṉṟiṟamai palavē.
	35.	ērpōṅkun tūlacūk kumakāra ṇattiṟ
			kiyalyāḻi ṉamaippukaḷmā ṉuṭatēkat tamaippāyc
		cārpōṅkuñ curuticuraṅ kāraṇatē kattiṟ
			ṟakuṅkaṇakki ṉōcainilai yalaikaḷaḷa voṭumuṉ
		pūrvatamiḻ makkaḷ ṉātiyiṉi lāciyā
			pukaḻturukki yaiccārnta mespittō miyāvil
		ārvamuṭaṉ pāpilō ṉiṉivēyā mutalā
			mamaināṭu kaḷiṟceṉṟaṅ kamaittārpaṭ ṭiṉaṅkaḷ.
	36.	kiramamuṟaip paṭivekuvā yaracāṇṭa vaṉṉōr
			kiruṭṭiṉaṉkoḷ maccāva tāravaṭi vataṉai
		piramaiyilā voḻukkamuṭaṉ pūcitta tavantēr
			piratāṉa tamiḻmakkaṭ peruntokaiyā reṉṟuñ
		ciramamilā mīṉvaṭivāṟ kāppāṟṟap paṭṭōr
			ciṟappārcat tiyavirata pāṇṭiyaṉṟeṉ kūṭaṟ
		piramanilai terintāṇṭu mīṉkoṭiyai yuyarttum
			pirapalapāṇ ṭiyareṉṉap perituvakkiṉ ṟaṉarē.
	37.	iraṇṭāmā yiravaruṭa muṉṟamiḻnūṟ kaḷilē
			yēpirēyu cītyakirīk jarmaṉcāṉs kirīṭiṟ
		ṟiraṇṭamoḻik kalappukaḷaic ciṟituṅkaṇ ṭaṟiyāc
			ciṟappataṉāṟ ṟamiḻmoḻiyē mutalmoḻiyān takaimai
		taraṇimuḻu tōṅkivaḷarn tulaviyatoṉ maiyiṉāṟ
			ṟaṉitteyva moḻiyeṉat tavamakimai yuṟṟōr
		iraṇiyampōṟ pōṟṟutiru mantirantē vāram
			iyaṟṟiruvā cakavaruṭpā vēṟṟattiṟ palavē.
	38.	iyalamaippāṟ pirāṇikaḷi leḻumolika ḷellā
			mēṟkaṉavē tamiḻeḻuttā leḻutoṉiyaik koṇṭum
		ceyaṉmikumōr kacaṭatapa veḻuttukkaḷ ciṟappiṟ
			ṟērntaviṉa moṉṟākit teyvamoḻi yāki
		ayalākum pāṣaikaḷiṉ ṟuṇaiyetirpā rāmal
			aṟitaṟkum payilutaṟku matikavilē cāki
		mayaloḻiyat tutittaṟku mamiḻtiṉuntēr teyva
			vallapattait tarumoḻiyāy vaḻaṅkivanta tituvē.
	39.	kāraṇakā riyantērntu katimaiyara cāḷuṅ
			kaikkuvār makārājā kaṉakaparō ṭāvil
		āraṇacaṅ kītavitya kāṉparasi laruṭēr
			āpirakām kiḻattiyammāḷ pākkiyavarun tatiyum
		pūraṇacaṅ kītavitya vātyavī ṇaikaḷiṟ
			poruttamuṟap pāṭuṅkiṉ ṉaramituṉa makaṇmār
		tāraṅkoḷ marakataval liyumavaṇēr cakaca
			catittuvaṅkoḷ kaṉakavallik kiṇaiyāva revarē.
	40.	aṉṉāṇīḷ tavantēṟuñ cuntarapāṇ ṭiyappēr
			amaiputalvaṉ aṇiyicaiyiṟ ṟumpuruva raṉṉāṉ
		poṉṉārnta turairāja marumakaṉpāṇ ṭiyaṉum
			pukaḻpāka vatarāmpañ cāpakēca vācāṉ
		teṉṉāri rājacapai yiṉilāyap pālai
			tirikōṇa caturavaṭṭap pālaippaṇ kaḷaiyum
		maṉṉārntu pukaḻkūṟa makiḻperiyar vāḻttu
			valamaṅka ḷampāṭi varaiyaṟaiyuṟ ṟaṉarē.

caivacittānta makācamāja attiyaṭcarum, ceṉṉai meykaṇṭa cantāṉa capaikkāriyatariciyum, cempiyam hāṉareri mājesṭirēṭṭumākiya
paṇṭitaratṉam. makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi puḻalai tirunāvukkaracu mutaliyāravarkaḷiyaṟṟiyatu.
aṟucīr viruttam.
	pañcanati cūḻtañcai mānakarcey mutiyatavap payaṉāy vantu
	mañcaṇiyum vāṉulaka maruttuvaruṅ kaṇṭuvakka vakaiyiṉāyntu
	tañcameṉa vantaṭaivārk kañcaleṉa nōynāṭit tavirkku mēlām
	viñcaimiku muyarkaruṇā ṉantaṉavaṉ vaittiyattiṉ mikunta celvaṉ.(1)
	taṉṉikaril kaṭalpuṭaicū ḻulakamelān taṉikkuṭaikkīḻt taṉitteñ ñāṉṟum
	maṉṉiyavāṉ ṟoṭumuṉaiceṅ kōlōcci yaracāḷu makimai cāṉṟa
	tuṉṉiyavāṅ kilanaṟṟā yaṉaiyaturait taṉattavarāṟ ṟulaṅkat tanta
	paṉṉariya mikupukaḻvāyn tilakuperum rāvcāhep paṭṭam peṟṟōṉ(2)
	aṉṉavaṉyā reṉilaṉaittum peṟṟilaṅku miyalpuṟumey yaṟivāṉ mikka
	coṉṉatoru moḻikkumoḻi tittikku miṉimaitarun tūymai yāḷaṉ
	naṉṉalaṅkaṭ kuriyavelām puriyuñcaṟ kuṇaṅkaḷoṭu navilun toṉmai
	eṉṉavuṅkaṟ ṟuṇarntavuyar āppirakām paṇṭitareṉ ṟiyampu nallōṉ (3)
	palakalaikku muyariyatā yucciyiṉmēl vaittumakiḻ parivā leṉṟum
	nilavalaiya melāntutikkap poṟpamaraṟ putamviḷaitta nikari lāppēr
	nilaviṭuñcen tamiḻkkuriya palakōṭi muṟaipiṟaḻā niyāyaṅ kāṭṭi
	nalamikutaṉ perumuyaṟcik kuṟutuṇaiyā rāycciyiṉai naṉku nāṭṭi.(4)
	vaṭamoḻikku muṉmoḻiteṉ moḻiyeṉuntol kāppiyaṅkaḷ valivāc coṟṟa
	tiṭamuṟunaṟ pirapalapra māṇavakai vakuttatoru tiṟamai nōkki
	kaṭaṉikarkkuṅ kalviyiṉai yuṇṭēppa miṭupavaruṅ kaviṉcēr vāca
	aṭalamaruñ ciṟapputaṉai yeṭutturaikka varitariteṉ ṟaṟaiva tōrntu(5)
	pālaiyeva ruṅkaṇṭa vuṭaṉparuku vāralatu paḻippār yārin
	nūlaiyeṭut tōtavali yaṟivumilā nuṭpamatai nōkkuṅ kāṉāṉ
	mēlaivaru vatumaṟiyā vavāvataṉai muṉṉiṭṭu mīkkū ṟuñcīr
	cālaitavaḻn tōṅkukaru ṇāmiruta cākāranūl tantiṭ ṭāṉāl.	(6)
ceṉṉai mayilāppūr seṉtōm kalācālaiyiṟ ṟalaimait tamiḻp paṇṭitarāyiruntavarum kavaraṉmeṇṭil upakāraccampaḷam peṟṟu varupavarumākiya
tillaiyampūrt tamiḻppaṇṭitar sōmayāji
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi vēṅkaṭarāma aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ pāṭiyutaviya cāttukavi
āciriyappā
			ulakiṟ palakalai vittaika ḷōṅkum
			ikkali nāḷiṉ kaviṉai yuraikkiṉ
			aṟaneṟi yaruḷneṟi yaṉaittu muṉṉilai
			māṟip putunaṭai malintaṉa veṅkum
		5.	nūtaṉa vivēkikaḷ nuḻaintār palarum
			pataviyuñ cukamum pōkamum palikka
			maṟṟatai neñcil matiyā māṇpiṉar
			muṉṉilā nōyum muṟaiyilāk kalviyum
			iyāṉeṉa teṉpatu miṇaintu vērūṉṟiṉa
		10.	ittati kaṇṭē yiṇaiyilā varamām
			camayacañ cīvi taraimē leṉṉak
			karuṇaiyē nitipōṟ kaṇṭa toṉṟeṉa
			ānanta veḷḷa matuvē yuruveṉa
			orumā cukuṇarai yutaviṉaṉ paramaṉ
		15.	kaliyumiṅ killai kaṭunō yillai
			āviṉaik kāppatil āvalē mikkavar
			aṟiviṉ mikkavar arumaṟai kāppavar
			taṉvan taripōṟ ṟarainō yaḻippavar
			irupat taintā māṇṭi ṉaḷavum
		20.	kāṭum malaiyuṅ kaḷaittē tirintavar
			paḻanaṭai taṉṉaip pāriṟ kākka
			karuṇā nantarāyk kāṇavē vantavar
			curuḷi malaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya cittarai
			aṭipaṇin tavarpāl muppūc cūkkumam
		25.	 muṟaiyā yuṇarntē muṉiyaruḷ peṟṟā
			roppilāc cittarum ōrurai viṇṭaṉar
			nīyē yulakil nīḷicai peṟuvāy
			maṟṟoru cittarum makiḻntē yaruḷuvar
			āyuḷum perukum aṟivā rāyccit
		30.	 tuṟaiyuḷ mutaṉmai muṟṟuvai niccayam
			cōtiṭam vaittiyam cūkkuma nāta
			vintuviṉ cūkkumam viricaṅ kītam
			āmin nāṉkiṉu maṉaittula kōrum
			pōṟṟuvar cāṟṟuvar puṉitanī yeṉavē
		35.	cantati yuṭaṉē campat tellām
			tāmē vantiṭun taraimē laintu
			kirakamē kātacam kiṭaitta ciṟappāl
			kaṉṉiyē lakkiṉam kaṉacuka nāṉkiṟ
			cantiraṉ cevvāy catiruṟac cārntār
		40.	pakaiyē yulakiṟ pārā rivareṉa
			cūriyaṉ ṟōṉṟal cūḻntāṉ virayam
			cūriyaṉ ṟacaiyē mutalāy nērntatu
			kaṭali ṉeḻunta kaṭumā viṭattaik
			kaṇṭat taṭakkiya kāla kaṇṭar
		45.	pōlavē piṇiyaip pōkka vantavar
			uruttirar taṉmaiyai yulakiṟ kuṇartta
			iravutri yāṇṭi littarai vantavar
			caṅkīta caṅkan tañcaiyi lākkip
			pallāṇ ṭataṉaip pālit tappāṟ
		50.	parōṭā varacar paṇpuṟu capaiyi
			licainā lāṟeṉa vicaivuṟak kāṭṭiṉar
			tañcaiyil vāḻpavar taṅkiḷai yōṭum
			matuvaṉaṅ kaṇṭō maṟṟit tañcaiyil
			eṉavē pulakō rēttuñ cōlaiyum
		55.	ākki mūlikai yaṉaittuṅ kaṇṭaṉar
			rāvcā kippeṉa rāṇiyiṉ pōr
			tanta peyarun taraimēṟ peṟṟavar
			karuṇā mirutamām cākarap perunūl
			icainūl vakaiyi liyaṟṟiya mēlōṉ
		60.	mutaṉmaik kuṟimu muntuṟu peyarār
			āpira kāmeṉu mariyapaṇ ṭitarē.
The Opinion of Dr. Sir. Subramania Iyer, Avl. K.C.I.E.,L.L.D.,
Dewan Bahadur, Retired Judge High Court, Madras.

SIR. S. SUBRAMANIA IYER.					BEACH HOUSE,
								MYLAPORE,
								MADRAS, 9TH APRIL 1917.

Rao Saheb M. Abraham Pandither Avl’s work, Karunamrita Sagaram, is a technical treatise on which it is not competent for me to express any opinionworth recording. This much however must be clear to all, that the subject dealt with has been investigated with great throughness. The work cannot but prove invaluable and though my suggestion may seem strange yet I would venture to add that the substance of his discoveries should. if possible, be made accessible to English readers in a small book. I am sure the author will not find it impossible to carry out my suggestion and add to the great services he has rendered to the community during these many years. I wish his work every success.
(Sd.) S. SUBRAMANIA IYER.
Rao Sahib M. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ eḻutiya karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṟa nūlāṉatu caṅkīta cāstira viṣayattaip paṟṟip pēcuvatāl caṅkītan teriyāta nāṉ ataip paṟṟi apippirāyam colvatu takutiyāyirātu. āṉāl anta nūlai vācippōrukkut teḷivākat terivateṉṉa veṉṟāl, collappaṭukiṟa viṣayamāṉatu pūraṇa ārāycciyiṉ payaṉākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. inta nūlāṉatu attiyanta pirayōjaṉamuḷḷatāyirukku meṉpataṟkut taṭaiyēyillai. nāṉ colluvatu cilarukku vittiyāca mākat tōṟṟiṉālum tōṟṟalām. ateṉṉa veṉṟāl avarkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭitta putu muṟaiyāṉatu iṅkilīṣil ciṟu puttakamāka eḻutappaṭṭu iṅkilīṣ vācikkakkūṭiya yāvarukkum koṭukkappaṭum paṭcattil atika naṉmai payakkum. innūlāciriyarkku itu kūṭāta kāriyamalla. avarkaḷ itaiyum ceyvārkaḷāṉāl kaṭanta pala varuṣaṅkaḷāka jaṉaṅkaḷukku avarkaḷ ceytirukkum pala naṉmaikaḷōṭu ituvum oru naṉmaiyākum. inta nūl taḻaittōṅki naṉmai payakkavēṇṭumeṉpatē eṉṉuṭaiya maṉappūrvamāṉa eṇṇam."

Sir P.S. Sivaswamy Aiyer, Avl., K.C.S.I., C.I.E.,

SUDHARMA,
EDWARD ELLIOT’S ROAD,
MYLAPORE.
Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandither has for several years been an enthusiastic devotee and patron of the science and art of South Indian Music and has been with tireless industry prosecuting an investigation into the number of Srutis in vogue in Karnatic Music. He has also from time to time offered practical demonstrations of his theoris at the various musical conferences convened or attended by him.
Expert opinion may be divided as to the correctness of his conclusions but there can be no two opinions as to the zeal and learning of the author or the value of the example set by him of a scientific study of the theory of Hindu Music. He deserves to be congratulated on the issue of the portly volume named ‘Karunamirthasagaram which is the outcome of his labours in a neglected field.
12-4-1917.						(Sd.) P. S. SIVASWAMY AIYER
Rao Sahib M. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ anēka varuṣaṅkaḷākat teṉṉintiya caṅkīta cāstirattaiyum ataṉ vitikaḷai aṉupōkamāy upayōkikkum muṟaiyaiyumpaṟṟi veku cirattaiyuṭaṉ ārāyntiruppatu mallāmal, caṅkīta vittaikku oru pōṣakarāyum iruntirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷai niccayam ceyvatil iṭaiviṭāta muyaṟciyōṭu uḻaittirukkiṟārkaḷ. ituvu mallāmal tāṉ kūṭṭiya caṅkaṅkaḷilum tāṉ ājarāyirunta caṅkīta caṅkaṅkaḷilum itu viṣayattai anēkantaram rucuppaṭuttiyumirukkiṟārkaḷ.

							(oppam) P.S.  civacāmi aiyar
The opinion of  Dewan Bahadur, the Honourable
Mr. Justice, T. Sadasivier, Avl., B.A., M.L., Madras
A copy of Karunamirta Sagaram by M. R. Ry. Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandither of Tanjore has been sent to me. It is an elaborate treatise on the theory and practice of Music, covering about a thousand pages of the demi-quarto size. One of the many knotty problems that the work grapples with is the exact number of Srutis in the Octave. Whereas Saranga Deva and those who profess to follow him claim to have fixed the number at twenty two, Mr. Pandither seeks to establish that it is twenty four. His conclusion rests upon a scholarly investigation into the art of music in general and that of the ancient Tamils in particular - a conclusion, moreover, whose truth is said to have been verified in the actual singing of his daughters.
It is not given for a Iayman like myself to speak with authority on the relative merits of the two theories. But it may be observed that there are musical experts who have given their hearty support to the theory which Mr. Pandither claims to have discovered. There can be no doubt that those interested in the divine art, especially as it prevails and has prevailed in India, will find the present work highly interesting and instructive.
In the course of his researches into the state of Karnatic Music, the author has collected a body of valuable information regarding the greatness of the language, the literature and the arts of the ancient Tamils. which must prove very helpful to students of the history of Tamilagam.

T.V. SESHAGIRI AIYAR						ANNANDALE
								HALL’S ROAD, EGMORE,

Rao Saheb M. Abraham Pandither is well known throughout Southern India as a gentleman who has studied Indian Music scientifically, and one who has interested the public by his scholarly disquisitions on it. The present great work while it is primarily devoted to the exposition of the science and to correcting some popular misconceptions regarding it, indirectly gives a part of the history of Southern India as far as it is gatherable from musical compositions. The history of Tamil literature is also touched upon in the course of the book.
I have gone through portions of the book and I find the disquistions both instructive and highly interesting. The Pandither has laid the Tamil public under deep obligations to him.
