The opinion of M. R. Ry. T. Chelvakesavaroya Mudaliar, Avl., M.A.,
	SUPERINTENDENT OF VERNACULAR STUDIES,
	PAICHAIYAPPA’S COLLEGE, MADRAS.
Karunamrita Sagaram by Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandithar is a voluminous work in four parts on the theory and art of music. In his systematic and comparative study of the different systems, and from his original researches, the author arrives at the conclusion that the primeval system of the Dravidas (the Tamils) is indigenous and unexcelled. A revival of the study of the ancient classics and their publication have confirmed the status of the Dravidas in the civilization of the old world. Silappathikaram, one of these classics, has been instrumental in determining the age of the Madura Tamil Academy very approximately. The glossary of Jayangondan and the elaborate commentary of a major portion of this epic by Adiyarkunallar have been instrumental in the hands of Abraham Pandithar in proving beyond doubt the scientific development and finish of the Dravidian system of music. These commentaries were written at a time when only parts of some of the ancient treatises on music were extant.
The compilation and array of facts, and patient research in establishing his thesis/ show how the author has accomplished the Herculean task imposed by himself upon himself. Extracts from a number of out-of-the way authors of antiquities afford evidence of the vast erudition of the author and his collaborators.
The book is written in simple modern Tamil prose. Any ordinary student can read and understand the author’s ideas. There can be no doubt that a reader with some knowledge of the theory of music can be immensely benefited by a careful study of the work.
(Sd.) T. chelvakesavaroyan.
“Rao Sahib M. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa karuṇāmirtacākaram eṉṟa nūlāṉatu caṅkīta cāstirattaiyum aṉupava muṟaiyaiyum paṟṟippēcukiṟa 4 pākaṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya vistāramāṉa nūl. palamuṟaikaḷaiyum kavaṉamāy ottup pārttut taṉ conta ārāycciyiṉ palaṉāka innūlāciriyar, tirāviṭar allatu tamiḻaruṭaiya pūrva tamiḻmuṟaiyē ātimutal tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkivantateṉṟum ataṟku miñciya caṅkītam vēṟoṉṟu millaiyeṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭittirukkiṟār.
paḻaiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ piracurañceyyappaṭṭu yāvarālum paṭikkappaṭṭatiṉ palaṉ eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tirāviṭar ātikālamutal kalvi nākarīkam mutaliyavaṟṟil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṉum apippirāyamāṉatu uṟuticeyyappaṭṭatu. intac ciṟanta nūlkaḷil oṉṟākiya cilappatikāramāṉatu maturait tamiḻccaṅkam irunta kālattait tīrmāṉampaṇṇuvatil mukkiya utaviyāyiruntatu. jeyaṅ koṇṭāṉuṭaiya arumpata vuraiyum, cilappatikārattiṟku aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiya uraiyum tirāviṭa caṅkīta cāstirattaiyum ataṉ ciṟappaiyum stāpippa taṟkup paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kaiyil pōtumāṉa ātāraṅkaḷāy akappaṭṭaṉa. caṅkīta cāstira nūlkaḷ anēkam aḻintu maṟaintupōṉa kālattiltāṉ ivvuraikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
viṣayaṅkaḷai oḻuṅkupaṭuttiyirukkiṟa aḻakaiyum taṉ apippirāyattai stāpippataṟku attiyanta poṟumaiyuṭaṉ nūlāciriyar ceytirukkiṟa ārāycci yaiyum kavaṉittāl, taṉ mītu tāṉē ēṟṟikkoṇṭa malaipōṉṟa inta vēlai yiṉ ciṟappu maṟṟavarkku naṉkuviḷaṅkum. taṟkālam atikamāy vaḻakkattilirāta pala nūlkaḷiliruntu nūlāciriyar eṭuttuccollum mēṟkōḷkaḷaik kavaṉittāl, āciriyaruṭaiya kalvittiṟamaiyum avarkaḷōṭu vēlai ceytavarkaḷiṉ cāmarttiyamum viḷaṅkum.
yāvarum aṟintukoḷḷakkūṭiya culapamāṉa tamiḻ naṭaiyil innūl eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. cāmāṉiyamāṉa yāvarum nūlāciriyaruṭaiya apippirāyattai eḷitil aṟintukoḷḷalām. coṟpa caṅkīta ñāṉam uṭaiyavarkaḷuṅkūṭa in nūlaip paṭippatiṉāl atika pirayōcaṉattai aṭaivārkaḷ eṉpatil cantēkamillai."
(oppam) tirumaṇam. celvakkēcavarāyaṉ.
ceṉṉai, piraciṭeṉci kālēj talaimait tamiḻp paṇṭitar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi makā makōpāttiyāyar vē. cāminātaiyar avarkaḷ apippirāyam.

tañcāvūr makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāvsākip mu. āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ ulērakōpakāramāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa karuṇāmirutacākaram eṉṉum arumaiyāṉa pustakattiṟ ciṟcila pākaṅkaḷai uvappuṭaṉ paṭittup pārttēṉ.
teṉmoḻiyilum vaṭamoḻiyilum uḷḷa icai nūlkaḷil mikunta ārāycciyuṭaiyavarkaḷē pārttu apippirāyañ collakkūṭiya puttaka mākum itu. āyiṉum paṇṭitaravarkaḷuṭaiya viruppattai maṟuttaṟkañci eṉakkut tōṟṟiyavaṟṟait terivikkalāṉēṉ.
ettaṉaiyō varuṣaṅkaḷākap paṇṭitaravarkaḷ caṅkītattil uḻaittu varutalaip palarāṟ kēṭṭiruppataṉṟi nāṉ nērilum aṟintirukkiṟēṉ. ivarkaḷuṭaiya uḻaippu vīṇākāmal eṉṟum nilaipeṟṟirukkumpaṭi ippustakam veḷivantatu mikavum pārāṭṭattakkatē.
āṅkāṅkuḷḷa caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷaiyum, paṇṭitarkaḷaiyum varuvittu makācapaikūṭṭi avarkaḷuṭaiya apippirāyattaik kalantum, paḻaiya tamiḻnūlkaḷākiya cilappatikāram mutaliyavaṟṟaic cevvaṉē ārāyntum tamiḻ moḻiyil pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu amain tiruntaṉavum ikkālattu naṉku viḷaṅkātaṉavumākiya caṅkīta muṟaikaḷai yum avaṟṟiṉ pētaṅkaḷaiyum piṉṉumuḷḷa nuṭpamāṉa vaṟṟaiyum yāvarum eḷitil aṟintukoḷḷumpaṭi viḷakkip paṇṭitaravarkaḷ innūlai veḷippaṭuttiyataṟkākat tamiḻulakam ivarkaḷpāl naṉṟipārāṭṭak kaṭappāṭuṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. "muyaṟci tiruviṉaiyākkum" eṉpataṟku ivarkaḷ ceykaiyaiyē utāraṇamākac collalām.
							iṅṅaṉam
	(oppam) vē. cāminātaiyaṉ.
ceṉṉai kavarṇmeṇṭu mātar kalācālait tamiḻppōtakāciriyar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ū.cu. namaccivāya mutaliyār avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
C.R. Namasivaya Mudaliar,						‘Kaveri House,’
Lecturer in Tamil							130, Govindappa Naick St.,
Madras College for Women.						Madras, E.
valampaṭac cuḻalum varivaṭivu vāyntu taṉittiyaṅkum vaṉmai peṟṟu eṉṟum iḷamai māṟātu viḷaṅki niṟkum nantam tamiḻ moḻi toṉṟu  toṭṭu iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttiṟa amaippiṉaik koṇṭatōr mutumoḻiyākum. uṟṟu nērakkiṉ avai oṉṟiṉeraṉṟu iṇaintu muṟaiyē uyarvuṟa viḷaṅkuvateṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. eṉṉai, icaittamiḻukku iyaṟṟamiḻum nāṭakattamiḻukku ēṉaiya iraṇṭu tamiḻum iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavaṉṟō?
ippakutikaḷ iyaṟṟamiḻ, icaittamiḻ, nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅku māṟṟāl tamiḻ moḻiyē mūṉṟu vakaiyāka māṟupaṭṭateṉak karutuvār palar. avai tamiḻiyal, tamiḻicai, tamiḻ nāṭakam eṉat tamiḻiyalum icaiyum nāṭakamum ēṉaiya moḻip pakutikaḷukku māṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkum tiṟattaik kuṟittaṟkup pōnta toṭarkaḷēyāmeṉka. iṅṅaṉam vitantu kūṟutaṟkuṟṟa kāraṇam eṉṉaiyōveṉiṉ, āriya iyalum āriya icaiyum, āriya nāṭakamum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉkaṇ vantu kalanta kālattē tamiḻ makkaḷ avaṟṟaip pōṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭa kālattē avaṟṟiṉ ivai vēṟā meṉpatai uṇarttaṟ poruṭṭēyāmeṉka. āriyakkūttāṭiṉālum kāriyattil kaṇṇāyiru eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum. vaṭamoḻi eṉpataṟku māṟākat teṉmoḻi eṉpatum āriya maṟai eṉpataṟku māṟākat tamiḻ maṟai eṉpatum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉavaṉṟō?
iṉi nāḷaṭaivil iyaṟṟamiḻ oṉṟum nilaittu niṉṟu, ēṉaiya iraṇṭum iṟantu paṭṭaṉa. avai iṟantu paṭṭamaikkup palar pala kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar. kūrntu nērakkiṉ māntaratu vāḻkkaikkum nilaikkum nākarikat tiṟkum iyaṟṟamiḻ iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. icai nāṭakaṅkaḷō veṉiṉ avaratu makiḻccikkum maṉērallācamāṉa kālappōkkiṟkum karuviyāṉavai. nāṭeṅkum ceḻittu nalam paraviya kālattiṉē pāṭṭuṅ kūttum eṅkeṅkum parantu viḷaṅkum. nantam nāṭṭil nāḷēṟa vanta vaṟumaip piṇiyum araciyal māṟṟamumē avai iṟantu paṭṭamaikkuk kāraṇaṅ kaḷākum. ituvēyumaṉṟi, tamiḻar āriya moḻiyai mēṟkoṇṭu avartam mataṅkaḷaiyum, koḷkaikaḷaiyum kaippaṟṟic ciṉṉāḷiṉ piṉṉar tāmum āriyarē eṉakkūṟap pukunta alvaḻic ceykaiyāṉē āriyaratu icai nāṭakaṅkaḷaip peritum pōṟṟit tamakkuṟṟa icai nāṭakaṅkaḷaik kai naḻuvaviṭṭuk karutātiruntamaiyē ēṟṟakāraṇameṉalām. kūtteṉṉum tamiḻ moḻiyai oḻittu nāṭakameṉṉum āriyamoḻi ittoṭarkaḷil iṭam peṟṟatum itaṟkuccāṉṟākum.
iṉip paṇṭait tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil kāṇum icaikaḷum icaik karuvikaḷum ittakaiya veṉṟu pulappaṭuvatillai. antō, avaṟṟiṟkūṟum maraṅkaḷēṉum terikiṉṟaṉavā? malarkaḷēṉum terikiṉṟaṉavā? ētu millaiyē. poruḷkaḷai uṇarttak karuvikaḷā niṟkum coṟkaḷai uṇarkiṉṟōmaṉṟi avai yuṇarttum poruḷkaḷai uṇarntō millaiyē! ivviṉṉal tīra etteyvam etir niṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭumō! ikkālattē tamiḻ maṟaikaḷil kāṇum paṇkaḷiṉ icaittiṟamum paḷiṅkupōl terintapāṭillaiyē!
uraiyāciriyar kālattilēyē iṟantu paṭṭaṉa icai nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ eṉiṉ, innāḷil evaṟṟaikkoṇṭu avaṟṟai ārāyvatu? āḻiyiluḷḷa ariyamaṇikaḷai mūḻkutaṟkāṉa karuvi oru ciṟitumiṉṟi uyirait turumpāka eṇṇi avvāḻiyiṉ kaṇ mūḻki ammaṇiyai eṭuttuk kaṭaintu ulakiṟkaḷikkum uyarkuṇamum maṉavaṉmaiyum vāynta māṉiṭaṉēpōl vaḷaṅkuṉṟāta vaḷḷiyōṉum centamiḻc celvaṉum aruṅkalai vinōtaṉum uṇmaitērum uttamaṉum aṟiñaraip pōṟṟum āṇṭakaiyum nalliyal vāynta naṇpaṉum ākiya oruvaṉ vēṇṭum. aṉṉavaṉē ittakaiya ariya ceykaiyai yamaivuṟa āṟṟiṭavallāṉ. itaṟkut tañcai rāvcākip āpirakām paṇṭitar eṉṉum aṇṇalaik kūṟavē eṉ uḷḷam oruppaṭukiṉṟatu.
nantam paṇṭitar iyaṟṟiya karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum icaittamiḻ nūlaic ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil paṭittuppārttēṉ. paṇṭaittamiḻ nūlkaḷil āṅkāṅkē arukikkiṭakkum viṣayaṅkaḷait tiraṭṭi avaṟṟiṟku ātaravāṉa amicaṅkaḷaik kūṭṭi aiyamaṟa viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. tam matam niṟuvap pala aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiya aṭaivukaḷaiyum mēṟkōḷkaḷaiyum piṟanāṭṭiṉar kūṟum amaitikaḷaiyum taṭaiviṭaikaḷāṟ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. icainūṟ payiṟci yuṭaiyārē tuṇintu kūṟuvataṟkāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi yāṉ ētuṅ kūṟutaṟkillēṉ. icaiyaippaṟṟiya viṣayaṅkaḷyāvum oruṅkē ōriṭattiṟkāṇumāṟu, kāṇpōr kaṇṇaiyum maṉattaiyum kavarum vaṇṇam aḻaku peṟa acciyaṟṟi ariyaviṣayaṅkaḷai iṉiya naṭaiyil ulakukkaḷitta paṇṭitar paṇpu eṉṟum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatām. celvam peṟṟataṉāṟ ceyyattakka ceykaiyum aṭaiyat takka kīrttiyum iḵtēyaṉṟō?
nantam paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kīrtti muṉṉarē eṅkum paraviniṟpiṉum in nāḷil ivvicai nūlāl varum icaiyē iyainta ēṟṟamuṭaiyatākum. ennāḷum niṉṟu nilavum innūliṉ māṭciyē pōl ivartam kīrttiyum niṉṟu nilavumaṉṟē.
tēvāram mutaliya tamiḻ maṟaikaḷiṟ kāṇum paṇkaḷukku ottuvarum irākaṅkaḷai innūlil ōr aṭaiviṟkāṭṭi itaippaṟṟiya nūleraṉṟu viraivil veḷivarum eṉṟu paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kāṭṭiya kuṟippaikkaṇṭu perumakiḻvaṭaikiṉṟēṉ. avvakaiyil tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ maṉattai viraivil makiḻvippārāka.
innūl peritum tamiḻicaiyaip pōṟṟa vantatākum. āyiṉum itaṟkuk karuṇāmirutacākaram eṉappeyar puṉaintaṉar. itu tamatu ñāṉaciriyarām karuṇāṉantar karuṇaiyaittuṇaiyākak kōṭaṟkuk kuṟippāṉ oṟṟumai nayampaṭavaitta peyarātal viḷaṅkum. āyiṉum tāmiyaṟṟiya nūliṉ uḷḷuṟai teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅka icait tamiḻnūl eṉṉum karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉakkūṟiṉ cāla amaivuṭaittākum.
(oppam) kā. namaccivāyaṉ
The Opinion of Mr. J.S. Chandler
	Chairman, Tamil Lexicon Committee.
	ROYAPETTAH

I am very much interested in the work of Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandither of Tanjore in developing the theory and practice of Karnatic Music.
A glance at the table of contents makes one wish to read it. It is well worthwhile for scholars like him to do much work, and in the clash of expert opinions a solid basis will be found for treating these subjects.
His claims for Tamil as a language are large, but they are worth careful study.

								(Sd.) J.S. CHANDLER.
"karnāṭaka caṅkīta cāstirattaiyum atai upayōkikkum muṟai yaiyum cīrtiruttuvataip paṟṟip pēcukiṟa Rao Sahib 
M.  āpirakāmpaṇṭitar avarkaḷuṭaiya nūliṉ mēl eṉakku attiyanta pirīti uṇṭākiṟatu. poruḷaṭakkattaip pārttālē atai vācikkavēṇṭu meṉṟa avāvuṇṭākiṟatu. avarkaḷaippōlak kalviyil tērcci yuḷḷavarkaḷ anta vēlaiyaic ceyvatil atika naṉmaiyuṇṭu ippērp paṭṭa viṣayaṅkaḷaippaṟṟip palarum pala apippirāyam collip pōr purintāl tāṉ naṉmai piṟakkum. tarkkikkappaṭum viṣayattiṟkum tiṇṇamāṉa ātāram ēṟpaṭum.
tamiḻp pāṣaiyaippaṟṟi ati cilākkiyamāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷaic collukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl avaikaḷ kavaṉittu vācikkat takuntavaikaḷ."
(oppam) J. S.  cāṉṭlar.
tamiḻ leksikaṉ kamiṭṭit talaimaip paṇṭitar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷ apippirāyam.
tañcai rāvsāhip āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ eḻutiya "karuṇāmirta cākaram" eṉṟa caṅkīta kōcattaip pārvaiyiṭalāṉēṉ. itu nāṉku pākamākap pirikkappaṭṭu 1184 pakkaṅkoṇṭa oru peru nūlākavuḷḷatu. itara caṅkīta cāstirattiṉ āti carittiraṅkaḷum curutip pākupāṭukaḷaippaṟṟiya vaṭanūṟ kuṟippukkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ paṇṭaikkālattil vaḻaṅkiya icait tamiḻ viṣayaṅkaḷiṉ ārāyccikaḷum avvicaittamiḻ muṟaippaṭi amaiyum curutikaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷum piṟavum innūluḷ virittuk kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷilum nikaṇṭukaḷilum icainūṟ pakuti kaḷākak kaṇṭa vaḻakkukaḷai yellām ciṟanta ārāycciyuṭaṉ paṇṭitaravarkaḷ viḷaṅkacceytiruppatu mikavum pukaḻattakkatām.
cilappatikāram araṅkēṟṟukātai mutaliyavaṟṟil iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum aṭiyārkku nallār arumpatavuraikārarkaḷum eḻutiya icaittamiḻ viṣayaṅkaḷellām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ eḻutiya innūlāl karatalā malakamāka viḷakkamuṟuvataik kāṇalām. vaṭanūlkaḷilum, paḻaiya icaittamiḻ nūlkaḷiluṅkaṇṭa curutikaḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷaip paṇṭitaravarkaḷ nuṭpamāka ārāycci ceytiruppataippaṟṟi, icai nūlaṟivupōtā empōliyar muṭivu colla iyalātāyiṉum, icaittamiḻ viṣayamāṉa ariya periya nuṭpaṅkaḷait tam aṟivāṟṟalāl kaṇṭu paṇṭitaravarkaḷ ittakaiya nūlai mutaṉ mutalāka veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu tamiḻp pāṣaikkuc ceyta oru pērupakāra meṉpatil taṭaiyillai.
karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum peyarkkēṟpa muṟkālattum piṟkālattu muḷḷa caṅkīta viṣayaṅkaḷellām innūliṭaip parantu kiṭattalāl, itu tamiḻmakkaṭku oru periya nitiyē eṉṉalām. ittakaiya ariya peruṅ kāriyattaic ceytu tamiḻ nāṭṭārkku upakaritta paṇṭitaravarkaḷ, nammavaratu pōṟṟaṟkum pukaḻccikkum urimai pūṇṭu, itupōlum peruṅ kāriyaṅkaḷaic ceytu nīṭūḻi vāḻat tiruvaruḷaic cintittu vantirukkiṉṟēṉ.
						(oppam) mu. irākavaiyaṅkār.
u
tirucciṟṟampalam.
ceṉṉai, muttiyāluppēṭṭai haiskūl talaimait tamiḻppulavar maṇimaṅkalam
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tirunāvukkaracu mutaliyār avarkaḷ eḻutiya
ciṟappuppāyiram.
	ātiyiṟ ṟamiḻnūl akattiyark kuṇarttiya
	mātoru pākaṉai vaḻuttutum
	pōta meyññāṉa nalampeṟaṟ poruṭṭē
tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē moḻiyiyal varampu muṟṟum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya taṉic ciṟappu vāynta teṉpatu naṭunilai tiṟampā ārāycciyāḷar yāvarāṉum oruṅku oppukkōṭaṟpālatu. itu mikappaḻaiya moḻikaḷākiya ilattīṉ hīpru āriyam mutaliyavaṟṟeraṭu oppakkoḷḷum toṉmaipeṟṟu, avaiyāvum vaḻakkāṟu aṟṟeraḻiyavum iṉṟu varai iṟavāp perum pukaḻ koṇṭilaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉaip piṟantakamākak koṇṭeḻunta kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, malaiyāḷam mutaliyaṉavum oṉṟu pala vāy virintu māṟupaṭavum iḵtoṉṟē vēṟupāṭu oru ciṟitum iṉṟi niṟkum māṭcittāy cevviya viḻuppam vāyppap peṟṟeraḷirkiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ eḻutappaṭṭa ariya periya nūlkaḷāṉ it tīviya moḻiyaip payiṉṟōr ciṟanta nākarikamum aṉṉērar vaikiya tamiḻakam eṉappaṭum nāṭṭiṉ vaḷamum perumaiyum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāṟpāṉ oṉpatu naṟṟamiḻ nāṭukaḷ kaṭalkoḷḷap peṟṟaṉa eṉṉum uṇmai, pulavar perumāṉākiya āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum, piṟatēya carittirakkārarāṉum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉai aṟiyum ovvoruvar uḷḷamum ettuṇait tuṉpattuḷ aḻuntum eṉpatu aṟutiyiṭṭuraikka iyalātu. mēṟṟicaiyil nākarikam eṉpatē muḷaikiḷampāta akkālattut tamiḻmakkaḷ ellā nākarikac ciṟappum, kalvip perukkum, palakalaikaḷil nirampiya aṟivum, nuṇmaiyum āḻamum uṭaiyarāy miḷirntaṉar eṉpatu paṇṭait taṇṭamiḻ nūlkaḷākiya kuṟuntokai, naṟṟiṇai, akam, puṟam, tolkāppiyam mutaliyavaṟṟai oru muṟaip potu nērakkiṟ kāṇpōrkkum iṉitu viḷaṅkāmaṟ pōkātu. ivaṟṟaip pala āṅkilap pulavarkaḷum ārāyntu nūṉmukattāṉ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. pala tamiḻc cāṉṟōrum nūl vāyilākavum paṉuvalāṉum eṭutteḻuti niṟuviyuḷḷār. niṟka.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyiṉai aṟitaṟku ataṉpāl iyaṟkaiyiṉ amaintuḷḷa akapporuḷ iyal oṉṟē cālum eṉka. aḵtu ettuṇai nuṇṇiya āḻnta karuttukkaḷai uṭkoṇṭu, makkaḷatu mutal nilai toṭṭu vaḷarcci muṟai oḻuṅkukaḷait teḷittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. nālvakai nilaṉ vakuttatum avaṟṟukku uripporuḷ kiḷantu kūṟiyatum nuṉittārāyaṟpālaṉa. ikkaṇkoṇṭu pārkkum matukai illār tattamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟē kūṟi orutalaippaṭātu iṭarppaṭuvar. meyppāṭṭuvamam, iṟaiccip poruḷ mutaliyaṉa ettuṇaic ciṟanta nākarikampaṟṟi nikaḻvaṉa eṉpataik karuttūṉṟi yoruṅki yuṇarka. kalaivaḻakku, kāl vaḻakku, koṭi vakai, araciyal, maruttiyalpu, kōḷnilai, kuṟinūl, uṟuppunūl mutaliya yāvum tamiḻar uṇarntavaiyē eṉpataṟku uṟucāṉṟukaḷ pala nūṉmukattiṟ kiṭaikkaṟpālaṉa.
muccaṅkam pāṇṭi maṉṉarāṉ niṟuvappaṭṭut tamiḻ ārāyappeṟṟatu. iyaṟṟamiḻukkuc caṅkam iruntatu pōṉṟē icaikkūṟṟukkum caṅkam viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpataṟkut tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāriṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa ēḻicaiccūḻalpukkō eṉṉum toṭar moḻiyum iṉṉum cila kāṭṭukkaḷum ētukkaḷeṉa pulavar oruvar ārāyntu kūṟiṉār. avvāṟē icaiccaṅkam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku eṭṭuṇaiyum aiyamiṉṟu. kūttu nūlkaḷum pala nilaviṉa eṉpatu uraiyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār eṭuttōtum nūṟkaḷāṉ aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
iṉi, ivvāṟellām toṉmaiyum mellēracaiyum maṉa vamaitikku icainta poruttamum parappum nuṇmaiyum virikkattakka taṉmaiyum piṟamoḻikkalappiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkum pērāṟṟalum ciṟantu viḷaṅkun tamiḻ moḻiyiṉai oṉṟum vēṇṭātār cilar, uṇmai kāṇum viḻaiviṉṟit tamiḻil yātum illai eṉṟu vāy kūcātu moḻivar. avarait teruṭṭutal mikavum aritu eṉka. tamiḻiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvāṉa eḻuttukkaḷ pala iruttaliṉ avaiyāṉ ākkappaṭṭa moḻikaḷaik kēṭṭa tuṇaiyāṉē vaṭamoḻi eṉpar. ellāvaṟṟaiyum āriyam eṉṟu ivar iṉpuṟuvatu eṟṟukku? ivar pōl ēṉaiyarum niyāyamiṉṟik kūṟa muntuvarēl ivaratu koḷkai talaicāykku maṉṟō. ākavē, ovvoruvarum uṇmaiyiṉaip palatiṟattāṉum kūṟupaṭuttu vaittu ārāyntu kūṟutaṟku muyaṟal vēṇṭum. oru cilar uṇmai kūṟuvār pōṉṟu avinayittut tamatu kōṭpāṭukaḷaiyē cūḻcci valiyāl kāṭṭuvar. aḵtu aṟiñar kaṇkaḷukkup poḷḷeṉat tōṉṟum eṉpatai avar uṇarvārāka.
allatūum ēṉai nāṭukaḷ pōlātu nan tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉkaṇ maṭṭum maṟṟeraru peruṅkuṟai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. aḵtu antaṇar tamiḻait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭum ataṉiṟ pulavarkaḷākap polintum atu koṇṭu piḻaittum perumaiyiṟ ciṟantum ammoḻikkaṭpaṟṟiṉṟi iruttalām. ikkuṟai nīṅkiṉ nalam pala palkum. tamiḻ muṟpōntu vaḷarcciyaṭaiyum maṭṭil tamiḻnāṭu taḻaiyavē taḻaiyātu eṉpatu mukkālum uṇmai.
iṉi, piṟkālattuc camaya nūlkaḷ veḷippaṭṭu paṟṟumikkut tamiḻc cuvaiyiṉaip perumpāṉmaiyum kuṟaikkat talaippaṭṭaṉaveṉpatu iḻukkātu. pallāyiram centamiḻc coṟkaḷ viṭupaṭṭaṉa. vaṭacoṟkaḷ kalantaṉa. tamiḻc col oruvaṉ karutiyataṉai iṭattukku icaiya uṇarttuvatāyiruppavum ataṉai viṭuttu vaṭacollaip peyturaippōṉ tamiḻkkup peruntīṅku iḻaittavaṉāvāṉ eṉpatait tamiḻar ovvoruvarum uḷḷattamaippārāka.
tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pāṭṭuttuṟaiyilum, urai vakaiyilum, ārāyccit tiṟattiṉum palap palavākap palkutal vēṇṭum. paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cevvaiyāka acciṭappaṭṭu ellērarukkum kiṭaikkattakka vakaikaḷil vaikkappaṭavēṇṭum. tamiḻar ittakaiyamuyaṟcikaḷ ceytu tam arumaruntaṉṉa moḻiyiṉai vaḷarttu ulakiṉpuṟakkaṇṭu, tāmum iṉpuṟuvārāka.
ikkālattut tōṉṟiyuḷḷa cila kiḷarccikaḷālum karuttu nuṭpaṅkaḷālum tamiḻ muṉpōl piṟaimatipōl vaḷarntu ciṟakkum kālam vantu viṭṭatu eṉṟu niṉaittuk kaḷikūrukiṉṟōm. tamiḻar pala paḻaiya nūlkaḷaic ceppañceytu acciṭukiṉṟaṉar. palar pāṭṭiyalpai viḷakki niṟkiṉṟaṉar. palar pala vakai ārāycciyiṟ pukkaṉar. kālañceṉṟa pulavar perumāṉ kārttikēya mutaliyār avarkaḷ pōṉṟa cāṉṟōrkaḷāl moḻi nūl pōṉṟa taṉiyārāycci nūlkaḷ eḻuvaṉavāyiṉa.
ittakaiya kālattup patamamaiyak kālai eḻūuṅ katiravaṉ eṉṉat tōṉṟiyatu nam naṇpar tañcai rāv cākip āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ karuṇāmiruta cākaram eṉṉum viḻumiya nūl. iḵtu ovvoru tamiḻar uḷḷattaiyum uyirppikkum amiḻtat tuḷikaḷ pala aṭaṅkap peṟṟuḷḷa pariya nūl eṉpatai oru muṟai nērakkuvērarum teḷḷat teḷiya uṇarvar. nam paṇṭitar avarkaḷ pala āṇṭukaḷākap peritum muyaṉṟu uḻaittataṉ payaṉāka iccīriya nūl veḷivantulava nērntatu. celvamuṭaiyāruḷ perumpāṉmaiyōr ikkālattuk kalvi eṉṉum iṉpak kaṭaluḷ tiḷaittalaip peruṅkuṟaiyāka niṉaikkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ eṉṟāl kaippar; pulavareṉṟāl eḷḷuvar; aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi ivarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kaṇ uṇmaippaṟṟuvaittuḻaittup pulavar pala tīṭṭiyuḷḷa ceḻuntamiḻ nūlkaḷait tuṟaipōkak kaṟṟu nuṇukkamāka ārāyntu cemporuḷai uḷaṅkoḷa amaittut tamatu nūlil yāvarum pulaṅkoḷa viḷaṅka virittuḷḷār. tamiḻiṉ toṉmai kuṟittu eḻutiya āṅkila nūlāciriyaṉmār palar poṟitta oṇporuḷai āṅkāṅku eṭuttukkāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭaip paṟṟiya pala poruḷkaḷ ataṉkaṇ potin tuḷḷaṉa. kaṭalpōl parantu oḷirum itaṟkuk karuṇāmirta cākaram, eṉak kuṟiyiṭṭamai cālapporuntum eṉka. iyaṟṟamiḻc caṅka varalāṟṟait tiṟampaṭa muṟṟum eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār. tamiḻai iḻitturaippār kūṟṟai niyāya vāyilākak kaṇṭittu aṟivu  koḷuttum nam paṇṭitar avarkaḷ niyāya vaṉmai peritum pārāṭṭaṟpālatu. aṅṅaṉam pōlik koḷkaikaḷ pala parikarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṉi, icaittamiḻārāycci kuṉṟiliṭṭa maṇiviḷakkeṉa eññāṉṟum oḷiyārntu miḷirum polivupeṟṟut tikaḻum itaṉaic cevikkamuteṉa evarum pōṟṟal vēṇṭik karuṇāmirta cākaram, eṉat talaikkuṟi puṉaintaṉarēra eṉṟum eṇṇi makiḻkūrntu uṭal pūrikkiṉṟōm. ivvārāycciyil vaṭanūliṉkaṇ icai nūliṟkūṟum irupattiraṇṭu curuti, nuṭpak kaṇakkiṟku ottuvārāmaiyait tērntu teḷintu ataṉaik kaṇṭittut tamiḻiṉkaṇ cilappatikārattu urai mukattukkāṭṭiyuḷḷa cila kuṟippukkaḷāṉ tamiḻc curuti muṟai irupattu nāṉkātalē ellā nuṭpa tiṭpaṅkaṭkum icaintatu eṉṉum uṇmaiyiṉai yāppuṟuttu niṟuviyuḷḷār. icai nuṇukkam, icaimarapu, paripāṭal mutaliya toṉṉūl tērcciyum maṟṟaiya nūlkaḷiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷaiyum puṭaipaṭavaittuṇarntu pala kūriya poruḷkaḷai valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. iḵtu āṟṟavum matittaṉ mālaiyatu. kālak kaṇakku vakaiyiṉaiyum kōḷkaḷiṉ nilai iyakkaṅkaḷaiyum jīvacuraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa veraṭukkaṅkaḷaiyum naṉkuṇarntu putitu putitākap pala curuti pētaṅkaḷait tāmē kaṇṭuṇarum tiṇmaiyum amaiyap peṟṟuḷḷār. itaṉai nūlaḷavil kaṇṭu makiḻtal pōtāteṉavuṉṉit tam arumaic celvikaḷukku ivaṟṟiṉ oṭpa nuṭpaṅkaḷaip payiṟṟuvittu avarkaḷāl kēṭṭu makiḻntu anupava vāyilākavum viḷakkivarukiṉṟaṉar. ammuṟai innūlil cevvaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉ poruṭṭut tañcai caṅkīta mahājaṉa caṅkam oṉṟu niṟuvip pala caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷai varuvittuc ciṟappuṟa naṭattikkāṭṭit tamiḻicaiyiṉ oppaṟṟa uyarvai yāvarum uṇaravaikkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉai viyantu pala caṅkīta vitvaṉmaṇikaḷ pārāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. atuvēyumaṉṟi, makkaḷ uṭaṟkūṟu pañcākkarattiṉkaṇ aṭaṅku muṟai innūliṉkaṇ viḷaṅkat tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻ icaic ciṟappu ippōtu iṉitu oḷirvatāyiṟṟu. ittuṇai arumaip pāṭṭai nantan tamiḻmoḻi amaiyappeṟṟatu nammavar aruntavamaṉṟō? avaṟṟai uṇarātiruttal perum piḻaiyākum eṉpatu mikaiyākātu.
tamiḻaip pāṇṭiyarēpōl ivarkaḷum iṉṉum paṉṉūlait tāmāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭum, pala nūlkaḷaip piracurittum, palavaṟṟiṟku urai kaṇṭum, palavaṟṟiṟkum paṉuval avvattuṟaivallērarāl eḻutuvittum vaḷarttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻaikiṉṟōm.
iṉi, innūl iṉimaiyāṉa naṭaipeṟṟut taṭṭaṟa āṟṟeraḻukkāka naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu. nalla kākitattil muttuppōṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷāl acciṭappeṟṟuk karuttukkaḻaku taruvatēpōl kaṇṇukkum aḻakaiyūṭṭik kavarkiṉṟatu. tamiḻar ovvoruvar pālum iḵtu iṉṟiyamaiyātu iruttaṟkuriyatākum. pala ariya poruḷkaḷai ōriṭattu oruṅkē kāṭṭum ciṟappuḷḷatu. tamiḻiṉkaṇ iḵtōr cintā maṇi eṉṟu kūṟutal uyarvu naviṟciyākamāṭṭātu.
iḵtu eññāṉṟum niṉṟu nilavavēṇṭum eṉṟum itaṉaip paṭikkum naṇparkaḷ, tamiḻp perumai uṇarntu kalviyiṟ ciṟantu aṟivup peruñcelvam tēkkappeṟṟu iṉpuṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ataṉāṉ ittamiḻnāṭu ōṅkippolital vēṇṭumeṉṟum ellām valla muḻumutaṟ ceḻumporuḷai maṉamoḻimeykaḷāṉ cintittu vantittu vāḻttukiṉṟōm.
nēricai āciriyappā
			toṉmai meṉmai tōṟṟupu viḷaṅku
			naṉmai cāṉṟa naṟṟamiḻ māṭci
			irunila varaippiṉi liṉitē tulaṅka
			arumai yaṟiyā rataṉala muṇara
			aḻukkaṟu māntar iḻukkuṟ ṟiṉaiyac
			centamiḻc celvar cevviya viṉpiṉ
			muntupu pukku nantātu tiḷaippat
			tamiḻnāṭ ṭarumaiyun tavappala mēṉmaiyum
			amiḻteṉa yāvaru maṟintuḷaṅ kūra
			iṉṟamiḻ viḻaiyārk kiruntuyar cēra
			naṉṟuṟu moḻiyāṉ naṟporuḷ koḷuvit
			tañcai vāḻun tamiḻmaṇi yeṉṉum
			viñcai potitaru vittaka āpirakām
			paṇṭitap periyōṉ paḷakaṟu nūlāk	
			kaṇṭitaik karuṇā cākara māka
			vaiyak kaḷittu makiḻntaṉaṉ mātō
			aṉṉōṉ eḻutiya arumporuḷ nūlum
			iṉṉēraṉ ṟāṉu miruṅkiḷai yōṭu
			tuvaṉṟip polintu nivanta pēṟuṭaṉ
			tīñcuvait tamiḻait tiruccevi yārntu
			vāñcai yōṅka vāḻiya mātō
			kāviri karaiyiṭu māviri maṇaliṉum
			palanā ḷulakiṉiṟ paṇpuṟa
			vāḻiya mātō vāḻiya mātō.


eṭṭaiyāpuram camastāṉam teluṅku vitvāṉ
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ti.cu. murukēca piḷḷai avarkaḷ apippirāyam.
tañcaimānakar makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāv cākēp ā. āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa karuṇāmiruta cākaram eṉṟa icai nūlaik kūṭiyavarai yāṉ pārttatil annūl icaipayil māṇavar yāvarkkum iṉṟiyamaiyāp payaṉaḷikkum peṟṟiyuṭaittu. ataṉuṭ poruḷkaḷerav veraṉṟum paṉṉūlaiyumeraruṅkēyāyntu avaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷait tiraṭṭik kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.
tamiḻmoḻi maṟupulac coṟkaḷiṉ cārpiṉṟit taṉiyēyiyaṅku māṇpuṭait taṉit toṉ moḻiyeṉṟum, ammoḻikkōraṇiyeṉa veraḷirum cilappatikārattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa icaittamiḻiṟ cārnta āyappālai mutaliya paṉṉiru pālaikaḷāṟ kiṭaitta pallāyiram paṇkaḷuḷ vaṭṭappālaiyiṉaṭiyāyp piṟanta paṇkaḷ iraṇṭu curuti kuṟaittu irupāṉiraṇṭu curutikaḷōṭu pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, uṇmaiyil oru stāyikku irupattunāṉku curutikaḷē uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
taṟkāla vaḻakkilicaikkappaṭum narampuk karuvikaḷuḷ yāḻiṟkiṭakkum paṉṉiru vīṭu (curaṅ) kaṭku vaṭamoḻi icai nūlāciriyar pallērarum tatta nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷa irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ appaṉṉiru vīṭṭiṉum ottiyaṅkum aḷavaikkāṭṭāmaiyiṉ, kaṇakkiṟkovvāvaḻi vaḻakkiṟ kevvāṟerakku meṉṟa aiyappāṭeraṉṟu eṉ maṉatuṭṭēraṉṟik kiṭantatu. 22 curutikaḷaip pākupaṭuttalilaḷavu ovvāviṭiṉum 12 vīṭukaḷiṉerali yottiyalkiṉṟatu.
sa, ri, ka, ma, pa, ta, ni eṉṟa ēḻu svaraṅkaṭku muṟaiyē curutikaḷ 4, 3, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2 eṉṟa vaḻi, yāḻiṉiṟuviya vīṭukaḷil ri eṉṟa mutal vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 3, cutta kāntāramum pañca curuti riṣapa mumeralikkāniṉṟa iraṇṭāvatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 2, sātāraṇa kāntāramum ṣaṭcuruti riṣapamumeralikkāniṉṟa 3-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 1; antara kāntāra veraliyaik kiḷappum 4-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 2; cutta mattiyama veraliyai yeḻuppum 5-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti1 piratimatyamaveraliyaip piṟappikkum 6-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 3; pañcamam olikkāniṉṟa 7-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 1 eṉa vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ākavē pūrvāṅkattiṉ 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 vīṭukaṭku muṟaiyē curutikaḷ 3, 2, 1, 2, 1, 3, 1 eṉak kiṭaittatu. āyuṅkāl, otta aḷavaippeṟṟa vīṭukaṭku ēṟṟat tāḻvāṉa curutikaḷevvāṟu poruntumeṉṟa kaṭāviṟku emmuṟaiyāṉēra muṉṉērar vakutta muṟaikku nām evvāṟṟāṉeraḻuṅku paṭuttakkūṭum eṉattamiḻ icai nūlaik kāṇṭalum peṟāta vaṭanūṟ payiṟciyār palarum viṭaiyaḷikkā niṉṟaṉar. mutal vīṭuniṟka, 2-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 2 eṉṟum mūṉṟāvatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 1 eṉṟum kūṟiṉ, tāḻnta veraliyaiyuṭaiya 2-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 2 āyirukka ataiviṭa valittolikkum 3-vatu vīṭṭiṟku amaitta curuti 1 eṉpatu evvakaittāṉēra?  niṟka, yāḻil mēlē pōkap pōka oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu yarnta veralikku aḷaviṭam muṟaiyē caṟṟuk kuṟaintēyiruttalāl 2-vatu vīṭṭaiviṭa 3-vatu vīṭu kuṟaivāṉa curutiyeṉa ovvāviṭai pukalvārāyiṉ 3-vatu vīṭṭaiviṭa uyareraliyuṭaiya 4-vatu vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭum avvāṟiṉṟi ēṟṟamāṉa 2 curutikaḷamaitaleṅṅaṉēra? ivvāṟē kuṟaintuṅ kūṭiyum pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷa curutikaḷiṉ muṟai muraṇuṭaiyatu. ivvitam aḷavu muraṇpaṭṭa pākupāṭṭaiyuṭaiya vīṭukaḷ otta aḷaviṉaiyuṭaiyaṉa pōliyoli yolikkāniṉṟaṉaveṉpatu mikkaviyappē! itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaṇ kūṟiya curutip pākupāṭu neṟitavaṟiyateṉak kōṭalē cālpuṭaitteṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
nam paṇṭitaravarkaḷ mikavārāynteṭuttīnta putaiporuḷ pōṉṟa tamiḻp paṇ muṟaiyāṉa 24 curutikaḷē, mēṟkūṟiya paṉṉiru vīṭukaṭkum vīṭeraṉṟukku iraṇṭiraṇṭu curutikaḷāyp pakutta uṇmai neṟiyē emataiyattai yoruṅkēyakaṟṟiyatu. otta aḷavuṭaiyaṉavāyp pakukkap paṭṭa vīṭu (curaṅ)kaḷē iyaleraliyai yeḻuppavallaṉaveṉpatu yāvarkkum oppamuṭinta uṇmaiyē.
virivañci, pūrvāṅkattaippōl uttarāṅkattaiyum viḷakkātu viṭuttu, ulakukkōrariya periya naṉmai payakkattakka ivvicainūlaippaṟṟi ulakil nilaviya curuti campantamāṉa aiyappāṭṭai nīkki viḷakkiyamaip palarum koṇṭāṭattakkateṉṟē collat tuṇintēṉ.
							(oppam) murukēcaṉ, eṭṭayāpuram.
tirunelvēli tirikūṭarācappakkavirāyar paramparaiyaic cārntavarum muṉ
iñciṉīrumākiya kāci vāci srī murukānantam cuvāmikaḷ iyaṟṟiya veṇpā.

	kītattā lummutti kiṭṭumeṉu muṇmaiyelā
	nītiyā yīti ṉikaḻttiṉār - ātaravā
	yaṇṭiṉaraik kākkavala āpirakām paṇṭitaraik
	kaṇṭārkku miṉpamuṟuṅ kāṇ

tirunelvēli nellaiyappar kōyil tēvārappaṇiviṭai
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cālivāṭīcura ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ paramparai
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi caṇmuka cuntara ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
tañcāvūr rāvcāypu āpirakāmpaṇṭitaravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya "karuṇāmirta cākaram" eṉṉum nūliṉ mutaṟpākattaip pārvaiyiṭṭēṉ,
atil iṉiya tamiḻiṉ paḻamaiyāṉa mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷum virivāy viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
iyal, icai, nāṭakameṉṉu muttamiḻil icaittamiḻiṉ ēḻicai kaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṟkātāramāy viḷaṅkum irupattu nāṉku curutikaḷaiyum, "paripāṭalkaḷ" "cilappatikāram" mutaliya caṅka nūlkaḷiṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷaik koṇṭu viḷakki maṟṟapāṭaiyiṟ kūṟum irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ niyāyattiṟkum, aḷavuppiramāṇaṅkaḷukkum aṉupavattiṟkum ovvāmaiyai naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
aṉṟiyum tamiḻvētameṉṉun tēvārattirumuṟaiyiṉ paṇkaḷiṉ muṟaikaḷaiyum avaikaḷai yōtavēṇṭiya pārampariyak kiramattaiyum paṟṟi viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
innūl icai nūṟpayiṟci ceyya virumpuvērar aṉaivarukkum mikka payaṉ paṭuvatuṭaṉ intiyā tēcattil 12000 varuṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ caṅkīta mirunta vuyarnilaimaiyaiyun teḷivuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
ātalā linnūlaṟiñarkaḷaṉaivarālum pārāṭṭattakkatē.
Remarks of Mr. A.G. PICHAIMUTTHU, B.A., L.T., Headmaster,
S. Peter’s High School. Organist, S. Peter’s, and
Honorary Secretary to the Sangeetha Vidya Mahajana Sangam, Tanjore.
It has been said against Indians that they generally lack originality and application. But this statement has been given the lie by the voluminous treatise on Indian Music, ‘Karunamirthasagaram’ written by Rao Sahib M.A. Pandither of Tanjore, S. India. Music is a subject fought shy of by many able scholars, who are experts in other departments, as it is highly technical in character. Those who are credited with a practical knowledge of it at the present day are those musicians who make it their means of livelihood, and most of these are utter strangers to the science of it. It is, therefore, a matter for extreme gratification that the author has combined in himself a complete knowledge of the Science and Art of Music, and has published this great work as the result of his disinterested labours.
This exhaustive work which is the result of nearly 15 years of the author’s life, while dealing directly with the musical system of South India, throws a flood of light also on the literature and history of South India in general and that of the Tamil country in particular, as its literature and Music are inseparable. The indisputable originality displayed throughout the Book by the author, the innumerable authorities quoted in support of statements, the wealth of illustration he has brought to bear on various subjects, the fund of knowledge he possesses in many departments of life, the sparkling humour that now and then relieves the seriousness of such a technical work and the fearlessness and thoroughness with which the author establishes his theory on Srutis - all these clearly show his remarkable genius. The reader may find repetitions in many places, but the author has been quite conscious of them, and they have been made with the definite purpose of striking the ideas home. The author, no doubt, richly deserves a very high place among the literary writers of the day.
The chief aim of the author is to show what the music of the ancient Tamil country was, and how modern Karnatic music is its counterpart. In this he has admirably succeeded. He proves by apt quotations from Tamil works, after careful study, how in the ancient Tamil music of S. India, the Octave was divisible into a number of equal tones. This is his fundamental position. This fact is supported by no less an authority than the great Sanskrit writer on Indian Music - Sarnga Dev of Kashmere - who lived about the 13th Century A.D. According to Sarnga Dev the Swarams of an Octave should be a gradually ascending series arranged like the equal rungs of a ladder without the possibility of admitting any other sound between.  According to the author, the Octave is so divisible into 12, 24, 48 and 96 equal intervals, the series in all cases rising gradually by G.P. The author not only supports this theory by minute mathematical calculations, logarithams, decimals down to eight places as regards length of string, number of vibrations, cents etc., but it has also stood the rigid test of practice and demonstration vocally as well as on instruments. The practical side of it will be clear even to a close reader of the book, but to those who have actually witnessed the demonstration thereon the truth, of this will be quite evident and they will certainly testify that there could be no other system of music possible. Any ordinary Indian musician will accept that the Octave is divisible into 12 equal intervals, that a number of Indian Ragas are sung in these 12 Swarams only and that when change of Graham is made many different Ragas result from the process. This one fact alone strikes at the root of any theory which advocates unequal and irregular intervals for Indian music.
In the course of the treatise the author, condemns the theory of 22 Srutis for the Octave and says that the advocates of it altogether misinterpret their leader Sarnga Dev and that their confusion is worse confounded by the fact that ganam in ancient India was made in the 22 Srutis of the series 24.
I should personally like to see an array of these advocates with their Srutis and instruments in front of them. No two of them will agree as to the location of the different Swarasthanams. If they begin to argue they will be cutting each other’s throats in two minutes! A look into page 416 of the book will convince one of the truth of my statement. Except for the Swaram SA and its Octave there will be no unanimity!
Many readers may think there is not much difference between the 22 and the 24 Srutis. It is not so. The two series are entirely different. All ancient and modern Karnatic Ragas are sung in the 24 Srutis only. The series 22 is only in the imagination of the singer! The professionals sing in the 24 Srutis only, but they are unconscious of the fact. If the advocates of the Dwavimsati Srutis would examine the Srutis they are using in singing, they will find that their theory is one thing and their practice another! If they accept the Saptaswarams, the 12 Swarams of the octave, the mother ragas derived from them and the principle of change of graham - which most of them do accept - then the 22 Srutis exist nowhere.
The members of this school along with Mr. Clements, their captain, base their theory on the system of obtaining their so called harmonic or natural series by proceeding by perfect fifths or taking 2/3 the length of the string for each step or by the SA PA principle. But, to speak the truth, a few steps of this progression will land them in difficulties and they will tumble into the ditch! and never live to see the end of the Octave! How could the product of a recurring decimal like 2/3 ever come to an end? English musicians know that progression by fifths can never completely end the octave unless some manipulation is made. Piano tuners always tune the pianos by slightly flattening the fifths or else the perfect fifths in the course of progress will gather momentum and the result will be like the howling of the wolf! ‘Beware of the wolf” is the caution given to Piano tuners! The trouble will commence even at the third step when you come to decide the interval from D to A and from A to E. From this step forwards a number of artificial aids will be necessary such as cutting down a few vibrations here and there to keep up the farce of the progression by 2/3!! This is why they cry down artificial intervals and say that “Artificial music is an abomination”. I quite endorse this opinion and say, “yes, it is execrably so”. Only he advocates of the 22 srutis have recourse to this abomination. But the author does not base his system on this “equalised abomination” but establishes the existence of a gradually ascending equal series in nature which was in vogue among the Tamilians for centuries. The demonstration of the practical side of this system has been very ably done on several occasions by the author’s two daughters Srimati Maragathavalli Ammal and Kanagavalli Ammal, about whose admirable efficiency in Indian and English music I can personally testify.
The author has given a number of tables and statements which give the calculations & c., of the various systems, reducing them all to one common standard (32 inches wire) for purpose of easy comparison, and also Rasi chakrams where his series are arranged in the four Palais of the ancient Tamils. This part of his work reflects, in a remarkable degree, the intelligence and patience of the author. A close study of them will be well worth the trouble.
The Indian is a highly conservative being, and any theory, whether right or wrongs, which has been honoured by time will never be easily given up by him. Yet in the midst of this conservative nation we find many rational beings who are not afraid to proclaim the truth from the housetops when they know it to be true. It is a matter for pride that the truth of this theory has been accepted by such distinguished musical experts like Seshanna and H.P. Krishna Row of Mysore, Veenai Venkataramanadoos of Vijianagaram, Mutthaya Bhagavater of Harikasavanallur and others. Truth will triumph.
I have not the slightest doubt that the publication of this book, thought it may cause a fllutter in the dovecotes of the Dwavimsatiites, will be an epoch making event in the history of the Music of South India. That it will create a wide interest in musical circles is certain. But, I hope, it will popularise the ancient Yal or the Veena used so much by the ancient. Tamils on account of its capability to reproduce faithfully the human voice and the minutes Srutis. It is sure to rouse the Karnatic Music from its present lethargy; it is sure to rescue it from the hands of the few professionals who blindly follow it and keep it as their heritage and throw its portals open to all without distinction of caste or creed so that it may become a part of the daily life of every Indian.
Ist May, 1917.							(Sd.) A.G.. PICHAIMUTTHU.
tañcai, ceṉṟ pīṟṟars haiskūl heṭmāsṭarum ceṉṟ pīṟṟars carc ārkaṉisṟumāṉa Mr. A.G. piccaimuttu B.A., L.T., avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
intiyarkaḷ putitāṉa oru kāriyattaic ceyvatilāvatu ataṟkeṉṟu pirayācappaṭuvatilāvatu ēlātavarkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷmēl cātāraṇamāyk kuṟai kūṟuvatuṇ L.āṉāl tañcai, Rao Sahib M.A, paṇṭitaravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya karuṇāmirtacākaram eṉṟa intapperiya icaittamiḻ nūlaippārppavarkku appaṭic colluvatāṉatu poyyeṉṟu viḷaṅkum. caṅkītam arumaiyāṉa cāstiramāṉatāl maṟṟa cāstiraṅkaḷil tērnta nipuṇaruṅkūṭa caṅkīta meṉṟāl vilakiviṭuvārkaḷ. taṟkālam anta vittaiyil tērntavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum vitvāṉkaḷuṅkūṭa atai vayiṟṟuppiḻaippiṟkāka upayōkippatāl avarkaḷil perumpāṉmaiyōrukku cāstira viṣayam kiñcittēṉum teriyātu. kāriyaṅkaḷ taṟkālam innilaimai yilirukkaiyil, nammuṭaiya nūlāciriyar caṅkīta cāstirattilum atai aṉupōkamāy upayōkikkum muṟaiyilum nipuṇarāyirukkiṟār eṉṟu inta nūliṉāl veḷiyāvatu mallāmal, yātoru pirati upakārattai eṇṇi itaic ceyyavillai yeṉṟum terikiṟapōtu namakku mikunta makiḻcciyuṇṭākiṟatu.
intap perunūlai eḻutuvatil innūlāciriyar 15 varuṣam iṭai viṭāmal  uḻaittiruk kiṟārkaḷ. innūlāṉatu teṉṉintiya caṅkītattaip paṟṟi nērāyccolluvatōṭukūṭa teṉṉintiya carittirattaiyum ataṉ pūrva nūlkaḷaiyum paṟṟip perukkamāyp pēcuvatumallāmal vicēṣamāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ carittirattaiyum yāvarukkum naṉku viḷaṅkumpaṭi eṭuttuccollukiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ nūlkaḷaiyum caṅkītattaiyum pirittuc collutal kūṭāta kāriyamaṉṟō? mutal toṭaṅki puttakattiṉ kaṭaicivarai collappaṭṭirukkum putitu putitāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷaiyum, viṣayaṅkaḷai stāpippatil nūlāciriyar upayōkittirukkum kaṇakkillāta mēṟkōḷkaḷaiyum, apippirāyaṅkaḷai maṉatil paṭumpaṭi collukiṟa viṣayattil upayōkittirukkum ērāḷamāṉa upamāṉaṅkaḷaiyum, anēka cāstiraṅkaḷil nūlāciriyar aṭaintirukkum pāṇṭittiyattaiyum, iṭaikkiṭaiyē maṉakkiḷarcciyai yuṇṭupaṇṇum paṭikkāka avarkaḷ upayōkittirukkum cilēṭaippirayōkaṅkaḷaiyum (avaiyillāviṭṭāl pustakam paṭikkak kaṣṭamāy irukkumē) caṟṟum payamiṉṟi taṉṉuṭaiya apippirāyattai muṟṟilum cariyāka stāpikkum mēraiyaiyum nām nērakkumpōtu, nūlāciriyaruṭaiya āccariyappaṭattakka pāṇṭittiyam naṉku viḷaṅkum. innūlai vācippavarkaḷ aṅkaṅkē cila kāriyaṅkaḷait tirumpattirumpac colliyiruppataik kāṇalām. āṉāl nūlāciriyar vēṇṭumeṉṟē appaṭic colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ. appaṭic coṉṉāltāṉ kāriyaṅkaḷ naṉṟāy maṉatiṟkuppulappaṭum eṉpataṟkākavē appaṭic collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivaikaḷai yellām nām kavaṉikkumpōtu taṟkāla nūlāciriyaruḷ ivarkaḷukkum oru perumaiyāṉa iṭam kiṭaikkavēṇṭiyatu eṉṟu cantēkamaṟaccollalām.
nūlāciriyariṉ mukkiya nērakkam pūrvatamiḻariṉ kāṉam iṉṉatu eṉṟu eṭuttuc colvatum, taṟkāla karnāṭaka cṅkītattiṟkum ataṟkumuḷḷa oṟṟumaiyai stāpippatum tāṉ. ituviṣayattai āccariyappaṭattakka vitamāyc carivara ceytu muṭittirukkiṟārkaḷ. ātitteṉṉintiya caṅkītattil oru stāyi eppaṭi camamāṉa iṭaiveḷikaḷuḷḷa curutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntateṉṟu pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ takutiyāṉa mēṟ kōḷkaḷaik koṇṭu veku jākkirataiyuṭaṉ stāpikkiṟārkaḷ. itutāṉ avarkaḷuṭaiya muṟaikku ātāra apippirāyam. itai stāpikkum viṣayattil avarkaḷukku akappaṭṭa periya ātāram ēteṉṟāl caṅkīta cāstirattaic camaskiruta pāṣaiyil eḻutiṉavarum, yāvarālum periya caṅkīta nūlāciriyareṉṟu oppukkoḷḷappaṭupavarumāṉa kāṣmīr tēcattaic cērnta cāraṅkatēvartāṉ. ivar 13-m nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntavar. curuti viṣayamāy ivar colluvateṉṉaveṉṟāl, oru stāyiyiluḷḷa curaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāy paṭippaṭiyāy oru ēṇiyiṉ paḻukkaḷaippōla cama aḷavuḷḷavaikaḷāy naṭuvil vēṟu curaṅkaḷ vara iṭamillāmal oḻuṅkāṉavaiyāyirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatē. itaṟkicaintē nammuṭaiya nūlāciriyarum oru stāyiyai 12, 24, 48, 96 camapākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟārkaḷ. curaṅkaḷ ellām oḻuṅkāy oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāy Geometrical Progression il amaikiṉṟaṉa. inta apippirāyattai stāpippatil nuṭpamāṉa kaṇakkukaḷum, lākiritamum, eṭṭustāṉam varai tacāmsa piṉṉaṅkaḷum, tantiyiṉ nīḷamum, ōcai alaikaḷ ceṉṭskaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭiruppatu māttiramalla, avaikaḷ muṟṟilum cariyeṉṟu pāṭiyum vīṇai mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷil vācittum rucuppaṭuttiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. pustakattaik kavaṉamāy vācikkum evarum avarkaḷ apippirāyam aṉupōkattiṟku ottirukkiṟatu eṉpataip pustakattiliruntē eḷitil kaṇṭukoḷvārkaḷ. āṉāl avarkaḷ ceytu kāṭṭiya rucuvai nēril pārttavarkaḷukku anta muṟaiyiṉ uṇmaiyum ataiviṭa vēṟuvitamāṉa muṟaiyeṅkum akappaṭātu eṉṟa cattiyamum uṭaṉē maṉatil utikkum. enta intiya cātāraṇa caṅkīta vitvāṉum, oru stāyi 12 cama pākaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭukiṟateṉṟum, kaṇakkillāta intiya irākaṅkaḷ inta 12 curaṅkaḷilēyē pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, kirakamāṟṟum poḻutu inta 12 curaṅkaḷiliruntu vevvēṟu irākaṅkaḷ jaṉikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum taṭaiyillāmal oppukkoḷvāṉ. intiya caṅkītam oḻuṅkaṟṟa iṭaiveḷikaḷuḷḷa curaṅkaḷālāṉatu eṉṟu collum enta muṟaiyaiyum aṭiyōṭē veṭṭic cāyppataṟku inta oru viṣayamē pōtum.
oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷuṇṭu eṉṟu cātikkum koḷkaik kārarai maṟukkiṟār nūlāciriyar. cāraṅkatēvar colluvatiṉ karuttai maṟṟavar tappāy arttam paṇṇiṉārkaḷeṉṟu pōtumāṉa niyāyaṅkāṭṭic collukiṟār. pūrva intiyāvil 24 curutikaḷil 22 curutiyil tamiḻar kāṉam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ eṉṟa apippirāyamum itōṭē cērntavuṭaṉ periya kuḻappamāy muṭintatu.
tuvāvimcati curutikkāraril cila vitvāṉkaḷaiyum avarkaḷuṭaiya curutikaḷaiyum vāttiyaṅkaḷaiyum eṉ muṉ nēril pārkka virumpukiṟēṉ. uṭaṉē avarkaḷuṭaiya veṭṭaveḷiccamellām terintuviṭum, avarkaḷuṭaiya curastāṉaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi iraṇṭu pērkūṭa oṟṟumaiyāṉa apippirāyam collamāṭṭārkaḷ. tarkkampaṇṇa ārampittuviṭṭālēra iraṇṭu nimiṣattiṟkuḷ oruvar kaḻuttai yoruvar aṟuttuk koḷvārkaḷ. nāṉ colluvatu nijamō tappō eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭu meṉṟu virumpukiṟavarkaḷ innūl karuṇāmirta cākarattil pārkkalām. caṭjamattaiyum mēl caṭjamattaiyum tavira maṟṟa enta curaṅkaḷilāvatu oruvarukkoruvar oṟṟumaiyillaiyeṉṟu aṅkē teḷivākak kāṇpōm.
caṅkītan teriyātavarkaḷ 22 curutikkum 24 curutikkum atika vittiyāca millaiyeṉṟu niṉaippārkaḷ. atu tappu. iraṇṭu curavaricaikaḷukkum koñcamāvatu campantamillai. pūrva karnāṭaka irākaṅkaḷum taṟkālam upayōkattilirukkum karnāṭaka irākaṅkaḷum pāṭappaṭuvatu 24 curutikaḷil tāṉ. 22 curutikaḷ antak koḷkaikkārariṉ maṉatil māttiramtāṉ irukkiṟatu. uṇmaiyākavē avaikaḷ vēṟeṅkum illai. caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ pāṭuvatellām 24 curutikaḷiltāṉ. āṉāl avaikaḷil pāṭukiṟōmeṉṟu avarkaḷukkut teriyātu. avarkaḷ pāṭum curutikaḷai ārāyntu pārppārkaḷāṉāl tāṅkaḷ cātippatu oṉṟu pāṭuvatu vēṟoṉṟu eṉṟu aṟivārkaḷ. captacuraṅkaḷ, stāyikku 12 curaṅkaḷ, avaikaḷil uṇṭākum tāy irākaṅkaḷ, kirakamāṟutal uṇṭu eṉṉum mukkiyamāṉa caṅkatikaḷai oppukkoḷvārkaḷāṉāl - perumpāṉmaiyōr oppukkoḷvatum vāstavam-22 curutikaḷ eṉṟa curutikkoḷkai eṅkum irātu.
intak koḷkaikkārar, avarkaḷuṭaiya kāpṭaṉākiya kiḷameṇṭs turaiyuḷpaṭa, ca-pa, ca-pa vāka 2/3 , 2/3 ākap pōvatiṉāl taṅkaḷuṭaiya ārmōṉik curastāṉaṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu cātikkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl uṇmaiyaic collavēṇṭumāṉāl ippaṭi iraṇṭaṭi tūram pōvārkaḷāṉāl avarkaḷ ellārum tiṭīreṉakkuḻiyil viḻuvārkaḷ. stāyiyiṉ muṭivaik kāṇamāṭṭārkaḷ. talaiyilum pakkattilum puḷḷiyōṭu tirumpat tirumpa varum tacāmcapiṉṉamākiya 2/3 eppaṭi muṭivukku varum? iṅkilīṣ caṅkītam terintavarkaḷ, aintuaintākac curaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittuk koṇṭupōkumpōtu ciṟcila kuṟaittu vaikkāmal pōṉāl stāyi muṭivukku varātu eṉṟu aṟivārkaḷ. piyāṉāviṉ curaṅkaḷukkuc curuti cērkkumpōtu ataic ceypavarkaḷ
ca-pa vāka varum aintukaḷaic caṟṟukkuṟaittu vaittukkoṇṭē pōvārkaḷ. appaṭic ceyyāviṭṭāl ca-pa muṟaiyāy varum ovvoru curamum vara varak kūṭik kaṭaiciyil ōṉāy ūḷai pōlāyviṭum. ākaiyāl "ōṉāy varāmal pārttukkoḷ." eṉṟa eccarikkai koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟāvatu aṭukkilēyē atāvatu riṣapattiliruntu taivatamum, taivatattiliruntu kāntāramum uṇṭākum pōtē tuṉpam ārampittu viṭum. appoḻutu ataiccarippaṭuttumpaṭi aṅkē koñcam ōcai alaikaḷaiyum iṅkē koñcam ōcai alaikaḷaiyum kuṟaittuc cimpukaṭṭa nēriṭum. appaṭic ceyyāviṭṭāl 2/3 ākap pōkiṟateṉṟa kūttu muṭivukku varātē! itaṟkākavē "kaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa curutikaḷuḷḷa caṅkītamāṉatu aruvaruppu" eṉṟu collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. nāṉum atu cariyeṉṟu colli oppukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. āṉāl nūlāciriyar 22 curutikkārarkaḷaip pōla ippaṭik kaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa curutikaḷālāṉa aruvaruppait taṉ muṟaikku ātāramākak koḷḷāmal  pūrva tamiḻarukkuḷḷē vaḻaṅki vantatum cama iṭaiveḷikaḷuḷḷatum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāy iyaṟkaiyilē uḷḷatumāṉa curutikaḷālāṉa taṉ caṅkīta muṟaiyai stāpikkiṟār. inta muṟaiyāṉatu anēka taṭavaikaḷil nūlāciriyaratu kumārattikaḷ srīmati marakatavalli ammāḷālum kaṉakavalli ammāḷālum vistāramāy rucuppaṭuttik kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṟatu. inta iraṇṭu peṇmaṇikaḷum intiya caṅkītattilum iṅkilīṣ caṅkītattilum aṭaintirukkiṟa āccariyappaṭattakka vitamāṉa pāṇṭittiyattaip paṟṟi nāṉ nēril naṟcāṭci koṭukkakkūṭum.
ivaikaḷaiyallāmal nūlāciriyar anēka Tables, Statements mutaliyavaikaḷaik koṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivaikaḷ pala muṟaiyaiyum 32 aṅkulam eṉṟa orē muṟaikkuk koṇṭuvantiruppatāl paṟpala muṟaikaḷai eḷitil ottuppārppataṟku upayōkamāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷ pala muṟaikaḷiṉ kaṇakku mutaliyavaṟṟaic collukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷaiyallāmal pūrvatamiḻariṉ 4 pālaikaḷilum curavaricaikaḷaik kāṇpippataṟku irāciccakkaraṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. itaik koṇṭu nūlāciriyaruṭaiya putti kūrmaiyum poṟumaiyum iṉṉateṉṟu ilēcil aṟintukoḷḷalām. avaikaḷaik kavaṉamāyp paṭippavarukku avarkaḷ atil celavaḻittata kālam vīṇpōkāmal payaṉaiyuṇṭākkum.
paḻaiyakāriyaṅkaḷē tiṟam, avai cariyōtappō, veku kālam avaikaḷ iruntapaṭiyāl avaikaḷaiyē cariyākak koḷḷavēṇṭum, ilēcil viṭṭuviṭakkūṭātu eṉpatu intiyariṉ iyaṟkai. ippērppaṭṭa iyaṟkaiyuṭaiya jātiyiṉ mattiyil, oru kāriyattai uṇmaiyeṉṟu aṟinta piṟaku ataik kūraikaḷiṉmēl niṉṟu yāvarukkum teriya pirastāpikka payappaṭakkūṭātu eṉṟu niṉaikkum anēka ñāṉikaḷaip pārkkiṟōm. inta muṟai caritāṉeṉṟu pērpōṉa caṅkīta vitvacirēramaṇi kaḷākiya maicūraic cērnta makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cēṣaṇṇā, makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi
H.P.  kiruṣṇarāv, vijayanakaram makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi vīṇai vēṅkaṭa ramaṇatās, makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi muttaiyāpākavatar mutaliyavarkaḷ oppuk koḷvatāṉatu namakkup perumaiyē, cattiyam jeyam peṟumallavā?
inta nūliṉ piracuramāṉatu tuvāvimcati curutip puṟākkūṭṭaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ ciṟakukaḷai aṭittup pītiyai aṭaiyumpaṭi ceyta pōtilum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṟku oru mukkiyamāṉa putu uyirkoṭutta campavamāy irukkumeṉpatil koñcamāvatu cantēkamillai. caṅkītam aṟintavarkaḷukkuḷ itu oruvita maṉakkiḷarcciyaiyum pirītiyaiyum uṇṭupaṇṇum eṉpatu niccayam. āṉāl itaṉ mūlamāy pūrva tamiḻarukkuḷ maṉitacārīrattaiyum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷaiyum appaṭṭamāy captittukkāṭṭakkūṭiya yāḻ allatu vīṇai atikamāy upayōkikkap paṭṭatupōla taṟkālamum atu upayōkikkappaṭṭuvarumāṉāl nalamā yirukkum. kṣīṇittuppōṉa karnāṭaka caṅkītattait tirumpavum uyirp pikkavum, taṟkāla caṅkītattait taṅkaḷuṭaiya conta celvamākap pūṭṭi vaittu ataik kuruṭṭuttaṉamāy upayōkikkum vitvāṉkaḷiṉ kaiyiliruntu tappuvikkavum jāti matavittiyācamillāmal yāvarum paṭippatiṉāl ovvoru intiya vīṭṭilum atu upayōkappaṭuttappaṭavum inta nūl pirayōjaṉamāyirukkumeṉpatu niccayam. 
(oppam) A.G. piccamuttu
Opinion of Mr. N.P. SUBRAMANIA IYER, M.R.A.S., Journalist
Author of “Astrological Series”
Secretary, Sangeetha Vidya Mahajana Sangam, etc. Tanjore.
Having had the good fortune to follow closely, for years, the labors undertaken by Mr. Pandither for the solution of the problem of the number of Sutis in the Hindu musical scale, I am thoroughly convinced of the immense value of this book to the enrichment and advancement of Indian music. It is, I think, safe to assert that a knowledge of the Srutis is an important factor in the ground-work of musical training. It is the law of the Srutis that makes Music an art, and a knowledge of that law is so much capital, held in reserve, against the encroachments of inharmony, however cleverly concealed. We have too few among professional musicians to-day who have a taste to cultivate a knowledge of the scienctific side of the art. That there are but 22 Srutis for the Octave is to them an inherited theory which lies somewhere at the back of their minds, but neither in vocal nor in instrumental music are they able to express the twenty two Taking this theory as a text, Mr. Pandither examines each assertion with the fairness of one trained to critical methods and with the exactness of a scientist, and we have a full and luminous presentation of the theory of 24 Srutis for the Octave, as also of the minute Srutis, 48 and 96. I am aware that this theory, so lucidly maintained in the pages of this book, has proved disquieting to the so-called votaries of the 22. But to talk of 22 without being able to demonstrate it, it in any manner, and, at the same time, to attack the conclusions of one who has handled the problem in a reverent andserious spirit is compatible neither with national pride nor with professional sincerity.
The book is replete with information that must add to the culture of the reader while the historical facts, the tables and the illustrations lead him from page to page with increasing fascination. The reader is brought into touch with the civilization of the ancient Tamils and the marvellous efficiency attained by them in the practice of Music. This is another merit about the book and herein we see that the system of 24 Srutis is no forcible creation or re-arrangement but rather an interpretation of the ancient Tamil works whichallude to the theory of 7, 12, 24, 48 and 96 Srutis-a system which flourished in the blood of our ancestors thousands of years ago.
No examination of Mr. Pandithers theory of 24 and the minute Srutis could have been more thorough than the one that was held at the All India Music Conference in Baroda, last year. There the exposition of the theory appealed to the reason of every one in the grand assemblage of musicians, while the practical demonstration thereof, by the accomplished daughters of Mr.Pandither, elicited unqualified praise. Similarly, the demonstration in the Palace, in the presence of their Highnesses the Gaekwar and the Maharani, in response to their special invitation, was a unique success. I had the happiness of being present on both, among many other, occasions. At the 7th Conference of the Sangita Vidya Mahajana Sangam, the theory and its practice, the mathematical calculations of Swarams and other details were minutely gone into by experts who were qualified to pronounce an opinion on the different aspects of the question. And, as will be seen from the following pages, they have borne unanimous testimony to the soundness of the theory and the conformity of the practice to the same. Scepticism and timid conservatism may feel dissatisfied and try to impair the value of the work, for some time, but the secrets of the science lay open to those who study it in a disinterested spirit, unhampered by prejudice. It is they who will try to follow in the footsteps of careful thinkers like Mr. Pandither who has spoken at length after years of painstaking and extensive research.
(Sd.) N.P. SUBRAMANIA IYER.
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi N.P. cuppiramaṇiya aiyar avarkaḷ apippirāyam.
intiya caṅkīta stāyiyil vaḻaṅkum curutikaḷaippaṟṟi ārāycci ceyta viṣayattil Mr. paṇṭitaravarkaḷ anēka varuṣaṅkaḷākac ceyta muyaṟciyai nēril pārkkum cilākkiyattai nāṉ aṭaintavaṉāṉatāl intap puttakam intiya caṅkītattai vaḷaracceyyum viṣayattilum ataṟku ariya pala viṣayaṅkaḷaic cērkkum viṣayattilum atika pirayōcaṉattait tarumeṉṟu niccayamāṉa apippirāyam koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. caṅkītattai appiyācikkum viṣayattil ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mukkiyamāṉa mūla ātāram curutikaḷaippaṟṟiya ñāṉamtāṉ eṉṟu taṭaiyillāmal collalām. curutikaḷaippaṟṟiya piramāṇamē caṅkītattai aṉupavattukkēṟṟa oru cāstiramākkukiṟatu. kātukku iṉimaiyaṟṟa caṅkītattai evvaḷavu mūṭivaittup pirayōkam paṇṇiṉapōtilum atu cariyāṉa caṅkītattaik keṭuttuviṭātapaṭi intac curutip piramāṇamāṉatu eppōtum kaiyiliruntu utavukiṟa mūlataṉampōlirukkiṟatu. taṟkālam caṅkītattai vayiṟṟup piḻaippukkāka upayōkikkiṟa anēkar atai aṉupōka cāstiramāka viruttikkukkoṇṭu varuvataṟku vēṇṭiya caktiyillātavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ. stāyikku 22 curutikaḷ uṇṭeṉṟa apippirāyam vamca paramparaiyāy avarkaḷ oppukkoṇṭu vanta oru koḷkaiyāy avarkaḷ maṉatil māttiram irukkiṟatēyallāmal antac curutikaḷai vāyiṉāl colliyāvatu vāttiyaṅkaḷil vācittāvatu kāṭṭamuṭiyātavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ. intak koḷkaiyaip paṇṭitaravarkaḷ taṉmuṉ vaittuk koṇṭu ataippaṟṟip palarum colvatai oru cāstiriyiṉ nuṇmai yuṭaṉum takutiyāṉa ārāycciyuṭaṉum eṭuttuk kaṇṭittirukkiṟatu mallāmal, oru stāyiyil 24 curutikaḷum maṟṟum nuṭpamāṉa 48, 96 curutikaḷum uṇṭeṉṉum muṟaiyait teḷivākavum vistāramākavum eṭuttuc colli stāpikkiṟārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu teḷivāy intap puttakattil inta muṟaiyaic coṉṉatāṉatu 22 curutikkārar maṉatil oruvitak kuḻappattaiyuṇṭu paṇṇiṉateṉpatu eṉakkut teriyum.  22 curutikaḷ eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu atai rucuppaṭutta muṭiyāmal viḻippatum, atē camayattil anta muṟaiyaic caṅkiyaiyōṭum pakti vinayattōṭum ārāycci ceytavarkaḷuṭaiya muṭivāṉa apip pirāyaṅkaḷaik kāraṇamillāmal etirppatum nammuṭaiya kulattiṉ perumaikkāvatu caṅkītattaik kaiyāḷupavariṉ uṇmaikkāvatu aḻakalla.
pustakamāṉatu pala viṣayaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa kuṟippukaḷuḷḷa tāṉatāl vācippavaruṭaiya aṟivai virutti ceyyak kūṭiyatu. atiluḷḷa carittira viṣayaṅkaḷum aṭṭavaṇaikaḷum upamāṉaṅkaḷum vācikka vācikka maṉatai vacappaṭuttakkūṭiyavai. ātittamiḻaruṭaiya nākarīkam, caṅkīta viṣayattil avarkaḷ aṭaintirunta āccariya vitamāṉa pāṇṭittiyam mutaliya viṣayaṅkaḷ vācippavarukkut teḷivāyt teriyum. pustakattaip paṟṟiya iṉṉeraru mukkiyamāṉa pukaḻccikkuriya viṣayam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, avarkaḷ collum 22 curutikaḷ muṟai kaṭṭāyappaṭuttiyāvatu maṟṟa curutikaḷai vēṟu vitamāṉa oḻuṅkuppaṭuttiyāvatu uṇṭākkappaṭṭatā yirāmal ātittamiḻ nūlkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa 7, 12, 24, 48, 96 curutikaḷ muṟaiyiliruntu piṟantateṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum. immuṟai namatu muṉṉērarkaḷiṉ irattattōṭu āyiramāyiram varuṣaṅkaḷākak kalantu vantirukkiṟatu.
pōṉavaruṣam parōṭāvil naṭanta kāṉpareṉcil paṇṭitaravarkaḷiṉ 24 curuti muṟai parīkai ceyyappaṭṭatupōl vēṟentac camayattilum ceyyappaṭavillai. accamayam ammuṟai viḷakkikkāṭṭappaṭṭa pōtu aṅkuk kūṭiyirunta pirapala vitvāṉkaḷ yāvarālum atu cariyeṉṟu aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭatumallāmal paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kalvi vāynta kumārattikaḷāl ammuṟai rucuppaṭuttikkāṭṭappaṭṭapōtu avarkaḷ ellārālum pukaḻappaṭṭārkaḷ. avvitamākavē kaikvār makārājāviṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉ pēril avarkaḷ muṉṉilaiyilum makārāṇiyavarkaḷ muṉṉilaiyilum araṇmaṉaiyil ammuṟai rucuppaṭuttappaṭṭapōtum avarkaḷ peṟṟa kīrttip piratāpam collamuṭiyātu. iraṇṭu camayaṅkaḷilum maṟṟavarkaḷōṭukūṭa nāṉum ācarāyirukkum cilākkiyattaip peṟṟēṉ. tañcai caṅkīta makājaṉa caṅkattiṉ 7-vatu kāṉpareṉcilum immuṟaiyum ataṉ rucuvum ceytu kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. atōṭu curaṅkaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷum maṟṟum nuṭpamāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷum anta viṣayattaip palavitamāyum nērakki ārāyntu apippirāyam collak kūṭiya vitvāṉkaḷāl nuṭpamāyp parīkṣikkappaṭṭatu. intap pustakattiṉ piṉ pakkaṅkaḷil collappaṭṭirukkiṟapaṭi ammuṟai cāstira viṣayamāyc cariyeṉṟum aṉupavattiṟkum atu ottatākavē irukkiṟateṉṟum avarkaḷ ellārum naṟcāṭci koṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. cantēkakkārarum paḻaiyatē cariyeṉṟu cātikkum kōḻaikaḷum ipputtakattaippaṟṟit tiruptippaṭāmal atiṉuṭaiya nalla vēlaiyaik keṭukkum paṭi pirayācappaṭalām. atuvum cila nāḷaikkuttāṉ āṉāl niṣkaḷaṅka maṉatuṭaṉum uṇmaiyai aṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa avāvuṭaṉum ataip paṭippavarukkuc cāstirattiṉ maṟaiporuḷāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷellām eḷitil viḷaṅkum. paṇṭitaravarkaḷ anēka varuṣaṅkaḷāy vistāramāṉa muyaṟciceytuvantatiṉ palaṉāka inta muṟaiyai stāpikkiṟārkaḷ. appērpaṭṭa niṣkaḷaṅka maṉatuḷḷavarkaḷ tāṉ jākkirataiyāṉa maṉatuṭaṉ ivarkaḷ ceyta vēlaiyaip pōlac ceyyakkūṭiyavarkaḷ.
(oppam) N.P. cuppiramaṇiyaaiyar.
ceṉṉai eppā haiskūl tamiḻppaṇṭitar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ā. tēvappiracātam piḷḷai avarkaḷ koṭutta cāttukavi.
	1.	kaṭalcūḻum nilavulaki luḷavuyirkaḷ yāviṉumā katitta tāṉa
		uṭalcūḻuṅ kaṟaiyaṭiyai naṭampuriya mikkamakiḻ vūṭṭu vikkum
		aṭalcūḻuṅ koṭumpuliyai vayavariyaic cātukuṇat tamara vaikkum
		miṭalcūḻu mivuḷiyiṉaic cerukkaḷattē yuṟcāka mēvac ceyyum.
	2.	iraiyuṇṇa nīrarunta veṇṇātu neṭuṅkaḻutti ravaṇan taṉṉuḷ
		karaiyilāk kaḷippeytik kaḷaippiṉaiyuṅ karutātu kālā ṟātu
		viraivākap pālaivaṉaṅ kaṭantiṭavē celuttiṭumim meymai tēril
		taraimītu caṅkītan taṉakkiṇaiyā vēṟeraṉṟaic cāṟṟa lāmē.
	3.	vaṇṭikaḷai yōṭṭukiṉṟōr pāṭukiṉṟa tempāṅkum maṟṟuṅ kēṭṭuc
		caṇṭiniṟa māṭukaḷu miṟumāntu kaṭumpāran tāvi yīrttuk
		koṇṭivaru māṉiraikaḷ kōpāla rūtupullāṅ kuḻali ṉēracaik
		kaṇṭavarum pālkaṟakku muṭṭavanta cutaipāṭaṟ kāṉan tittē.
	4.	vaṉavēṭar pāṇkēṭṭu maṉamuruki yacaiyātu māṉka ṇiṟkum
		cuṉakaṉunal licaikēṭkiṟ kuraippiṉṟi yamarntiruntu cukittup pōkum
		puṉaliṟcel vaṅkamatiṟ kuḻaleraliyaic ceviyuṟṟup puṇari nāykaḷ
		iṉamellān tiraṇṭeraṉṟāyt toṭarntuvarun tolaitūra mekka ḷittē
	5.	kāṉaraca maṉupavittuk kaccakamun talaiyacaittuk kaḷiyaik kāṭṭum
		vāṉamatiṟ cañcarikkuñ cātakamuṅ kiṉṉaramu maṟṟum puḷḷum
		ñāṉamuṭa ṉicaikēṭṭaṅ kēmuṟuva taṉṟittan nāvāṟ pāṭi
		mōṉamiku muṉivaraiyu mēṉaiyava raiyumiṉpiṉ mūḻttu mātō
	6.	mātavameṉ paruvattē matiyaniṟain toḷikāla malaimēṟ kāṉil
		pātavaṅkaḷ ceṟintuḷḷa paṇaiyōram vīṇaiyiṉaṟ paṇaivā cikkil
		ātaramō ṭavvicaikēṭ ṭāṉantit tantarattē yacuṇa mākkaḷ
		pōtamuñcērarn tacaivaṟṟu niṟkumeṉi licaipperumai pukalvār yārē.
	7.	taṇṭalaiyiṟ kampalaiyiṟ ṟāruviṉiṟ ceṭikoṭiyiṟ ṟaṭattiṟ kāṉil
		uṇṭupaṭu malarmatuvai yuṇṭumika vuḷamatarttē yuḻaika ṭēraṟum
		vaṇṭukaḷcev vaḻimuralum vāviyiṟṟā maraivaḷaiya macca mārntu
		koṇṭematu cevikkamutañ corivateṉak kātampaṅ kūvip pāṭum.
	8.	maṇṇilat taṟpamā macakan tāṉumē
		paṇṇuṟap pāṭiyē paṟantu lāviṭum
		taṇṇiya vēracaiyaic cārntu kēṭṭiṭum
		eṇṇiliv vicaivalai yāvai tāmpaṭā.
	9.	illiṭan tōṟumaṟ ṟeṅku muḷḷatām
		palliyu micaimikap parintu kēṭkumāl
		nalliya lēracaiyai nayantu pōntupiṉ
		melliya nūlvalai mīḷum lūtaiyē.
	10.	kuttivā ḻuyirkaḷuṭ koṭiya nākamum
		mattamuṟ ṟatuveṉa makaṭi vāyeḻu
		nattupuṉ ṉākava rāḷi kēṭṭalum
		uttiyār paivirit tuvappo ṭāṭumē.
	11.	toṭṭiliṟ kiṭantaḻat tuvakku mōrcicu
		kiṭṭivan tammaṉai kiḷattu tālcevip
		paṭṭiṭap porumalpōyp parumi taṅkoḷum
		iṭṭaḷan tīrtticai yiṉpu mīyumē.
	12.	avviya nīkkiyē yaruḷa ḷittiṭum
		cevvaiyil cērampiṉait tīrttut tāṭarum
		kavvaicēr mūrkkamuṅ kaḻittuc cāntamē
		ovvuṟac ceytiṭu muyarcaṅ kītamē.
	13.	kavalaika ṭīrttuḷak kaḷippai yīntiṭum
		puvaṉiyai yuvaṇaipōṟ poliyac ceytiṭum
		avalama toḻittumik kāṟṟa ṉalkiṭum
		tuvalkolum viṭattaiyun tolaikkuṅ kītamē.
	14.	naḷḷalar tammaiyu naṇpa rākkiṭum
		uḷḷapal luyirkaṭam muḷḷa mīrttiṭum
		aḷḷaiyaik kūḷiyai yaṭakkik kaṭṭiṭum
		eḷḷarum valiyuṭait tiṉiya kītamē.
	15.	toḻilkaḷaip purintu taḷarcciyuṟ ṟavarkkut toyvunīk kuvatucaṅ kītam
		oḻivilā tōti yeyttavar mūḷaik kuṟutinal kuvatucaṅ kītam
		kaḻiperun tuyara maṉattaṭain tavarkkuk kaḷippaiyī kuvatucaṅ kītam
		aḻivilāṉ puvikkō ramutaṉa vitaimuṉ ṉaḷittaṉa ṉaṟiñarā laṉṟē.
	16.	vilaṅkukaḷ puṭka ḷūrvaṉa nīril viḷaṅkupal luyirkaḷu micaiyiṉ
		valaṅkoṭiṇ maiyiṉā liḻupaṭu muruki makiḻntumē paravaca mākum
		nalaṅkoḷu maṉitap piṟaviyiṟ piṟantum nallicai kēṭṭuru kātāṉ
		ilaṅkaṟi villāk kaṟkaḷō virumpō viṉṉaveṉ ṟuraikkaṟi yōmāl.
	17.	kāmaraṅ kēṭṭu murukiṭā maṉitar kacaṭariṟ ṟāḻvuṟu kacaṭar
		īmanēr maṉamu narakiṉu miruṇṭa teṇṇamō carppaṉai catiyē
		yāmavar naṭpum vañcaka niṟainta taṉpumē kaṉaviluṅ koḷḷār
		tīmaicē ravarai nampoṇā teṉavē ceppiṉar pulamaiyiṉ mikkōr.
	18.	nallicai valiyai nāṭṭuvāṉ karuti nakkaṉmā tavaṉeraṭā ṉāyaṉ
		mullaiyam paṇṇai yāḻiṉi licaikka muraḷiyi liṉṉicai terikkat
		tollaiyain teḻuttai vēṇuvi leralikkat tōṟṟiya carācara maṉaittum
		ollaiyi ṉurukik kaḷittaṉa veṉṟiṅ kuraittiṭum paṟpala purāṇam.
	19.	irunilat taṭiyā revarkaḷē yeṉiṉu miṉimaiyāyp pāṭalkaḷ pāṭit
		tiruvaṭi tutikkiṉ ṟaṉatuḷaṅ kaṉintu tikaḻuva raṉpeṉum valaiyiṉ
		maruviyē cikkuṇ ṭaṉṉavar kēṭkum varaṉelām poruḷelām maṟukkā
		torutaṉip parama ṉaruḷuva ṉeṉṉi luyarntatu pāṇiṉu muṇṭēra.
	20.	eṉṉuyirk kuyirai yepporuḷukku miṟaivaṉai yimaiyavar kaṇaṅkaḷ
		tuṉṉiyem maruṅkuñ cūḻntukoṇ ṭaiyaṉ cupakuṇaṅ kaḷaiyeṭut tōti
		iṉṉicaik karuvi muḻakkiyē pāṭi yēṟṟavā ṟāṭiyē tutippār
		poṉṉula kiṉilum polintilaṅ kuvatu poruvaṟu kītamē yaṉṟō.
	21.	ittuṇaip perumai vāynta caṅkīta mēṉaiya kalaikaḷaip pōlum
		atticū ḻulakil varavarac curuṅki yarilpala kalantiṭal kaṇṭu
		cittamē caliyā ticaivalērar yāruñ cērntiṭa verarucapai nāṭṭi
		vittakak karuṇā mirutacā karappēr mēmpaṭu nūlvaḻaṅ kiṉaṉāl.
	22.	aṉṉāṉ yāreṉ ṟemaiviṉavi ṉaṟaivēraṅ kēṇmi ṉippuviyil
		muṉṉā ṭeṉṉā ṭāṇṭuvanta mutaṉmai peṟumū varacarkaḷuḷ
		oṉṉā rañca nakuvēlā ṉucitaṉ vaḻiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉavar
		eṉṉā naviluñ cāṉṟārtam miṉatti lutitta kuṇamēru.
	23.	tañcai nakaruk katipaṉeṉat taraiyil viḷaṅku vāḻvuṭaiyōṉ
		eñca liṉṟit tikkeṭṭu meṭṭuṅ kīrtti mikappaṭaittōṉ
		kañcam pōṉṟa vataṉamatiṟ karuṇai yoḻuku miruviḻiyōṉ
		nañca meṉavē poyveṟuttu nāḷum vāymai pukaṉāvāṉ.
	24.	nāḷuṅ kalvi racamataṉai naccip paruku maṇicceviyāṉ
		kāḷa mukilu maintaruvuṅ kaṇṭu veṭkap poḻikarattāṉ
		vēḷiṉ carattuk kiṭamiṉṟi vimalai vāḻun taṭamārpāṉ
		āḷuṅ kēḷu mōvāma laṇukip paṇiyu malarttāḷāṉ.
	25.	tēca māḷu matipatikaḷ ciṟanta pirapuk kaṇmikkōr
		pācam vaittē koṇṭāṭip paḻaki yuṟavā ṭuṅkuricil
		nēcaṅ koṇṭu caṟkuruvāy nērnta karuṇā ṉantarupa
		tēca mataṉai maṟavātu tiṉamuñ cevvai neṟiniṟpōṉ.
	26.	muṉṉam muttu cāmiyeṉu mutalva ṉaṉṉam māḷēraṭum
		paṉṉāṇ muyaṉṟa varuntavattiṉ payaṉā vanta pākkiyavāṉ
		piṉṉa rutitta jēcu tācaṉ pīṭār tāṉi yēlvilyam
		eṉṉu mūvar tamaiyunta ṉiṉiya vaṉuca rāyuṭaiyōṉ.
	27.	teṉṉaṉ kulattut tōṉṟaleṉat teḷivāyk kāṭṭuntirunāmam
		maṉṉuñ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉai maṉērakya jēratip pāṇṭiyaṉai
		tuṉṉuṅ kuṇamā pāṇṭiyaṉaic cērarvil cavuntara pāṇṭiyaṉait
		taṉṉiṉ putalva rāyppaṭaitta taṉṉē rillāt tāṭṭīkaṉ.
	28.	eṉṟun tēvap piracāta meṉṉu mempōṟ pāvalarai
		yoṉṟuṅ kuṟaivey tāmaṟṟaṉ ṉuyirpōṟ ṟāṅki yātarippōṉ
		iṉṟu maṉṟē pōlaṉpi liṟaiyuṅ kuṉṟā vuttuṅkaṉ
		naṉṟun tayaiyuṅ kaimmāṟu nāṭā tevarkkum purimakipaṉ.
	29.	pūtalatti lātavaṉai yottaperu viḷakkam
			poruntiyeṉṟum nīṅkāta peruṅkoṭaiyō ṭirakkam
		ōtukalai yuṇarccitavā māṇpu peruṅkīrtti
			orunāḷuṅ kuṟaiyāma lēraṅku peruvipavam
		nātaṉaṭit tāmaraiyai maṟavāta vaṉpu
			nallakuru paktitaḷa rāvūkkap pāṭu
		cātukuṇa mītaṉaittu merarucēra vāyntōṉ
			tañcainaka rāpirakām paṇṭitaṉā mālē.
	30.	aṉṟu tirumā lamarark kamutakuṭam
		oṉṟutan tāṉatika muṇṭukolēra-ceṉṟutañcai
		yākaramē yeṉṉakkoṇ ṭāpirakā mālamuta
		cākaramē tantiṭṭāṉ ṟāṉ.

tañcāvūr, catāvatāṉam
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cuppiramaṇiya aiyaravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya
āciriya viruttam.
	
	1.	annāḷi liyaṟṟamiḻi lakattiyaṉā merarumuṉiva ṉaṉaittu mārāyn
		teṉṉāku moḻikkumuṭi vāmeḻuttuc curutiyeṇmūṉ ṟeṉṉak kaṇṭāṉ
		innāḷi licaittamiḻiṟ curamuṭivāñ curutimūveṭ ṭeṉṉak kaṇṭāṉ
		maṉṉāku māppirakām paṇṭitaṉṟāṉ pulamaippēr māṇpi vaṟkē.
	muṭivām eḻuttu e-tu āvi ñaṇanamaṉa yaralavaḻaḷamey cāyumukara nālāṟu mīṟē eṉṟapaṭi,
	curuti-oli. curam-catta curaṅkaḷ. pulamaip pēr e-tu paṇṭitaṉ eṉṉum peyar. māṇpu - ciṟappu. ivaṟku-inta āppirakām eṉpavaṉukku. ēkāram pirinilai.

	2.	muttamiḻu ḷiyaṟṟamiḻiṉ muṭivāku meḻuttukkaḷ mūveṭ ṭeṉṟa
		cattiyaṉṟērn tāṉāṭa kattamiḻi ṉiruttavakai kaḷaimūṉ ṟeṭṭeṉ
		ṟattamiḻnā ṭaṉṟērntā ṉicaittamiḻiṟ curaccuruti yāṟu nāṉkeṉ
		ṟattamiku māppirakām paṇṭitaṉṟērn tāṉaṭunā yakamī tāmē.
muttamiḻ- iyal, icai, nāṭakam. nirutta vakaikaḷ-kūttiṉ vikaṟpam. attamiḻnāṭaṉ-oru pāṇṭiyaṉ. curaccuruti-catta curaṅkaḷiṉ oli. naṭunāyakam-naṭuviluḷḷa icaittamiḻccuruti. mītu ām-mēlākum. ītu āmeṉṟuṅ koḷḷalām.
periya kuḷantālūkā pūcārikkavaṇṭaṉ paṭṭi
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ca. tirumalaivēluk kavirāyar avarkaḷ colliya kavikaḷ.

viruttam.
	poṅkutiraik kaṭaṟpuviyiṟ paṇṭainū letuveṉattāṉ porunta nāṭic
	caṅkaiyaṟat teripunaṟcaṅ kītacaṅka meraṉṟamaittut taṉatu cīrtti
	yeṅkumviḷak kiyateṉaviṉ ṉicaittamiḻai viḷakkiṉaṉā leḻilcēr tañcait
	tuṅkamuṟu māpirakām vaḷḷalperun taṉmaiyaiyār colaval lārē.
veṇpā.
	nāmē vicaittamiḻai naṉkuviḷak kikkaṭalcūḻ
	pūmē laḻiyāp pukaḻpaṭaittāy-māmēṉmai
	taḷḷā viṉimait tamiḻuṇarnta vāpirakām
	vaḷḷā luṉakkevarnēr vār.
viruttam.
	karavaṟucīrt tañcaiyiṟcaṅ kītavityā makācaṉacaṅ katti runtu
	caratakuṇa vāpirakām paṇṭitamē lēraṉarumait tavattāl vanta 
	marakata valliyuṅ kaṉaka valliyumpā ṭiyavatika maturakīta
	viratamuṇarn tavartamakku velappākuṅ kacappāku mitumey tāṉē.

makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ca. tirumalaivēluk kavirāyaravarkaḷ kumārar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi caṅkukkavirāyar iyaṟṟiyatu.

viruttam.
	muttamiḻu ṇaṭuttamiḻā micaittamiḻi ṉilakkaṇa nūṉ muṭivai yellāñ
	cittamakiḻ vuṟṟumika puttiyaṉu pavattiṉāṟ ṟērntu naṉṟāyp
	puttamuta vācakamā varaintulakeṅ kaṇumviḷakkip pukaḻpeṟ ṟāṉā
	letticaiyum pukaḻnturaikkun tañcainaka rāpirakā meṉumē lēraṉē
	pañcakā viyattuḷeraṉṟāñ cilappatikā rakkavikaḷ payiṉmēṟ kōḷā
	veñcalilā vakaiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭiyicai yilakkaṇamu meṭuttuk kāṭṭit
	tañcainaka rāpirakām vaḷḷalpuri caṅkapira caṅka mōrntu
	miñciyamut tamiḻvalaru mikaviyantā revarataṉai viyavā tārē.
	patamuṟunan tamiḻnūliṟ pakarumicai yilakkaṇattaip pārttē miṉṟi
	yituvaraiyav vitippaṭiyē pāṭiṉā reraruvaraiyu mīṅkuṅ kāṇēñ
	catataḷamā malarttaṭañcūḻ tañcaiyā pirakāmvit tāraṉ peṟṟa
	putalviyarav vitippaṭiyē pāṭiṉā raṟputamaṟ putamī tammā.



cōḻatēcattil viḷaṅkum pañcanatapurattil stāpikkappaṭṭirukkum kavarṇmeṇṭu
samaskiruta pāṭacālai mukkiya upāttiyāyarum rātāmaṅkala kirāma vāciyumāṉa
kāṉakavi pirammasrī nārāyaṇa cāstirikaḷ apippirāyam.
1. ulakattaikkāppāṟṟa vēṇṭiya apilāṣaiyāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa aḻakāṉa tōṭṭaṅkaḷālum amirutampōla maturamāṉa auṣataṅkaḷālum rāvsāhēp āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ jaṉaṅkaḷukku upakāram ceytum taṉporuḷ utaviyāl tamiḻppāṣaiyil eḻuti taṉ apip pirāyattai veḷiyiṭṭu taṉ kirantattil kīta cāstirattaip paravacceytu ataṟkuc cērapai uṇṭākkiṉār.
2. saṅkīta ratṉākara kirantattai eḻutiyavariṉ iṣṭamāṉa curuti kiramattaic caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ ikkālattil pāṭa caktiyaṟṟavarkaḷ. ākaiyāl paṇṭitar avarkaḷ yatṉam ceytatu jayamaṭaintateṉpatu eṉ apippirāyam.
3. cemmaiyāka vicāraṇaiceytu paṇṭitarāl vistāramāka eḻutappaṭṭirukkum kirantattaip paṟṟi vistāramāka eḻuta muṭiyātatāl saṅkirahamāka eḻutukiṟēṉ.
4. tamiḻppāṣaiyil 3 caṅkaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭṭa vātakkalakkattuṭaṉ kūṭiya saṅkīta campiratāyam vekukālamāka iruppatākac cemmaiyāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.
5.	tēcāntaraṅkaḷil saṅkīta piracuram niṟaintirukkum kālattil intat tēcattil saṅkīta sampiratāyattai ēṟpaṭutti, eṭuttuc colpavar illai.
6. tamiḻp pāṣaiyil vekukālamāka ēṟpaṭṭa caṅkatitāṉ itara pāṣaikaḷil veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭateṉṟu taṉ kirantattil vistāramāka eṭuttuk kāṇpikkiṟār.
7. tamiḻ vitvāṉkaḷāl appiyasikkappaṭṭa caṅkīta cāstira pirastāpa kōlāhalattāl vēṟu apippirāyam kolpavarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam kaṇṭikkappaṭṭatākat teriyavarukiṟatu.
8. ratṉākara kirantattil colli irukkum uṇmaiyaik kaṇita mūlamākap piracuram ceytal caṅkīta cāstirattil ācaiyuḷḷavarkaḷaic cantōṣappaṭutti vaikkiṟatu.
9. tamiḻ vitvāṉkaḷukkup paḻakkamākiya 96 curutiyai paricīlaṉai ceytu ataip pātipātiyāka vakuttu curuti kiṭaikkiṟateṉṟu kaṇakkiṉāl kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
10. kāyatrī mantirattiṉ akṣaraṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyil 2 akṣaraṅkaḷai kuṟaittu 22 curuti kiramam cāraṅkatēvar ēṟpaṭuttiṉār.
11. curutikaḷ 96 ākavum 48 ākavum 24 ākavum 12 ākavum pirikkappaṭukiṟatu.
12. intak karṉāṭaka kītam kāyakarkaḷāl pāṭappaṭukiṟateṉpatu piracittam. eṇṇikkaiyil pētam ēṟpaṭuvatu rākam pāṭuvatil uṇṭākum pētam.
13. mēṟkūṟiyavaikaḷil rāvcākēpu eṉṟu paṭṭappeyar aṭainta āpirakām paṇṭitar taṉṉuṭaiya karuṇāmirutasākaram eṉkiṟa kirantattil vistāramāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟār.
14. vaittiyam, yōkam, āyurvētam, jēratiṭacāstiram, vētānta cāstiram campantam iruppatāka avar curutikaṇakku eṭuttatil kāṭṭap paṭṭiruppatāka nāṉ aṟintu koṇṭēṉ.
15. vīṇai rūpam atiṉ curam, curuti, captam mutaliyavaikaḷ uṇṭāvatu maṉuṣiyaṉuṭaiya kaḻuttu, cura cuvāsam kalai mutaliyavaikaḷai ottu ceyyappaṭṭatu.
16. mēṟku tēcattiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ collum parimāṇattiṟkut takkappaṭi tvaṉicāmyam muṉkālattilēyē tamiḻ vitvāṉkaḷāl aṟiyap paṭṭateṉpatu pala vitamāka intak kirantattil collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
17. maṟṟavarkaḷ pāṭum sa ma pa ni eṉkiṟa 4 svaraṅkaḷiluṇṭākum 4 vīṇaikaḷil sa ka pa ni eṉkiṟa svaraṅkaḷiliruntu 4 jāti uṇṭākiṟatu.
18. *   *   *  *   *   *  *   *   *
19. ratṉākaram ceytavarum, paratamuṉiyum muṉ irunta tirāviṭarkaḷum ivvitamāṉa saṅkīta rahasyattai collavillai eṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
20. oru stāyiyil curuti 22 eṉpatu oru iṭattilum collappaṭavillai eṉṟum curuti 24 tāṉ eṉpatu collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉṟum collappaṭṭatu.
21. curuti 22 eṉṟu colpavarkaḷiṉ koḷkaiyai taṭuppataṟku ivvaḷavu pirayāsam eṭuttuk koṇṭu taṉ apippirāyam eḻutappaṭṭatu.
22. curutisvaravivēkam ciramattai eṭuttukkoṇṭu ituvaraiyil oruvarālum aṟiyappaṭavillai. intak kirantattil tirāviṭa piramāṇattāl kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
23. tamiḻ coṟkaḷ mirukapakṣi, tvaṉikku camāṉamāṉa vinyācattuṭaṉ kūṭiyavaikaḷāka veku kālamāyirukkiṟatu. pāṣaiyiṉ uṟpattikkuk kāraṇam pirākiruta mūlaṅkaḷ eṉṟum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
24. maṟṟavarkaḷālaṟiyakkūṭāta kīti, curuti svarastāṉaṅkaḷaic coṉṉa kāraṇattiṉāl tirāviṭarkaḷ muṉṉamē aṟintavarkaḷeṉṟum keḷarava maṭaintavarkaḷeṉṟum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
25. parōṭā rājāviṉāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatum caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ camūkamum aṭaṅkiyatumāṉa capaiyil taṉ peṇkaḷ pāṭi rājā mantiri mutaliyavarkaḷ cantōṣamaṭaintataik koṇṭē ivar apippirāyam oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu.
26. āpirakām paṇṭitar ciramappaṭṭu eḻutiya kirantattil cūkṣamamāka aṟiyakkūṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷai vicāraṇai ceytirukkiṟār.
27. jaṉaṅkaḷukkuk koṭuppataṟkākavē taṉārjaṉam ceyta ip pirapu mēṉmaiyāka irukkak kaṭavar. ivvitam nārāyaṇakavi taṉ apippirāyattai oḷikkāmal veḷiyiṭṭār.


eṭṭaiyāpuram camastāṉam samaskiruta vitvāṉ
pirammasrī kurunāta cāstirikaḷ apippirāyam
1. muṉkālattil mahāviṣṇu camuttirattaik kaṭaintu amirutam eṭuttatupōla caṅkīta cāstira camuttirattiliruntu rāvcāhēp āpirakām paṇṭitar karuṇāmirutam eṉkiṟa curuti viṣaya kirantamāṉa amirutattai aṭaintār.
2. evvitam muṉkālattil camuttirattiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa amirutam tēvarkaḷukkup pirītiyai uṇṭākkiyatō avvitamākac caṅkīta sāhityam aṟintavarkaḷum kāṉam ceypavarkaḷumāṉa vitvāṉkaḷukku intak karṇāmiruta kirantam pirītiyai uṇṭākka vēṇṭum.
3. pāṭumantirattai uccāraṇam ceypavarkaḷai rakṣippatāl kāyatrī eṉkiṟa peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu. antak kāyatrī mantirattil irukkum 24 akṣaraṅkaḷum 24 curutistāṉaṅkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
4. japam ceyvatil mantirattil uḷḷa akṣarattai viṭuvatu tōṣa mākum. kāṉam ceyvatil curutiyaik kuṟaippatu tōṣamākum. antac curutiyiṉ eṇṇikkai 24 curutistāṉaṅkaḷālum 24 eṉpatil santēka millai.
5. ākaiyāl uttamavitvāṉkaḷ ellā pirayatṉaṅkaḷ ceytum 24 eṇṇikkaiyuḷḷa curutistāṉaṅkaḷai aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
(tañcāvūr vakkīl makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi pi.vi. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷāl tamiḻil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭatu.)
caṅkītattil tērnta pala vitvacirēramaṇikaḷiṉ apippirāyaṅkaḷai karuṇāmirta cākaram mutal puttakam - nāṉkām pākattil kāṇalām.
"kuṟainilattāṉattiyaṉṟa pāṭalamutam parukiṉāṉ" eṉṟapaṭi kuṟainta curamāka varum nuṭpacurutikaḷiṉ iṉimaiyaiyaṟinta pala vitvacirēramaṇikaḷ caṅkīta vityāmakājaṉa caṅkattiṉ ēḻāvatu kāṉpareṉsil koṭutta apippirāyaṅkaḷum piṟavum ipputtakattiṉ nāṉkām pākattil cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.	

1.	parōṭā, tivāṉ cākip makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi V.P. mātavarāv C.I.E.  avarkaḷ.
2. 	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi vaiṇīkacikāmaṇi cēṣaṇṇā avarkaḷ, maicūr.

7.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi pañcāpakēca pākavatar avarkaḷ, tañcai.

tañcai caṅkīta vityā makājaṉa caṅkattiṉ ēḻāvatu kāṉpareṉciṉ pañcāyattār.
