karuṇāmirta cākaram
mutal puttakam - mutal pākam
intiya caṅkīta carittirac curukkam
innūluḷ . . 
mutaṟpākam : caṅkītam pūrvakālattil iyal, icai, nāṭakameṉṉum, muttamiḻil oṉṟāyiruntateṉpataiyum, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ pūrvīkattaiyum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum, icait tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya vivarattaiyum cila icai vallōraiyum paṟṟic collum.
kaṭavuḷ tuṇai.


karuṇāmirta cākaram.
mutal puttakam.
mutal pākam.
intiya caṅkīta carittirac curukkam.
I. caṅkītattiṉ perumaiyum ataṉ uṟpattiyum.
ulakellām taṉatu oru collāl uṇṭākki, avai yāvaṟṟiṟkum tāṉē uyirāy, āṉanta mūrttiyāy, aṉanta kalyāṇakuṇacīlaṉāy āṇṭu naṭattivarum karttaṉ iru poṟpāta kamalaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅki,  karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum iccaṅkīta cāstirattai eḻutat toṭaṅkukiṟēṉ.
ulakukku mutalvaṉāṉa karttaṉ, mutal mutal nātacorūpiyāy, piṉ jīvaṉāy, jīvarkaḷiṉ uṇarvāy, uṇarvil aṟivāy, aṟivil āṉantamāy, āṉantattil nātamāy, nātattil kītamāy, kītattil layamāy, āṉanta narttaṉañceytu ulakaip paripālikkiṟāṉ.
cattu cittu āṉantaṉāy, aṇṭapuvaṉa carācaraṅkaḷ aṉaittum niṟaintu niṉṟa apparamaṉai uḷḷuṇarnta periyōr, tamatu tūla tattuvaṅkaḷaiyum cūṭcama tattuvaṅkaḷaiyum kāraṇaṉākiya ak karttaṉiṭattil oṭukki, avaṉil viḷaṅkum kōṭi cūriya pirapaiyil tāṅkaḷum pirapai peṟṟu, jīvakāruṇyam pārāṭṭi, mutti nilaipeṟa catā tutittu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. vaṇaṅkiya periyōr aṭainta mēmpataviyaik kaṇṭu, tāmum appatam peṟa ācittu, āṉanta mūrttiyai iṭaiviṭātu tutittu, avaravarkkākum aḷavaṟṟa kirupai peṟṟār maṟṟavarum. kirupai peṟṟōr yāvarum, mēlāṉa avaṉ pukaḻaic collit tutittuk kāṉam ceykiṟārkaḷēyoḻiya, tammai iḻivu paṭuttum laukīka kāṉaṅkaḷaic ceyyār. ikkāṉamum, kāṉa mūrttiyiṉ ciruṣṭi titi cammāram eṉṉum muttoḻil pōlavē, tōṟṟam viruttilayattaiyuṭaiya apinayam rākam tāḷam eṉṉum mūṉṟu aṅkaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyaJ. immūṉṟaiyum oṉṟākap pāvittu, muttoḻil mūrttiyaip patticeyyavēṇṭiyatu avaciyam. appaṭic ceyvatāl avaṉ aruṭceyalil kāṇappaṭum yāvaṟṟilum, avaṉē aṅkaṅkup paripūraṇaṉāy iruntu apinayittu āṭippāṭi āṉanta narttaṉañ ceykiṟāṉeṉṟu, pirattiyaṭcamāy aṟiyalām. āṉantak kūttāṭum mutalvaṉaik kāṇum paktaṉ, tāṉum iṉṉicaiyuṭaṉ āṭippāṭik karttaṉaik koṇṭāṭuvāṉ.
karttaṉaik koṇṭāṭiya paktarkaḷuḷ taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ aṉu pōkattiṟku ēṟṟavāṟu teyvattai rājaṉākavum, peṟṟeṭutta tāy tantai yākavum, kuruvākavum, āpatcakāyaṉākavum, tuyartīrtta maruttuvaṉākavum, arumaimakavākavum, iṉiya maṇavāḷaṉākavum pāvittu vāyāra vāḻttiṉar cilar; tōttirittu vaṇaṅkiṉār palar; iṭar tīrkka iṟaiñciṉār cilar; niṉaittavai peṟa vēṇṭiṉar anēkar; pirivāṟṟāmaiyāl piralāpittār cilar; kaṇṭaṭainta cilar āṉantak kūttāṭiṉār; kāṇavirumpiya palar tūtupāṭi ucāviṉār; uḷḷaṉpu mēliṭṭu, uvantututittār. avaṉ kuṇaṅkaḷai ovvoṉṟāy eṭutteṭuttu vistarittu, maṟṟavar aṟiya viyantu pāṭiṉār. taṅkaḷ apāttirattai niṉaintu iraṅkiṉār. tam tavaṟutalkaḷai niṉaittu maṉṉikkap pirārttittār. kātal mikunta cilar, avaṉ tiru uruvai niṉaittu, uṇavaiyum ulakaiyum maṟantār. uttamarkaḷiṉ jīviyattaiyum avarkaḷ pakavāṉait tutittup pāṭiyavaṟṟuḷ uttamamāṉa pākaṅkaḷaiyum oṉṟāyt tiraṭṭi, maṟṟavarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟap paramparaiyāyc collivaittārkaḷ nam muṉṉōr. piṉṉuḷḷōr, ataiyē vētameṉa eḻuti vaittārkaḷ. vētattai ātāramākakkoṇṭa ovvoruvarum, teyva cannitikaḷilum potu stalaṅkaḷilum, taṅkaḷ vaṇakkattilum, vētattiluḷḷa cilapākaṅkaḷait tāmē pāṭṭākappāṭi ārātittu, āṉantamaṭaintu vantārkaḷ. pāṭaccaktiyillātavarkaḷ, pāṭakarkaḷai niyamittup pāṭaccollik kēṭṭu āṉantittu vantārkaḷ. pāṭutaṟkut tuṇaikkaruvikaḷ palapalaceytu, mikunta toṉiyuṭaṉ ārpparittārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ varuttam yāvum maṟantu, ēkamaṉatāy iṉṉicaiyil īṭupaṭṭu, pakavāṉai ārātikkum innilai naṉṉilaiyeṉṟu niṉaittu, mēlulakattilum teyvattai ippaṭiyē ārātippōmeṉṟu ācittu, mēlum mēlum ūkkam koṇṭārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ kāṉattaip pala mukamāy virutticeyya, iṉiya curaṅkaḷaiyum avaikaḷiṉ ālāpaṉattaiyum, ellōrum ēkōpittuc collakkūṭiya kāla aḷavākiya tāḷattaiyum, oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāykkaṇṭu viruttiyaṭaintu vantārkaḷ ivaṟṟil aṉukūla maṭaintu varukaiyil, taṅkaḷukkuc cantōṣamāṉa kālaṅkaḷilum, teyva cannitiyilum, tāṅkaḷ cāppiṭuṅ kālattiluṅkūṭa, pāṭattuvaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
teruvil viḷaiyāṭum ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ kāṉaviruttiyaippōla, nām kēṭkum iṉiya kāṉamum, palayukaṅkaḷākap pala upakaraṇaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu viruttiyāyiṟṟeṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. eppaṭiyeṉṟāl, ōcaitarum palakaikaḷ peṭṭikaḷ katavukaḷ takarattakaṭukaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷait taṭṭi, jati kaṇam layameṉṉum tāḷattukkuriya aṅkaṅkaḷ oruvarum pōtikkāmalē cuyamāy aṟintukoṇṭa pēccaṟiyāta pālarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ koñcam periyavarkaḷākumpoḻutu, ciṟu tampaṭṭam koṭṭāṅkaccittampaṭṭam keñjirā mutaliyavaikaḷil paḻaki viḷaiyāṭukiṟārkaḷ. ataṉ mēl atil piriyamuḷḷa cilar periyavarkaḷākumpoḻutu, irupakkamum tōl kaṭṭiya mattaḷam mirutaṅkam pēri uṭukkai nakarā tapilā pōṉṟa koṭṭuṅ karuvikaḷaicceytu, kaṭippiṉāl aṭittut tāḷattil viruttiyāṉārkaḷ. tāḷa ñāṉam mutal mutal ellārukkum iyalpāy amaintirukkiṟaJ. kuḻantaikaḷ piṟantu nālaintu mātattiṟkuḷ caṅkītattaik kēṭkumpōtu tāḷattuṭaṉ cāyntāṭuvatai nām aṟivōm. mēlum ovvoruvaruṭaiya cuvācamum irattācayattuṭippum, kālappiramāṇattai yuṭaiya tākavē yirukkiṟatu.
itupōlavē, teruvil viḷaiyāṭum ciṟuvarkaḷ, pūvaracu pōṉṟa marattiṉ ilaikaḷaic curuṭṭi, atil ciṟutta oru pākattaik kaiyiṉāl amukki, oṭuṅkiya tuvāramirukkumpaṭi ceytu atil ūtiyum, ataṉpiṉ kuḻaliṉ parumaṉukkum ataṉ vāykkum takunta vitamāyc cattam piṟakkiṟateṉṟu kaṇṭu, kīḻ mēlāyuḷḷa iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu nālu cattaṅkaḷaic cērttum atil oṟṟumai kaṇṭu cantōṣittārkaḷ. piṉpu tuvāramuḷḷa pūcaṇi ilaikkāmpukaḷai kuḻalkaḷāka naṟukki avaikaḷiṉ oru pakkattil ilaik kuḻalkaḷai vaittu ūta, kuḻalkaḷ evvaḷavu nīḷukiṟatō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu cattam mantamākiṟateṉṟum, kuḻalkaḷ kuṟukakkuṟukat toṉiyuḷḷa nātam piṟakkiṟateṉṟum kaṇṭu, ēkōpittu āṉantak kūttāṭiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil iṉṉicaiyil piriyaṅkoṇṭa cilar, periyavarkaḷāṉapoḻutu kompu caṅku mūṅkil īrakkaḻi nāṇal mutaliyavaikaḷil tuḷaiyiṭṭum, maram poṉ veḷḷi pittaḷai mutaliyavaṟṟil pala vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kuḻalceytu tuḷaiyiṭṭum, iṉṉicai piṟakka ūtiṉārkaḷ. itupōlavē, narampuk karuvikaḷilum paṭippaṭiyāy viruttiyāṉārkaḷ. vaḷaiyakkūṭiya oru kucciyil, melliya kayiṟu allatu narampukaḷaik kaṭṭi mīṭṭa, ōcai piṟappatai aṟintārkaḷ. atiṉiṉṟu periya mūṅkilkaḷai vil pōlum vaḷaittut tōl vaṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi, kuṇattoṉiceytu maṇikaḷaiyum cataṅkaikaḷaiyum kōttut tāḷattiṟku iṇaṅkak kuṟukiya kaṭippiṉāl aṭittu, villaṭippāṭṭukaḷ paṭittārkaḷ. tantikaḷ allatu narampukaḷ, ākāyamaṭaṅkiya oru pāttiram peṭṭi allatu curaik kuṭukkai vaḻiyāyc cellumpoḻutu, atika nātam koṭukkiṟa teṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāyk kaṇṭu, tuntiṉāmā, oru tantic curaikkāykkiṉṉari pōlotta vāttiyaṅkaḷ ceytu, taṅkaḷ cārīrattiṟkēṟṟa curuti vaittuk koṇṭu, pakavāṉuṭaiya kuṇaṅkaḷai varṇittup pāṭiṉārkaḷ. kaikaḷāl mīṭṭuvataṟkup patil, villukaḷāl tantikaḷai iḻuttu vācikkakkūṭiya akappaikkiṉṉarikaḷ pōṉṟavai ceytu vācittārkaḷ. piṟaku māṉiṭa cārīrattiṟku ottatum curastāṉaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭatumāṉa mikac ciṟappup poruntiya vīṇai ceytārkaḷ. ataṉiṉṟum caṅkītam, ataṟkuriya cila vitikaḷuṭaṉ viruttiyākikkoṇṭē varukiṟatu.
ciṟuvarkaḷ taṭṭi viḷaiyāṭum palakaik kataviṉ ōcaiyiliruntu, tampaṭṭam pērikai mattaḷam mirutaṅkam tapilā uṭukkai kaittāḷam cēmakkalam callari maṇi cataṅkai mutaliya koṭṭuṅ karuvikaḷum, ūti viḷaiyāṭiya ilaikkuḻalkaḷiliruntu, tārai ottu nākacuram mukavīṇai makaṭi pullāṅkuḻal kompu caṅku mutaliya tuḷaikkaruvikaḷum, palliṉāl kaṭittu kaiyiṉāl piṭittiḻuttu oru kaiyiṉāl mīṭṭip piṟanta iṉiya nātattiliruntu, tuntiṉāmā kiṉṉari tampuru akappaikkiṉṉari cuntari vīṇai ruttiravīṇai pēriyāḻ makarayāḻ mutaliya mīṭṭum tanti vāttiyaṅkaḷum ciṟuvar viḷaiyāṭṭiliruntē tōṉṟi, paṭippaṭiyāy viruttiyāyiṉaveṉṟu nām aṟikiṟōm. nātaṉatu tiruviḷaiyāṭṭai eṭuttukkūṟat tiṟamaiyuṭaiyōr yāvar!
mēṟkūṟiya vāttiyaṅkaḷ, tēcattāriṉ kaittoḻil tiṟamaikkut takuntapaṭi, vevvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷaiyum pāṣaiyāl vevvēṟu peyarkaḷaiyum peṟuvatēyaṉṟi, kāriyattil caṅkītattiṟkut tuṇaik karuvikaḷākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāṉa vitiyiṉ arumai paṟṟiyum, vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ utavipaṟṟiyum, ovvoru tēcattārum, tāṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkum kāṉattait teyvamē koṭuttāreṉṟum, upatēcittāreṉṟum, tāmē ceytu kāṭṭiṉāreṉṟum mēṉmai pārāṭṭun takutiyuṭaiyatā yirukkiṟatu. tiṉai aḷavu paṉittuḷi, etirniṟkum malaiyaiyum veku tiṭṭamākat taṉakkuḷ piratipimpittuk kāṭṭi niṟpatu pōla, ovvoru tēcattiṉ kāṉamum, antantat tēcattār maṉataip paravacappaṭutti, tēṭarum tiraviyakkuṉṟākiya teyvattaiyum taṉṉiṟkāṭṭa caktiyuṭaiyatā yirukkiṟatu.
“ātiyilē vārttai (nātam) iruntatu, anta vārttai tēvaṉiṭatti liruntatu, anta vārttai (nātam) tēvaṉāyiruntaJ. avar ātiyilē tēvaṉōṭu iruntār. cakalamum avar mūlamāy uṇṭāyiṟṟu; uṇṭāṉa toṉṟum avaraiyallāmal uṇṭākavillai. avarukkuḷ jīvaṉiruntatu, anta jīvaṉ maṉitarukku oḷiyāyiruntaJ. ulakattilē vantu enta maṉitaṉaiyum pirācippikkiṟa oḷiyē anta meyyāṉa oḷi. avar ulakattil iruntār, ulakam avarmūlamāy uṇṭāyiṟṟu, ulakamō avarai aṟiyavillai. inta vārttai (nātam) māmicamākik kirupaiyiṉālum cattiyattiṉālum niṟaintavarāy namakkuḷ vācam paṇṇiṉār.” (yōvāṉ 1 - 1 mutal)
ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ātikāraṇamāyirunta nātattiṉāl ulakam paṭippaṭiyāy cūṭcuma tūla tōṟṟattaiyaṭaintaJ. tūla cūṭcama kāraṇa meṉṉum munnilaikaḷaiyum aṭainta jīvarkaḷuṭaiya uḷḷattiliruntu, avarkaḷ karuttait terivikkac cūṭcamamāṉa palapala iṉiya toṉikaḷum, tūlamāṉa palapala apinayaṅkaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa. karuttait terivikkum toṉikaḷum apinayaṅkaḷum, kālakkiramattil pāṣaiyāki varivaṭivamāy eḻutappaṭṭu, nūlkaḷum cāstiraṅkaḷum kalaikaḷumāyiṉa. tāṅkaḷ mikavum arumaiyāka niṉaittavaikaḷum, vāḻaiyaṭivāḻaiyāy vanta paramparaiyārukkup pāṭam collivaittu vantavaikaḷumāṉa teyva tōttiraṅkaḷaiyē, mutal mutal eḻutivaittārkaḷ. atiṉālēyē ataṟku mutal nūl eṉṟu peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kātiṉālēyē kēṭṭup pāṭamākkikkoṇṭiruntakālattil, ataic curuti eṉṟārkaḷ. mutal nūl uṇṭāṉapiṉ, ataṉ uṭporuḷai aṟivataṟku ētuvāṉa upāyaṅkaḷaiyum upaniṭataṅkaḷaiyum āṟu cāstiraṅkaḷaiyum aṟupattunālu kalaikaḷaiyum, avaikaḷai nuṭpamāy viḷakkuvataṟku ētuvāka itikācaṅkaḷaiyum purāṇaṅ kaḷaiyum ceytārkaḷ. teyva paktarkaḷiṉ irutayamākiya kaḷañciyatti liruntu varum ceyyuḷkaḷil maṟaintirukkum karuttukaḷ, uḷḷapaṭi arttamāvatu kūṭiyatallavē. nātap piramattaiyē kītamāyt tōttirikkum vētam eppaṭi mukkiyamāṉatō, appaṭiyē nātappiramattaiyē vistarikkum caṅkīta cāstiramākiya kāntarva vētamum mukkiyamāṉaJ. vētattiṉ upāṅkamākiya kāntarvavētattai allatu caṅkītattai, kailācattil niruti mūlaiyil niruttam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ parama civaṉ upatēcittatāka iṉṉum collappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. immutalnūlukkuppiṉ, anēkavaḻi nūlkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
    itai vācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē! iṟṟaikku aṉēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷatāka eṇṇappaṭum mutal nūlil, atāvatu, cāma vētattil, iṉṟu nām ellā vāttiyaṅkaḷilum iṉṉicaiyuṭaiyateṉṟu matikkum vīṇaiyum, ataṉ ceymuṟaiyum kūṟappaṭṭiruppatumaṉṟi ataik koṇṭē pakavāṉait tutikkavēṇṭumeṉṟum colliyirukkumāṉāl, intiya caṅkītattiṉ pūrvīkattai eṉṉeṉṟu colvatu carasvati nāratar tumpuru anumāṉ mutaliya periyōrkaḷ, taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṇaiyaik koṇṭu iṭaiviṭāmal teyvattait tutittuk koṇṭirukkiṟār kaḷeṉṟum, paramacivaṉ tamatu irukātilum kampalar acuvatarar eṉṉum vaiṇīka cirōmaṇikaḷaik kuṇṭalamākat tarittukkoṇṭu avarkaḷ kāṉattaic catā kēṭṭukkoṇṭirukkiṟāreṉṟum collappaṭumāṉāl, vīṇākāṉattiṉ mēṉmaiyaiyum caṅkītattiṉ pūrvattaiyum nitāṉikkattakka vallavar yāvar?
aruḷ nātaṉ ulakil avataritta camayattil, tēva cēṉaikaḷ vāṉattil tōṉṟi, pūmiyiluḷḷōr kēṭkumpaṭi “uṉṉataṅkaḷilirukkiṟa tēvaṉukku makimaiyum pūmiyilē camātāṉamum maṉuṣarmēl piriyamu muṇṭāvatāka” veṉṟu pāṭiṉārkaḷāṉāl, caṅkītattiṉ upayōkamum upayōkikkum camayamum evvaḷavu mēlāṉavaiyeṉṟu kāṇkiṟōm. mēlum, tēvalōkattil tēvācaṉattiṉmuṉ cakala paricuttavāṉkaḷum tēvatūtarkaḷum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ curamaṇṭalaṅkaḷai vācittukkoṇṭu, putuppāṭṭaic catā pāṭukiṟatākavum, “cēṉaikaḷiṉ karttar paricuttar, paricuttar, paricuttar; vāṉamum pūmiyum avaruṭaiya makimaiyāl niṟaintirukkiṉṟaṉa” veṉṟu muḻaṅkukiṟatākavum collappaṭumāṉāl, immai maṟumai eṉṉum irunilaikaḷilum caṅkītattiṉ mukkiyattai eṭuttuc collavum vēṇṭumō? immukkiyamāṉa viṣayattil, atāvatu, caṅkītattiṉ mēṉmaiyaiyum ataṉ pūrvattaiyum collavēṇṭiya viṣayattil, teyvattiṉ nittiya kālattaiyum, pūmi uṇṭāṉa kālamutal ituvaraiyum ceṉṟa nāṭkaḷaiyumē ataṟkuk kālamākavum, mōṭca pirāptiyaiyē atu tarum mēṉmaiyākavum collak kūṭiyatēyaṉṟi, vēṟu ciṟappuccolla iyalāta mēmpāṭuṭaiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
mēlum, periyōrkaḷāl collappaṭṭa paṟpala pākkaḷum, pakavāṉait tutikkum pākkaḷum, iṉṉicaikalantapiṉpē, maṉataip paravacappaṭuttak kūṭiyavaikaḷā yirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉpatai nāmellārum aṟivōm. pūmiyil maṟaintukiṭakkum vittukkaḷ maḻaiyiṉāl muḷaittut taḻaittup pūttuk kāyttu naṟpalaṉaittaruvatupōla, muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ pākkaḷ yāvum, iṉṉicai kalantapiṉpē uyarvāṉa arttaṅkaḷai maṉatiṟkup pulappaṭutti āṉantam aṭaivikkiṟaJ.
	“kallēṉum aiya orukālattil urukum eṉkaṉṉeñcam urukavilaiyē.”
	“nāṭakattāl uṉṉaṭiyārpōl naṭitJ.”
eṉṟa pāṭṭukaḷ oru irākattil vevvēṟu vitamāṉa ālāpaṉam ceyyappaṭum pōtu avaṟṟil aṭaṅkiya karuttukaḷ ettaṉaiyō, attaṉaiyum maṉatukkup pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum, iṉṉicaiyāṉatu, maṉavaruttam tīrttuc camātāṉattaittarum; carīra tuṉpattaiyum pōkkaṭittu iḷaippāṟutal tarum; vēlaiyiṉkaṣṭam tōṉṟātapaṭi uṟcākam ceytuvaikkum. iravil kaṇviḻittu irāṭṭiṉam nūṟkum stirīkaḷ, taṅkaḷ varuttamtīra iṉṉicaiyōṭu pakavāṉait tutikkiṟārkaḷ. ēṟṟam iṟaikkum camucāriyum, mā araikkum stiriyum, ōṭamtaḷḷum ōṭak kāraṉum, nāṟṟunaṭum peṇkaḷum, cāntu iṭikkumkūlikaḷum, pakavāṉait tutittup pāṭit taṅkaḷ varuttattai maṟantu, taṅkaḷ vāḻnāṭkaḷai veku ullācamāyk kaḻikkiṟārkaḷ. kapparai ēnti yācikkum piccaikkāraṉum cēmakkalaṅkoṭṭi yācikkum tātaṉum, upātāṉam vāṅkum piramacāriyum, aṉṉamyācikkum aṉṉak kāvaṭikkāraṉum ekkālakkaṇṇi, parāparak kaṇṇi, uṭaṟkūṟu, neñcaṟiviḷakkam mutaliya ñāṉappāṭalkaḷai iṉiya paṇṇōṭu paṭittut taṅkaḷ nāṭkaḷaik kaḻikkiṟārkaḷ. tāyiṉ iṉiya tālāṭṭup pāṭṭaik kēṭṭut tūṅkum kuḻantai iṉṉum kēṭka virumpi ūmkoṭṭukiṟaJ. kaṉṟukkup pālkoṭāta muraṭṭup pacukkaḷ, kītāri pāṭum icaikku vacappaṭṭup pūraṇamākap pālkoṭukkiṟaJ. piṟar kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭāmal maṟaintu vacikkum nākappāmpum, pāmpāṭṭikaḷ ūtum makaṭiyiṉ iṉiya nātattaikkēṭṭut taṉṉai maṟantu āṉantattiṉāl paṭamvirittāṭukiṟaJ. kītāriyiṉ iṉiya pullāṅkuḻal ōcaiyaikkēṭṭu, māṭukaḷ piṉ cellukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭāḷattil vācikkum vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ ōcaiyāl, pōrvīrarkaḷ uṟcākamaṭaikiṟārkaḷ. kutiraikaḷ, vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ tāḷaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavitamāy kālkaḷ vaittu naṭantu veku tīviramāy muṉṉēṟic cellukiṉṟaṉa. iṉiya ōcaiyuṭaiya paṭcikaḷ, atikālaiyil viḻittuppāṭuvataiyum, atē kālattil kōvilkaḷilum rāja araṇmaṉai kaḷilum caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkuvataiyum, maṇikaḷ aṭippataiyum, nām kēṭkiṟōm. mālaiyilum appaṭiyē nām yāvarum pakavāṉai iṉṉicaiyuṭaṉ ārātikkiṟōm. ippaṭi, palavitattilum maṉuṣaṉukkuc cantōṣattait taruvataṟkuc caṅkītattaiviṭac ciṟantatu vēṟoṉṟu millai. caṅkītamē, ivvulakattilum maṟu ulakattilum mēṉmaiyuṭaiyaJ. caṅkītattiṉāl tutippatē nammai uṇṭākkiṉa karttaṉukkup piriyamāṉaJ. nāratar tumpuru anumār irāvaṇaṉ tāvītaracaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ caṅkītattaik koṇṭē teyvattiṉ kirupaiyaip peṟṟārkaḷ.
caṅkītamāṉatu maṉataic cāntappaṭutti, teyvattōṭu oṟṟumaippaṭac ceykiṟaJ. caṅkītamāṉatu cakala naṟkuṇaṅkaḷaiyum vaḷarttut teyvapatam peṟac ceykiṟaJ. immēmpāṭuṭaiya caṅkītattai appiyācikkum jaṉaṅkaḷ evarkaḷō, avarkaḷai atu uyartti avarkaḷirukkum tēcattai mēṉmaip paṭuttic cakala kalaikaḷilum celvattilum teyvapaktiyilum virutti yaṭaiyacceykiṟaJ. rājaṉum kuṭikaḷum caṅkītattai mēlāṉatāka matittu appiyācippārkaḷāṉāl, avarkaḷait tēvarkaḷeṉṟum tēvajaṉaṅka ḷeṉṟum, avarkaḷ kuṭiyirukkumiṭattait tēvapūmiyeṉṟum, maṟṟavarkaḷ koṇṭāṭum uṉṉata nilaikkuḷḷākac ceykiṟaJ. caṅkītattaikkoṇṭu teyvattai ārātikkum uttama aracarkaḷ, teyva piramāṇaṅkaḷ kaṭukaḷavum picakāmal, puliyum pacuvum oru tuṟaiyil taṇṇīr kuṭikka, maṉṉuyir yāvum taṉṉuyirāka niṉaittup paripālaṉamceytu, nīti irakkam poṟumai aṉpu camātāṉam mutaliya uttama kuṇaṅkaḷōṭu kuṭikaḷai naṭatti, ivvulakattilēyē mōṭcāṉantattai nilai nāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu mēmpāṭuṭaiya caṅkītattai aṟpamāka niṉaittulaukika vaḻikaḷil upayōkappaṭuttukiṟavarkaḷ puṉṉeṟiyaṭaintu maṟaintu pōvārkaḷ eṉpatu niccayam. teyvattait tutippataiyē mutaṉmaiyākakkoṇṭa nam muṉṉōr caṅkīta cāstirattaippaṟṟi mika virivāka eḻutiyiruntārka ḷeṉṟālum, eḻutappaṭṭavaikaḷ ikkālattil perumpālum aḻintum nūtaṉamāṉavaikaḷ uṇṭākiyum palatappaṟaikaḷ kalantum iruppataṉāl, teḷivāy aṟintu koḷvataṟku aritāyirukkiṟatu. caṅkītam uṟpattiyāṉa kālam iṭam ātarittu vaḷarttavarkaḷiṉ peyar mutaliyavaikaḷait tiṭṭamāy aṟintu koḷvatu ilēcāṉa kāriyamalla. eṉṟālum, oruvāṟu ulakattavar vaḻakkattilum paḻakkattilum muṉ nūlkaḷilum vaḻaṅkivarum cila kāriyaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu, nām aṟiyakkūṭiya cila ātāraṅkaḷaip pārppatu, oruvāṟu namakkut tiruptiyait tarumeṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
II. caṅkītam pūrvamāyuḷḷateṉpaṟkuc cattiyavēta ātāramum akkālattil vaḻaṅki vanta caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷum.
1. jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉ kiṉṉaramum nākacuramum
iruntaṉa veṉpaJ.
teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ kālattai nām pārppataṟkumuṉ, itaṟku appuṟamāyirunta tēcaṅkaḷil oruvāṟu niccayikkakkūṭiyavaiyum carittira ārāyccikkārar oppuk koḷḷakkūṭiyavaiyumākiya cattiya vētattil uḷḷa cila ātāraṅkaḷaip pārppōm: iṟṟaikkuc caṟṟēṟak kuṟaiya 3400 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta mōcē muṉivarāl eḻutappaṭṭa ātiyākamam 4-m atikāram 20, 21, 22-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil
“ātāḷ yāpālaip peṟṟāḷ. avaṉ kūṭāraṅkaḷil vācam paṇṇukiṟavarkaḷukkum mantai mēykkiṟavarkaḷukkum takappaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ cakōtaraṉuṭaiya peyar yūpāl. avaṉ kiṉṉarakkārar nākacurakkārar yāvarukkum takappaṉāṉāṉ. cillāḷum tūpāl kāyīṉaip peṟṟāḷ. avaṉ pittaḷai irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ toḻilāḷar yāvarukkum ācāriyaṉāṉāṉ.”
eṉṟu eḻutiyirukkiṟatu. kiṉṉarakkārarukkum nākacurakkārarukkum tantaiyākiya yūpālum, pittaḷai irumpu mutaliya lōkaṅkaḷil vēlai ceyyum toḻilāḷikaḷukkut tantaiyākiya tūpālkāyīṉum kiṟistu piṟantataṟkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya 4000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉiruntatāka vētacāstirikaḷ kaṇittirukkiṟārkaḷ. kiṟistuvukkup piṉṉuḷḷa 1914 varuṣaṅkaḷaiyum atōṭu cērttup pārppōmēyāṉāl iṟṟaikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya 5900 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷatākum. ivarkaḷ irunta kālattiṟkuc cumār 1650 varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ jalappiraḷayam vantatākat terikiṟatu. jalappiraḷaya kālattiṟku muṉṉālēyē ciṉṉa āciyāviluḷḷa pūrvattār, caṅkīta vittaiyilum maṟṟum kaittoḻilkaḷilum pēr pōṉavarkaḷāy iruntārkaḷeṉṟu veku tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu.
mēlum kāyīṉ oru paṭṭaṇattaikkaṭṭi ataṟkut taṉ kumāraṉākiya ēṉōkkuṭaiya peyaraiyiṭṭāṉeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. akkālattil rākṣatar pūmiyiliruntārkaḷeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟa maṉuṣarākiya palavāṉkaḷ 969, 962, 900, 800, 700 mutaliya varuṣam varaikkum nīṭitta āyuḷuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum, aṅkē collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivarkaḷ jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉiruntavarkaḷ. itupōlavē, jalap piraḷayattiṟkumuṉ intiyāvilum, nīṇṭa āyuḷ peṟṟa maṉitarkaḷum irākṣatarkaḷum pirapalamāṉa paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum aracarkaḷum kiṉṉaram nākacuram vīṇai pullāṅkuḻal tāḷam apinayam mutaliyavaikaḷaippaṟṟic collum caṅkīta cāstiramumiruntaṉaveṉṟu itaṉpiṉ pārppōm.
2. mōcē muṉivariṉ pāṭṭum, miriyāmiṉ naṭaṉamum
tampurōṭu kūṭiya pāṭṭum
jalappiraḷayattiṟkup piṉpu, isravēlarkaḷ ekiptaiviṭṭup puṟap paṭukaiyil ekiptiyar ceṅkaṭalil amiḻntapōtu, catturupayam nīkkit taṅkaḷaik kāttaṟkāka mōcē muṉivarum isravēl puttirarum, karttaraip pukaḻntu pāṭiṉārkaḷeṉṟu yāttirākamam 15-m atikāram mutalāvatu vākkiyattilum,
“ārōṉiṉ cakōtariyākiya miriyām eṉṉum tīrkkat tariciyāṉavaḷum taṉ kaiyilē tampurai eṭuttukkoṇṭāḷ. cakala stirīkaḷum tampurukaḷōṭum naṭaṉattōṭum avaḷukkup piṉceṉṟu karttaraip pāṭiṉārkaḷ”
eṉṟu atē atikāram 20-m vacaṉattilum colliyiruppataip pārkkalām. avarkaḷ pāṭiya vacaṉaṅkaḷaip pārkkaiyil, taṟkālattiṉ nākarīkattilum mēlāṉatākavē teyvattaip pukaḻntu tutittirukkiṟārka ḷeṉṟu aṟiyalām. avarkaḷ pāṭṭuppāṭap paḻakiyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum stirīkaḷ aṉēkar tampurōṭu pāṭi naṭaṉam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum colvataik kavaṉikkaiyil, iṉṉicaiyaittarum tampuru eṉṉum vāttiyam anēkamiruntatākavum atōṭu pāṭap paḻakiyiruntatākavum tāṅkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭṭukkiṇaṅka naṭaṉampaṇṇa stirīkaḷum paḻakiyirunta tākavum aṟikiṟōm. itu iṉṟaikkuc cumār 3400 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ eṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu.
3. teporāḷiṉ pāṭṭu
ataṉpiṉ niyāyātipatikaḷ 5-m atikārattil, lapitōttiṉ maṉaiviyāki teporāḷ eṉṉum tīrkkatarici, 900 iruppu rataṅkaḷōṭu etirttu vanta cicerāviṉmēl taṉakku veṟṟikiṭaitta camayattil, teyvattait tutittup pāṭiṉatākac collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. avaḷ pāṭiya vacaṉaṅkaḷai avvaintām atikārattil muḻutum kāṇalām. appāṭṭu oru yuttattaiyum atil palavāṉkaḷkāṭṭiya parākkiramattaiyum, yuttam naṭanta iṭattaiyum catturukkaḷ aṭainta tōlviyaiyum tāṉ aṭainta veṟṟiyaiyum, nēriṟ kaṇṭatu pōl yāvarum teḷivāy aṟintukoḷḷak kūṭiya vitamāy, mikavum alaṅkāramāy eḻutap paṭṭirukkiṟaJ. oru stirī mikunta karppaṉālaṅkārattuṭaṉ kavi ceytiruppataip pārttāl akkālatti lēyē cākittiyam ceyyavum alaṅkāramāyp pāṭavum kūṭiyavarkaḷāyp pūrvattār iruntirukkiṟārkaḷeṉṟu terikiṟatu. atu iṟṟaikku 3200 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟu aṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
4. yeptāviṉ makaḷ naṭaṉamum tampurōṭu kūṭiyapāṭṭum
ataṉpiṉ niyāyātipatikaḷ 11-m atikāram 34-m vākkiyattil, yeptā eṉṉum niyāyātipati, ammōṉ puttirarai muṟiyaṭittu veṟṟivēntaṉāyt taṉ vīṭṭukkut tirumpivarukaiyil, avaṉ kumāratti tampuru vācittu naṭaṉamceytu avaṉukku etir koṇṭuvantāḷ eṉṟu collap paṭṭirukkiṟaJ. atu iṟṟaikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya 3070 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟu aṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
5. tāvītu rājāviṉ caṅkītamum paktiyum naṭaṉamum avar
kālattil vaḻaṅki vanta vāttiya vakaikaḷum
i cāmuvēl 16-m atikāram 23-m vākkiyattil
“oru pollāta āvi cavulaippiṭikkumpōtu tāvītu cura maṇṭalattaiyeṭuttu, taṉ kaiyiṉāl vācippāṉ; atiṉālē pollāta āvi avaṉai viṭṭu nīṅka, cavul āṟutal aṭaintu costamāvāṉ.”
eṉpataik kavaṉikkaiyil pollāta āvikaḷiṉ cēṣṭai caṅkītattāl caukkiyamākumeṉṟu ataṉ muṉvākkiyaṅkaḷil colli yirukkiṟataiyum appaṭiyē caukkiyamaṭaintataiyum nām kāṇkiṟōm. itil collappaṭum tāvītu eṉpavar, caṅkītattil mikavum tērntavar. cākittiyam ceyvatilum ataic curamaṇṭalam vīṇai mutaliya vāttiyaṅ kaḷil vācippatilum, vitamvitamāṉa vāttiyakkaruvikaḷ uṇṭākkuvatilum kaitērntavar. teyvattiṉiṭattil mikunta paktiyuḷḷavar. teyvacamūkattil pāṭuvatum naṭaṉampaṇṇuvatum maṟṟavarkaḷaiyum appaṭippāṭiyāṭac ceyvatum avarukku mikavum piriyam. 2 cāmuvēl 6-m atikāram 5, 14, 15-m vākkiyaṅkaḷ:
“tāvītu isravēl cantatiyār aṉaivarum tēvatāru marattāl paṇṇappaṭṭa cakalavita kītavāttiyaṅkaḷōṭum curamaṇṭalam tampuru, mēḷam, vīṇai, kaittāḷam ākiya ivaikaḷōṭum karttarukku muṉpāka āṭip pāṭikkoṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ. tāvītu caṇal nūl ēpōttait tarittuk koṇṭu taṉ muḻuppalattōṭum karttarukku muṉpāka naṭaṉam paṇṇiṉāṉ. appaṭiyē tāvītum isravēl cantatiyār aṉaivarum karttaruṭaiya peṭṭiyaik kempīra cattattōṭum ekkāḷa toṉiyōṭum koṇṭu vantārkaḷ.”
mēl vākkiyaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu, caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya iṟṟaikku 3000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉē, cakalavitamāṉa vāttiyaṅkaḷum naṭaṉamum caṅkītattōṭu cērntu tēvacannitiyil upayōkikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu nām kāṇkiṟōm. mēṟkāṭṭiya caṅkītakkāraṉākiya tāvītu, isravēl jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ oru mukkiyamāṉa rājaṉ eṉṟum, caṅkītamē uruvāka avatarittavaṉeṉṟum nām aṟiyavēṇṭum. ivvācaṉ eḻutiya caṅkītaṅkaḷai, nām cattiya vētattil mikavum vistāramākap pārkkalām. avaikaḷil, oru uttama paktaṉatu neñcattiṉ kaṉivum, teyvattil avaṉ vaittirukkum uṟutiyum, avaṉ maṉanērmaiyum mikatteḷivāy viḷaṅkukiṟaJ. avvaḷavu makimai poruntiya pāṭakarkaḷ taṟkālattil kiṭaippatu ariJ. avar eḻutiya caṅkītaṅkaḷ, avaikaḷai vācikkum uṇmaiyāṉa paktarkaḷukku, avaravarkaḷ camayaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavaikaḷāyp putitu putitāṉa karuttukaḷai maṉatil uṇṭākkakkūṭiya vitamāy amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. uṇmaiyāṉa oru paktaṉ, tāṉ putitāka teyvattait tutikkac cila vārttaikaḷ colla ārampippāṉēyāṉāl, av vārttaikaḷ tāvītu aracaṉ caṅkītattil irukkiṟatākak kāṇpāṉ. avvaḷavu mēṉmai poruntiya paktaṉākiya tāvītu aracaṉ tām eḻutiya 92-m caṅkītam 1, 2, 3-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil
“karttarait tutippatum , uṉṉatamāṉavarē, umatu nāmattaik kīrttaṉam paṇṇuvatum, pattu narampu vīṇaiyiṉālum tampuruviṉālum, tiyāṉattōṭu vācikkum curamaṇṭalattiṉālum, kālaiyilē umatu kirupaiyaiyum iravilē umatu cattiyattaiyum aṟivippatum nalamāyirukkum.”
eṉṟum, 150-m caṅkītattil
“allēlūyā, tēvaṉai avaruṭaiya paricutta stalattil tutiyuṅkaḷ; avaruṭaiya vallamai viḷaṅkum ākāya virivaip pārttu avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ. avaruṭaiya vallamaiyuḷḷa kiriyaikaḷukkāka avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ; māṭcimai poruntiya avaruṭaiya makattuvattiṟkāka avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ. ekkāḷa toṉiyōṭē avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ; vīṇaiyōṭum curamaṇṭalattōṭum avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ. tampurōṭum naṭaṉattōṭum avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ; yāḻōṭum tīṅkuḻalōṭum avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ. ōcaiyuḷḷa kaittāḷaṅkaḷōṭum avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ; pērōcaiyuḷḷa kaittāḷaṅkaḷōṭum avarait tutiyuṅkaḷ. cuvācamuḷḷa yāvum karttarait tutippatāka. allēlūyā.”
eṉṟum pāṭiyirukkiṟataik kavaṉippōmāṉāl, avar kālattiluḷḷa caṅkītattiṉ uyarvaiyum vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ mikutiyaiyum avaikaḷ yāvaiyum koṇṭu teyvattaiyē pakticeytā reṉpataiyum nām kāṇalām. ivvuttamaṉē tēva cannitiyil pāṭakarkaḷaip palapākaṅkaḷākap pirittu, irākat talaivarkaḷai ēṟpaṭutti, muṟai muṟaiyāyp pala vāttiyaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu teyvattait tutikkumpaṭi mutal mutal niyamittavar. ivar puttiraṉākiya cālomōṉ aracaṉum mikunta ñāṉamuṭaiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ.
6. cālomōṉ rājaṉ caṅkītamum 288 pāṭakarkaḷai
ālayattil niyamittatum.
i. irājākkaḷ 4. 32, 33-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil.
“avaṉ mūvāyiram nītimoḻikaḷaic coṉṉāṉ; avaṉuṭaiya pāṭṭukaḷ āyirattu ainJ. līpaṉōṉilirukkiṟa kēturu maraṅkaḷ mutaṟkoṇṭu kavaril muḷaikkiṟa īcōppup pūṇṭu varaikkumuḷḷa maram mutaliya tāparaṅkaḷaik kuṟittum mirukaṅkaḷ paṟavaikaḷ ūrum pirāṇikaḷ maccaṅkaḷ ākiya ivaikaḷaik kuṟittum vākkiyaṅkaḷaic coṉṉāṉ.”
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppataik kāṇalām. inta ñāṉamuḷḷa aracaṉum, taṉ takappaṉaip pōlavē tēvālayattil caṅkīta vāttiyattōṭu kaṭavuḷait tutittu ārātittuvantāṉ. tāṉ kaṭṭiṉa makimaiporuntiya tēvālayattil muṟaimuṟaiyāyk kīrttaṉampāṭattiṭṭam ceytiruntāṉ eṉṟu 1nāḷākamam 25, 6, 7-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇpōm. atāvatu,
“ivarkaḷ aṉaivarum rājāvuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷaippiramāṇamāy karttaruṭaiya ālayattil tāḷaṅkaḷ tampurukaḷ curamaṇṭalaṅkaḷākiya kītavāttiyam vācikka, tēvaṉuṭaiya ālayattiṉ ūḻiyamāka avaravar taṅkaḷtaṅkaḷ takappaṉmārākiya ācāp, etuttūṉ, ēmāṉ eṉpavarkaḷ vacattil iruntārkaḷ. karttaraip pāṭum pāṭṭukaḷaik kaṟṟukkoṇṭu, nipuṇarāṉa taṅkaḷ cakōtararōṭuṅkūṭa avarkaḷ ilakkattiṟku irunūṟṟeṇpatteṭṭup pērāyiruntārkaḷ.”
inta irunūṟṟeṇpatteṭṭup pērum ovvoru vakuppukkup paṉṉiraṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu pērāka 24 pākaṅkaḷākkappaṭṭu, anti canti mattiyāṉaṅkaḷil ovvorunāḷum taṅkaḷtaṅkaḷ muṟaippaṭi tēvālayattil kāṉam ceytuvantārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu ērāḷamāṉa pāṭakarkaḷ taṅkaḷtaṅkaḷ muṟaippaṭi ālayattil kāṉam ceytu vantataippōla, ulakattil vēṟu enta rājjiyattilāvatu enta ālayattilāvatu naṭantu vantateṉṟu nām ituvaraiyum kēḷvippaṭṭatillai. itu caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya iṟṟaikku 2930 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉṉeṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟaJ.
7. pāpilōṉil nēpukātnēccār niṟuttiya poṟcilaiyum
ataṉ muṉ vācikkappaṭṭa vāttiyak karuvikaḷum.
pāpilōṉ eṉṉum kaltēyar talai nakaril aracāṭci ceytu vanta nēpukātnēccār eṉṉum rājaṉ, 60 muḻa uyaramum 6 muḻu akalamumāṉa oru poṟ cilaiyaip paṇṇuvittu, pāpilōṉ mākāṇattilirukkiṟa tūrā eṉṉum camapūmiyil niṟutti, accilaiyiṉ piratiṣṭaikkuc cakalarum varumpaṭi aḻaittiruntāṉ eṉṟu tāṉiyēl tīrkkataricaṉam 3-m atikārattiṉ tuvakkattil nām pārkkalām. avvatikārattiṉ 4-m 5-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil.
“kaṭṭiyakkāraṉ uratta cattamāy: cakala jaṉaṅkaḷum, jātikaḷum, pāṣaikkārarumāṉavarkaḷē, uṅkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭukiṟatu eṉṉa veṉṟāl: ekkāḷam, nākacuram,kiṉṉaram, vīṇai, curamaṇṭalam tampuru mutalāṉa cakalavita kīta vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ cattattai nīṅkaḷ kēṭkumpoḻutu, nīṅkaḷ tāḻa viḻuntu, rājāvākiya nēpucātnēccār niṟuttiṉa poṟ cilaiyaip paṇintukoḷḷakkaṭavīrkaḷ.* * * eṉṟāṉ.” 
ivvacaṉattai nām kavaṉikkaiyil, kītavāttiyaṅkaḷ ātikālattil eppaṭi upayōkikkappaṭṭu vantaṉaveṉpatait tiṭṭamāyk kāṇkiṟōm.
8. pāpilōṉ araṇmaṉaiyil kālai mālai mattiyāṉaṅkaḷil
caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷ vācikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu.
tāṉiyēl 6-m atikāram 18-m vākkiyattil
“piṉpu rājā taṉ araṇmaṉaikkuppōy, irā muḻuvatum pōjaṉam paṇṇāmalum, kītavāttiyam mutalāṉavaikaḷait taṉakku muṉpāka varavoṭṭāmalumiruntāṉ; avaṉukku nittiraiyum varāmal pōyiṟṟu.”
eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟataik kāṇpōm. itaṉāl, rājākkaḷ kālaikaḷil tāṅkaḷ tūṅki eḻuntirukkumpoḻutum, cāyantaram vēlai yoḻintu ullācamāyirukkum kālattilum, irāttiri pōjaṉam paṇṇiṉa piṉpum, araṇmaṉaiyil vāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkumpaṭi ceyvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatākat terikiṟatu. taṟkālattilum itaik kāṇalām. ivvaḻakkam, iṉṟu nēṟṟalla, cumār 2450 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa teṉṟu cattiyavētattil collappaṭukiṟa kaṇakkiṉāl teriyavarukiṟatu. ippaṭic caṅkītattai rāja araṇmaṉaikaḷil upayōkittu vanta pāpilōṉ eṉṉum periya nakaraippaṟṟic cila kāriyaṅkaḷai oruvāṟu kavaṉippatu, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ pūrva nilaiyai vicārikkum namakkup pirayōjaṉamāyirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
9. pāpilōṉum niṉivēyum avaikaḷaic cērnta paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum nimirōt eṉpavaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaJ.
pāpilōṉ niṉivē eṉṉum nakaraṅkaḷ, ciṉṉa āciyāvil tikaris natiyiṉ mēl kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ātiyākamam 10-m atikāram 8m vākkiya mutal innakaraṅkaḷaiyum avaikaḷaic cērntaṉavākac collappaṭum maṟṟum cila paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷaiyumpaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷaik kāṇalām. atāvatu, nōvāviṉ kumārarkaḷ, cēm kām yāppēt.
“kāmuṭaiya kumārar, kūṣ misrāyīm pūt kāṉāṉ eṉpavarkaḷ. kūṣ nimrōtaip peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ pūmiyilē parākkiramacāliyāṉāṉ. ivaṉ karttarukku muṉpākap palatta vēṭṭaikkāraṉāyiruntāṉ; ākaiyāl karttarukku muṉpākap palatta vēṭṭaikkāraṉāṉa nimrōtaippōla eṉṉum vaḻakkac col uṇṭāyiṟṟu. cineyār tēcattiluḷḷa pāpēl, ērēk, akkāt, kalṉē eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷ avaṉ āṇṭa rājyattiṟku ātistāṉaṅkaḷ. antat tēcattiliruntu ācūrukkup puṟappaṭṭuppōy niṉivēyaiyum rekepōt paṭṭaṇattaiyum kālākaiyum niṉivēkkum kālākukkum naṭuvāka recēṉaiyum kaṭṭiṉāṉ; itu periya paṭṭaṇam.”
ivvākkiyaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkaiyil, pāpilōṉ, nimrōttuṭaiya rājyattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa stāṉam eṉṟu kāṇkiṟatu. itu tavira, niṉivēp paṭṭaṇattaiyum atilum peritāṉa vēṟu cila paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷaiyum kaṭṭiṉatākak kāṇkiṟōm. ākavē, pāpilōṉ cattiya vētākamattil colliyapaṭi 4261 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ uṇṭāṉaJ. nēpukātnēccār pāpilōṉil aracāṭci ceyta kālamō, kiṟistuvukku 580 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉum iṟṟaikku 2494 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉumāṉaJ. kiṭṭat taṭṭa ki.mu.  540-m varuṣattil cairas (kōrēs) eṉpavaṉāl intappāpilōṉ rājyam aḻikkappaṭṭup pōyiṟṟu.
10. pāpilōṉ eṉṉum nakarattiṉ ciṟappum ataṉ aḻivum.
innakaram ulakattiluḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟilum mikunta pūrvīkamum atika vicālamumāṉatāka viruntaJ. taṟkālattil, ulakattil periyapaṭṭaṇamāka matikkappaṭum laṇṭaṉmā nakarattaippārkkilum pāpilōṉ mūṉṟupaṅku periJ. laṇṭaṉmānakarattait taṟkālattup pāpilōṉ eṉṟaḻaikkakkūṭiyatāka atu avvaḷavu ciṟappuṭaiyatāyiruntaJ. laṇṭaṉ, tems (Thames) āṟṟiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum amaintiruppatu pōlavē, pāpilōṉ, aipirāttu āṟṟiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum ceṅkalkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaipiṭiccuvarkaḷuṭaṉum paṭittuṟaikaḷuṭaṉum irupakkaṅkaḷilum vicālamāṉa pātaikaḷuṭaṉum pātaikaḷiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil uṉṉatamāṉa mūṉṟu nāṉku aṭukku māḷikaikaḷuṭaṉum veku oḻuṅkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭu, mika aḻakāṉa tōṟṟamuṭaiyatāyiruntaJ. itaṉ naṭumattiyil irupākattaiyum iṇaikkakkūṭiya oru periya pālam vekupalamākavum uṉṉatamākavum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaJ. camamāṉa iṭattil caricaturamākap pakkattukkup pakkam 15 mail aḷavuṭaṉ 225 catura mail parappuḷḷatākak kaṭṭappaṭṭaJ. ippaṭṭaṇam mottattil 625 caricaturaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu, avaikaḷ ovvoṉṟum 25, 25 terukkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭiruntaJ. perum terukkaḷ ovvoṉṟum natiyiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum vantucērumpaṭi nērnērāka amaikkappaṭṭu, ovvoṉṟum piramāṇṭamāṉa pittaḷaik katavukaḷuṭaiyatāyiruntaJ. nilaikaḷum katavukaḷum, muṟṟilum pittaḷai. atupōlavē kōṭṭaiyiṉ 120 katavukaḷum muḻutum pittaḷaiyiṉāl ākiyavai. ippaṭṭaṇattaiccuṟṟilum 75-aṭi uyaramum mēlpākattil 32-aṭi akalamumuṭaiyatāka piramāṇṭamāṉa kōṭṭai kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaJ. naṭuvil ōṭiya āṟṟiṉjalam atikamākuṅ kālattil vaṭintu pōkiṟataṟkākak kōṭṭaikkuveḷiyē 40 mailcaricaturamum 1600 caturamail vistīraṇamum 35-aṭi āḻamumuḷḷa oruperiya kuḷamum, paṭṭaṇattaic cuṟṟilum 200 muḻa āḻamum 50 muḻa akalamumāṉa akaḻum veṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa. cāmakkārarkaḷirukkumpaṭi kaṭṭiya kōpurattiṉ naṭumatti vaḻiyākak kōṭṭaiyiṉmēl 4 kutirai pūṭṭiya periya rataṅkaḷ tārāḷamāyc cuṟṟivarumpaṭi pātaiyumiruntaJ. kōṭṭaiyiṉ naṭumattiyil vaṭṭamāṉa iraṇṭu uḷkōṭṭaikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil, rājaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyum ataiccērnta uttiyāṉavaṉaṅkaḷum maṟṟoṉṟil, pīlas eṉṉum cukkirapakavāṉukkukkaṭṭiya ālayamum mikap piramāṇṭāyiruntaṉa. avvālayattil mikavum uyaramāṉa pittaḷaik katavukaḷ anēkamiruntaṉa. ataṉ nilaikaḷum paṭikaḷum ellām pittaḷai. uḷpākattil aṉēka pirākāraṅkaḷ mikavum nērttiyāy amaikkap paṭṭiruntatōṭu atiluḷḷa vikkirakaṅkaḷum avvālayattiṉ taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷum camaiyal pāttiraṅkaḷum muṟṟilum pacumpoṉṉāka viruntaṉa. iravu pakal iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyamuṭiyāmal mayaṅkumpaṭi atika jaṉappaḻakkamuḷḷatākavum viyāpāram peruttatākavum annakaram viḷaṅkiṟṟu. aṅkuḷḷōr uyarnta rattiṉak kampaḷaṅkaḷ melliya cālvaikaḷ aracarkaḷukkuriya pītāmpara mutaliya uyarntavastiraṅkaḷ ceyvatil mikavum kai tērntavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. iṟṟaikku 3365 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ isravēl jaṉaṅkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭa erikōpaṭṭaṇattil (yōcuvā 7-21l)
“koḷḷaiyilē nērttiyāṉa oru pāpilōṉiya cālvaiyaiyum, 200 cēkkal veḷḷiyaiyum 50 cēkkal niṟaiyāṉa oru poṉ pāḷattaiyum nāṉ kaṇṭu avaikaḷai iccittu eṭuttukkoṇṭēṉ.”
eṉṟu ākāṉ colliyirukkiṟataip pārkkumpoḻutu, mikunta pūrva kālattilēyē mika alaṅkāramāṉa cāyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ nāṇayamāṉa vēlaip pāṭuṭaiyatāy maṟṟa tēcattavar virumpum paṭiyāṉa vilaiyuyarnta vastiraṅkaḷ neyyappaṭṭu vantaṉaveṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.
mēlum, paṭṭaṇattiṉ naṭumattiyil vāṉamaḷāviya uyarattuṭaṉum mikunta vistārattuṭaṉum ākāyattil toṅkuvatupōl kāṇappaṭum oru tōṭṭamiruntaJ. ovvoṉṟum 50 aṭi uyaramuṭaiya eṇṇiṟanta ārccu (vaḷaivu) kaḷāl tāṅkappaṭṭa mēlmāṭiyum, ataṉmēl cuṟṟi oru aṅkaṇamiṭṭu ārccukaḷāl tāṅkappaṭṭa iṉṉoru mēlmāṭiyum ippaṭiyē paṭippaṭiyāy oru aṅkaṇam kuṟaittu anēka māṭikaḷuḷḷatāṉa oru piramāṇṭamāṉa kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭuvittāṉ. akkaṭṭattiṉ mēlmāṭiyil cuṟṟi viṭṭirukkum ovvoru maṭṭattilum pōtumāṉa kaipiṭiccuvarkaḷ eḻuppi, atiliruntu taṇṇīr oḻukātapaṭi īyattakaṭṭiṉāl mūṭi, atu niṟaiyap periya maraṅkaḷum vērviṭumpaṭiyāṉa āḻattiṟku nalla maṇkaḷaikkoṭṭi, atil tūratēcattiluḷḷa yāvarum kaṇṭukaḷikkum paṭiyākap palatēca virukṣaṅkaḷaiyum kaṉitarum maraṅkaḷaiyum puṣpacceṭikaḷaiyum koṭikaḷaiyum nāṭṭivaittāṉ. ippaṭiyē oṉṟukkumēl oṉṟāy uyarnta ovvoru taḷattiṉ maṭṭaṅkaḷilum mēlmaṭṭattilum mikavum alaṅkāramāyc ceyyappaṭṭiruntaJ. ivaikaḷai oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāy ēṟippārppataṟku aṉukūlamāṉa paṭikaḷum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivvuṉṉatamum vistāramumāṉa tōṭṭam pārppataṟku maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta oru ciṟu kuṉṟaippōl mika alaṅkāramāyt tōṉṟumām. ākāyattōṭṭattiṉ kīḻppākam mikavum palamāy amaikkappaṭṭiruntaJ. ovvoṉṟu nālaṭikkunālaṭi kaṉamuḷḷatum 16 aṭikkumēl nīḷamuḷḷatumāṉa anēka kal uttiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ piramāṇṭamāṉa kampaṅkaḷāl tāṅkappaṭṭiruntaJ. ovvoru māṭiyilum mikavum vistāramāṉa aṟaikaḷ ērāḷamāyiruntaṉa. avaikaḷiṉ kīḻēyuḷḷa mutal māḷikaiyil, jaṉaṅkaḷ yāvarumkaṇṭu āṉantappaṭakkūṭiya nāṭaka cālaikaḷum, kaṇkākṣicālaikaḷum, puttakacālaikaḷum, viḷaiyāṭum iṭaṅkaḷum, iḷaippāṟum iṭaṅkaḷum, kaṭaikaḷum, iṉṉum palavitamāṉa cantōṣaṅkaḷukkuriya iṭaṅkaḷum amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ataṉmēluḷḷa māṭikaḷil, rājya nirvākattukkuriya kaccērikaḷ oṉṟilum nītistalaṅkaḷ maṟṟoṉṟilum rājāṅkattukku upayōkamum anēkavita āṭamparamumāṉa stalaṅkaḷ vēṟoṉṟilumāka amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. atu, kālā kālaṅkaḷil tōṭṭakkaccēri ceyyavum āṉantam koṇṭāṭavumkūṭiya stalamāyiruntaJ. ivvuṉṉatamāṉa tōṭṭattukkut taṇṇīr tūkkik koṇṭupōvatu kūṭiya kāriyamāyillātatiṉāl āṟṟiliruntu taṇṇīr iṟaippataṟkāka oru piramāṇṭamāṉa yantiram (nupēinē) iruntatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. ippaṭṭaṇam mika vistīraṇamum jaṉa aṭarttiyu muṭaiyatāyiruntatiṉāl, oru pakkattil naṭakkum kāriyam maṟṟoru pakkattukku ilēcāyt teriyātām. ipperiya paṭṭaṇattait taṅkaḷ vācastalamākkikkoḷḷa anēka viyāpārikaḷum pirapukkaḷum aṅku vantucērntārkaḷ. atu, ulakattiluḷḷa ellāp paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷilum mikavum periyatākavum atikat tiraviyamuṭaiyatākavum palamuḷḷatākavumiruntaJ. aṅkuḷḷavarkaḷ ellā vittaikaḷilum kaittoḻilkaḷilum mikac ciṟantavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. aṅkē kolai, kaḷavu, vipacāram mutaliya tīmaikaḷum atikamāyiruntaṉa. pāpilōṉ rājaṉ, pakkatturājyaṅkaḷil kālākālaṅkaḷil paṭaiyeṭuttu attēcattiṉ poruḷkaḷaiyum jaṉaṅ kaḷaiyum taṉ paṭṭaṇattil koṇṭuvantu nirappuvatu vaḻakkam. atupōlavē, pakkattiluḷḷa yāvarum, ippāpilōṉ rājyattaiyum ataṉ makimaiyaiyum tāṅkaḷ aṭaiyavēṇṭumeṉṟu ācaippaṭṭuk koṇṭiruntārkaḷ.
caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya 2000 varuṣaṅkaḷāka nilaittirunta pāpilōṉ rājyattai, pāpilōṉiyar taṅkaḷukkuc ceyta koṭumaikaḷai niṉaittu atai aḻittu akkiṉiyiṉāl koḷutti atai nācamākkavēṇṭumeṉṟu, palar tuṭittukkoṇṭiruntārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyar pīlas eṉṉum teyvattiṟku oru periya uṟcavam naṭattikkoṇṭirunta camayattil acīriyā tēcattu rājaṉākiya kōrēs eṉpavaṉ, iṟṟaikku 2454 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ ataṉmēl paṭaiyeṭuttu aipirāttuāṟṟiṉ taṇṇīraip paṭṭaṇattukkuḷ tiruppip perukaviṭṭum akkiṉiyiṉāl koḷutti nācamākkiyum anēka jaṉaṅkaḷai veṭṭiyum araṇmaṉai vācal varukiṟavaraiyum pāpilōṉ rājaṉ aṟiyātiruntāṉ. ataṉmēl, rājaṉaiyum koṉṟu paṭṭaṇattait taṉ vacappaṭuttikkoṇṭāṉ. nāḷ cellaccella ataṉ makimaiyum kuṟaintu paṭippaṭiyāy muṟṟilum aḻintu, maṇmēṭukaḷāyk kaṟkuviyalkaḷāyt tuṣṭa mirukaṅkaḷum jīvajentukkaḷum ceṭikaḷum niṟainta catuppu nilamāy nāḷatu varaiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. yāttirai ceykiṟavarkaḷil ataṉ pūrva carittirattaiyum ataṉ ciṟappaiyum kēṭṭiruntavarkaḷ atiṉ naṭuvil pōkumpoḻutu piramippiṉālum āccariyattiṉālum oruvitat tikil piṭittavarkaḷā vārkaḷām. āntaikaḷiṉ alaṟalum narikaḷiṉ ūḷaiyum tuṣṭa mirukaṅkaḷiṉ kūccalum mikap payaṅkaramāyirukkumām. yāttirīkarkaḷ pūrvakālattiṉ nākarīkattaik kuṟikkum cila aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷaiyum kallil veṭṭappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷaiyum taṅkaḷ tēcattiṟkuk koṇṭu pōvārkaḷām.
11. niṉivē nakaramum ataṉ ciṟappum ataṉ aḻivum
immakā pāpilōṉ eṉṉum nakarattiṟkum peritāyirukkavēṇṭu meṉṟu tikarīs āṟṟil nimirōttiṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa niṉivē eṉṉum nakaram, mūṉṟunāḷ payaṇam naṭantu cellakkūṭiya vistāramuṭaiyatāka iruntateṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. 19 mail nīḷamum 11 mail akalamum 60 mail cuṟṟaḷavumuḷḷatāy āṟu laṭcattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa  jaṉaṅkaḷuṭaiyatā yiruntaJ. nūṟu aṭi uyaramum, mēl uyarattil mūṉṟu vaṇṭikaḷ pakkam pakkamāka ōṭakkūṭiya vicālamum, oṉṟoṉṟu 200 aṭi uyaramuḷḷa 1500 kōpuraṅkaḷuṭaiya kōṭṭaiyumiruntaJ. kōṭṭaikkuḷḷāka uḷkōṭṭai iraṇṭum akaḻ iraṇṭumāka mikavum araṇippāyk kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaJ. kiṟistuvukkumuṉ 862-m varuṣattil yōṉā piracaṅkikkappōṉa nakaram ituvē. immakānakaramum, kiṟistuvukkumuṉ 753-m varuṣam akāsvēru rājaṉāl, ippōtu kāṇappaṭum cila cuvarkaḷum akaḻkaḷum tavira, maṟṟa yāvum kuppai mēṭākkappaṭṭaJ.
12. akāsvēru rājaṉum rāja stirīyum ceyta viruntukaḷiṉ ciṟappu
akāsvēru rājaṉ intutēcamutal ettiyōppiyā tēcam varaikku muḷḷa 127 nāṭukaḷai aracāṇṭāṉeṉṟu colliyiruppatōṭu, mikavum campiramāṉa viruntu taṉ atikārattiṟkuḷpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷiṉ atipatikaḷukkum pirapukkaḷukkum ūḻiyakkārarkaḷukkum 180 nāḷ ceytāṉeṉṟum, atu muṭintapiṉ taṉ rāja araṇmaṉaiyiluḷḷa ciṅkārattōṭṭa maṇṭapattil taṉ araṇmaṉaikku vantirunta periyōr mutal ciṟiyōr varaikkumuḷḷa yāvarukkum ēḻunāḷ viruntu ceytāṉeṉṟum, appaṭiyē rājastirīyākiya vastiyumstirīkaḷukku oru viruntu ceytāḷeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. estariṉ carittiram 1-m atikāram 6-m vākkiyattil maṇṭapattiṉ ciṟappaip piṉ varumāṟu kāṇalām.
“aṅkē veṇkalattūṇkaḷiṉ mēluḷḷa veḷḷi vaḷaiyaṅkaḷil melliya nūlum civappu nūlumāṉa kayiṟukaḷāl veḷḷaiyum paccaiyum iḷanīlamumākiya toṅkutiraikaḷ vitāṉittiruntatu; civappum nīlamum veḷḷaiyum kaṟuppumāṉa kaṟkaḷ patittirunta taḷavaricaiyiṉmēl poṟ carikaiyum veḷḷiccarikaiyumāṉa mettaikaḷ vaikkappaṭṭiruntaJ. poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa nāṉāvita pāttiraṅkaḷilē pāṉam koṭukkap paṭṭaJ.”
iṉṟaikku 2435 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ naṭanta oru tōṭṭak kaccēriyiṉ (Garden Party) ciṟappum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivvaḷavu ciṟappup poruntiya akāsvēruviṉ rājyamum cūcāṉ paṭṭaṇattuṭaṉ piṉṉoru kālattil aḻintupōyiṟṟu.
ivai yāvaṟṟaiyum nām kavaṉikkaiyil, jalappiraḷayattiṉpiṉ nimirōt mikavum pirapalamāṉa paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatum, pirapalamāṉa cila paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷ avaṉ rājyaṅkaḷil talainakarkaḷā yiruntaṉaveṉpatum, nammuṭaiya kavaṉattiṟku varāmalpōkā. ap pūrvakālattilē, taṟkāla nākarīkamuṭaiyōrum mikavum viyantu pārāṭṭakkūṭiya kāriyaṅkaḷ anēkam iruntatākak kāṇalām. pāpilōṉatu vistīraṇamum, ataṉ amaippiṉ oḻuṅkum, toṅkutōṭṭamum, periya yantiramum (nupēinē), eṇṇiṟanta pittaḷaik katavukaḷum, piramāṇṭamāṉa ēriyum, 60 muḻa uyaramum 6 muḻa akalamumuḷḷa poṟcilaiyum, akkālattilirunta vāttiyakkaruvikaḷum, avaikaḷai upayōkitta muṟaiyum, 70 muḻa uyaramuḷḷa kōṭṭaiyum, ataiccuṟṟiyuḷḷa akaḻiyum, mikunta viyappaittarāmalpōkā. akkālattil mikavum ērāḷamāṉa jaṉaṅkaḷukku 6 mātam allatu 180 nāḷvaraikkum toṭarntu ceyyappaṭṭa rājaviruntum araṇmaṉait tōṭṭaviruntum ammaṇṭapattiṉ ciṟappum stirīkaḷukkeṉṟu rājastirīyāl ceyyappaṭṭa viruntumpōlat taṟkālattil nām kāṇpatu ilēcāṉa kāriyamō? jalappiraḷayattiṟkumuṉ 900, 930, 960 eṉṉum nīṇṭa āyuḷōṭu irunta jaṉaṅkaḷum rākṣatarkaḷum palavāṉkaḷum piraḷayattiṉāl aḻintapiṉ, maṉitar 120, 100, 80, 70 eṉṉum kuṟukiya āyuḷuṭaiyavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. piraḷayattiṟkup piṉṉuḷḷavarkaḷē ivvaḷavu pirapalamāka iruntiruppārkaḷeṉṟāl, cumār āyiram āyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa varuṣam āyuḷpeṟṟa vīrarkaḷum rākṣatarkaḷum kaṭṭiya paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷ evvaḷavu periyavaikaḷum ciṟappuṭaiyavaikaḷumāka iruntirukkavēṇṭum? ulakattil mutalmutal piṟanta oru maṉitaṉ oru paṭṭaṇattaikkaṭṭi ataṟkut taṉ makaṉpeyarai yiṭṭāṉeṉṟu colvatu, caṟṟu yōcaṉaikkiṭamāyirukkiṟatu. jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattai oru yukattiṉ kaṭaiciyāka vaittukkoḷvōmāṉāl, avarkaḷukkup paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum, nīṇṭa āyuḷum, kiṉṉaram nākacuram mutalāṉa vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ viruttiyum, irumpu pittaḷai mutaliya kaittoḻilkaḷiṉ mikutiyum iruntaṉaveṉṟu collattakutiyāyirukkum.
13. jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa ēṉōk,
nōvā eṉṉum uttamarkaḷiṉ tapaR.
mēlum, maṉitarkaḷ jalappiraḷayattiṟkumuṉ eppaṭi atika āyuḷum tiṟamaiyumuṭaiyavar kaḷāyiruntārkaḷō,appaṭiyē teyva paktiyilum mēmpāṭaṭaintiruntārkaḷeṉṟu teḷivāy aṟikiṟōm. ulakai veṟuttut teyvattōṭēyē cañcarittukkoṇṭiruntu uyirōṭē paralōkattukku eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭa ēṉōkkiṉ tapacum, akkālatti liruntavarkaḷukkuḷḷē nītimāṉum uttamaṉumāyt teyvattōṭē cañcarittukkoṇṭiruntu, 300 muḻa nīḷamum (taṟkāla muḻamalla) 50 muḻa akalamum 30 muḻa uyaramumāṉa mūṉṟu aṭukkuḷḷa pēḻaiyeṉṉum kappalaicceytu koṇṭu, maṟṟavarkaḷ pāvavaḻiyai viṭṭu maṉantirumpap piracaṅkamceytu, jalappiraḷayattiṟkup piṉṉuḷḷa jaṉattiraḷukku mutalvaṉāṉa nōvāviṉpaktiyum, viḷaṅkiyatu antap pūrvakālattilēyē.
III. jalappiraḷayattāl aḻikkappaṭṭa tamiḻ nāṭukaḷum kalaikaḷum.
1. jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa carittiraṅkaḷaippaṟṟik kāla niccayam ceyvatu caṟṟup pirayācaiyeṉpaJ.
jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa carittiraṅkaḷai nuṭpamākac collap pōṉāl, taṟkālattavarukkup piriyamāyirāJ. avaikaḷ paḻamaiyāṉa kataikaḷāka eṇṇappaṭumēyoḻiya, uṇmaiyāy naṭantavaiyeṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭuvatu caṟṟukkaṭiṉamākum. uṇmaiyai aṟiyap pirayācaippaṭum carittira ārāyccikkārar, pūmiyil putaintirukkum piramāṇṭamāṉa pirāṇikaḷiṉ elumpukaḷaikkoṇṭum maṉitarkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷaik koṇṭum, kallil veṭṭappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷaikkoṇṭum, pūrva cācaṉaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭum, kaṇṭeṭukkiṟa paṇaṅkaḷiṉ muttiraikaḷaik koṇṭum, putaintirukkiṟa nakaraṅkaḷaikkoṇṭum, iyaṟkaiyaikkoṇṭum mēṟcolliyavaikaḷaip pārkkilum atikakkālaṅkaḷaic colluvārkaḷ; atuvum uṇmaiyē. intiyāvil atipūrvamāka eṇṇappaṭum ettaṉaiyō paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum kōṭṭaikaḷum, oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiyaḻintu, ikkālattil maṇmēṭukaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷiṉ pērumpirastāpamum, akkālattavar parākkiramamum, kalaikaḷiṉ ciṟappum, paktiyiṉ uyarvumpaḻaṅkataiyāyc collappaṭṭuvarukiṉṟaṉavē yaṉṟi, nitaricaṉamāka oṉṟaiyum kāṇōm. itu kālattiṉ iyaṟkai.
intiyāvil, piraḷayattukku muṉṉuḷḷakālattil, parākkiramamuḷḷa rākṣatarkaḷum, tapacil ciṟanta riṣikaḷum, cattiyamum nītiyum tavaṟāmal āṇṭukoṇṭirunta rājākkaḷum, mikunta parākkiramamuḷḷa pōrvīrarum, poruḷiṉ arumaiterinta varttakarkaḷum, kaittoḻililtērnta toḻilāḷarkaḷum, kalaikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum tērnta vitvāṉkaḷum iruntārkaḷeṉṟu nām atikamākak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm. intiyāviṉ pūrva carittiraṅkaḷukkut tiṭṭamāṉa kālavaraiyaṟai illātatiṉālum, avaikaḷil mikuntakaṟpaṉaikaḷ kalantiruppatiṉālum tiṭṭam collak kūṭāta nilaiyil niṉṟu tattaḷikkavēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. āṉālum, pūrvamāṉa cila nūlkaḷil aṅkaṅkē collappaṭum cilakāriyaṅkaḷiṉālum uṇmaiyai oruvāṟu aṟiyavum niccayikkavum kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. ippaṭi niccayikkakkūṭiyavaikaḷaippaṟṟiyum oṉṟukkoṉṟu ovvāmaiyāṉa apippirāyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oruvāṟu camātāṉattukku varavēṇṭiyatā yirukkiṟatēyoḻiya, uḷḷattu uḷḷapaṭi niccayikka iyalātatāyirukkiṟatu. appaṭiyiruntālum, ulakattiṟkup potuvāṉa cila campavaṅkaḷaik koṇṭum nūlkaḷaikkoṇṭum intiyāviṉ pūrvīkattaippaṟṟi nāṉ colvatu, uṇmaiyaivicārikkum vivēkikaḷukku avaikaḷai ñāpakap paṭuttak kūṭiyatāyāvatu irukkumeṉṟu eṇṇic cila vārttaikaḷaic collattuṇintēṉ.
2. tirāviṭa tēcat taracaṉākiya cattiyavirataṉum jalappiraḷayamum.
pākavata vacaṉam, eṭṭāvatu skantam, 24m attiyāyam.
“pōṉa kalpattiṉuṭaiya antattil pirammāviṉuṭaiya nittirai yiṉāl uṇṭāṉa naimittika meṉṟu collappaṭṭa piraḷayam uṇṭāccuJ. antap piraḷayattil pūmi mutalāṉa lōkaṅkaḷellāñ camuttirattil muḻukippōccuJ. appōtu kālavacattiṉāl nittiraiyaṭaintu cayaṉikka vēṇumeṉkiṟa iccaiyaiyuṭaiyavaṉāṉa piramatēvaṉuṭaiya mukattiṉiṉṟum uṇṭākāniṉṟa vētaṅkaḷaic camīpattilirā niṉṟa ayakkirīvācuraṉāṉavaṉ apakarittāṉ. appōtu srī pakavāṉāyum carva niyantāvāyumirukkiṟa srī hariyāṉavar, tāṉavēntiraṉāṉa ayak kirīvaṉuṭaiya cēṣṭaiyai aṟintu macciya rūpattait tarittār. antak kālattil cattiya virataṉeṉṟu peyaraiyuṭaiyavaṉāyum makāṉāyum pakavāṉiṭattil paktiyuṭaiyavaṉāyumirukkiṟa oru rāja riṣiyāṉavaṉ, jalattaiyē pāṉam paṇṇikkoṇṭu tapacu ceytukoṇṭiruntāṉ. yātoru anta cattiya virataṉeṉkiṟa rājāvē intak kalpattil vivacuvāṉuṭaiya piḷḷaiyāyum cirārtta tēvaṉeṉṟu piracittaṉāyum srī hariyiṉāl maṉuvākak kalpikkappaṭṭirukkiṟāṉ. anta rājariṣiyāṉavaṉ, orukkāl kirutamālā natiyil jalatarppaṇañ ceyyumpōtu, avaṉuṭaiya kaiyil irāniṉṟa jalattil oru macciyamāṉatu iruntaJ. tiraviṭa tēcātipatiyāṉa antac cattiya virataṉeṉkiṟa rājariṣiyāṉavaṉ, taṉkaiyilirāniṉṟa anta macciya jalattai natijalattil cērttuviṭṭāṉ. appōtu anta macciyamāṉatu makā tayāḷuvāṉa antac cattiya virataṉaik kuṟittu mikavum tairiyattōṭu oru vārttai colliṟṟu.
“vārāy rājariṣiyē! iṉṟaikku ēḻānāḷ intap pūmi mutalāṉa mūṉṟu lōkamum piraḷaya camuttirattil muḻukappōkiṉṟaJ. appōtu nammāl ēvappaṭṭatāyum vicālamāyum oru ōṭamāṉatu uṉṉai aṭaiyappōkiṉṟaJ. nīyum camastamāṉa ōṣatikaḷaiyum nāṉāvitaṅkaḷāṉa vittukkaḷaiyum anta ōṭattil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu, captariṣikaḷōṭum carva palattōṭuṅ kūṭiṉavaṉāy antap peritāṉa ōṭattil ēṟikkoṇṭu, makā antakāramāṉa camuttirattil makāriṣikaḷuṭaiya kaṭāṭcattiṉāl tīraṉāyc cañcarikkappōkiṟāy. appōtu makā palavāṉāṉa vāyuviṉāl alaikkap paṭṭa anta ōṭattaic camīpattil varā niṉṟa eṉṉuṭaiya kompilē cērttu, makā carppattiṉāl iḻuttuk kaṭṭakkaṭavāy. appōtu nāṉ, riṣikaḷōṭukūṭa irāniṉṟa uṉṉaiyum ōṭattaiyumiḻuttukkoṇṭu, piramāviṉuṭaiya rāttiri kālamāṉatu evvaḷavō, avvaḷavu kālamum camuttirattil cañcarikkappōkiṟēṉ. appōtu nī paṇṇappaṭṭa piraciṉaṅkaḷiṉāl parappirama corūpamāṉa eṉṉuṭaiya makimaiyai yatārttamāka aṟiyappōkiṟāyeṉṟu colliṟṟu. piṉpu camuttiramāṉatu varuṣikkappaṭṭa mēkaṅkaḷiṉāl virttiyaṭaintu, karaipuraṇṭu, pūmiyeṅkum viyāpittaJ. anta rājariṣiyum pakavat pātāravintattait tiyāṉam paṇṇikkoṇṭirukkum pōtu taṉṉaṇṭaiyē varāniṉṟa ōṭattaip pārttu, anta ōṭattil ōṣatikaḷaiyellā mēṟṟik koṇṭu captariṣikaḷōṭuṅ kūṭa tāṉumēṟikkoṇṭāṉ. appōtu anta riṣikaḷellām cantuṣṭāḷāy anta rājariṣiyaippārttu, pakavāṉuṭaiya pātāravintattait tiyāṉam paṇṇuvīrākil nammai intac caṅkaṭattiṉiṉṟum raṭcippāreṉṟu collāniṉṟa anta riṣikaḷuṭaiya vārttaiyaikkēṭṭu, rājariṣiyum pakavāṉaiyē tiyāṉam paṇṇikkoṇṭiruntār.”
௸ pākavata vacaṉam oṉpatāvatu skantam, mutalām attiyāyam.
“tirāviṭatēcātipatiyāṉa cattiyavirataṉeṉkiṟa yātoru rāja riṣi yāṉavaṉ pūrva kalpāntattil makāpuruṣaṉāṉa srī pakavāṉuṭaiya cēvaiyiṉāl uttamamāṉa kiyāṉattaiyaṭaintāṉō, anta rājariṣiyē ippōtu vaivacu tamaṉuvāka irukkiṟāṉeṉṟu eṉṉāl kēṭkappaṭṭaJ. iṭcuvāku mutalāṉa rājākkaḷ anta maṉuviṉuṭaiya puttirāḷeṉṟu ummālē collappaṭṭārkaḷ.”
itiṉiṉṟum aṟiyavēṇṭiyatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tirāviṭa tēcātipatiyāṉa cattiya virataṉ eṉkiṟa rājariṣiyāṉavar mikunta tapacupaṇṇikkoṇṭiruntāreṉpatum, akkālattil pūmi jalap piraḷayattiṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭateṉpatum, cattiyavirataṉ eṉkiṟa tirāviṭa tēcātipatiyum oru vicālamāṉa kappalil camastamāṉa ōṣatikaḷuṭaṉum nāṉāvitamāṉa vittukkaḷuṭaṉum captariṣikaḷuṭaṉum ēṟikkoṇṭu, malaiyiṉ aṭivārattil taṅkiṉāreṉpatum, makāviṣṇu ippaktaṉaik kāppāṟṟuvataṟkākat tamatu mutal avatāramākiya maccāvatārattai eṭuttāreṉpatumē, ivviruttāntam, cattiya vētākamattil jalap piraḷayattaiyum nōvāvaiyum nōvā kāppāṟṟappaṭṭa pēḻaiyaiyum paṟṟic collappaṭṭirukkum varalāṟṟukku muṟṟilum ottirukkiṟaJ. iṭam māttiram āciyātturukkiyiṉ (Asiatic Turkey)  orupākamākavum intiyāviṉ teṉpākamākavum pētappaṭukiṟaJ.
3. tuvārakaikkaracaṉākiya kiruṣṇapakavāṉum jalappiraḷayamum.
pākavatam, patiṉērāvatu skantam, 30-m attiyāyam.
srī pakavāṉ vākkiyam.
“vārāycārati! nī tuvārakāpaṭṭaṇattukkuppōy, pantukkaḷukku yātavarkaḷ oruttarukkoruttar yuttampaṇṇi aṭipaṭṭuppōṉataiyum, yōkamārkkattiṉāl palarāmar paramapatattaiyaṭaintataiyum, nāṉ iccācarīrattai viṭṭuviṭṭataiyuñ collakkaṭavāy. pantukkaḷōṭē kūṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟa nīṅkaḷ tuvārakaiyilirukka vēṇṭām. eṉṉāl viṭappaṭṭirukkiṟa intat tuvārakāpaṭṭaṇattaic camuttiramāṉatu muḻukippōṉatākap paṇṇappōkiṟaJ. ākaiyiṉāl nīṅkaḷ camastamāṉa pērkaḷum avāḷavāḷ pantujaṉaṅkaḷaiyum nammuṭaiya mātā pitākkaḷaiyum aḻaittukkoṇṭu arccuṉaṉālē rakṣikkappaṭṭavarkaḷāy intirappirastattaip pōyaṭaiyuṅkaḷeṉṟu pantukkaḷ poruṭṭuc collakkaṭavāy.”
௸pākavata vacaṉam, ௸ skantam, 31-m attiyāyam.
srī cukar vākkiyam.
“vārāymakāṉupāvaṉē! antakṣaṇattil camuttiramāṉatu srī kiruṣṇaṉuṭaiya kirakattai viṭṭuviṭṭu avarāl viṭappaṭṭirukkiṟa tuvārakāpaṭṭaṇam camastattaiyu muḻukap paṇṇiyaJ.
*   *   *   *   *    *    *   *   *   *
“piṟpāṭu arccuṉaṉāṉavaṉ acēṣarkaḷāyirukkiṟa pālaviruttarkaḷai yaḻaittukkoṇṭu intirappirastattai vantaṭaintu, avviṭattil vacciraṉukkup paṭṭāpiṣēkam paṇṇivaittāṉ.”
௸ pākavata vacaṉam paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu skantam, 2-m attiyāyam.
“eppōtu pakavāṉāṉa srī viṣṇuviṉuṭaiya amcamāyum cattuva corūpamāyumuḷḷa tēkamāṉatu srī vaikuṇṭattaiyaṭaintatō, appōtē kaliyāṉatuṇṭāṉaJ. srī laṭcumipatiyāṉa srī kiruṣṇarāṉavar tammuṭaiya pātapatmaṅkaḷāl sparicittukkoṇṭu pūmiyiliruntavaraiyil antak kaliyāṉatu pūmiyai ākkiramikkaikkuc camarttuḷḷa tallāmaṟpōyiṟṟu.”
mēlē pākavatattiṉiṉṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya cilapākaṅkaḷiṉāl, tuvāparayukattiṉ kaṭaiciyil kiruṣṇapakavāṉiruntatākavum, tuvārakaiyum ataiccērnta iṭaṅkaḷum jalappiraḷayattil aḻintu pōkappōvatai yaṟintu atil tam jaṉaṅkaḷ aḻintu pōkātapaṭi arccuṉaṉukku muṉṉeccarikkaiceytu tamatu piṉṉaṭiyārkaḷai intirappirastam allatu taṟkālattil ṭilli eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭum paṭṭaṇattiṟkuk koṇṭupōyk kuṭiyēṟṟac coṉṉatākavum, appaṭiyē arccuṉaṉ intirappirastat tiṟkuppōy avviṭattil vacciraṉukkup paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytuvaitta tākavum, kiruṣṇapakavāṉuṭaiya tēkam maṟaintavuṭaṉ kaliyukam uṇṭāṉatākavum terikiṟatu. iṟṟaikku 5014 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ kaliyukam ārampittateṉṟu cātāraṇamāy nām yāvarum aṟivōm. tuvārakāpaṭṭaṇamō  (Dwaraka)  intiyāviṉ mēlpākattil sintu (Indus) natiyiṉ mukattuvārattukkuc camīpattiluḷḷa kac (Cutch)  nāṭṭiṉ teṉpuṟamāy kattiyēvāriṉ (Kathiawar) mēlkōṭiyil kaṭal ōramāyirukkiṟatu. kac nāṭum kattiyēvāriṉ perumpākamum jalappiraḷayattāl orukālattil mikavum aḻikkapṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu. intak kattiyēvārukkuc camīpattiluḷḷa kac (Gulf of Cutch) kuṭāvum arapikkaṭalaiccērnta pāracīkak (Persian Gulf) kuṭāvum ātēṉ (Gulf of Aden) kuṭāvum, ceṅkaṭalum (Red Sea) oru kālattil intiyāviṉ teṉpākattiluṇṭāṉa pūmimāṟutalāl taṅkaḷukku etirppaṭṭa tāḻnta pūpākaṅkaḷai aḻittuviṭṭaṉaveṉṟu nām niṉaikka iṭantarukiṟaJ. pūmiyiṉ naṭuppākattil akkiṉiyirukkiṟa paṭiyāl, avvakkiṉi uṣṇam mikunta cilapākattiṉvaḻiyākap pūmikkuccamīpittup pūmiyiṉ mēṟparappai mēṭupaḷḷamākkic camuttirattait taraiyākavum taraiyaic camuttiramākavum māṟṟivaikkiṟa iyaṟkaiyai aṉucarittu, arapiyā (Arabia) intiyā (India) parmā (Burma)  mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷukku etiriluḷḷa pūmiyiṉ perumpākaṅkaḷ jalappiraḷayattāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭirukkakkaṇṭumeṉṟu ūkikka iṭantarukiṟaJ. tuvārakā piratēcam aḻintakālattilēyē nōvāviṉkālattu jalappiraḷayamum uṇṭāyirukkalāmeṉṟu nām oruvāṟu niṉaikkalām. iṟṟaikku 4263 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuṇṭāṉa teṉṟu collappaṭum piraḷayamum 5000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuṇṭāṉateṉṟu collappaṭum piraḷayamum kālavittiyācamāyc collappaṭukiṟatētavira, maṟṟappaṭi orēkālattil naṭantatāka niṉaikka ētuvirukkiṟatu. teṉṉintiyāvukkut teṉpākattilirunta 49 nāṭukaḷum ataṉ mukkiyanakaramākiya teṉmaturaiyum kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉāl pulappaṭukiṟaJ.
4. teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa āviṭaiyārkōyilum jalappiraḷayattukkut tappiya 300 cōḻiyap pirāmaṇarkaḷum.
ikkālattil mikavum nērttiyāṉa ciṟpavēlaikaḷuḷḷatāka eṇṇappaṭum āviṭaiyār kōyilil naṭanta cilakāriyaṅkaḷ intiyāvil cuṟṟuppirayāṇamceyyum pirayāṇikaḷukku ñāpakamirukkalām.

South India Railway Illustrated Guide 1913.
“In the village of Avudyarkoil is an ancient temple which though small, is considered one of the most perfect specimens of its class in Southern India. *   *  *  *  *  *
According to legends the temple occupies the site where, after the deluge, Siva with a colony of three hundred disciples called Solias was established for the purpose of propagating the Brahmin Religion.”
“āviṭaiyārkōyililirukkum pūrvamāṉakōyil ciṟitāyirun tālum teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa kōyilkaḷuḷ mikavum mukkiyamāṉa ciṟpa vēlaippāṭukaḷuḷḷavaikaḷil mutaṉmaiyāṉaJ. ālayam ippōtu irukkumiṭattil, jalappiraḷayattiṟkuppiṉ cōḻiyarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkap paṭukiṟa munnūṟu peyarkaḷai aṅkē koṇṭuvantu, pirāmaṇamatattai viruttipaṇṇumpaṭiyāka, civaṉ kuṭiyēṟṟiṉāreṉṟu purāṇaṅkaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu.”
itaik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu, intiyāviṉ teṉpākam orukālattil kaṭalāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, tuvārakaiyiliruntu intirap pirastattukku uyir tappa ōṭippōṉavarkaḷaippōla itteṉpākatti liruntavarkaḷum taṅkaḷukkuc camīpattiluḷḷa uyarnta iṭaṅkaḷukku uyirtappa ōṭippōyk kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷeṉṟum colla ētuvirukkiṟatu.
5. jalappiraḷayattiṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷum
piraḷayakālattiṉ kaṇakkum.
ippaṭi jalattiṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa piratēcaṅkaḷiṉ cilapākam oruvāṟu kaṭalāl viṭappaṭṭup piṉ jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppukkut takutiyāyiṟṟeṉṟu niṉaikkavēṇṭum. ceṅkaṭalukkup pakkattiluḷḷa āppirikkāviṉ (Africa) cilapākamum arapiyāvum āciyāt turukki pāracīkam (Persia) pelukistāṉ (Baluchistan) ittēcaṅkaḷiṉ cilapākamum teṉṉintiyāviṉ kīḻpākamum kaṭaṟkaraimaṇalkaḷāl nirappappaṭṭiruppatum, maṇal niṟainta iṭaṅkaḷil anēka kirāmaṅkaḷum paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum aḻintukiṭappatum, maṇalukkuk kīḻē pūrvamāyuḷḷa taṉtaraikāṇappaṭuvatum, orukālattil jalappiraḷayam uṇṭāki aṉēkatēcattai aḻittateṉṟu colla iṭantarukiṉṟaṉa.
jalappiraḷayam uṇṭāṉatu yūtarkaḷ (tunaṟaḷa) tāṅkaḷ collum kaṇakkiṉpaṭi ki.mu.  2015-m varuṣam atāvatu iṟṟaikku 4019 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟum, kiḷemeṉṭ alakcāṉṭriṉās (Clement Alexandrinas) eṉpavar ki.mu.  3475-m௵ atāvatu iṟṟaikku 5389 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟum, iyūripiyas (Euribius) eṉpavar ki.mu.  2459-m ´ atāvatu iṟṟaikku 4373 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟum, pīṭ (Bede)eṉpavar ki.mu.  3544-m௵ ´ atāvatu iṟṟaikku 5458 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ  eṉṟum, arṣar (Ursher) eṉpavar ki.mu.  2349-m௵ ´ atāvatu iṟṟaikku 4263 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟum, hēls (Hales) eṉpavar ki.mu.  3153-m ´ atāvatu iṟṟaikku 5067 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟum collukiṟārkaḷ.
ivaikaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu ovvāmaiyāyirukkiṟatu iyalputāṉ; ēṉeṉṟāl, ikkālaṅkaḷai nirṇayam paṇṇukiṟavarkaḷ, piraḷayamuṇṭāṉa akkālattiliruntavarkaḷalla. ataṟkuc cumār 1000, 2000, 3000, 4000, 5000 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṉṉiruntavarkaḷ. jalappiraḷayam uṇṭāṉakālattil allatu ataṟkumuṉ vaḻaṅkiyakālattil uṇṭāṉa kālakkaṇakkukaḷ oṉṟum tiṭṭamāy akappaṭavillai. kālakkaṇakkukaḷ ovvoru rājyattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷiluḷḷa pattiraṅkaḷ mutaliya eḻuttu ātāraṅkaḷ mūlamāy aṟiyappaṭavēṇṭum. appaṭi aṟivataṟkētu villāmal piratāṉa nakaraṅkaḷ aḻintupōṉapaṭiyiṉāl, ataṟkuppuṟampē yirukkum maṟṟavarkaḷcollum kālakkaṇakkōṭu nām tiruptiyāyirukka vēṇṭuvataṉṟi vēṟu eṉṉa ceyyakkūṭum? mikavum pirapalamāyirunta tuvārakaiyum intirappirastam eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭa paṭṭaṇamum veku kālattukku muṉṉēyē aḻintu aṭaiyāḷam teriyāmal pōṉatupōlavē maṟṟum anēka iṭaṅkaḷum pōyirukkavēṇṭum.
6. jalappiraḷayakālattil aḻintupōṉa teṉṉintiyāviṉ perumpākam.
jalappiraḷayattiṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭa pala ciṟupākaṅkaḷaittavira intiyāviṉ teṉpākattiluḷḷa perumpākamum, camuttirattil muḻukip pōyirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu kāṭṭap pala cāṭcikaḷ akappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
itaivācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē, jalappiraḷayattukku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattil, kiṉṉaram nākacuram ceykiṟavarkaḷum avaikaḷai upayōkap paṭuttukiṟavarkaḷum iruntārkaḷeṉṟu cattiyavētattil colliyapaṭi, teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉpākattil kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭa teṉmaturai yilum, āyiramtantikaḷ pūṭṭiya nāratappēriyāḻ mutaliya vīṇaikaḷum nāratīyam akattiyam perunārai peruṅkuruku eṉṉum icainūlkaḷum iruntaṉavākac collappaṭukiṟatiṉāl, teṉṉintiyacaṅkītattiṉ pūrvīkattai aṟivataṟkāka annāṭṭaippaṟṟi iṉṉum cilakāriyaṅkaḷai vicārippatu nallateṉṟutōṉṟukiṟatu. ivviṣayam caṟṟu virivākumākaiyāl, eṉcuyamāka atikam collāmal, teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉ pākattaip paṟṟi maṟṟavarkaḷcollum apippirāyattil cilapākaṅkaḷai eṭuttuttokuttu iṅkē kāṭṭukiṟēṉ.
7. jalappiraḷayamum teṉṉintiyāvum.
mutalmutal intiyāviṉ teṉpākam oru kālattil jalap piraḷayattiṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭateṉṟum, atumikavum vicālamāṉa pūmi pākameṉṟum, atilēyē ātimaṉitarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷeṉṟum, piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
Vol. I of the Manual of the administration, Madras Presidency P. 33 Foot-note (2).
Hypothesis of the geneology and general migrations of the races of man:- “There are a number of circumstances (especially chronological facts), which suggest that the primaeval home of man was a continent now sunk below the surface of the Indian ocean, which extended along the south of Asia, as it is at present (and probably in direct connection at some points with it); towards the east as far as Further India and the Sunda Islands, towards the west as far as Madagascar and the south-eastern shores of Africa. Many facts in animal and vegetable geography render the former existence of such a South Indian continent very probable. To this continent has been given the name of Lemuria, from the primitive mammals of that name which were characteristic of it. By assuming Lemuria to have been man’s primaeval home, the explanation of the geographical distribution of the human species by migraion is much facilitated.”
“maṉitaṉiṉ āti iruppiṭam, intumakā camuttirattil pūrvam iruntu piṟaku muḻakippōṉa ōr kaṇṭam eṉṟum, atu āciyāvil ippōtu teṉpuṟattilirukkiṟa aḷavavvaḷavāyum, kiḻakkil intucīṉatīpa kalpam (Further India.)  caṉṭā (Sunda)) tīvukaḷ varaikkum vicālittiruntatu eṉṟum, mēṟkē akkaṇṭam matakāskār  tīvuvaraikkum āppirikkāviṉ teṉkiḻakkuk karaivaraikkum vicālittiruntatu eṉṟum niṉaikka anēka ētukkaḷ uṇṭu. mirukaṅkaḷ ceṭi koṭi maraṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ amaippaik kavaṉikkaiyil, appērppaṭṭa teṉṉintiya kaṇṭam oṉṟu iruntatu eṉṟu ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu. intakkaṇṭattukku lemūriyā (Lemuria) eṉṟapēr koṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. antakkaṇṭattil vacitta Mammals (atāvatu kuṭṭikaḷukkup pālkoṭuttu vaḷarkkum mirukaṅkaḷ) mūlamāy antappēr uṇṭāṉaJ. inta lemūriyāk kaṇṭamē ātimaṉitar kuṭiyēṟiṉakaṇṭam eṉṟu vaittukkoḷvōmāṉāl, maṉitar iṭamviṭṭu iṭam pērntu kuṭiyēṟiṉa carittirattai veku eḷitil tīrttuviṭalām.”
Vol. I of the Manual of the administration of the Madras Presidency P. III, 110.
“Investigations in relation to race show it to be by no means impossible that Southern India was once the passage-ground by which the ancient progenitors of Northern and Mediterranean races proceeded to the parts of the globe which they now inhabit. Human remains and traces have been found on the East coast of an age which is indeterminate but quite beyond the ordinary calculations of History.”
“jātikaḷ ōriṭamiruntu vēṟiṭam pērnta carittirattaik kavaṉittup pārkkumpōtu, ippōtu vaṭakaṇṭattilum mattiyataraik kaṭalaiyaṭuttum vacikkum jātiyāriṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyāyttāṉ tāṅkaḷ ippōtu irukkum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉaikka iṭamuṇṭu. intiyāviṉ kīḻkaraiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa maṉita elumpukaḷiṉ taṉmaiyaik kavaṉittāl, carittiram toṭaṅkāta kālattuḷḷa maṉitar intiyāviṉ kīḻkaraiyil orukālam vacittiruntārkaḷeṉṟu tīrmāṉikkalām.”

These results are specially to be regretted, as without doubt the population who have for many ages occupied this portion of the peninsula are a great people, influencing the world not much perhaps by moral and intellectual attributes, but to a great extent by superior physical qualities.”
“kalvimāṉkaḷ ituviṣayattil ceyyum muyaṟcikaḷiṉāl, ippōtutāṉ ātikālattu maṉitaraip paṟṟiya anēka caṅkatikaḷ veḷiyā kiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷ muṉṉālēyē veḷikkuvarāmaṟ pōyiṉavē eṉṟu varuttappaṭa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, intiyāviṉ kīḻkaraiyil ātiyil vacitta jaṉaṅkaḷ oru periya jātiyāreṉpataṟkum oḻukka viṣayattilāvatu kalvi viṣayattilāvatu avarkaḷ atikamāyt tērcci aṭaintirāviṭṭālum tēka amaippil maṟṟum aṉēka jātiyaraiviṭa vicēṣittiruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟkum cantēkamillai.”
itil kaṇṭapaṭiyē, intiyāviṉ teṉpākattilē pūrvīkattil irunta jaṉaṅkaḷ mikunta parākkiramamuḷḷavarkaḷāy, tēka amaippil jalap piraḷayattukkumuṉ 900, 1000 varuṣam jīvittirunta maṉitarkaḷiṉ tēka amaippiṟku ottiruntatākak kāṇkiṟōm. avarkaḷ kālattilirunta vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷ nākarīkam vittai mutaliyavaikaḷaip paṟṟi atikam colluvataṟkut takutiyāṉa ātāram itai eḻutiyavarukkuk kiṭaikka villai yeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
Manual of the administration of the Madras Presidency Vol. I Chapter. I Page (4).
“The Sanskrit authors of the Pooranas, writing in the north described Ceylon as much more extensive than it now is, and as stretching especially towards the west and south; thereby not representing, no doubt, the fact in Pooranic times, but embodying nevertheless traditions current among Indian Nations. * *   *   *   *    *
The Sanskrit astronomers place their chief Meridian in Lunka, but it was a line to the west of the present Ceylon. These remarks bear on the theory, that in the most ancient times there was a connection between Southern India and Madagascar. *   *   *   *    *
It also accords with the local tradition recorded by the Buddhists which state that Ceylon was gradually contracted by submergence. *   *   *   *    *
The date assigned to the Noachian deluge of Scripture in 2348 B.C. That of the severance in detail a great submergence on the west, and a tradition exists that the great and little Basseo rocks on the east are left by an eastern submergence.”
“vaṭatēcattil purāṇaṅkaḷai camaskirutattil eḻutiṉa nūlāciri yarkaḷ ilaṅkai tēcam ippoḻutu iruppataiviṭa atika periyatā yiruntatāyum, vicēṣamāka mēṟkēyum teṟkēyum atikamāy vicālittu iruntatāyum collukiṟārkaḷ. ippaṭi avarkaḷ colluvatāṉatu carittiraviṣayamāy niccayattaik kāṇpikkāviṭṭālum akkālattil intiyatēcattavarukkuḷ pāramparaiyāy nicam eṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭa oru caṅkatiyai niccayappaṭuttukiṟatāyirukkiṟatu.*  *  *  *  *  *
camaskiruta vāṉacāstirikaḷ taṅkaḷ yāmiyōttara rēkaiyaik kaṇippatu laṅkaiyai mattiyāy vaittuk koṇṭutāṉ. āṉāl anta rēkai ippōtu irukkiṟa ilaṅkaikkuc caṟṟu mēṟkiliruntaJ. atipūrva kālaṅkaḷil teṉṉintiyāvukkum matakāskar tīvukkum campantam iruntatu eṉṟa koḷkaiyai itu niccayappaṭuttukiṟaJ. *  *  *  *  *  *
ilaṅkaiyāṉatu koñcaṅkoñcamāy kaṭalāl cūḻappaṭṭuc ciṟiya tāyp pōṉatu eṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa putta matastarāl eḻutivaikkappaṭṭa pāram pariyac caṅkatikkum itu ottirukkiṟaJ.*  *  *  *  *   *
nōvāviṉ kālattil uṇṭāṉa jalappiraḷayam cattiyavētattil ki.mu.  2348-m srī ttil uṇṭāṉatākac collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. mēṟkē anēka tēcaṅkaḷ kaṭalukkuḷ muḻukippōṉatāyum kiḻakkil uṇṭāṉa jalap piraḷayattiṉāl kṣayaḷaḷauṭi caṭiumaḷa eṉṟa peritum ciṟitumāṉa kaṉmalaikaḷ pūmi maṭṭattukku mēl terintatāyum pārampariyamāyc collik koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.”
mēṟkaṇṭa vacaṉaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkaiyil, nōvāviṉ kālatti luṇṭāṉa jalappiraḷayampōl oru piraḷayamuṇṭāki intumakā camuttirattil mikavum vicālittirunta ilaṅkait tīviṉ perumpākattai aḻittuviṭṭateṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. kālattiṉ varaiyaṟaiyai mēṟṟicaiyārum kīḻtticaiyārum taṉittaṉiyē kuṟittu vaittirukkiṟārkaḷ. ākaiyiṉāl niccayamākavē oru piraḷaya muṇṭāki yirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu colvatu tappākamāṭṭāJ.
8. pauttariṉ jalappiraḷaya kālakkaṇitam.
I x Manual of the Administration, of the Madras Presidency Vol. I, P. 110, III
“The most ancient facts regarding Southern Indian are remarkable. Geology and Natural history alike make it certain that at a time within the bounds of human knowledge, this country did not form part of Asia. A large southern continent, of which this country once formed part has been assured as necessary to account for the different circumstances. The Sanskrit Pooranic writers, the Ceylon Booddhists, and the local traditions of the West Coast, all indicate in different manners a agreat disturbance of the point of the Peninsula and Ceylon within recent times. The date given by English theologians to the Noachian deluge is 2348 B. C; and that given by the Ceylon Booddhists to the latest submergence in the region of Ceylon is 2387 B. C. The two dates cannot have been arrived at with mutual knowledge. Investigations in relation to race show it to be by no means impossible that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of Northern and Mediterranian races proceeded to the parts of the globe which they now inhabit.”
“teṉṉintiyāvaippaṟṟiya pūrvakālattuc caṅkatikaḷ mikavum kavaṉikkappaṭattakkavai. teṉṉintiyāvāṉatu, jaṉaṅkaḷ nākarīkamaṭaintu carittiraṅkaḷai eḻutiya kālattukkuḷ, āciyākaṇṭattiṉ ōr pākamā yirukkavillaiyeṉṟu, pūkarppa cāstiramum iyaṟkai cāstiramum niccayamāyk kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. intac cāstiraṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭum caṅkatikaḷukku mukāntaram kūṟuvataṟkāka, oru periya teṉkaṇṭam iruntatākavum intat teṉṉintiyā atil ōr pākamāyiruntatākavum vaittukkoḷvatu veku avaciyamāyirukkiṟatu. coṟpa varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ intiya tīpakalpattiṉ teṉ muṉaippākamum ilaṅkaittīvum veku māṟutalkaḷaṭaintaṉaveṉṟu, purāṇaṅkaḷai eḻutiya camaskiruta nūlāciriyarālum, ilaṅkaiyiluḷḷa putta matattārālum, intiya mēṟkukkaraiyiluḷḷa pārampariyaṅkaḷiṉālum teriyavarukiṟatu. nōvāviṉ kālattiluṇṭāṉa jalappiraḷayam ki.mu. 2348-m ௵´ttil uṇṭāṉatāka āṅkilēya vētacāstirikaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ilaṅkaiyil kaṭaiciyil uṇṭāṉa piraḷayam ki.mu. 2387-m ௵ ttil uṇṭāṉatāka ilaṅkaiyiluḷḷa putta matakkurukkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. iraṇṭukālaṅkaḷum, oruvarukkoruvar muṉṉēcollivaittukkoṇṭu eḻutiṉavaiyalla. ātiyil jātikaḷ uṟpattiyāṉataippaṟṟic collum cāstiraṅkaḷai ārāyntu pārkkaiyil eṉṉa terikiṟateṉṟāl, vaṭakkukkaṇṭattilum mattiyataraikkaṭalaiyaṭutta nāṭukaḷilum ippōtu vacikkum jātiyāriṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ tāṅkaḷ nāṭicaceṉṟa tēcaṅkaḷukkup pōkum vaḻiyil teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyāyttāṉ pōṉārkaḷ eṉṟu niṉaikka anēka kāriyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṟatu”.
mēlē kāṭṭiya vacaṉaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkaiyil, putta camayak kurukkaḷāl jalappiraḷayam uṇṭāṉatākac collappaṭum ki. K.  2387-m ´ atāvatu iṟṟaikkuc cumār 4,300 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ ilaṅkaiyiṉ perumpākam kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭirukkiṟateṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. mēlum teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉpakkamāyuḷḷa pūmiyiṉ perumpākam kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭa kālattil aṅkuḷḷōr teṉṉintiyāviṉ vaḻiyākavē maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuk kuṭiyēṟic ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
9. cattiya virataṉatu kappalum malayamalaiyum.
The Tamilian Antiquary by Pandit D. Savariroyan, M.R.A.S.
“Before this diluvial catastrophe, the western Ghats were known as the Northern mountains in relation to the southern Land which was submerged by the ocean. The Satapata Brahmana relates that the ark of Manu rested in the Northern mountains and the Puranas mention that he, the ‘Lord of the Dravida’, underwent austere penance in the Malaya. The Mahabharata and the Puranas give an account of seven other Rishis who accompanied Manu and settled in the new colony. This indicates the advent of other clans led by other Rishis who followed the footsteps of the “Lord of the Dravida”. Thus it appears that the Tamilian race that settled in the Pandu land belonged to these eight Rishis or Prajapatis, one of whom was the famous Rishi Pulastya of the extreme south, from whom were descended Agastya, the Tamil Muni and Ravana, the king of South.
The Satapata Brahmana, in which the story of Manu first occurs, does not mention the name of the Nothern Mountains. However, there is ample evidence in the Puranic accounts to identify the ‘Northern Mountains’ with the Western Ghats, and the particular spot on which the ark rested with Malaya.”
“jalappiraḷayattiṉāl nēriṭṭa inta vipattukkumuṉ kaṭalāl mūṭappaṭṭu aḻinta teṉ tēcattaippaṟṟic collumiṭattil, mēṟkuk kaṇavāy malaikaḷai vaṭakkēyuḷḷa malaikaḷ eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatu. ‘cata pata pirāmaṇam’ eṉṟa nūlil maṉuviṉ pēḻaiyāṉatu vaṭakku malaikaḷil taṅkiṉatāyum, purāṇaṅkaḷil ‘tirāviṭa atipati’ malaiya malaiyil koṭuntavam purintatāyum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. vēṟu ēḻu riṣikaḷ maṉuvuṭaṉ avar putitāyk kuṭiyēṟiṉa iṭaṅkaḷil avaruṭaṉkūṭac ceṉṟiruntatāka makāpāratamum purāṇa nūlkaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itiṉāl eṉṉa veḷiyākiṟateṉṟāl ‘tirāviṭa atipati’ ceytatupōl maṟṟa riṣikaḷāl vaḻi naṭattappaṭṭu maṟṟa jātikaḷum putukkuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉpatutāṉ. ivvitamāy, pāṇṭitēcattil kuṭiyēṟiṉa tamiḻarkaḷ mēṟcoṉṉa eṭṭu riṣikaḷ allatu pirajāpatikaḷaic cērntavarkaḷāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum avarkaḷil oruvar teṉ kōṭiyil vacitta pērpōṉa pulastiya riṣi eṉṟum teḷivākiṟaJ. intap pulastiya riṣi vaḻiyiltāṉ tamiḻ muṉiyākiya akastiya riṣiyum teṉtēcattaracaṉākiya irāvaṇaṉum tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ.
maṉuviṉ carittiram mutalmutal collappaṭṭirukkiṟa nūlākiya catāpata pirāmaṇattil vaṭakku malaikaḷaippaṟṟi oṉṟum collappaṭa villai. eṉṟālum, vaṭakkumalaikaḷ mēṟkukkaṇavāy malaikaḷtāṉ eṉpataṟkum pēḻai taṅkiṉa iṭam ‘malayam’ eṉpataṟkum purāṇaṅkaḷil pōtumāṉa ātāraṅkaḷuṇṭu.”
mēṟkaṇṭa cilavarikaḷil tirāviṭa tēcattaracaṉākiya cattiyavirataṉ jalappiraḷayattukkut tappumpaṭiyāka ēṟicceṉṟa kappal ataṟku vaṭaticai yiluḷḷa malaiyamalaiyil riṣikaḷuṭaṉ vantu taṅkiṉatākak kāṇkiṟatu. malayam eṉpatu potikai malaikkuppeyar; kirutamālā natiyeṉpatu ikkālattil illāta natiyeṉṟu carittirakkārar collukiṟārkaḷ. maturaik karukiluḷḷa vaikainatik karaiyil tirāviṭatēcattācaṉākiya cattiyavirataṉ tavacu ceytu koṇṭiruntāṉ eṉpatu poruntāJ. mēlum, malayamalai eṉpatu uttara maturaikkut teṟkiluḷḷa malaiyākumē yoḻiya vaṭakkiluḷḷa malaiyākamāṭṭāJ. āṉāl mēṟṟicaimalaikaḷil uṟpattiyāṉa kumariyāṟu teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vaṭavellaiyāy niṉṟu aḻintu pōṉatu pōla, kirutamālānatiyum potiyamalaiyiluṟpattiyākit teṉ maturaikkarukil ōṭikkoṇṭiruntu cattiyavirataṉ tavañceyvataṟ kēṟṟa iṭamāyiruntu piṉpu aḻintu pōyirukkavēṇṭum. appaṭiyāṉāl, teṉṉintiyāviṟku vekutūram teṟkēyirunta teṉmaturai kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭa kālattil, aṅkirunta cattiyavirataṉeṉṉum tirāviṭa tēcattaracaṉ kappalēṟit teṉmaturaikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa malayamalaikku vantu cērntāṉeṉṟu colvatē poruntum.
10. kaṉṉiyā kumariyiṉ purātaṉa kōyilkaḷ.
Essays by S.V. Thomas, M.A. Page 84.
“The earliest mention of Cape Comorin by European  writers is contemporaneous with the time of Alexander the Great. We have reason to believe that so early as the time of the Periplus the Cape was already famous as a sacred shrine of the Hindus. The temple was dedicated to Siva’s wife, called Kumari, or young woman as emblematic of eternal youth and beauty. It is impossible to decide the precise site of the ancient temple seen by the Greek sailors. There are at present three temples at Cape Comorin. One is in complete ruins, and the sea is every day swallowing it up. This is probably the ancient of the three. Another is situated on an elevated rock the foot of which is constantly washed by the waves. The temple is antique in appearence and structure, but is dedicated evidently to Siva’s son, not wife, as it contains a rude stone image of Ganesa. The third is the temple which pilgrims now go to visit. This has a comparatively modernlook about it. *   *   *   *   *   *
It is very probable that the most ancient temple has been buried under the sea, and that when the encroachments of the sea were arrested by the solid rocks that now stand out to check its progress, another temple was built and dedicated to the same goddess. This view is supported by the report that is current among the fishermen living in the neighbourhood. They say that in 1883, when the green sun perplexed the scientific world, the sea receded all of a sudden some furlongs that  there were then scen the ruins of old buildings with brazen gates and other accompaniments, that some of the fishermen ventured out to drage them ashore, but that before they could succeed the sea came back to assert her ancient dominion, and the enterprise was given over. If this be true, we have strong reasons for supposing that the temple which astonised the Greeks is imbedded in sea, and that the present temple afterwards rose to the honour of the tutelary goddess of the Cape.”
“airōppiya carittirakkārar kaṉṉiyākumariyaippaṟṟi mutal mutal colluṅkālam makā alekcāntaruṭaiya kālattukkuc cariyāy irukkiṟaJ. peripḷas eṉṉum nūl eḻutappaṭṭa kālattilēyē anta muṉaiyāṉatu intukkaḷukku oru puṇṇiya stalamāka iruntatu eṉṟu niṉaikka ētuvuṇṭu. atiluḷḷa kōyil civaṉuṭaiya maṉaiviyākiya pārvatipērāl piratiṣṭai paṇṇappaṭṭiruntaJ. (“kumari” eṉṟāl aḻakiya iḷampeṇ, nittiya iḷamaiyaiyum aḻakaiyum uṭaiya pārvati.) kirēkka mālumikaḷāl pārkkappaṭṭa kōyil enta stalattil kaṭṭappaṭṭirunta teṉṟu ippōtu collamuṭiyāJ. ippōtu kaṉṉiyākumari muṉaiyil mūṉṟu kōyilkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷil ellāvaṟṟilum paḻamai yāṉatu eṉṟu lēcil oppukkoḷḷakkūṭiyatu aḻintupōyk kaṭalil muḻukikkoṇṭēvarukiṟatu. maṟṟoṉṟu uyaramāṉa kaṉmalaiyiṉmēl kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu; atiṉ aṭivārattil kaṭal alaikaḷ eppōtum aṭittukkoṇṭēyirukkiṉṟaṉa. intak kōyil veḷittōṟṟattilum kaṭṭaṭa amaippilum atikap paḻamaiyāṉatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu: atu pārvati pērāl piratiṣṭaipaṇṇappaṭāmal, civaṉuṭaiya kumāraṉpērāl piratiṣṭai yāyirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, kallāl karaṭumuraṭāyc ceyta kaṇēcaruṭaiya vikkirakam atil irukkiṟaJ. mūṉṟāvatu kōyiltāṉ ippōtu cuvāmi taricaṉattukku varupavarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum kōyil. ataippārttāl taṟkālam kaṭṭappaṭṭatupōl terikiṟatu.
āṉāl nām nitāṉamāyc collakkūṭiyatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, avaikaḷil ellāvaṟṟilum atika pūrvamāṉa kōyil kaṭalukkuḷ muḻukippōyirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟum, nāḷāvaṭṭattil kaṭal antak kaṉmalaiyait tākkamuṭiyāteṉṟu kaṇṭapōtu anta malaiyiṉmēl pārvatikku vēṟoru kōyil  kaṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟum niṉaikkalām. antap pakkattil vacikkum cemapaṭavarukkuḷḷum itē tāṟpariyamtāṉ taṟkālamvarai iruppatākatterikiṟatu. avarkaḷ colluvatu eṉṉa veṉṟāl, 1883-m varuṣattil cūriyaṉ paccainiṟamāki, cāstirikaḷellām atiṉimittam mayaṅkiṉapōtu, avviṭattil kaṭalāṉatu cila parlāṅkukaḷ tūram uḷḷukkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭatām. appōtu tiṭīreṉṟu pala iṭinta kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kaṭalil tōṉṟiṉatākavum avaikaḷellām veṇkalak katavukaḷuḷḷavaikaḷāyiruntamaiyāl cila mīṉparavar kaṇṭu avaikaḷaik karaikku iḻuttuvara muyaṉṟatākavum, āṉāl avarkaḷ tirumpivarumuṉ kaṭal avaikaḷai maṟupaṭiyum mūṭippōṭṭatākavum, piṟaku avarkaḷ avaikaḷaik karaikkuk koṇṭuvara muṭiyāmal viṭṭuviṭṭatākavum collikkoḷḷukiṟārkaḷ. itu niccayamāy irukkumāṉāl, kirēkkarkaḷ kaṇṭu piramippaṭainta kōyil kaṭalil muḻukippōyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum ippōtu pārvatikkuk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kōyil piṉṉāl uṇṭāṉateṉṟum niṉaikka iṭamuṇṭu.” 
mēṟkaṇṭa vacaṉaṅkaḷil, teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṟkuk kaṭaiciyil irukkum kaṉṉiyākumariyil nittiyakumariyāyviḷaṅkum pārvati tēviyāriṉ kōyil kaṭalukkuḷ amiḻntiruppatākavum ataṟkup piṟkālattil kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷum kaṭalāl mōtappaṭṭu niṟkiṟatākavum kaṇēcar ālayaṅkaḷum aṅkēyirukkiṟatākavum kāṇkiṟōm. kaṭalāl mūṭappaṭṭa ikkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ 1883-m varuṣattil oru camayam kaṭalāl cila tūram viṭappaṭṭakālattil, akkaṭṭaṅkaḷilirunta veṇkalak katavukaḷai mīṉ piṭippōr kaṇṭu karaicērkkappirayattaṉap paṭukaiyil, kaṭal maṟupaṭiyum poṅki varuvataippārttu appirayattaṉattai viṭṭuviṭṭārkaḷeṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. akkālattiluḷḷōr taṅkaḷ aracamāḷikaiyilum kōṭṭai vācalilum kōyil vācalkaḷilum poṉṉālum veṇkalattālum katavunilaikaḷ ceytu upayōkittu vantārkaḷeṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. mēlum kaṉṉiyākumariyiluḷḷa pūrva ālayaṅkaḷ kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum terikiṟatu.
11. lemūriyā irunta iṭam.
Castes and Tribes of Southern India Vol. I, Intro. P. 20, 21 by E. Thurston.
“In the chapter devoted to ‘Migration and distribution of organisms, ‘Haeckel, in referring to the continual changing of the distribution of land and water on the surface of the earth, says; “The Indian ocean formed a continent, which extended fromthe Sunda Islands along the Southern Coast of Asia to the east coast of Africa. This large continent of former times Sclater has called Lemuria, from the monkey-like animals which inhabited it, and it is at the same time of great importance from being the probable cradle of the human race. The important proof which Wallace has furnished by the help of chronological facts, that the presentMalayan Archipelago consists in reality of two completely different divisions, is particularly interesting. The western division, the Indo-Malayan Archipelago, comprising the large islands of Borneo, Java, and Sumatra, was formerly connected by Malacca with the Asiatic continent, and probably also with the Lemurian continent just mentioned. The eastern division, on the other hand, the Austro-Malayan Archipelago, comprising Celees, the Moluccas, New Guinea, Solomon’s Islands, etc., was formerly directly connected with Australia.”
“intu makācamuttirattil oru kaṇṭam iruntatu; akkaṇṭam cantāttīvukaḷiliruntu āciyākkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkarai neṭukacceṉṟu āppirikkākkaṇṭattiṉ kīḻkaraivaraikkum eṭṭiyiruntaJ. muṟkālaṅkaḷi lirunta intapperiya kaṇṭattukku, atilvacitta kuraṅku pōṉṟa pirāṇikaḷi liruntu skleyṟṟar eṉpavar lemūriyā eṉṟu pērkoṭuttirukkiṟār. maṉitajātiyiṉ āti piṟappiṭamāyiruntirukkalām eṉṟu uttēcikkap paṭuvatiṉāl ikkaṇṭam atika mukkiyamāṉaJ. taṟkālattilirukkum malāyāttīpakaṇam uṇmaiyil oṉṟukkoṉṟu muṟṟum vittiyācamāṉa iraṇṭu pirivukaḷaṭaṅkiyatu eṉpatākak kālavaricaiyāy ēṟpaṭṭa caṅkatikaḷaikkoṇṭu uvāles eṉpavar tiruṣṭāntappaṭuttiyatu vicēṣitta iṉimaiyuḷḷaJ. mēṟpirivākiya intumalāyāt tīpakaṇam, pōrṇiyō jāvā cumāttirā eṉṉum periya tīvukaḷaṭaṅkiyaJ. atu muṟkālattil āciyākkaṇṭattōṭum anēkamāy iṅkē colla yirukkappaṭṭa lemūriyākkaṇṭattōṭum malākkāviṉāl iṇaikkap paṭṭiruntaJ. itaṟkumāṟākak kīḻppirivākiya āstirōmalāyāt tīpakaṇam, celipis molukkās niyukiṉiyā cālomōṉ tīvukaḷ mutalāṉavaikaḷ aṭaṅkiyatāyp paḻaiyakālattil āstirēliyāvōṭu iṇaikkap paṭṭiruntatu eṉṟu hekkēl eṉpavar kūṟukiṟār.”
mēṟkaṇṭa vacaṉaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkaiyil, intumakācamuttirattil teṉṉintiyāvukkut teṉpakkattilirunta intiyakaṇṭamāṉatu āti maṉuṣarkaḷ uṟpattiyāvataṟku iṭamāyiruntateṉṟum, ellājātikaḷum cukamāyt taṅkiyirunta toṭṭilāyiruntateṉṟum, atu aḻintapiṉ atiliruntu taṅkaḷukku etirppaṭṭa karaikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu aṅku viruttiyāṉārkaḷeṉṟum terikiṟatu. intu makācamuttirattaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa tīvukaḷilum tēcaṅkaḷilumirukkum jaṉaṅkaḷaiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum ceṭikoṭi maraṅkaḷaiyum iyaṟkaiyiṉ amaipputterinta cāstirikaḷ lemūriyā eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum intiyakaṇṭattiliruntē jaṉaṅkaḷ paraviyirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṟārkaḷ. mēlum, jalappiraḷayamāṉatu uṇṭāṉakālattil ellārum aḻintupōka, teyva aṉukkirakattāl eṅkaḷ tēcattil iṉṉapaktaṉ kappal mūlamāy tappippiḻaittā reṉṟum, avaruṭaiya cantatitāṉ nāṅkaḷeṉṟum, ovvoru tēcattārum collik koḷḷukiṟārkaḷeṉpatai itaṉ piṉvarum varikaḷil kāṇalām:
12. jalappiraḷayattaik kuṟitta pala apippirāyaṅkaḷ.
The New Popular Encyclopedia Vol. IV, Page 325.
“Many other nations mention, in the mythological part of their history, inundations which in their essential particulars, agree with the scriptural account of Noah’s preservation. Hence many persons have inferred the universality of this innundation. To this, it has been replied that each nation localizes the chief events and actors as connected with itself, necessitating an Ararat, an ark and a Noah in each instance. Fohi in the Chinese mythology, Sottivrata or Satyavrata in the Indian, Xisuthrus in the Chaldaean, Ogyges and Deucalion in the Greek, have each been recognized by many as the Noah of the sacred scriptures under a different name. Even the American Indians have a tradition of a similar deluge, and a renewal of the human race from the family of one individual. All these individuals are said by their respective nations to have been saved, and to have become a second father of mankind.”
“vēṟu anēka jātiyārum taṅkaḷ purāṇak kataikaḷil piraḷayaṅkaḷaik kuṟittuc collukiṟārkaḷ. avvaralāṟukaḷellām, mukkiyamāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷil, nōvā kāppāṟṟappaṭṭatākac cattiyavētattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷa varalāṟṟukku ottirukkiṟaJ. ākaiyāl, anēkar intappiraḷayam ulakameṅkum uṇṭāyirukkalāmeṉṟu yūkikkiṟārkaḷ. ataṟku ovvoru tēcattārum piraḷayattiṉ mukkiya caṅkatikaḷait taṅkaḷ tēcattilēyē naṭantatākavum atil campantappaṭṭavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tēcattārkaḷēyeṉpa tākavum kūṟi, ārarātpōṉṟa oru malaiyaiyum, pēḻaiyaippōṉṟa oru kappalaiyum, nōvāvaippōṉṟa oru muṉiyaiyum ēṟpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷē yeṉṟu āṣēpaṉai kūṟappaṭukiṟaJ. cīṉaruṭaiya purāṇak kataikaḷil collappaṭum ‘pōhi’yum intupurāṇaṅkaḷil collappaṭum ‘cattiya virataṉu’m, kaltēyaruṭaiya kataikaḷiṟ collappaṭum ‘cicutturacu’m, kirēkkaruṭaiya kaṭṭukkataikaḷiṟ collappaṭum ‘ojījecu’m ‘ṭiyukeliyaṉu’m, cattiya vētattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa nōvātāṉeṉṟum nōvāvukkē, vevvēṟu tēcattil vevvēṟu peyar collappaṭukiṟa teṉṟum palar apippirāyappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. amerikka intiyarkaḷukkuḷ ḷēyum, ippaṭip paṭṭa oru piraḷayamuṇṭāṉatākavum piṉpu orēyoru maṉitaṉuṭaiya kuṭumpattiliruntu tirumpavum maṉitar viruttiyāṉatākavum pārampariyamāka oru katai collappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. ippaṭiyē avaravarkaḷ tēcattilum oru maṉitaṉ tappip pātukākkappaṭṭāṉeṉṟum, avaṉ maṉita jātikaḷukku iraṇṭāvatu tantaiyāṉāṉ atāvatu, iraṇṭāntaram maṉitar avaṉiliruntu uṟpattiyākip perukiṉārkaḷeṉṟum collappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.”
