iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷukku uraiyeḻutiya nakkīraṉār jalap piraḷayattiṟku aṉēka varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyē mutaṟ caṅkamirunta teṉṟum talaiccaṅkattil akastiyarum tiripurameritta viricaṭaik kaṭavuḷum kuṉṟameṟinta murukavēḷum ciṟanta caṅkap pulavarkaḷāyiruntu naṭattiṉārkaḷeṉṟum collukiṟār. itaikkoṇṭu paramacivaṉ tāmē teṉmaturaiyil aracāṇṭu vantāreṉṟum, avar kumāraṉākiya cuppiramaṇiyarum teṉmaturaiyiliruntavareṉṟum, iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻaiyum virutti ceytu caṅkat talaimai peṟṟiruntārka ḷeṉṟum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. kaṭaiccaṅka kālattil civaperumāṉ tarmiyeṉpavarukku akkālattil uttara maturaiyil āṇṭu koṇṭirunta vaṅkicacūṭāmaṇi yeṉṉum caṇpakapāṇṭiyaṉ maṉatil niṉaitta karuttiṟkiṇaṅkiya kaviyoṉṟu eḻutikkoṭuttu 1,000 poṉ paricu peṟumpaṭi ceytār eṉpataiyum,
	“tamiḻ aṟiyum perumāṉē
	taṉṉaiccērntār naṉṉitiyē
	tiruvālavāy eṉṉum maturaikkaracaṉē” - eṉṟum
	“pāṭṭukkurukun tamiḻc cokkanātar” - eṉṟum
	“teyvat tamiḻkūṭal” - eṉṟum
	“caṅkam poṅkum paṇmuttamiḻkkōrpayaṉē cavuntirapāṇṭiyaṉē”
eṉṟum pala vitvāṉkaḷ pukaḻvataiyum nām kavaṉikkumpoḻutu tamiḻppāṣai paramacivaṉ nēcitta pāṣaiyeṉṟu teḷivāyt terikiṟatu. uttara maturaiyil taṭātakaip pirāṭṭiyāraik kaliyāṇañ ceytu koṇṭa cuntira pāṇṭiyaṉum avar kumāraṉākiya ukkira pāṇṭiyaṉum maturaiyil tamiḻ aracarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum ivarkaḷē civaperumāṉ murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ avatārameṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum pāṇṭiyarājarkaḷ yāvarukkum tamiḻnāṭaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ, tamiḻar kōmāṉ, kūṭaṟ kōmāṉ, kaṭampavaṉanātaṉ, maturēcaṉ (maturaikkaracaṉ), pāṇṭi vaḷa nāṭaṉ eṉap pala peyaruṇmaiyum, paramacivaṉaiyum cuppiramaṇiyaraiyum avarkaḷ paramparaiyil utitta pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷaiyum maṟṟavarkaḷ pukaḻntiruppa taiyum nām kāṇalām. civaperumāṉum avar paramparaiyilutitta pāṇṭiya varacarkaḷum teṉmaturai, koṟkai, uttaramaturai eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil āṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻaiyum vaḷarttu vantārkaḷeṉṟum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. ivarkaḷil mikunta piracitti peṟṟa aracarkaḷukkuk kōyil kaṭṭi avarkaḷait teyvaṅkaḷāka vaṇaṅki vantārkaḷ. ipparamparaiyiluḷḷōr yāvarum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil mutal caṅkattukku mutaṉmaiyāṉa paramacivaṉaiyum avar kumāraṉākiya murukak kaṭavuḷaiyum ārātittu vantārkaḷ. teṉṉintiyā vilum, cumattirā, jāvā eṉṉum tīvukaḷilum, cuppiramaṇiyar ālayaṅkaḷum vikṉēsvarar ālayaṅkaḷum iruppatai nām iṉṟum kāṇalām. intiyāviṉ vaṭapākattilum maṟṟum iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvālayaṅkaḷirukkak kāṇpatu ariJ.
uttaramaturaiyil cumār 130 talaimuṟaiyāka āṇṭu koṇṭu vanta rājākkaḷ civa perumāṉ eṉṉum cōmacuntirapāṇṭiyaṉiṉ vamcattārka ḷeṉpatai, tiruvālavāy eṉṉum uttaramaturaiyiṉ stalapurāṇattil allatu tiruviḷaiyāṭal purāṇattil teḷivākak kāṇalām. atil viṟaku viṟṟapaṭalam allatu yāḻ vācitta paṭalattilum icaivātuveṉṟa paṭalattilum kāl māṟiyāṭiya paṭalattilum caṅkītattaip paṟṟiyum paratattaip paṟṟiyum tāḷattaip paṟṟiyum curukkamāyc colliyirukkiṟatu.
25. muccaṅkaṅkaḷiṉ kālattaikkoṇṭu intiyāviṉ pūrva carittirattai oruvāṟu tiṭṭamāy aṟiyalām eṉpatu.
itai vācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē! intiyāviṉ kālakkaṇakku taṟkālattaippōla oṉṟu, iraṇṭu eṉṟa ilakkattōṭu collappaṭāmal iṉṉa maṉuviṉ kālameṉṟum iṉṉa yukameṉṟum ittaṉaiyāvatu parivirutti yeṉṟum (120 varuṣam) iṉṉa peyaruṭaiya varuṣameṉṟum (pava, yuva, eṉpatu pōl) iṉṉa rāci mātam eṉṟum (mēṭaravi) colluvatu vaḻakkam. itōṭu maṉitaṉiṉ aṟpa āyuḷai niṉaittu vekutāṉiya varuṣam kaṭakaraviyil piṟantāṉeṉṟum colluvatu vaḻakkam. itil enta vekutāṉiya varuṣameṉṟum ettaṉaiyāvatu pariviruttiyeṉṟum enta āyiliyat tiruviḻāveṉṟum, entayukameṉṟum collāmaiyiṉāl niccayam collakkūṭāmal mayaṅkuvatu iyalpē. mēlum piturpakti atikamāyuḷḷa intiya rājavamcaṅkaḷil, muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ peyarē aṭikkaṭi vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu vaḻakkam. ippaṭi varuvatiṉāl intiyāvil kāla niccayam colluvatu caṟṟu varuttamākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
mēlum pūrvattil purāṇaṅkaḷaiyum itikācaṅkaḷaiyum eḻutiyavarkaḷ kāla niccayattiṉ arumaiyai aṟiyāmal avarkaḷ maṉampōṉapaṭi kāla nirṇayam colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ. 360 nāḷ koṇṭatu maṉuṭa varuṭameṉṟum, appaṭi 360 koṇṭatu tēva varuṭameṉṟum vaittukkoṇṭu, ovvoru rājākkaḷum tēva varuṭattil 60,000, 40,000, 10,000 varuṭaṅkaḷ āṇṭārkaḷeṉṟum aṉēka riṣikaḷ aṉēka ūḻi kālaṅ kaḷākavum anēka caturyukaṅkaḷākavu miruntārkaḷeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. itiṉāl intiya carittirattiṉ kāla nirṇayam cariyākac colvatu kūṭiya kāriyamākat tōṉṟavillai. entac carittiramum ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṟpaṉaikaḷ kalantatākavēyirukkiṟatu. itōṭu nīti nūlkaḷ eḻutiya vitva cirōmaṇikaḷai kavaṉippōmāṉāl kāppu mutal kaṭai varaiyilum taṅkaḷ ūraiyāvatu peyaraiyāvatu piṟanta varuṣattaiyāvatu colvatu vaḻakkamāyillai. tirukkuṟaḷ, ātti cūṭi, koṉṟaivēntaṉ, mūturai, naṉṉeṟi, nalvaḻi mutaliyavaikaḷaik kavaṉittāl ivvuṇmai teriyum. taṟkālattaippōl pustakam pativu ceytu vaikkiṟa (Register) vaḻakkamu millai. appaṭiyē eḻuti vaittālum mutal ēṭṭilāvatu kaṭaici ēṭṭilāvatu eḻuti vaippārkaḷ. vaitta koñca kālattiṟkellām mutal ēṭṭaiyum kaṭaici ēṭṭaiyum mutal mutal cellukaḷ arippatu vaḻakkam. ippaṭi arittu viṭukiṟatum kālanteriyāmal pōvatum iyaṟkai. cila nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa nūlkaḷai anēka caturyukaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷateṉṟu collik koḷvatu perumaiyeṉṟu oru tappāṉa apippirāyam vaḷarntuviṭṭatu. ippaṭik kālanteriyāmal pōvatōṭukūṭa cilar tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya nūlukkup periyavarkaḷ peyarai pōṭṭuviṭṭāl ati pūrvamāka eṇṇappaṭumeṉṟu niṉaittup peyar māṟṟi vaikkiṟārkaḷ. vicēṣamāka nītinūlkaḷilum uṇmai viḷakkum cāstiraṅkaḷilum uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyē aṉupōkattukku varakkūṭiya cāstiraṅkaḷilum ippaṭic ceykiṟatillai. āṉāl kaṟpaṉaikaḷum kaṭṭukkataikaḷum niṟainta nūlkaḷilē ivaikaḷai nām kāṇalām. tiruṣṭāntamāka stala purāṇaṅkaḷ putitu putitāy uṇṭākkappaṭuvatai aṟivōm. akkatai colvataṟku viyāca pakavāṉum ataikkēṭṭu jaṉamējayarukkuc colla naṭuvil oru cūtapurāṇikarum, atait tūṇṭiviṭa oru nāratarum eḻutuvataṟku maṟṟoru viyācarum, eṅkiruntāvatu vantuviṭuvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ colvatai viyācar eppaṭiyāvatu eḻuti viṭuvār. ataṉ piṉ ivarkaḷ eḻutiṉatellām vētamum purāṇamumākiviṭum. cūtapurāṇikar eṉpatum nāratar eṉpatum viyācar eṉpatum peyar vēṟṟumaiyāl vēṟākat tōṉṟiṉum purāṇam eḻutiṉavar oruvar eṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. eḻutiṉavar iṉṉāreṉṟu namakkut terintiruntum ivar ippaṭi anēka stalaṅkaḷukkup purāṇaṅkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṟār eṉṟu nām aṟintum cūtapurāṇikar, nāratar, viyācar pōṉṟa makāṉkaḷ peyarkaḷum, cila tēvarkaḷ peyarkaḷum varuvatiṉāl māttiram avarāl eḻutappaṭṭa yāvum uṇmaiyeṉṟu nampi viṭukiṟōm. paricīlaṉai paṇṇāta ikkuruṭṭu nampikkai intiyāvukkē ciṟantatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ākaiyiṉāl purāṇaṅkaḷ colvataik koṇṭu māttiram nām intiyāviṉ kālattai niccayippatu kūṭāta kāriyam.
āyiram āyiramāṉa varuṣaṅkaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa caṅkatikaḷai pūrva carittiraṅkaḷaik koṇṭē oruvāṟu collalāmēyoḻiya niccayamāyc colluvatu kūṭiya kāriyamāyillai. muccaṅkamum avaikaḷirunta varuṣamum evvita āṣēpaṇaiyumiṉṟi nām oppuk koḷḷak kūṭiya carittiramuṭaiyatākat terikiṟatu. avvaruṣattaik koṇṭu maṟṟa naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaiyum nām ottup pārttāl intiya pūrvīka carittiraṅkaḷiṉ uṇmai viḷaṅkum. teṉ maturaiyum ataic cērnta 49 nāṭukaḷum taṇṇīrukkuḷ mūḻkip pōṉatāl vēṟu evvitamāṉa tuppun tulaṅka villai. appaṭiyillātiruntāl namakku vēṇṭiya anēka ātāraṅkaḷ aṅkē kiṭaittirukkum.
aḻintupōṉa lemūriyāvaip paṟṟiyum ataṉ cuṟṟiṭaṅkaḷilirukkum iyaṟkai amaippiṉ cila kuṟippukaḷaip paṟṟiyum tamiḻppāṣaiyiṉ pūrvattaip paṟṟiyum tamiḻ moḻikaḷ pala pāṣaiyil kalantiruppataip paṟṟiyum cāttira ārāyccikkārarkaḷ colpavai uṇmaiyeṉṟu pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉumcila nāḷ cellac cella itilum mēlāṉa cila ātāraṅkaḷ kiṭaikkalām. atipūrvamāyuḷḷa lemūriyāvil tamiḻp pāṣaiyē pēcappaṭṭu vantateṉṟum tamiḻppāṣaiyē maṟṟellā pāṣaikaḷukkum muntiṉateṉṟum cāstirikaḷ kūṟum apippirāyam cariyeṉṟu nāmum oppuk koḷḷukiṟōm. intumakā camuttirattiṉ teṉpakkattiluḷḷa kerkiyūlaṉ tīvuvarai vicālittirunta pūmiyē oru kālattil atāvatu mutal ūḻikkumuṉ tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṭiyirunta nāṭākavum 4,400 varuṣaṅkaḷāka mutaṟ caṅkamirunta pūmiyākavum tōṉṟukiṟatu. itōṭu innāṭṭiṉ teṉpāricattil pala malait toṭarkaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭa kumariyeṉṟa malaic cikaramiruntatākavum atiliruntu paḵṟuḷiyāṟu vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉāl veṭṭap paṭṭatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. mēṟṟicai malaikaḷiliruntu arapik kaṭalil viḻum periya āṟṟaik kiḻakkut ticaiyil varumpaṭi vaikai vāykkāl veṭṭi viṭṭatu pōla ituvumirukkalāmeṉṟu colla vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. itu oru periya jīvanatiyāyirukka vēṇṭum. innatiyum kumari āṟum imayamalaipōlotta kumarimalaikaḷiṉ toṭarcci yiṉiṉṟu uṟpattiyāyirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. immalaiyum natiyum aḻinta kālattil annāṭṭiṟku vaṭa ellaiyā yirunta kumariyāṟum aḻintu pōyiṟṟu. itaṉuṭaiya pūrvīkattaiyum aṟintu koḷvataṟku atika ētukkaḷillātiruntālum aḻintupōṉa mutaṟ caṅkattiṉ kaṭaici kālattiliruntu tappik kapāṭapurattukku vanta muṭattirumāṟaṉ eṉṟa kaṭaici pāṇṭiyaṉum akastiyar tolkāppiyar pōṉṟa mutaṟ caṅkap pulavarkaḷum oruvāṟu mutaṟ caṅkattiṉ perumaiyai iraṇṭāñ caṅkattukkup pulappaṭutti yiruppārkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. maṟṟappaṭi 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷ naṭanta tamiḻ caṅkattai 4,449 vitva cirōmaṇikaḷ talaimai vakittārkaḷeṉṟa tiṭṭamāṉa kaṇakku ēṟpaṭṭirukkāJ. itaṟku cumār 3,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku piṉṉuṇṭāṉa maṟṟoru aḻiviṉāl muntiṉa ūḻiyiṉ aḻivu, nuṭpamāṉa cila carittiraṅkaḷallāta aṭṭavaṇaiyāka māttiram collappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. appaṭiyiruntālum vitvaparamparaiyāka itu vaḻaṅki vantatiṉāl itu uṇmait tavaṟutal eṉṟu niṉaikka iṭamillai. mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam aḻintu iṟṟaikku cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷ āyiṟṟeṉpatu yāvarum aṟinta viṣayam. appaṭiyiruntālum oru tamiḻc caṅkamiruntatākac collappaṭavillai. kiṟistu piṟanta iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil mūṉṟām caṅkam kalaintupōṉapiṉ cumār 1,000 varuṣaṅkaḷāka atāvatu ki.pi. 1,200 varuṣaṅkaḷ varaikkum pāṇṭiya rājakulattavarkaḷālē aracākṣi ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. ataṉpiṉ cumār 50 varuṣaṅkaḷāka āti cultāṉ eṉṉum makamatiya aracaṉum ataṉpiṉ vicuvanāta nāyakkar, tirumalai nāyakkar pōṉṟa cila nāyakka vamcattavarkaḷum ataṉpiṉ murāri rāyar, appāji rāyar, cantā cāyapu, kāṉ cāyapu pōṉṟa anēkam cillarai rājākkaḷum oru varuṣam iraṇṭu varuṣam pōṉṟa aṟpa kālaṅkaḷ oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka kalakañ ceytu koṇṭu aracākṣi ceytu vantārkaḷ. 1,801-m varuṣam maturaiyum ataiccērnta nāṭukaḷum māṭcimai taṅkiya āṅkilēyar āḷukaiyil varukiṟa varaiyum nāṭṭil camātāṉamillātiruntaJ. taṉ uyiraiyum kuṭumpattār uyirkaḷaiyum taṉatu poruḷkaḷaiyum kāppāṟṟa caktiyillātu tavittuk koṇṭirukkuṅ kālattil oru tamiḻccaṅkam vaittu naṭattuvatu kūṭiya kāriyamākumā? atōṭu aṉṉiya pāṣaikaḷil eḻutiya pala nūlkaḷum vārttaikaḷum iṉiya tīñcoṟ ṟamiḻp pāṣaiyaik kalaṅkaṭittaJ.
26. māṭcimai taṅkiya vikṭōriyā cakkaravarttiṉi avarkaḷ kālattil maturai nālāvatu tamiḻccaṅkam ārampamāyiṟṟu.
evvuyiraiyum taṉṉuyirpōl niṉaittu aracāṭci ceytu varum āṅkilēya turaittaṉattār uḷ nāṭṭuk kalakaṅkaḷaiyaṭakki veḷināṭṭār paṭaiyeṭuppait taṭuttu tuṣṭa rājarkaḷai nīkki kuṭikaḷ cukamuṟṟu vāḻvataṟku aṉukūlamāṉa tanti, tapāl, perum pātaikaḷ, iruppup pātaikaḷ, nīr pācaṉa vacatikaḷ, vaittiyacālaikaḷ, kalviccālaikaḷ, kalācālaikaḷ, niyāyastalaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷai ēṟpaṭutti intiyāvaic camātāṉa nilaikkuk koṇṭu vantārkaḷ. tāyillāk kuḻantaikaḷukku uṟṟa tāy pōla karuṇai curantu taṉ makkaḷilum arumaiyāy niṉaikkum āṅkilēya araciyāy viḷaṅkiṉa vikṭōriyā makārāṇi avarkaḷ intiyāviṉ rāja cutantaramum piṉ ēka cakkarātipatyamum peṟṟut taruma nīti tavaṟāmal 1,837 mutal 1,901 varaiyum cumar 64 varuṣaṅkaḷāka mikunta camātāṉattōṭu aracāṭci ceytu vantārkaḷ. ivvuttama aracāṭciyiṉ payaṉāka intiya tēcattiṉ uḷnāṭṭuk kalakaṅkaḷ ōyntaṉa. nīti tavaṟiya pala koṭuñceyalkaḷ oḻintaṉa. jaṉaṅkaḷ oṉṟu cēravum cakōtara urimai pārāṭṭavum pala caṅkaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. tamiḻp pāṣaiyiṉ pūrvīkattaiyum ataṉ coṟcuvai poruṭcuvaiyu maṟinta palar paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷait tēṭavum atai ārāycci ceyyavum acciṭavum oru capai kūṭṭavum ārampittārkaḷ. āṅkilēya aracāṭciyil intiyāvil mutal cakkirātipatyam peṟṟa vikṭōriyā cakkaravarttiṉi avarkaḷiṉ aracāṭciyiṉ 64vatu varuṣamākiya 1901vatu varuṣattil nālāvatu caṅkam ārampamāyiṟṟu. kaliyukam 3,200 il mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam kalaintu pōka ataṟkuc cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṉ kaliyukam 5,002 il maturai nālāvatu tamiḻc caṅkaṅkūṭi 13 varuṣaṅkaḷāka naṭantu varukiṟataiyum atil 251kku mēṟpaṭṭa pala vitvacirōmaṇikaḷ cērntiruppataiyum tamiḻaip paṟṟiya ārāycci naṭantu varuvataiyum nām kāṇkaiyil, itaṟku muṉ naṭanta mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷaiyum ataṉ perumaiyaiyum uṇmait tavaṟutaleṉṟu yār maṟukkakkūṭum? pālavaṉattam jamīṉtār srīmāṉ pāṇṭitturai tēvar avarkaḷiṉ perumuyaṟciyāl innālām caṅkam ārampittaJ. innālāñ caṅkattiṟku pālavaṉattam tamiḻc caṅka meṉṟāvatu pāṇṭitturai tamiḻccaṅkameṉṟāvatu peyar vaikkalāmē. appaṭiyillāmal maturait tamiḻc caṅkam eṉṟu vaittatāṉatu, itaṉ muṉ maturaiyil naṭanta tamiḻc caṅkattiṉ peyaraiyē mutaṟkāraṇamākak koṇṭu vaikkappaṭṭateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. itaṉ muṉ pirapalamāṉa mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiruntatiṉāl itaṟku nālām caṅkameṉṟa peyar vantatu.
innālām caṅkattaip paṟṟiya cila mukkiya kuṟippukaḷ atāvatu, ārampitta kālam, kūṭṭappaṭṭa caṅkattil talaimai vakittavarkaḷ, caṅkattai ātarittu varum rājarkaḷum kaṉavāṉkaḷum, caṅkattai naṭatti varuvatil pirayācai eṭuttuk koḷḷuṅ kaṉavāṉkaḷum, caṅkattil aṅkattiṉarāka irukkum vitvāṉkaḷum ākiya ivarkaḷaip paṟṟic curukkamāyp pārppatu muntiya mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai aṟiyātavarkaḷukku avaṟṟiṉ uṇmaiyai veḷippaṭuttumeṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
nālāvatu maturait tamiḻc caṅkam 1901m௵ ´ mē௴ 24²
srīmāṉ po. pāṇṭitturait tēvar avarkaḷāl ārampikkappaṭṭaJ.
ituvarai 13 varuṣamāka caṅkam kūṭiṟṟu
	1. 	srīmāṉ pāṇṭit turait tēvar avarkaḷ
	2. 		”	S. cāminātavijayatēvar avarkaḷ
	3. 		”	P.S.. cuppiramaṇiya aiyar avarkaḷ
	4. 	rājarājēsvara mutturāmaliṅka cētupati avarkaḷ
	5. 	srīmāṉ rāvpakatūr M. āṟumukam piḷḷai avarkaḷ

itu tavira aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭa kaṉavāṉkaḷ caṅkattiṉ viruttikkāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷaik kavaṉittu caṅkattai naṭatti varukiṟārkaḷ.

27. muccaṅkaṅkaḷirunta kālattaip paṟṟi oruvāṟu collakkūṭiyavai.
kiṟistu piṟantu iṟṟaikku 1,914 varuṣaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. kaliyukam piṟantu 5,014 varuṣaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. kiṟistu piṟantu 100 varuṣaṅkaḷ varaiyilum mūṉṟāvatu caṅkamiruntatākavum ataṉpiṉ illāmal pōṉatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟāvatu caṅkamō 1,850 varuṣaṅkaḷ uttara maturaiyil nilaittiruntaJ. ākaiyiṉāl iṟṟaikku 3,664 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam ārampamāyiṟṟeṉṟu teḷivāyt terikiṟatu. kaliyukam tuvārakai yaḻintapiṉ ārampamāyiṟṟeṉṟum caṟṟēṟak kuṟaiya akkālattil intu camuttirattiṉ ōramāyuḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷil piraḷayam uṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟum itaṉmuṉ nām pārttirukkiṟōm. ap piraḷayattil iraṇṭām caṅkamirunta kapāṭapuram aḻipaṭṭateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. appaṭiyāṉāl kaliyukattil 1,350 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṟakē mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam ārampittatākac colla vēṇṭum. inta 1,350 varuṣaṅkaḷil koṟkaiyilirunta pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ eṅkē yiruntārka ḷeṉṟu kēṭka nēriṭum. avarkaḷ ippōtirukkum uttara maturaikku aintu āṟu mailukku kīḻpākattirunta maṇavūril vantu taṅki aṅkē araṇmaṉai kaṭṭi atiliruntārkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. avviṭattiliruntu ippō tirukkum maturaiyil oru ciṟiya ālayamum paṭṭaṇamum kaṭṭi maturaiyil aracāṭci ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ muṉṉirunta iṭam paḻa maturaiyeṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. iṭintupōṉa araṇmaṉaikaḷum mēṭukaḷum iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaikaiyāṟṟaṅ karaiyilirunta maṇavūril arccuṉaṉ vantu taṅkiyatākavum appōtirunta cittarāṅkataṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya makaḷai avaṉ kaliyāṇañ ceytatākavum purāṇaṅkaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu. itaikkoṇṭu mūlap piraḷayattiṉāl kapāṭapuram aḻikkappaṭṭapiṉ paḻa maturaiyil allatu maṇavūril pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ neṭunāḷ iruntārkaḷeṉṟum maṇavūri liruntē maturaiyaik kaṭṭi ataṉpiṉ caṅkam stāpittārkaḷeṉṟum nām niṉaikkalām. kaliyuka ārampattiluṇṭāṉa piraḷayam pōka ataṉmuṉ oru piraḷayamum uṇṭāṉatāka nām aṟikiṟōm. atu cattiya virataṉuṭaiya kālattil naṭantatāka eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. kaliyukam ārampikkum muṉ 3,700 varuṣaṅkaḷ kapāṭa purattil pāṇṭiya rājāṅkamiruntatākavum caṅkamirunta tākavum collappaṭuvataik koṇṭu iṟṟaikku 8,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ oru piraḷayamuṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. antap piraḷayattukku muṉ teṉmaturaiyil āṇṭukoṇṭirunta nilantarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ kālattu irunta tolkāppiyar iṭaiccaṅkatti liruntāreṉṟu colvataik kavaṉikkaiyil tolkāppiyam 8,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷateṉṟum akkālattil teṉ maturai kaṭalāl aḻikkap paṭṭateṉṟum tōṉṟukiṟatu. teṉ maturaiyil 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷāka caṅkamiruntatākac colluvatai nām kavaṉikkaiyil iṟṟaikku caṟṟēṟak kuṟaiya 13,000 varuṣattukku muṉ mutaṟcaṅkam ēṟpaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. itaṉ talaimuṟaik kaṇakkukaḷaip pārttālum ātaritta rājarkaḷil kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavarkaḷ kaṇakkaip pārttālum akkālattut tamiḻ naṭaiyaip pārttālum iccaṅka kālam uṇmaiyāṉateṉṟu teḷivākat teriyum. kaṟpaṉaikaḷ pala kalanta purāṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu itaṉ niccayam collak kūṭātu. purāṇaṅkaḷ anēkamāy iṟṟaikku cumār 1,000, 1,500 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉtāṉ eḻutappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu aṟivāḷikaḷ palar niṉaikkiṟārkaḷ. maturait tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟpurāṇattil 5,000, 8,000, 10,000, 15,000 varuṣaṅkaḷāka ovvoru pāṇṭiyaṉum āṇṭāṉeṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl muccaṅkaṅkaḷaip paṟṟic collumiṭattu 50, 63, 38 varuṣaṅkaḷ ovvoru talaimuṟaikku varukiṟatu. itilum cantati yaṟṟu iṭaiyiṟ kaḻinta kālamum cērntirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. ivaikaḷaik koṇṭu tamiḻppāṣai mikap pūrvamāṉa teṉṟum iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻil caṅkītamum 13,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyēyiruntateṉṟum colvatu uṇmaiyeṉṟu teḷivākak kāṇkiṟōm.
itai vācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē! iṟṟaikku 13,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ oru tamiḻc caṅkamiruntateṉpataiyum muṭimaṉṉarkaḷum muṉivarkaḷum ariya kalai vallōrum atil cērntu iyal icai nāṭakameṉṉum muttamiḻaiyum virutti ceytārkaḷ eṉpataiyum oruvēḷai āccariyamākavum maṟṟoruvēḷai cantēkamākavum eṇṇuvōm. pūrvakālattiluḷḷōr kālaṅkaḷait tiṭṭamāyk kuṟikkakkūṭiya tāṉa kuṟippukaḷ vaikkātataṉāl, nām ippōtu aṟintu koḷvatu pirayācaiyāyt tōṉṟukiṟatu. kālam tiṭṭamāka aṟiya vēṇṭumāṉāl puṟa carittiraṅkaḷaiyum ciṟcila ētukkaḷaiyum koṇṭu māttiram aṟiyakkūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. avvaḻakkamē ippōtu eḻutappaṭum pustakaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ippōtu nām vaḻaṅki varum anēka puttakaṅkaḷil itaik kāṇalām. ipputtakaṅkaḷ rijisṭar ceyyappaṭā tiruntāl kālavaraiyaṟai colla muṭiyāmal niṟkum. itōṭu pūrva nūlkaḷil tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya varuṣattai māttiram vekutāṉiya, virōtikirutu eṉṟu pōṭṭuviṭukiṟatu vaḻakkam. itiṉāl muntiṉa pustakam pintiṉatākavum pintiṉa pustakam muntiṉatākavum eṇṇa nēriṭukiṟaJ. iruntālum taṟkālattil nākarīkamaṟṟatu eṉṟu nām niṉaikkum pūrva eḻuttukkaḷil, tiruṣṭāntamāka, e, ma eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷi peṟum vaḻakkattait tolkāppiyar colvatai nām kavaṉikkaiyil tamiḻppāṣai mikap pūrvamuḷḷateṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. mēlum pūmiyiṉ iyaṟkai vaḷarcciyaik kuṟittuk kūṟum ekkēl (Haeckel) eṉṉum tattuva cāstiriyiṉ apippirāyam iṉṉateṉṟu terintirukkiṟōm. avar ippūmiyil jīvarācikaḷ 5 kōṭi varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉamēyē iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭumattiyil kāṇappaṭum ippi, caṅku, nattai pōṉṟa ciṟu pirāṇikaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷiṉ vaṭivattaik koṇṭu tīrmāṉikkiṟār. ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṟkaḷai nām aṭikkaṭi pārttirukkiṟōm. ivar jīvappirāṇikaḷiliruntu maṉuṣaṉ eppaṭi uṇṭāṉāṉ eṉkiṟa uṇmaiyaip pirāṇikaḷiṉ elumpukaḷaik koṇṭum ataṉpiṉ karuppācayattiṉ amaippaik koṇṭum karuviṉ mutal tōṟṟattaik koṇṭum paṟkaḷaik koṇṭum, talaiyiṉ amaippaik koṇṭum mika virivākac colkiṟār. avaikaḷil maṉuṣaṉ entak kālattil uṇṭāṉāṉ eṉpataip paṟṟiyum ātiyil avaṉ vacitta iṭattaip paṟṟiyum aṅkiruntavarkaḷ pēci vanta pāṣaiyaip paṟṟiyum avar collum cila apippirāyaṅkaḷaip pārppatu pirayōcaṉamā yirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
28. kuraṅkiṉattiliruntu maṉuṣaṉ uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉpatu.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 352.
“Hence in the genealogy of the mammals we must derive man immediately from the Catarrhive group, and locate the origin of the human race in the Old World. Only the early root form from which both descended was common to them.”
“mammēliya vakuppaic cērnta mirukaṅkaḷiṉ vamca aṭṭavaṇaiyai nām pārkkumpōtu maṉitaṉ kāṟṟarkiv (Catarrhive) eṉṉappaṭṭa kuraṅkukaḷiṉ iṉattiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉavaṉ eṉṟum, maṉita jātiyē paḻaiya ulakattiliruntu jeṉittatu eṉṟum colla vēṇṭiyatā yirukkiṟatu. āṉāl iru jātikkum potuvāyiruntatu etuveṉṟāl, avai iraṇṭum ātiyil uṟpattiyāvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyirunta ātijeṉaṉa rūpamē.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷaik kavaṉikkumpōtu maṉitaṉ kuraṅkiṉattiliruntē uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉṟum maṉuṣarkaḷukkum kuraṅkukaḷukkum uṟpattikkuk kāraṇamāyirunta āti mūlastāṉam orē vitamāyirukkiṟateṉṟum collukiṟār. appaṭi uṟpattiyāṉa ātimaṉuṭa jāti paḻaiya ulakattil jeṉittatākac collukiṟār. ippaḻaiya ulakam intu camuttirattil iruntu muḻukippōṉatāka nām niṉaikkum lemūriyāk kaṇṭamē. ikkaṇṭattiṉ iyaṟkai amaippiluḷḷa cila ceṭi koṭikaḷaiyum maraṅkaḷaiyuṅ koṇṭu ataṟku cuṟṟilumuḷḷa cila tīvukaḷaiyum tēcaṅkaḷaiyum ellai yāka avar colluvatai nām itaṉpiṉ pārppōm.
kuraṅkukaḷiṉ iṉattil kiḻakku tēcaṅkaḷil vacikkum kuraṅkukaḷi liruntē maṉitaṉ uṟpattiyākiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 259.
“The other group, to which man belongs, are the Eopitheca or eastern apes; they are found in Asia and Africa, and were formerly in Europe. All the eastern apes agree with man of the features that are chiefly used in Zoological Classification to distinguish between the two Simian groups, especially in the dentition.”
“maṉita jātiyaic cērttuc collum maṟṟoru vakuppu kuraṅkukaḷukku īyōpittakkā allatu kīḻ tēcattuk kuraṅkukaḷ eṉṟu pēr. avaikaḷ taṟkālam āciyāvilum āppirikkāvilum akappaṭum. muṟ kālattil avaikaḷ airōppāvil iruntaṉa. cimiyeṉ vakuppaic cērnta iraṇṭuvita kuraṅkukaḷiṉ vittiyācattaiyum miruka cāstirattil pirittu eḻutumpōtu kiḻakkutēcak kuraṅkukaḷukkum maṉitarukkum anēka viṣayaṅkaḷil, mukkiyamāy pal viṣayattil, atika poruttam irukkiṟatākat terikiṟatu.”
kiḻakkut tēcam eṉṟu āciyāvaiyum āppirikkāvaiyum kuṟikkiṟār. āciyāviṉ karaiyōraṅ kaḷilum āppirikkāviṉ karaiyōraṅkaḷilum vacikkum kuraṅkukaḷil oru vakuppē maṉita jāti uṇṭākuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruntateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 261.
“The apes of the Old World, or all the living or fossil apes of Asia, Africa and Europe, have the same dentition as man.”
“paḻaiya ulakattiluḷḷa kuraṅkukaḷum, āciyā, āppirikkā, airōppāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷil taṟkālam irukkum kuraṅkukaḷum, oru kālam iruntu aḻintu pōṉatāka kaṟkaḷil patitta rūpam mūlamāy nām aṟiyum kuraṅku jātikaḷum maṉitaṉaip pōlotta paṟkaḷaiyuṭaittāyiruntaṉa.”
itil paḻaiya ulakattiliruntu aḻintupōṉa kuraṅkukaḷ kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭuvil kāṇappaṭuvataiyum av uruvaṅkaḷ maṉitaṉaip pōlotta paṟkaḷaiyuṭaitāyiruntaṉa eṉpataiyum teḷivākac collukiṟār. avaikaḷil aḻintu pōkāmal tappi mīntavai ippōtu ententa iṭaṅkaḷilirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇpōm.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 257.
“These living survivors are scattered far over the southern part of the Old World. Most of the species live in Madagascar, some in Sunda Islands, others on the mainland of Asia and Africa. Some of these were almost as big as men, such as the diluvial lemurogonon Megaladapis of Madagascar.”
“avaikaḷil tappi mīntu uyirōṭiruppavaikaḷ paḻaiya ulakattiṉ teṉpākaṅkaḷil anēka iṭaṅkaḷil citaṟuṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. anta jātiyil miccamāṉavaikaḷ matakāskār tīvilum, cila cantā tīvukaḷilum, maṟṟavai āciya āppirikkā kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ taraipākaṅkaḷilum vacikkiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷil cila maṉitaraippōl periyavaikaḷ, mukkiyamāy matakāskār tīviluḷḷa ṭailūviyal lemurākaṉāṉa mekalaṭēppis (diluvial lemurogonon Megaladapis) eṉṉum jātikkuraṅkukaḷ.”
avaikaḷil aḻivukkut tappi uyirōṭiruppavai paḻaiya ulakam allatu lemūriyāviṉ pakkattu iṭamāṉa matakāskār, cantā mutaliya tīvukaḷilum āciyā āppirikkā kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ taraipākaṅkaḷilum vacikkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatākat teḷivāyt terikiṟatu.
29. maṉita uṟpattiyiṉ kālattaip paṟṟi.
maṉitarkaḷ uṟpattiyāṉa kālattaiyum avarkaḷ pēcum cakti peṟṟa kālattaiyum oruvāṟu iṉṉateṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 203.
“The first appearance of man, or, to be more precise, the development of man from some closely related group of Apes, probably falls in either the miocene or the pliocene period, the middle or the last section of the Tertiary period. Others believe that man properly so-called man endowed with speech was not evolved from the non-speaking ape-man (Pithecan thropus) until the following, the anthropozoic age. In this fifth and last section of the organic history of the earth we have the full development and dispersion of the various races of men, and so it is called the Anthropozoic as well as the Quaternary period. In the imperfect condition of palcontological and ethnographical science we cannot as yet give a confident answer to the question whether the evolution of the human race from some extinct ape or lemur took place at the beginning of this or towards the middle or the end of the Tertiary period. However this much is certain the development of civilisation falls in the anthropozoic age, and this is merely an insignificant fraction of the vast period of the whole history of life. When we remember this, it seems ridiculous to restrict the word “history” to the civilised period. If we devide into a hundred equal parts the whole period of history of life, from the spontaneous generation of the first monera to the present day, and if we then represent the relative duration of the five chief sections or ages, as calculated from the average thickness of the strata they contain, as percentages of this, we get something like the following relation :
	(i) 	Archeolithic or archeozoic (Primordial) 	age 	... 	53.6
	(ii) 	Paleolithic or paleozoic (Primary) 	”	...	32.1
	(iii)	Mesolithic or Mesozoic (Secondary)	”	...	11.5
	(iv)	Cenolithic or cenozoic (Tertiary)	”	...	2.3
	(v)	Anthropolithic or anthropozoic (Quaternary)”	...	0.5
					100.0
In any case, the “Historical period: is an insignificant quantity compared with the vast length of the precedings ages, in which there was no question of human existence on our planet. Even the important Cenozoic or Tertiary period, in which the first placentals or higher mammals appear, probably amounts to little over two per cent of the whole organic age.”

“maṉitaṉiṉ āti uṟpattiyiṉ kālam, allatu karārāyc colla vēṇṭumāṉāl, maṉitaṉ avaṉukku neruṅkiya campantamuḷḷa oruvitak kuraṅku jātiyiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉa kālamāṉatu miyōcīṉ (Miocene) kālattilāvatu, piḷaiyōcīṉ (Pliocene) kālattilāvatu atāvatu Tertiary kālattiṉ matti allatu kaṭaicikkālam eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. maṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṉa niṉaikkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟāl, maṉitaṉ atāvatu cariyāyp pēcakkūṭiya tattuvamuḷḷa maṉitaṉ, pēca caktiyaṟṟa kuraṅku maṉita jātiyiṉiṉṟum uṟpattiyāṉa kālam intak kālattilalla, itaṟkup piṉṉuḷḷa Anthropozoic kālattil eṉkiṟārkaḷ.
pūmiyil pul pūṇṭu miruka uṟpatti uṇṭāṉa inta aintāvatu āṟāvatu kaṭaici kālattil tāṉ maṉitaṉiṉ pūraṇa uṟpattiyum avaṉ pala jātiyāyp pirinta viṣayamum naṭantaṉa. atiṉāltāṉ ataṟku Anthropozoic atāvatu maṉitaṉ uyiraip peṟṟa kālameṉṟum nāṉkāvatu (Quarternary Perios) kālameṉṟum peyar. pūrva mirukaṅkaḷaip paṟṟip pēcum cāstiramum pūrva jātikaḷaip paṟṟip pēcum cāstiramum pūraṇappaṭātatāl maṉita jāti, aḻintupōṉa oru vālaṟṟa kuraṅku allatu Lemur eṉṉum mirukattiliruntu inta nālāvatu kālattiṉ tuvakkattilō allatu Tertiary  kālattiṉ naṭuvilō kaṭaiciyilō uṟpattiyāṉateṉṟa kēḷvikkup patil uraippatu kaṣṭamāyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl oṉṟu māttiram niccayam. nākarīkam virttiyāṉatu Anthropozoic kālattiltāṉ. āṉāl jīvarācikaḷiṉ muḻu carittirattaiyum aṭakkikkoṇṭirukkum piramāṇṭamāṉa kāla aḷaviṟkuḷ inta havōcaṭiyīṭiṇaṭiiu kālamāṉatu oru ciṟu aṇṭamtāṉ. itai nām niṉaikkaiyil carittiram eṉpatu nākarīka kālattaik kuṟikkiṟatu eṉṟu colvatu ēḷaṉam paṇṇuvatu pōl irukkiṟaJ. āti aṇu (Monera) tāṉāy uṟpattiyāṉa kāla mutal taṟkālam varaikkumuḷḷa jīvarācikaḷiṉ kālam muḻutaiyum nūṟu camapākaṅkaḷākap pirittu, aintu mukkiya kālaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavaiyum avaikaḷukkuḷḷa saṟṟēṟṟāviṉ (Strata) parumaṉukkut takkatāka avaikaḷiṉ kālattaiyum niccayittāl, piṉvarum Percentage (atāvatu nūṟṟukkum antantak kālaṅkaḷukkumuḷḷa tāratammiyam) varum :-
(i) 	āti tuvakkak kalliṉ kālam (Archeolithic or Archeozoic) (Primordial)  ... 	53.6
(ii) 	paḻaiya kalliṉ kālam (Paleolithic or Paleozoic) (Primary)		...	32.1
(iii)	mattiya kalliṉ kālam (Mesolithic or Mesozoic (Secondary)		...	11.5
(iv)	pintiya kālak kalliṉ kālam (Cenolithic or Cenozoic) (Tertiary)	...	2.3
(v)    	maṉitar uṟpatti kalliṉ kālam (Anthropolithic or Anthropozoic)
		(Quaternary)								...	0.5
											100.0
eṉṟālum carittira kālattai maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttāl nammuṭaiya kirakamākiya pūmiyilēyē maṉita vācaṉaiyillāta pūrva kālattiṉ aḷavukkum itaṟkum aṉantakāla vittiyācam irukkiṟaJ. antappūrva kālaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭappaṭumpōtu itō atika ciṟiyatāy irukkiṟaJ. mammēliyā vakuppaic cērnta uyarnta pirāṇikaḷ uṇṭāṉa mukkiyamāṉa Cenozoic kālaṅkūṭa mottattil nūṟṟil iraṇṭutāṉ.”
itil iṟṟaikkumuṉ jīvaṉ uṟpattiyāṉatāka niṉaikkum kālattai 5 kōṭi varuṣamāka vaittuk koṇṭu atai nūṟu pākamākap pirittu atil aintu kālam collukiṟār. atil nālāvatu kālattiṉ mattiyil kuraṅkiṉamum ataṉpiṉ maṉuṣaṉum uṇṭāyiruntāṉeṉṟum aintāvatu kālattil maṉuṣaṉ pēcavum taṉ iṣṭam pōl maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkup pōkavum varavum kūṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉeṉṟum collukiṟār. inta aintāvatu kālam iraṇṭu laṭcattu aimpatiṉāyiram varuṣameṉṟu avar kaṇakkāl terikiṟatu. inta iraṇṭu laṭcattu aimpatiṉāyiram varuṣattiṉ tuvakkattil pēcavum cañcarikkavum ārampitta maṉuṣaṉ cumār iṟṟaikku 50,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālāvatu cila nākarīkamum tērcciyum peṟṟu viruttiyākiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nām niṉaikkalām. iṟṟaikku cumār 20,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyē avaṉ eḻutat terintavaṉākavum pala kalaikaḷ terintavaṉākavumiruntirukka vēṇṭum.
30. mutalmutal maṉita jāti uṇṭāṉa iṭam lemūriyāveṉpaJ.
ippaṭi mutalmutal tērcci peṟṟa maṉuṣa jāti paḻaiya ulakam allatu lemūriyāk kaṇṭattilēyē yiruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum akkaṇṭam aḻintapiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintu pōyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 264.
“The third, and last, stage of our animal ancestry is the true or speaking man (Homo), who was gradually evolved from the preceding stage by the advance of animal language into articulate human speech. As to the time and place of this real “creation of man” we can only express tentative opinions. It was probably during the Diluvial period in the hotter zone of the Old World either on the mainland in tropical Africa or Asia, or on an earlier continent (Lemuria now sunk below the waves of the Indian Ocean), which stretched from East Africa (Madagascar, Abyssinia) to East Asia (Sunda Islands, further India). I have given fully in my “History of creation” the weighty reasons for claiming this descent of man from the anthropoid eastern apes and shown how we may conceive the spread of the various races from this “Paradise” over the whole earth. I have also dealt fully with the relations of the various races and species of men to each other.
“nām mirukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu uṟpattiyāṉa mūṉṟupaṭikaḷaiyum collu miṭattu avaikaḷil kaṭaicip paṭiyēteṉṟāl ōmō (Homo) atāvatu pēcak kūṭiya maṉitaṉē. inta ōmō (Homo) eṉṟa maṉitaṉ muntiṉapaṭiyiṉiṉṟum mirukapāṣai maṉitapāṣaiyāy virttiyāṉatil nāḷā varttiyil jeṉittavaṉ. inta maṉitaṉ eppaṭi uṇṭāṉāṉeṉṟāvatu evviṭattil uṇṭāṉāṉeṉṟāvatu atika niccayamākac colla muṭiyāJ. anēkamāy paḻaiya ulakattiṉ kāṅkaiyāṉa pākattil jalappiraḷaya kālattil āppirikkā allatu āciyāviṉ taḷapākattilāvatu, allatu ataṟku muṉṉirunta lemūriyā eṉṉum aḻintu pōṉa kaṇṭattilāvatu uṇṭā yirukka vēṇṭum. (intu camuttirattil mūḻkippōṉa intak kaṇṭamāṉatu kīḻ āpirikkā atāvatu matakāskar apiciṉiyā tēcam mutal kīḻ āciyā atāvatu cantā tīvukaḷ kiḻakkuk kōṭi intiyā varai vicālittiruntaJ.) “ciṣṭippiṉ carittiram” eṉṟu nāṉ eḻutiya nūlil maṉitaṉ eppaṭi (yavōcaṭiyīṭiṉai) kiḻakku tēca kuraṅkukaḷiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉpataṟkum eppaṭi ātiyil palajātiyārum nāṉ colliya kaṇṭamākiya intap paratīciṉiṉṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintu pōṉārkaḷ eṉpataṟkum tiṭamāṉa ātāraṅkaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟiyirukkiṟēṉ. pala jātiyārukku muḷḷa vittiyācaṅkaḷaiyum, orēvitamāṉa jātikaḷukkuḷ irukkappaṭṭa neruṅkiya campantattaiyum antap pustakattil vistāramāyc colli yirukkiṟēṉ.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷai nām kavaṉikkaiyil pēcakkūṭiya maṉitaṉ, pēcakkūṭāta vālillāk kuraṅkiṉiṉṟum uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉṟum, miruka pāṣaiyiṉiṉṟē maṉita pāṣai nāḷaṭaivil viruttiyāṉateṉṟum, appaṭi viruttikku vanta atāvatu pēcakkūṭiya maṉitaṉ uṣṇap piratēcattil vacittāṉeṉṟum, atu oru kālattil muḻukippōyiṟṟeṉṟum, am muḻukip pōṉa iṭamē lemūriyāveṉṟum, atu āppirikkā, matakāskār, apiciṉiyā, cantā, intiyā mutaliya pūpākaṅkaḷukku camīpattilirunta teṉṟum, inta iṭamē ātiyil maṉitar uṇṭāṉa pāratīcāyirunta teṉṟum, atu aḻintu pōṉa piṉ ataṟku cuṟṟup pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintu pōṉārkaḷeṉṟum teḷivākat terikiṟatu. lemūriyā eṉṉum inta paḻaiya ulakattil teṉ maturaiyiruntateṉṟum, teṉmaturaiyil tamiḻc caṅkam stāpikkappaṭṭu 4,400 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṉ kaṭalāl aḻintupōṉateṉṟum, avviṭattil pēcivanta tamiḻp pāṣai pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭu pala pāṣaikaḷilum aṅkaṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟateṉṟum itaṉmuṉ pārttōm. ittamiḻp pāṣaiyē iyaṟkaiyāyuḷḷa mirukapāṣaikaḷiṉ cila ōcaikaḷai yuṭaiyatāyirukkiṟa teṉṟum itu oppaṟṟa taṉippāṣaiyāy nāḷatu varaiyum iruntu varukiṟateṉṟum itaṉmuṉ kavaṉittōm. āṉāl appaṭi oru pāṣai ellā pāṣaikaḷukkum ātiyāyiruntirukkumā eṉṟu nām niṉaippōm. avviṣayattil ekkēl (ḻayanaumanaṭa) eṉṉum makā tattuva cāstiriyār colluvataip pārppōm.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 203.
“All philogists of any competence in their science now agree that all human languages have been gradually evolved from very rudimentary beginnings.”
“taṅkaḷ cāstirattil nipuṇar eṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟa ellāp pāṣā cāstirikaḷum maṉitarāl pēcappaṭum ellā pāṣaikaḷum ciṟu tuvakkaṅkaḷiliruntu koñcam koñcamāy viruttikku vantavai eṉṟu oppukkoḷkiṟārkaḷ.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷaik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu mirukapāṣaiyiliruntu maṉitapāṣai pēca ārampittavarkaḷirunta lemūriyāvai āti pāṣai yirunta iṭameṉṟum, anta lemūriyā nāṭṭil ēḻu perumpākaṅkaḷilum pēcappaṭṭu vanta pāṣaiyē ātipāṣaiyeṉṟum colla niyāyamirukkiṟaJ.
31. lemūriyā nāṭṭilē mutal mutal maṉitarkaḷ uṟpattiyāṉatu pōla mutal mutal pēcappaṭṭa pāṣaiyum lemūriyā nāṭṭilēyē uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpatu
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 204.
“As we have been convinced from Comparative anatomy and ontogeny, and from paleontology, that all past and living vertebrates descent from a common ancestor, so the comparative study of dead and living Indo-Germanic tongues proves beyond question that they are all modifications of one primitive language. This view of their origin is now accepted by all the chief philologists who have worked in this branch and are unprejudiced.
“mirukaṅkaḷiṉ tēkakkūṟukaḷaip parīṭcippatālum, avaikaḷiṉ āti uṟpattiyai nōkkuvatālum, muṟkālattil uḷḷa mirukaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam tēkakkūṟu mutaliyavaikaḷaik kavaṉippatālum, ātikāla taṟkāla mutukelumpuḷḷa iṉaṅkaḷellām oru potu muṟpitāviṉiṉṟu uṟpattiyāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nām niccayippatupōlavē, iraṇṭu jermāṉik (Indo-Germanic)  vakuppaic cērnta iṟantupōṉa pāṣaikaḷaiyum ippōtu uyirōṭirukkum pāṣaikaḷaiyum nām parīṭcittup pārkkaiyil avaikaḷellām oru ātipāṣaiyiṉiṉṟu uṇṭāyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīrmāṉikkalām. ippaṭi avaikaḷ uṟpattiyāyiṉa eṉṟa koḷkai yāṉatu inta viṣayattil cirattaiyeṭuttut tāratammiyam pārkkāmal uḻaitta ellā pāṣā vitvāṉkaḷālum oppuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.”
mēṟkaṇṭa mirukaṅkaḷiṉ tēkakkūṟupāṭukaḷaiyum mutukelumpaiyum kavaṉikkaiyil ellā jīvarācikaḷum oru muṟpitāviṉiṉṟu uṇṭāṉavai yeṉṟu nām niṉaikkiṟataṟku ētuvirukkiṟatu pōlavē, pūrvamāyuḷḷa aṉēka pāṣaikaḷ ātiyil oru pāṣaiyiliruntē uṇṭāṉavaiyeṉṟu vicārikkum vivēkikaḷ oppuk koḷḷuvārkaḷ eṉkiṟār.
āti maṉitarkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintupōṉapiṉ avarkaḷ ātiyil pēciya pāṣaiyē pala māṟutalkaḷaiyaṭaintu vevvēṟu peyarkaḷai aṭaintateṉṟu itaṉmuṉ pārttirukkiṟōma. appaṭi vaḻaṅkiya pāṣaikaḷuḷ ātipāṣaiyiṉ vārttaikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatu pirāṇikaḷiṉ tattuva cāstirattaik kāṭṭum tiruṣṭāntaṅkaḷaip pārkkilum atika niccayañ collak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟateṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil collukiṟār.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 205.
“We find just the same thing in comparing the various dead and living languages that have developed from a common primitive tongue. If we examine our genealogical tree of the Indo-Germanic languages in this light, we see at once that all the older or parent tongues, of which we regard the living varieties of the stem as divergent daughter grand-daughter languages, have been extinct for some time. The Aryo-Romanic, and the Slavo Germanic tongues have completely disappeared; so also the Aryan, the Greco-Roman, the Slavo-Lettic, and the ancient Germanic. Even their daughters and grand-daughters have been lost; all the living Indo-Germanic languages are only related in the sense that they are divergent discendants of common stem forms. Some forms have diverged more, and some less, from the original stem-form.
This easily demonstrable fact illustrates very well the analogous case of the origin of the vertebrate species. Phylogenetic comparative philology here yields a strong support to phylogenetic comparative zoology. But the one can adduce more direct evidence than the other, as the palentological material of philology the old monuments of the extinct tongue have been preserved much better than the paleontological material of zoology the fossilised bones and imprints of vertebrates.”
“orē ātipāṣaiyiṉiṉṟum uṟpattiyāṉa palavita iṟantupōṉa pāṣaikaḷaiyum upayōkattilirukkum pāṣaikaḷaiyum oppiṭṭup pārppōmāṉāl nām atēvita muṭivāṉa apippirāyattukkuttāṉ varuvōm. intak koḷkaiyai maṉatil vaittuk koṇṭu nammuṭaiya iṇṭu jermāṉik (Indo-Germanic)  pāṣaikaḷiṉ vamca aṭṭavaṇaiyai nām cōtippōmāṉāl, ippōtu vaḻaṅkukiṟa ententa pāṣaikaḷai pūrva pāṣaikaḷiṉ kumāratti allatu pētti eṉṟu niṉaikkiṟōmō antanta pūrva pāṣaikaḷellām vekukālamāy upayōkattilillāmal iṟantu pōṉavaikaḷāyt terikiṉṟaṉa. āriyōrōmāṉik (Aryo-Romanic) pāṣaikaḷum, cilavō jermāṉik (Slavo-Germanic) pāṣaikaḷum muḻutum kāṇāmal maṟaintaṉa. appaṭiyē āriyaṉ (Aryan)  kirikkōrōmaṉ (Greco-Roman) cilavō leṭṭik (Slavo-Lettic)  pūrva jermāṉik (Germanic) mutaliya pāṣaikaḷellām oḻintupōyiṉa. ivaikaḷiṉ kumārattikaḷum pēttikaḷuṅkūṭa oḻintu pōyiṉa. taṟkālam vaḻaṅkum iṇṭu jermāṉik (Indo-Germanic) pāṣaikaḷellām etu viṣayattil ottirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟāl, orē aṭiyil piṟantum pala vittiyācamāṉa piṉ cantatiyārai uṭaiyavaikaḷāyiruppatiltāṉ āti pāṣaiyiliruntu cila anēka māṟutalkaḷaṭaintum cila koñca māṟutalkaḷaṭaintum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
eḷitil rūpakārappaṭuttakkūṭiya inta viṣayamāṉatu itaṟku oppāṉa mutukelumpuṭaiya jīvarācikaḷiṉ uṟpattiyai viḷakkakkūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. pala pāṣaikaḷiṉ uṟpattiyaik kāṭṭukiṟa pāṣā cāstiramāṉatu pala mirukaṅkaḷiṉ uṟpattiyaik kāṭṭum miruka cāstirattiṟku palatta utaviyāyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟaiviṭa tiṭamāy rūpakārap paṭuttum viṣayattil atika ciṟantatāyirukkiṟatu. eppaṭiyeṉṟāl pāṣā cāstirattiṟku vēṇṭiya ātāraṅkaḷ mutaliyavai, atāvatu aḻintupōṉa pāṣaikaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, miruka cāstira ātāraṅkaḷākiya elumpu mutaliyavaikaḷiṉ kal accukaḷaiviṭa tiṟamāy pātukāttu vaikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.”
mēṟkāṭṭiya cila varikaḷai nām kavaṉikkaiyil ippōtu vaḻaṅki varukiṟa ellāp pāṣaikaḷum orē oru pāṣaiyiliruntu uṇṭāṉavai yeṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. appāṣai lemūriyā eṉṉum paḻaiya ulakattil pēcappaṭṭu vantatu. anta āti pāṣaiyiṉ vārttaikaḷ palavum pala pāṣaikaḷōṭu kalantiruppataik koṇṭu pala jentukkaḷiṉ uṟpattiyaiyum mirukaṅkaḷiliruntu uṇṭāṉa maṉuṣaṉiṉ uṟpattiyaiyum tiṭṭamāyt terintu koḷvataṟku palatta utaviyāyirukkum eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṟatu. pāṣaikaḷil vaḻaṅkivarum āti pāṣaiyiṉ cila vārttaikaḷ kaṟkaḷil tōṉṟum mirukac ciṉṉaṅkaḷaip pārkkilum palatta cāṭciyāka viḷaṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
32. lemūriyā nāṭṭil pēcappaṭṭu vanta pāṣai tamiḻppāṣaiyē eṉpatu.
maṉita jāti uṟpattiyāṉa ātipūmi lemūriyā eṉṟu nām palavitattālum tiṭṭamāy aṟikiṟōm. lemūriyāk kaṇṭattiliruntu maṉita jātikaḷum maṉita jātikku muntiya vālillāk kuraṅkiṉamum maṟṟum cila pirāṇikaḷum tāvara varkkaṅkaḷum ataṟku cuṟṟilumuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiliruppataik koṇṭu teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉpākattiliruntu aḻintu pōṉatākac collappaṭum kumarināṭē lemūriyāveṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. kumariyāṟṟiṟkum, paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum naṭuvilirunta tākac collappaṭum mikunta nīr vaḷamuḷḷa ēḻteṅkanāṭum, ēḻ maturai nāṭum, ēḻ muṉpālaināṭum, ēḻ piṉpālai nāṭum, ēḻ kuṉṟanāṭum, ēḻ kuṇakāraināṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpaṉaināṭum ākiya 49 nāṭukaḷaṭaṅkiya teṉpāṇṭi nāṭē maṉita jātikku āti piṟappiṭameṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. ēḻu ēḻu nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭukaḷ eṉṟu colluvatai nām kavaṉikkaiyil nāval tīvu, (nāval maram) iṟalit tīvu, (ittimaram) kucait tīvu, (nāṇal) kiravuñcat tīvu, (aṉṟil) puṣkarattīvu, (yāṉai) teṅkut tīvu, kamukut tīvu eṉṟa ēḻu perum pūpākaṅkaḷum āsṭrēliyā, cumāttirā, jāvā pōla ēḻu tīvukaḷāyiruntirukkalāmeṉṟum avaikaḷ ov voṉṟum evvēḻu nāṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkalāmeṉṟum tōṉṟukiṟatu. teṉṉintiyāvil iyaṟkaiyāyk kāṇappaṭum nāval, itti, nāṇal, aṉṟil, yāṉai, teṅku, kamuku mutaliya maraṅkaḷum jīvarācikaḷum ēḻu tīvukaḷilum mikutiyāy iruntatākat terikiṟatu. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷi liruppatāka nām kāṇamāṭṭōm. capta curaṅkaḷum ivvēḻu tīvukaḷilēyē yiruntu uṇṭāṉatāka pūrva nūlkaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu. inta nāṟpat toṉpatu nāṭum kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭumuṉ mikunta ceḻippuṭaiyatāyum iyaṟkai amaippiṉ vaḷaṅkaḷ mikuntatāyum pāṇṭiya rājarkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭu vantatāyum nām itaṉmuṉ pārttōm. kaṭalāl aḻinta innāṭṭiṟku teṉmaturai talainakarāyiruntaJ. itil tamiḻaiyē pēci vantārkaḷ. ittamiḻ moḻiyē pala iṭaṅkaḷilum pala pāṣaikaḷilum vaḻaṅki varukiṟateṉṟu itaṉmuṉ vistāramākap pārttōm. tamiḻp pāṣaiyil eḻutappaṭṭa pala ariya viṣayaṅkaḷum nūlkaḷum piraḷayattāl aḻikkappaṭṭapiṉ pāṣaiyiṉ mikavum coṟpamāṉa pākamāttiram miñci niṉṟaJ. pūrvamuḷḷa nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅki varum cila vārttaikaḷ iṉṉum arttan teriyāmal appaṭiyē niṟkiṉṟaṉa. anēka arum pataṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkāmal oḻintaṉa. ippaṭi nūlkaḷum nūlkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷum poruḷkaḷai viḷakkum arumpataṅkaḷum oḻintapiṉ, neyyariyil miñciya ilai ciṟuvar vāy vantatu pōla nāṭōṭiya vaḻakkattilirukkum vārttaikaḷē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ittamiḻmoḻi pūrvamāy lemūriyāvil pēcappaṭṭu vantateṉpataiyum atu pala pāṣaikaḷil kalantirukkiṟateṉpataiyum iyaṟkai amaippiṉ ōcaikaḷukku mikunta poruttamuṭaiya tāyirukkiṟateṉpataiyum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ culapamāṉa vaṭivaiyum eḻuttut tavaṟāta uccarippaiyum koṇṭu tamiḻē ātipūrva pāṣaiyeṉṟu tiṭṭamākat terikiṟatu. 49 tamiḻ nāṭukaḷaṭaṅkiya lemūriyā aḻinta kālattil tamiḻarum, tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa kalaiñāṉaṅkaḷum aḻintu pōṉatiṉāl, tamiḻai aṟpamāka niṉaittu alaṭciyam ceyyum kēvala nilaikku vantatu. peruṅkāyamirunta pāttirattil eñci niṉṟa vācaṉaipōla mikac coṟpamāṉa pākam iraṇṭāñ caṅka kālattil nūlkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa. tolkāppiyattaiyum maṟṟum cila nūlkaḷaiyum nām uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl vaittiyam, vātam, yōkam, ñāṉam, cōtiṭam, cittiram, caṅkītam mutaliya 64 kalaikaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil mikunta perumai peṟṟu vaḻaṅki vantaṉaveṉṟu aṟivōm. mutaṟ caṅkamirunta teṉ maturaiyil kāyciṉa vaḻuti mutal kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāyuḷḷa 89 pāṇṭiyarkaḷ aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. avaruḷ ēḻu pāṇṭiya rājarkaḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ cayamākīrttiyaṉākiya nilantarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil eḻunūṟṟukkāvatam paraviyirunta 49 pāṇṭiya nāṭum kaṭalāl aḻintaṉa.
33. teṉṉāṭṭaip paṟṟiyum teṉṉāṭṭiṉ pāṣaiyaip paṟṟiyum rājāṅkattaip paṟṟiyum collum apippirāyattai ottuppārttal.
tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum teṉṉāṭṭilēyē tamatu jīviya nāḷellām taṅkiyiruntu tamiḻp pāṣaiyait tīra vicāritta kālṭvel (Caldwell) attiyaṭcar avarkaḷ tamiḻiliruntu camaskirutattilum, epiru pāṣaiyilum, āṅkilō cākcaṉ pāṣaikaḷilum, cīttiya pāṣaikaḷilum, maṟṟum cila pāṣaikaḷilum vaḻaṅki varum vārttaikaḷai vevvēṟākap pirittuc colvatai itaṉ muṉ pārttōm. ataikkoṇṭu maṟṟa ellāp pāṣaikaḷukkum tamiḻp pāṣaiyē āti ūṟṟāyirukkalāmeṉṟu itaṉ muṉ nām eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟōm. tamiḻp pāṣaiyiṉ vārttaikaḷ pala pāṣaikaḷilum kalantiruppataik koṇṭu ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ pala nāṭukaḷukkup pōyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟāvatu allatu maṟṟavar tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vantu kalantu uṟavāṭiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟāvatu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. āṉāl ekkēl (Haeckel) eṉṉum tattuvacāstiriyār colluvatai nām kavaṉippōmāṉāl teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉ pākattilirunta lemūriyā eṉṉum kaṇṭam mikappūrvamāyuḷḷa paḻaiya ulakameṉṟum, atuvē jaṉaṅkaḷ mutal mutal uṟpattiyāṉa paratīcāyiruntateṉṟum collukiṟār. pāṣaikaḷ pala māṟi māṟi putitākkappaṭṭu vantālum avaikaḷil kalantirukkum vārttaikaḷaik kavaṉittāl avaikaḷ mikappūrvamāṉa oru pāṣaiyiṉiṉṟē vantirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum avvārttaikaḷ maṉuṣa uṟpattiyaiyum avarkaḷ uṟpattiyāṉa nāṭṭaiyum annāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta pāṣaiyaiyum kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku mukkiya utaviyāyirukkumeṉṟum collukiṟār.
tamiḻ vārttaikaḷ pala pāṣaikaḷil kalantiruppataik koṇṭu tamiḻppāṣai taṉitta pāṣaiyāyirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇum kālṭvel attiyaṭcar avarkaḷum teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉpākattiliruntu aḻintupōṉa lemūriyāvē jaṉaṅkaḷ uṟpattiyāṉa paratīcu eṉṟu collum ekkēl tattuvacāstiriyārum iṟṟaikkuc cumār 50 varuṣaṅkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷeṉṟu nām aṟivōm. āṉāl iṟṟaikku cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷukku urai yeḻutiya nakkīrarum, akkālattirunta rājākkaḷ, tēcaṅkaḷ, kalaikaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷai oruvāṟu aṟintu koḷḷaṟkētuvāyirukkiṟa cilappatikāram eḻutiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉ pakkattiluḷḷa kumariyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṉ ēḻunūṟṟuk kāvataṅkaḷukku appuṟamuḷḷa paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum iṭaiyilirunta 49 tamiḻ nāṭukaḷ aḻintu pōṉatākac collukiṟārkaḷ. annāṭukaḷukku teṉmaturai rājatāṉiyāka iruntateṉṟum, atil 89 pāṇṭiyarkaḷ 4,440 āṇṭu paramparaiyāy aracāṭci ceytum tamiḻccaṅkattai ātarittum vantārkaḷeṉṟum collukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ colliyiruppavai itaṉmuṉ pāṣaiyaip paṟṟiyum lemūriyāvaip paṟṟiyum eḻutiya kālṭvel attiyaṭcar avarkaḷukkum ekkēl eṉṉum tattuva cāstiriyārukkum terintirukku māṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭaip paṟṟiyum tamiḻp pāṣaiyaip paṟṟiyum pāṇṭiya rājyattaip paṟṟiyum atika nuṭpamāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷaic colli yiruppārkaḷ.
immūṉṟaiyum cīr tūkkip pārppōmēyāṉāl oruvar tamiḻp pāṣaiyiṉ pūrvattaiyum ataṉ mēṉmaiyaiyum maṟṟoruvar tamiḻp pāṣai vaḻaṅki vanta lemūriyā nāṭṭaiyum ataṉ iyaṟkaiyaiyum paṟṟic collukiṟār. maṟṟavar, lemūriyā eṉṟu piṟar collum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷōṭukūṭiya 49 tamiḻ nāṭukaḷaiyum cila ūrkaḷaiyum, malaikaḷaiyum, āṟukaḷaiyum, rājarkaḷaiyum, caṅkap pulavarkaḷaiyum, akkālattu vaḻaṅki vanta nūlkaḷaiyum cayamākīrttiyaṉākiya nilan tarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil kaṭalāl aḻintupōṉataiyum collu kiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iṟṟaikku 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷavarkaḷ. itilum muṉṉatāka iṟṟaikku cumār 8,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ teṉmaturaiyil aracāṇṭu koṇṭirunta nilantaru tiruviṟpāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattil ataṅkōṭṭācāṉ muṉṉilaiyil tolkāppiyam araṅkēṟṟiyateṉṟu tolkāppiya pāyirattil collappaṭuvataiyum nām kavaṉippōmāṉāl taṉittut taṉittu vicāritta palaruṭaiya apip pirāyaṅkaḷ lemūriyā eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum pūrva tamiḻnāṭākiya teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟkē poruntumeṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. itōṭu teṉmaturaiyilum kapāṭapurattilum uttaramaturaiyilum cumār 13,000 varuṣaṅkaḷāka iṟṟaikku cumār 700 varuṣaṅkaḷ varaiyum pāṇṭiya rājarkaḷē aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭu vantirukkiṟārkaḷeṉpataip pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉālum, ataṉpiṉ stalapurāṇaṅkaḷiṉālum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. cāṉṟōr kulattavarāṉa ivarkaḷ nāṭaṉ, nāṭāṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ, tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉap pala pulavarkaḷāl pukaḻap peṟṟu vantārka ḷeṉṟum nām aṟikiṟōm. ivvaḷavu kālam āṇṭu koṇṭirunta rāja vamcattavarkaḷ ippōtu illāmal pōṉārkaḷāveṉṟu niṉaikka nēriṭum. mūṉṟāvatu caṅkattiṉ kaṭaiciyil pāṇṭiya rājaṉukkum caṅkap pulavarkaḷukkum cila maṉavaruttam nēriṭa caṅkaṅkalaintu tamiḻaippaṟṟi vicārikkum ūkkam kuṟaintu tamiḻ aracarkaḷai aṟpamāy niṉaikkum kaṣṭakālam ārampittaJ. ataṟkuppiṉ cumār āyira varuṣaṅkaḷāka aracāṭciyiruntālum pala uḷ kalakattiṉāl aracāṭci muṭintaJ. ataṉ piṉ vanta makammatiyarkaḷum, nāyakkarkaḷum maṟṟum evarum cāṉṟōr kulattavarukku catturukkaḷ āṉārkaḷ. ataṉāl avarkaḷ varavara pūmi urimai iḻantu coṟpattil jīvaṉam ceyyumpaṭiyāṉa nilaikku vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ nāḷatu varaiyum nilaittirukkum teyvapakti, rājapakti, tairiyam, uṇmai, muyaṟci, poruḷīṭṭal, aṭakkam mutaliya uttama kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum avarkaḷukkuḷ vaḻaṅkivarum kulappeyarkaḷaiyum paṭṭap peyarkaḷaiyum kavaṉikkum aṟivāḷikaḷ intiyāviṉ teṉ pākattil mikuntu vacikkum tamiḻ makkaḷākiya cāṉṟōr kulattavarē pūrva pāṇṭiyarāja vamcattavarkaḷeṉṟu kāṇpārkaḷ.
uṇmaiyuḷḷa tēvapaktaṉāyirunta tāvītu aracaṉum mikunta ñāṉi eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum avaṉ kumāraṉ cālōmōṉ aracaṉum pakkattu tēcattu rājākkaḷ kappaṅkaṭṭa pirapalamāy aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭirunta yūtēyā rājyam aḻiya, tirumpa avvirājyattukku varum eṇṇamaṟṟa ēḻaikaḷāyppōṉa yūtarkaḷaiyum, oru kālattil mikavum pirapalamāṉa rājākkaḷāyiruntu ippōtu rājyam iḻantu celvamum iḻantu vaṟiñarāy cañcarikkum, amerikkāviluḷḷa cikappuniṟa intiyarkaḷaiyum (Red Indians), āppirikkāvil oru kālattil mikunta celvākku peṟṟu neṭunāḷ aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭiruntu piṟaku avai yāvum iḻantu mikattāḻnta jātiyār eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum kēvala nilaiyil taṟkālam irukkum cūlu, kāvpar (Zulu, Kaffir) eṉṉum teṉ āppirikka jātiyāraiyum aṟivāḷikaḷ aṟivārkaḷ. itupōlavē pūrva tamiḻ aracarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiya pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷum avarkaḷ aracāṭciyum pōṉapiṉ avarkaḷ piṉṉaṭiyārum mika ēḻaikaḷākit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ ōr pākamākiya teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉ pakkattil nāḷatuvaraiyum jaṉapperukkuṭaṉ nilaittu viruttiyaṭaintu varukiṟārkaḷ eṉpataiyum aṟivāḷikaḷ aṟiyāmaṟpōkārkaḷ.
mēṣattil uccaṉāyirunta cūriyaṉ paṭippaṭiyāyk kuṟaintu tulā rāciyil nīcaṉākiṟatu pōlavum piṉ paṭippaṭiyāy vaḷarntu mēṣattil uccaṉākiṟatu pōlavum, oru kālattil talaiyāyiruntatu, maṟṟoru kālattil vālākavum, vālāka iruntatu talaiyākavum māṟum irutalai maṇiyaṉaip pōlavum ituvumākiyatu ulaka iyaṟkaitāṉē. mēl kīḻākavum kīḻ mēlākavum ākuṅkālattil ataṟkēṟpa naṭantu koḷvatu maṉuṣa iyaṟkai. rājarkaḷ muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭu tam kuṭikaḷē tamakkuc catturukkaḷākuṅ kālattil kāṭukaḷil maṟaintu kāy kaṉikaḷait tiṉṟu viṟaku veṭṭi jīvaṉam paṇṇuvataiyum nām kēḷvippaṭṭirukkiṟōm.
oru rākattil vaḻaṅkum curaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu stāyikaḷilum ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇa katiyāy cañcāram peṟṟu vāti camvāti vitippaṭi uṇṭākum pala pirastārattiṉāl āṉantam uṇṭākkuvatupōla aṇṭa puvaṉa carācaraṅkaḷukkuk karttaṉāṉa teyvattiṟku ituvum oru tiruviḷaiyāṭṭē.
ippaṭi pāṇṭiyarājyam aḻikaiyil caṅkīta nūlkaḷ palavum aḻintu pōyiṉa. atil tappip piḻaittavar pala tēcattil ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ vaḻaṅki vanta tamiḻppāṣai kālañcellac cella muṟṟilum māṟi vēṟu pāṣaiyāyiṟṟu. appaṭiyāṉālum tamiḻp pāṣaiyiṉ cātāraṇa coṟkaḷ palavum appāṣaikaḷil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. atupōlavē teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṟkuriya cila amcaṅkaḷ māṟit tēcattukkut tēcam kāṉam vēṟupaṭṭālum, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki vanta mukkiya curaṅkaḷē māṟāmal vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. curutikaḷ ittaṉaiyirukkalā meṉṟu vēṟu cilar collik koṇṭiruntālum, avaikaḷai upayōkikkum vaḻiyum vāttiyattiṉ utaviyumillāmal vāyccollōṭu māttiram niṟkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl teṉṉintiyāvilō paṉṉiraṇṭu curaṅkaḷaiyum avaikaḷiṉiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷaiyumuṭaiya kāṉam ceytukoṇṭu varukiṟārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ aṉupōkattilirukkum ammēṉmaiyāṉa kāṉam pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷil collappaṭṭiruppavaiyeṉṟu aṟiyārkaḷ. avaikaḷ cērum muṟai iṉṉateṉṟum atuvē caṅkītattiṟku piratāṉa meṉṟum niṉaikka maṟantu pōṉārkaḷ. paramparaiyāyuḷḷa pāṭamē taṟkālam varaikkum uṇmaiyaik kāppāṟṟik koṇṭu niṟkiṟaJ. teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum rakaciyaṅkaḷai vaṭa pāṣaiyil eḻutiyavarkaḷ ivviṣayattil tavaṟip pōṉārkaḷeṉpatai itaṉpiṉ pārppōm. caṅkītattukkuriya peyarkaḷaiyum irākaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum muṟṟilum māṟṟip pūrvat tamiḻppeyarkaḷ illāmal camaskirutattil pustakaṅkaḷ eḻuti vaittārkaḷ. ivvuṇmaiyai itaṉpiṉ aṟivōm. ataṉ muṉ intiya caṅkītattaippaṟṟiyum atil vaṭatēcattu caṅkītattaip paṟṟiyum intustāṉ caṅkītattaippaṟṟiyum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattaip paṟṟiyum maṟṟavar collum cila apippirāyaṅkaḷai nām kavaṉippatu nallateṉṟu niṉaikkiṟēṉ.
V. intiya caṅkītattaip paṟṟip palar collum vevvēṟu apippirāyam
1. intiya caṅkītattaip paṟṟiya potuvāṉa apippirāyam.
teṉṉintiyāviṟkut teṟkēyirunta lemūriyā eṉṟu taṟkālattil aḻaikkappaṭum ātipāṇṭināṭu nāṟpattoṉpatum kaṭalāl aḻikkap paṭṭapiṉ, pūrvattil atu aṭaintirunta ellā nākarikamum muṟṟilum aḻintaṉa. ataippaṟṟip piṟar collit terintu koḷḷavāvatu ōlaic cācaṉaṅkaḷ ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷ kalveṭṭukaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷiṉāl aṟintu koḷḷavāvatu ētuvillāmal pōyviṭṭatu. iruntālum, aṅkaṅkē citaṟik kiṭanta cila nūlkaḷiṉālum iṭaiccaṅkattilum kaṭaiccaṅkattilu mirunta vitvāṉkaḷiṉāl collappaṭṭa coṟpa ātāraṅkaḷiṉālum koñcam terikiṟatēyaṉṟi muṟṟum teriyavillai. āṉālum paramparai yāyk kēḷvi mūlamāyk kāppāṟṟappaṭṭu varum caṅkītattaik koṇṭum cila kaittoḻilkaḷaik koṇṭu ippaṭit tērcciyuḷḷa oru kāla miruntatu eṉṟu yāvarum niṉaikka iṭamirukkiṟatu. ippaṭi niṉaippavarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam oṉṟukkoṉṟu cila pākaṅkaḷil vittiyācamāyiruntālum pūrvīkattilirunta uṇmaiyai oppuk koḷvataṟkup pōtumāṉavaiyeṉṟē niṉaikkiṟēṉ. kiṟistu paramāttumā ulakil avatarikkum kālattil kuruccantirayōkamum cantira maṅkaḷayōkamum peṟṟa caṉi mīṉattil niṟka oru periya vāṉajōti tōṉṟumeṉṟum, accōti tōṉṟum rāci pākaikaḷukku ottatāṉa pūpākattil mika makattuvamuḷḷa oru avatāra puruṣaṉ piṟappāreṉṟum, lakkaṉātipati cūriyaṉ nīca paṅkarājayōkamum putaṉ uccamum puttira stāṉattil rākuvum niṟpataikkoṇṭu avar irāṭca´ aippaci mātam 18m tēti 483/4 nāḻikaikku jaṉaṉamāvār eṉṟum kaṇitattāl aṟintu avar piṟakkiṟa kālattil avaraik kaṇṭu kāṇikkaikaḷuṭaṉ taricikka vēṇṭu meṉṟu vekutūram yāttirai ceytu taricitta kiḻakku tēcattu cāstirikaḷiṉ kaṇitattiṉ nuṭpamum vāṉa cāstirattiṉ niccayamum avatāra puruṣarkaḷait taricikka virumpiya avarkaḷ paktiyum nām pārttu āccariyappaṭa vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. ivarkaḷ tamiḻ mūvaracarkaḷā yirukkalāmeṉṟu ūkikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. kaṇitattil mikunta nuṭpamāṉa piṉṉa pākaṅkaḷukkum 36 stāṉaṅkaḷ varai periya lakkaṅkaḷukkum peyar vaittu aḻaikkiṟa vaḻakkamāṉatu mikavum koṇṭāṭakkūṭiyatē. appaṭiyē ciṟpa cāstirattilum caṅkītattilum mikunta pāṇṭittiya maṭain tiruntārkaḷ. pattukkuppattu caturamāyuḷḷa oru melliya cālvaiyai oru kaikkuḷ aṭaṅkumpaṭiyākac ceytiruppārkaḷāṉāl avarkaḷ kait toḻiliṉ tiṟamaiyaip paṟṟic collavum vēṇṭumā? oru nelliṉ umiyai iraṇṭu pākamākap pirittu, uḷḷirukkum ariciyil vikṉēsparar corūpamum cuppiramaṇiyar corūpamum enta amcattilum kuṟaivupaṭāmal ceytu, paḻaiyapaṭi atil vaittu mūṭit taṅkaḷ kaittoḻil cāmarttiyattaik kāṭṭakkūṭiyavarkaḷum, mikunta palamuṭaiyaṉavum catturukkaḷ toṭuṅkālattil toṭṭa anēkarai atam paṇṇip pōṭakkūṭiyaṉavumāṉa poṟikaḷaiyum patumaikaḷaiyum ceyyak kūṭiyavarkaḷum anēkariruntārkaḷ. ippaṭik kaittoḻil, cittiram, caṅkītam, yōkacātaṉai, vaittiyam mutaliyavaikaḷ intiyāvil oru kālattil aṅkaṅkēyiruntu vantaṉaveṉṟu yāvarum colvārkaḷ. āṉāl ivvariya vittaikaḷ taṅkaḷ pirāṇaṉ pōkiṟa varaikkum taṅkaḷ contap piḷḷaikaḷukkuṅkūṭac colli vaikka maṉamillātavarkaḷiṉāl aṅkaṅkē maṟaintu pōyviṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷ pūrvacarittiraṅkaḷai aṟiya virumpum cilarukku vicāraṇaiyiṉmēl kiṭaikkak kūṭiyaṉavākavum ūkikkak kūṭiyaṉavākavu mirukkiṉṟaṉavēyoḻiya, tiṭṭamāy varakkūṭiyavai mikac cilavē. iruntālum, carittira ārāyccikkārar avarkaḷukkuk kiṭaittatākac collum kuṟippukaḷil cila kavaṉikkat takuntavai.
Hindu Music and the Gayan Samaj Part II. P. 8.
“But upon the testimony of works of great antiquity lying around us (some 4,000 to 8,000 years old), we can safely affirm that Hindu music was developed into a system in very ancient times, in times of which we have no genuine records, in times when all other nations of the world were struggling with the elements for existence, in times when Hindu Rishis were enjoying the fruits of civilization and occupying themselves with the contemplation of the mighty powers of the eternal Brahma.”
“nammaiccuṟṟi nām pārkkum 8,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa paḻamaiyāṉa nūlkaḷiṉ cākṣiyattai vaittuk koṇṭu intiya caṅkītamāṉatu ātikālattilēyē oru muṟaiyāka stāpaṉamāṉateṉṟu nām tairiyamāyc collalām. ippaṭic caṅkītam uṇṭāṉa kālamāṉatu namakku ataippaṟṟittiṭṭamāṉa carittira cāsaṉaṅkaḷ illātakālam. antakkālamāṉatu ulakattiṉ maṟṟellā jātiyārum tiṭa nilaimaikku vēṇṭiya ētukkaḷkūṭa illāmal tattaḷittuk koṇṭirunta kālam. antak kālamāṉatu intu makariṣikaḷ nākarīkamaṭaintatiṉāl uṇṭākum palaṉkaḷaip pūraṇamāy aṉupavittuk koṇṭu pirammattiṉuṭaiya aḷavilaṭaṅkā vallamaiyai eppōtun tiyāṉittuk koṇṭu taṅkaḷ vāḻnāḷai celavaḻitta kālam.”
itil iṟṟaikku 8,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉum intiyāvil caṅkītam iruntatai paḻaimaiyāṉa nūlkaḷ collukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu collukiṟār. caṅkītattil māttiramalla, necavilum, nilaiyuḷḷa cāyaṅkaḷ pōṭuvatilum, vitamvitamāṉa vāttiyak karuvikaḷ ceyvatilum tērcciyaṭain tiruntārkaḷeṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil terintu koḷḷalām.
Mill’s History of Br. India, Vol 1, Page II.
“Of the exquisite degree of perfection to which the Hindus have carried the productions of the loom, it would be idle to offer any description.
Among the arts of the Hindus, that of printing and dyeing their cloths has been celebrated; and the beauty and brilliancy as well as durability of the colours they produce are worthy of particular praise.”
Dr. Tennant says, “If we are to judge merely from the number of instruments and the frequency with which they apply them, the Hindus might be regarded as considerable proficients in music.”
“tariyupayōkittal, tuṇi neytal ākiya vittaikaḷil intukkaḷ ati campūraṇa tērcciyaṭaintiruntavarkaḷākaiyāl ataippaṟṟi iṅku vistarippatu acāttiyamāṉa kāriyam. intukkaḷ tērcciyaṭainta vittaikaḷuḷ tuṇikaḷil cittiraṅkaḷai acciṭuvatum avaikaḷukku cāyam tōyppatum veku vicēṣittavai. antac cāyaṅkaḷ aḻakilum, pakaṭṭilum, nīṭittu nilaittiruppatilum vicēṣittiruntaṉavākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
intukkaḷ upayōkappaṭuttukiṟa vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkattaik kavaṉikkiṟapōtum avarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi avaikaḷai upayōkikkiṟa viṣayattaik kavaṉikkiṟapōtum avarkaḷ caṅkītattil vekutūram tērcciyaṭaintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu teḷivākiṟatu, eṉṟu ṉuca. kūnanyēvē collukiṟār.”
mēlum āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil paṭaiyeṭuttu varuvataṟku muṉṉēyē intiyāvilirunta pūrva kuṭikaḷ mikunta nākarīkamuṭaiyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷeṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
India through the ages. Steels P.I.
“Even if we go so far back as B.C. 2,000, the voices of men who have lived and died are still to be heard in the earlier hymns of the Rig-Veda.”
“These same hymns incidentally tell us that the Aryan invaders found a people in India civilised enough to have towns and disciplined troops, to have weapons and banners, women whose ornaments were of gold, poisoned arrows whose heads were of some metal that was probably iron.”
“intutēca carittirattil rikku vētattil kaṭavuḷukkuc collappaṭum pirārttaṉaikaḷaik kavaṉittup pārppōmāṉāl antak kālattil  atāvatu ēṟakkuṟaiya 4,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta maṉitariṉ paḻakka vaḻakkam mutaliyavaikaḷ namakku naṉṟāyt teriya varukiṟatu. atē pirārttaṉaikaḷiliruntu nām maṟṟum kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum aṟikiṟōm. ateṉṉaveṉil, āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil paṭaiyeṭuttu vantapōtu aṅkirunta jaṉaṅkaḷ nākarīkattukkuriya anēka amcaṅkaḷai uṭaiyavarkaḷā yiruntataik kaṇṭārkaḷ. avai yāvaiyeṉil, intukkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷil vacittārkaḷ. naṉṟāyp payiṟṟuvikkappaṭṭa cēṉaikaḷ avarkaḷukku iruntaṉa. avarkaḷuṭaiya stirīkaḷ taṅka nakaikaḷaiyaṇintiruntārkaḷ. nuṉiyil viṣamēṟṟappaṭṭa irumpup pūṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya ampukaḷai upayōkittiruntārkaḷ eṉpavaikaḷē.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷai nām kavaṉikkaiyil āriyar intiyāviṟku paṭaiyeṭuttu vantapōtē teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa jaṉaṅkaḷ rājya pārattiṟkuriya kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷuṭaiya nakaraṅkaḷil yuttap payiṟcipeṟṟa cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉum āṭai āparaṇaṅkaḷiṉ aḻakōṭum viṣam ēṟṟappeṟṟa āyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉum vacittu vantārkaḷeṉṟu collukiṟār. tāṅkaḷ kuṭiyirukkum tēcattiṉ cītōṣṇa stitiyum pulpūṇṭu tāvaram tāṉiyaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟaivaiyum aṟintu tāṅkaḷum taṅkaḷ āṭu māṭukaḷum piḻaippataṟkēṟṟa vacatiyāṉa iṭam tēṭi vanta āriyar, yākañ ceytum cōmaraca pāṉañ ceytum vantārkaḷeṉṟu vētattiṉālēyē teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. ivarkaḷ varuvataṟkumuṉ teṉṉintiyāvil uyirkaḷaik kollā viratattaiyum māmicam pucikkāta caivaneṟiyaiyum mēṉmaiyākak koṇṭoḻukiyavar mikutiyāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟu pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷ mūlamāy aṟikiṟōm.
nāḷaṭaivil avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vētattiluḷḷa pirārttaṉaikaḷ collum caṅkīta muṟaiyai viṭṭut teṉṉintiyāviṟkuriya kāṉa muṟaiyai mikutiyāyp payiṉṟu varukiṟārkaḷ eṉpataiyum nām iṅkē maṟantu pōkakkūṭāJ.
caṅkītamāṉatu intiyarkaḷuṭaiya nākarīkattai virutti paṇṇiṉa cāstiraṅkaḷil oṉṟāyiruntateṉṟum iṟṟaikkuc cumār 2,300 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālattilēyē oḻuṅkuṭaṉ amaikkap paṭṭiruntateṉṟum intiyāviliruntē ekiptu (Egypt) pāracīkam (Persia) arēpiyā (Arabia) kirēk (Greece) mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭateṉṟum piṉvarum cila vākkiyaṅkaḷāl kāṇalām.
W.W. Hunter’s The Indian Empire P. 110-112.
INDIAN MUSIC.
“The Indian art of Music (Gandharva Veda) was destined to exercise a wider influence. A regular system of notation had been worked out before the age of Panini (350 B.C.), and the seven notes were designatred by their initial letters. This notation passed from the Brahmans through the Persians to Arabia and was thence introduced into European music by Guido L. Arezzo at the beginning of the 11th Century. Some indeed, suppose that our modern word gamut comes not from the Greek letter gamma, but from the Indian gama in Prakrit; (in Sanskrit, Grama) literally a musical scale”
“kāntarva vētam eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭa intiya caṅkītamāṉatu intiyaruṭaiya nākarīkattai virutti paṇṇiṉa anēka karuvikaḷil cirēṣṭamāṉaJ. ki.mu.  350 varuṣattukku muṟpaṭṭa pāṇiṉi (ñayanini) muṉivar kālattilēyē, intiya caṅkītamāṉatu oruvita oḻuṅkuṭaṉ amaikkappaṭṭu, irākaṅkaḷai curappaṭuttum muṟai, sapta curaṅkaḷaiyum avaikaḷait toṭaṅkum eḻuttukkaḷāl aḻaittal mutaliya oḻuṅkukaḷuṭaṉum iruntatākat terikiṟatu. irākaṅkaḷaic curaṅkaḷāka eḻutum immuṟai yāṉatu pirāmaṇarkaḷiṭamiruntu pāracīkar mūlamāy arēpiyāvukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭu, appuṟam avarkaḷiṭamiruntu 11vatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil, Guido L. Arezzo  eṉpavarāl airōppiya caṅkītattil amaikkappaṭṭaJ. taṟkālam caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum ‘‘Gamut’’ eṉṉum patamāṉatu kirēkku eḻuttākiya ‘Gamma’ eṉpatiliruntu uṇṭāṉatu eṉṟu cilar apippirāyappaṭuvatupōl niṉaiyāmal intiya patamākiya ‘kirāmam’ (atāvatu ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇam) eṉṉum patattiliruntu uṇṭāṉatākac cilar niṉaikkiṟārkaḷ.”
kiṟistuvukkumuṉ 2,500kkum 1,400kkum naṭuviluḷḷa pirāmaṇiya kālam (kirantaṅkaḷ uṇṭāṉa kālam) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum 4,400 ām varuṣattilēyē intiya caṅkītattukkuriya ārōkaṇa avarō kaṇaṅkaḷiruntaṉaveṉṟum iṉṉum nuṭpamāy vicārittāl intiya caṅkītattiṉ kālam mikappūrvamāṉateṉṟu aṟiyalāmeṉṟum caṅkīta cāstirattiṉ anēka pākaṅkaḷ intiyāvilēyē jeṉittaṉaveṉṟum piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷāl aṟiyalām.
Hindu Musical Scale and 22 Srutis By K.B. Deval Page I.
“It might be stated here at the outset that the Hindu musical scale dates as far back as the Brahman Period which is calculated, according to modern researches, to extend from 2,500 B.C. to 1,400 B.C. It is possible that further researches might modify this date or might, perhaps carry it still farther back. But we may be certain that our scale dates farther back than the Greek scale which is acknowledged to be the parent of modern European scales. Capt. Day in his ‘Music of Southern India; observes :- The Historial Strabo shows that the Greek influence extended to India, and also that Greek musicians of a certain school attributed the grater part of the science of music to India.”
“ārampattil ippōtu nām avaciyamāyc colla vēṇṭiyateṉṉa veṉṟāl intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkum ārōkaṇaṅkaḷ ‘pirāmaṇiya kālam’ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa atāvatu ki.mu.  2,500kkum 1,400kku iṭaiyiluḷḷa kālattilēyē upayōkattiliruntaṉa eṉṟu taṟkāla cāstira ārāycciyiṉ mūlamāyt teriya varukiṟatu. iṉṉum itilum anēka vicēṣamāṉa ārāyccikaḷ naṭakkumāṉāl intiya caṅkīta kālam iṉṉum muṉṉukku uḷḷatāyt teriya varalām. āṉāl nām stiramāyc collakkūṭiyatu, taṟkālam airōppāvil vaḻaṅkum ārōkaṇaṅkaḷukkut tāyāka eṇṇappaṭum kirēkka ārōkaṇaṅkaḷukku muṉṉāṉa kālattilum intiya ārōkaṇaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatām. ‘teṉṉintiya caṅkītam’ eṉṟa nūlil Captain Day  eṉpavar ‘carittira cāstiriyākiya Strabo collukiṟapaṭi kirēkkaruṭaiya caṅkītattiṉ pirakācam intiyā varaiyil eṭṭiyatu eṉṟum kirēk vitvāṉkaḷil oru cārār caṅkīta cāstirattiṉ atikamāṉa pākam intiyāviliruntu jeṉittatu eṉṟu oppuk koṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum carittirakkārarākiya ḷuvacayabṭi collukiṟār’ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.”
intiya caṅkītam iṟṟaikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya 3,000 - 4,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ pirāmaṇiya kālattiluṇṭāṉatākavum kiṟistuvukku 350 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa pāṇiṉi eṉpavar kālattiliruntatākavum pirāmaṇarkaḷiṭamiruntu maṟṟa tēcattārukkup paraviṉatākavum mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷil kāṇkiṟōm.
atil iṉṉum vicēṣamāṉa ārāycci ceytāl itaṟku veku kālattiṟku muṉṉālēyē intiyāvil caṅkītamiruntateṉṟu collalām eṉkiṟār. tolkāppiyattil nālvakai nilaṅkaḷiṉ karupporuḷkaḷ colla vanta iṭattil nālvakaiyāṉa yāḻkaḷaiyum paṟṟi viparañ collukiṟār. atil marutam, kuṟiñci, neytal, pālai eṉṟa nālu yāḻkaḷiṉ curutimuṟaikaḷaik kavaṉippōmāṉāl ṣaṭjamam, mattimam, pañcamam, niṣātam ākiya nālu curaṅkaḷai curutiyāka vaittuk koṇṭu kāṉampaṇṇappaṭuvateṉṟu itaṉ piṉviparamāy aṟivōm. tolkāppiyar taṉakku muṉ vaḻaṅki vanta caṅkīta nuṭpattaiyē aṅkē coṉṉār. itaippaṟṟi akastiyarum nāratarum virivāka nūl eḻutiyiruntatākavum terikiṟatu. itu teṉmaturaiyil caṅkamirunta kālamām. caṅkam uṇṭāvataṟku muṉpē caṅkītamiruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nām tiṭṭamākac collalām. itteṉmaturai aḻikaiyil iṅkiruntōr muṉṉamē tām kaṟṟukkoṇṭa ciṟ cila curaṅkaḷait taṅkaḷ tēcattil virutti ceytirukkalāmeṉṟum paḻaiyapaṭi teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa kāṉattaik koṇṭē taṅkaḷ kāṉattai virutti ceytu koṇṭārkaḷeṉṟum nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. itai aṟivuḷḷa evarum oppukkoḷvārkaḷ. teṉṉintiyāviṉ caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curaṅkaḷai paitākaras (Pythagoras) eṉṉum kirēkka tattuva cāstiri 2/3, 3/4 eṉṟa aḷaviṉ mūlamāyk koṇṭu pōṉār eṉpatai itaṉpiṉ aṟivōm. curaṅkaḷiluḷḷa veku nuṭpamāṉa kūṭutal kuṟaitalai aṟiyāmal palavitamāṉa cantēkaṅkaḷ jaṉittirukkiṟateṉṟu piṉ pārppōm.
intiyāvil yākañ ceyyumpoḻutu iraṇṭu pirāmaṇarkaḷ vīṇai vācikka, maṟṟoru pirāmaṇar vētaṅkaḷaip pāṭikkoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. appaṭippaṭṭa kāṉamillāta oru yākam pirayōcaṉamillai yeṉṟu piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
Hindu Music and the Gayan Samaj, Part I. P. 21.
“The system of instrumental music was in practice in the earliest times of the history of our land and it was held that sacrifical rites (yagams) had no efficacy unless two Brahmans played upon the Vina in concert with a third Brahman singing.”
“nam nāṭṭiṉ āti carittira kālan toṭaṅkic caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷai upayōkappaṭuttum muṟai ēṟpaṭṭiruntirukkiṟaJ. yākam ceyyumpōtu iraṇṭu pirāmaṇarkaḷ vīṇaiyil vācikka mūṉṟāvatu pirāmaṇar oruvar cērntu pāṭiṉāloḻiya anta yākattil oru pirayōcaṉamumillai eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭaJ.”
intiya caṅkītam maṉataic cāntappaṭuttit teyvattiṉiṭattil maṉatai nilainiṟkac ceykiṟateṉṟu piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil pārkkalām.
Hindu Music and the Gayan Samaj, Part II, P. 30.
“Music is one of the most innocent and elevating indoor amusements. It affords pleasure to all and delights specially those who cultivate and develop a taste for it. It softens and refines the mind and elevates its devotion to the Creator of the Universe. Relying upon the testimony only of works of great antiquity lying around us some 4,000 to 8,000 years old, we can safely affirm that Hindu music is of very ancient origin, and was developed into a system and science when Hindu Rishis resided and meditated in the primoeval forests, and inaugurated civilization.”
“caṅkītamāṉatu kālattai nalvaḻiyil celavaḻikkak kūṭiyatum, maṉacaip paravacappaṭuttak kūṭiyatumāṉa ōr nalla paḻakkam. atu yāvarukkum cantōṣattait tarattakkatum, mukkiyamāy atil viruppamuṭaiyavarāy ataik kaṟṟōrukku atika āṉantattait tarattakkatumāyirukkiṟatu. atu maṉataic cāntappaṭuttic cuttappaṭutti ulakaip paṭaitta ciruṣṭikariṭattil ammaṉatai vacappaṭuttukiṟaJ. 4,000 varuṣa mutal 8,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉē atu ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu nammaic cuṟṟiyirukkum paḻamaiyāṉa cāstira nūlkaḷ mūlamāy aṟinta nām intiya caṅkītamāṉatu veku paḻamaiyāṉateṉṟum, intiya makā riṣikaḷ kāṭukaḷil vacittu tapacu ceytu vanta kālattilēyē oru cāstira muṟaiyāy ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭirunta teṉṟum, cakala nākarīkattukkum atu āti uṟpattiyāyiruntateṉṟum taṭaiyillāmal collalām.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷai nām kavaṉikkaiyil caṅkītamāṉatu intiyāvil mikap paḻamaiyāṉa cāstirameṉṟum tapōtaṉarkaḷāl appiyācikkap paṭṭum cāstiramāy eḻutappaṭṭum vantateṉṟum cakala nākarīkattiṟkum atu āti uṟpattiyāyiruntateṉṟum collukiṟār. ulakiṉ uṟpattikku nātamē ātikāraṇamā yiruntatatupōla oru maṉuṣaṉiṉ nākarīkattiṟkum paktikkum avaṉ uḷḷattilirun tuṇṭākum nātamē mutalāyirukkiṟatu. eṅkumuḷḷatāy viḷaṅkum oruvaṉē ellā jīvappirāṇikaḷiṉ uḷḷattilumirukkiṟāṉ. avvuḷḷam ammeypporuḷai (uṇmaiyai) yuṭaiyatā yirukkumāṉāl ellāmuḷḷatākavum atu kuṟaiyak kuṟaiyap paṭippaṭiyāy oṉṟumillātatākavum ākiṟatai nām aṟivōm. nalla uḷḷam illātavaṉ nalla vārttaikaḷai yillātavaṉākiṟāṉ. uṇmai avaṉ vāyiṉiṉṟu varukiṟatillai eṉpataiyum nām aṟivōm. oruvaṉ coṉṉa iṉiya vacaṉaṅkaḷum iṉpamāṉa ōcaikaḷum coṉṉa avaṉiṭattilēyē tirumpap pōyc cērukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭi māṟimāṟi uṇṭākum ceyalē oruvaṉil nilaittirukkumpoḻutu avaṉ āṉanta muṭaiyavaṉākiṟāṉ. uṇmaiyāṉa āṉantattiṟku uṇmaiyāṉa nātamē ātikāraṇam. ivvuṇmai viruttikku mūlakāraṇattaiyē ātmam eṉṟum param eṉṟum aṟivuṭaiyōrkaḷ colvārkaḷ. uṇmai yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum āṉantamum āṉantattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum kītamum evviṭatti liruntu uṇ ṭāyiṟṟō anta iṭattaiyē alaṅkārappaṭuttukiṟaJ. atāvatu atil uṇmaiyāy viḷaṅkum teyvattiṉiṭattil nilaikkumpaṭic ceykiṟaJ. teyvattilēyē nilaittirunta tapōtaṉarkaḷ caṅkītattai utaviyākak koṇṭu taṅkaḷ tapacaic ceytu vantārkaḷ. jīvakāruṇyam, aṭakkam, poṟumai, kīḻppaṭital, aṉpu mutaliya uttama kuṇaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkit teyva puttirarāka ulakattil pirakācittārkaḷ. itaṟku māṟāka atāvatu uṇmaikku māṟākac ceyyum kāṉaṅkaḷ ceytavaṉaip pala allal ceytu poyyaṉākkumeṉpatu niccayam.
vētamuṇṭāṉa kālattilēyē avaikaḷ rākattōṭu pāṭappaṭṭa teṉṟum oru curattaik koṇṭum iraṇṭu curattaik koṇṭum mūṉṟu curattaik koṇṭum kāṉam paṇṇik koṇṭiruntārkaḷeṉṟum ataṉpiṉ aintu, āṟu, ēḻu eṉṉum curaṅkaḷaiyum antanta curaṅkaḷukkup poruttamuḷḷa mēl curaṅkaḷaiyum cērttu utātta, aṉutātta, svaritam eṉṟu vaḻaṅki vantārkaḷeṉṟum piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷāl terikiṟatu.
Hindu Music and the Gayan Samaj, P. 4.
“As has been already observed, our Rishi ancestors, in very early times, had been chanting vedic hymns and setting them to music, and mention of this fact in the vedas is frequently made in the Rigveda, as for instance, in such assertions as Archino Gayanti, Ganthino Gayanti and Samino Gayanti. Again, in later times Panini and other acharyas or teachers describe the science, and all this goes to show distinctly that music was cultivated among our ancestors to a large extent, and whith great assiduity and taste. The Arka system of music, it is said, was based upon only one note, the Gathika system upon two, and the Samika upon three, and to these was subsequently added another system termed the swarantara (another note) based upon four notes. There was thus vital difference between the system adopted by the Rishis and those adopted by the Acharyas; and Panini, to make up the difference, while regarding in his vyakarana Sutras, the three swaras” (Udatta, Anudatta and Swarita), as the main notes, points out in his (Siksha), the connection between the system by three and those by seven notes thus :
(Udatta) includes (ni and ga)
(Anudatta) includes (ri and dha) and
(Swarita) includes (sa, ma, pa)
“nām muṉṉē coṉṉapaṭi, nammuṭaiya muṉṉōrkaḷāyirunta riṣimārkaḷ ātikālattilēyē vētattiluḷḷa pirārttaṉaikaḷaip pāṭi curappaṭutti vaittirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaippaṟṟi rikku vētattil anēka iṭaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi collappaṭukiṟatu. utāraṇamāka, arcciṉōkāyanti, kāyantiṉōkāyanti mutaliya muṟaikaḷ collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil pāṇiṉi mutaliya āciriyarkaḷum vitvāṉkaḷum inta vittaiyai vistarittuc colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivaiyellām nām kavaṉikkumpōtu, caṅkītamāṉatu namatu muṉṉōrāl veku cirattaiyōṭum piriyattōṭum virutti paṇṇappaṭṭu vantatu eṉṟu niccayamāyc collalām. ‘arkkā’ eṉṉum muṟaiyāṉatu orē curattaiyum ‘kātikā’ eṉpatu iraṇṭu curattaiyum, ‘cāmikā’ eṉpatu mūṉṟu curattaiyum uṭaittāyiruntatu eṉṟum, ivaikaḷōṭukūṭa  nālu curattaiyuṭaiya ‘svarāntam’ eṉṉum vēṟoru muṟai uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟum terikiṟatu. ākaiyāl, riṣikaḷiṉ muṟaikkum ācāriyariṉ muṟaikkum aṉanta vittiyācamiruntatāl inta iraṇṭu muṟaiyaiyum campantappaṭuttum paṭiyāka eṉṉa ceytāreṉṟāl, tammuṭaiya ‘viyākaraṇa cūttirattil’ mūṉṟu curaṅkaḷākiya utāttam, aṉutāttam, svaritam eṉpavaikaḷai mūla curaṅkaḷāka vaittuk koṇṭum, tammuṭaiya “ciikṣa”yil anta mūṉṟu curaṅkaḷukkum captacuraṅkaḷukkumuḷḷa campantattai piṉvarumāṟu kāṇpikkiṟār”
“utāttam niṣātattaiyum kāntārattaiyum” “aṉutāttam riṣapattaiyum taivatattaiyum” svaritam caṭja mattima pañcamaṅkaḷaiyum uḷḷaṭakkiyirukkiṉṟatu eṉkiṟār.”
ivvākkiyaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkaiyil vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam coṉṉa pāṇiṉi (Panini) muṉivariṉ kālattil sapta curaṅkaḷum caṅkītattiṉ cila mukkiya amcaṅkaḷum collappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṟatu. vaṭatēcatti liruntu vanta āriyar mutal mutal oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu curaṅkaḷōṭu utātta aṉutāttamākak kāṉam ceytu vantārkaḷeṉṟu teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl intiyāviṉ teṉpākattiluḷḷa rāvaṇēsvaraṉ eṉṉum rājaṉ cāmavētattai sapta curaṅkaḷiṉālum pāṭiṉāṉeṉṟum ataṉ piṉpē sapta curaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ vikruti cuvaraṅkaḷum cāma vētattil paḻakkattiṟku vantaṉaveṉṟum eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. iṭaic caṅkattiṉ tuvakkattilirunta tolkāppiyariṉ kālattiṟku muṉṉālēyē caṅkīta cāstiram mika uṉṉata nilaiyiliruntateṉṟu nām muṉṉē pārttōm. itu pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālattiṟku anēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷaJ. pāṇiṉi rāvaṇaṉuṭaiya kālattiṟkum tolkāppiyariṉ kālattiṟkum mikap pintiṉavareṉṟum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kālattiṟkuc camīpittavar eṉṟum colla vēṇṭum. pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālattiṟku muṉṉā lēyē cāma vētam pāṭum vitam rāvaṇēsparaṉāl kaṟpikkappaṭṭa teṉṟum ataṟku muṉṉālēyē icai nūl akattiyarālum nāratarālum collappaṭṭiruntateṉṟum nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. rāvaṇēsparaṉāl caṅkīta vāttiyam ceyyappaṭṭatāka piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
History of Music by Hunt P. 141.
“The family of stringed instruments played with a bow has been a very numerous one. The most ancient Viol on record appears to be the ravenstrom (or ravanstrom), still played in India by the mendicant monks of Buddhah. Tradition says that this primitive instrument was invented by one of the kings of Ceylon, but the date assigned to this monarch is somewhat about five thousand years before Christ. It said, that the ravenstrom was the precursor of the gondok, or Russian Fiddle; and the Welsh crwth, which had six strings strung across a flat bridge, and was played partly with the bow, and partly by plucking with the fingers.”
“villiṉāl vācikkappaṭum tantivāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ tokai aṉantam. avaikaḷil veku pūrvamāyuḷḷatākac collappaṭum (Viol) kiṉṉaram eṉṟa vāttiyamāṉatu, taṟkālattilum intiyāvil pauttamata canniyāci kaḷāl upayōkappaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṟa rāvaṇēsvaram eṉṉum vāttiyamē. inta ātivāttiyamāṉatu ilaṅkaiyai āṇṭa aracarkaḷil oruvaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl anta aracaṉ aracāṇṭa kālam kiṟistuvukku muṉ 5,000 varuṣaṅkaḷ eṉṟu paramparaiyāyc collappaṭukiṟatu. curaḷaḷayain kuṉaiṉaṭana allatu ḻuṭiṉēṭima eṉṉum vāttiyamāṉatu, inta irāvaṇēsvaram eṉṉum vāttiyattiliruntu uṇṭāṉatu eṉpataṟkut taṭaiyillai. vēls tēcattil upayōkap paṭuttappaṭum ūcaṟavā eṉṉum vāttiyamum itiliruntu uṇṭāṉatutāṉ. ataṟku ēḻu tantikaḷ uṇṭu. atu villiṉāl vācikkappaṭalām, allatu viraliṉāl mīṭṭavum paṭalām.”
mēṟkaṇṭa vacaṉaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu rāvaṇēsvaram nām cātāraṇamāy teruvil pārkkum akappaik kiṉṉariyaip pōlirukku meṉṟu niṉaikka ētuvirukkiṟatu. itai allatu itaṟku caṟṟup peritā yuḷḷatum villiṉāl vācikkakkūṭiyatumāṉa oru vāttiyattai rāvaṇēsvaraṉ ceytāṉeṉṟu colvatai nampa iṭamillai. avaṉuṭaiya cāstira vallamaikkēṟpac caṅkītattil tērnta āriyarkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkāta curaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇṭupiṭittu atil cāmavētattaip pāṭip paramacivaṉ kirupaiyaip peṟutaṟkut takuntatāṉa uyarvuṭaiya vāttiyamoṉṟu ceytiruppāṉē yoḻiya, kuṟavar ceytu āṟu paicāvukku viṟṟu jīvaṉampaṇṇum oru akappaik kiṉṉariyaic ceytāṉeṉṟu colvatu muṟṟilum ovvāJ. rāvaṇaṉ āṇṭa tīvukaḷil tēṅkāy peritāyiruntatiṉāl akkoṭṭāṅkaccikaḷaik koṇṭu akappaik kiṉṉari ceytu ciṟuvarkaḷukkum coṟpattil jīvaṉam paṇṇum paratēcikaḷukkum viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷāka viṟṟu vantārkaḷ. itaṟku rāvaṇēsvaram eṉṟu peyar vantirukkalāmeṉṟu niṉaikkiṟēṉ. 10,417 culōkaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya rikku vētamē mikappūrvamāyuḷḷaJ. ivvētam ecur, cāmam, atarvaṇam eṉṟa mūṉṟu vētaṅkaḷaiyum taṉṉilaṭakkik koṇṭiruntatu. rāvaṇēsvaraṉ rikku vētattai naṉṟāyt terintavaṉā yiruntatiṉāl, avaikaḷuḷ paramacivaṉai tōttarikkak kūṭiyaṉavāyirunta cila culōkaṅkaḷait taṉ kālattil paḻakkamāyirunta teṉṉāṭṭuc caṅkīta muṟaippaṭi sapta curaṅkaḷaiyumuṭaiyatākiya iṉiya paṇṇil kāṉam paṇṇiṉāṉeṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṟaJ. ikkāṉattil mikap pirītiyuṭaiyavarāy rāvaṇaṉ piḻaiyai maṉṉittatiṉ nimittam civaṉai cāmakāṉappiriyar eṉṟu nāḷatu varaiyum collappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. cāma, pēta, tāṉa, taṇṭam eṉṉum rāja tantiram nāṉkaiyum aṟinta rāvaṇēsvaraṉ, pakavāṉ taṉṉai maṉṉikkumpaṭiyāka rikkuvētattiṉ cila pākattai vīṇaiyuṭaṉ kāṉam paṇṇiṉāṉ. cāmam + vētam = cāmavētam; cāmam = camātāṉam paṇṇutal. taṉ mēl koṇṭa kōpam taṇintu camātāṉamākumpaṭi pirārttaṉai paṇṇiṉatiṉāl ikkītattiṟkuc cāmakāṉam allatu cāmavētam eṉṟu peyar vantirukkalām.
cōmayākañ ceyyuṅkālattil teyvattait tōttarikkum pāṭalkaḷukkuc cāmakāṉam eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkukiṟaJ. cōmam eṉpatu oru virukṣattiṟkum oru ceṭikkum peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṟateṉṟu terikiṟatu. cōma virukṣam kāṭāyiruntateṉṟum cōmak koṭi (cōmalatai)yai koṇṭu vantāṉeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. cōmacceṭi yiliruntuṇṭākum oruvita kuṭinīr āṉantattai viḷaivittu mayakkattai uṇṭākkum vastuvāyiruntateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. yākattiṟkuriya patārttaṅkaḷai pākañceytu avaravarkaḷ yākapākam peṟṟukkoṇṭapiṉ taṅkaḷukkup pōtumāṉa aḷavu cōmaracapāṉam paṇṇum poḻutu cantira lōkattilirukkiṟatāka niṉaikkum taṅkaḷ pitirkaḷukkāka cōmaṉai allatu cantiraṉaip pārttu kāṉam paṇṇuvārkaḷ eṉṟu piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
Music of Hindustan by Fox Strangways P. 249-250.
“Samaveda. The symbol round which the elaborate ritual of the Samaveda gathers is that sacrifice of which the drinking of the juice of the Soma plant was the central point. The virtues of this juice are recapitulated in the ninth book of the Rig Veda, from which mainly the words for the Saman chants are taken. Soma is translated moon plant; and the Samaveda is specially connected with the worship of ancestors, whose abode was the moon. Great care was taken not to deviat from the original melody-types and rhythms and the religous efficacy of the hymns was held to depend largely on the right application of directions contained in the Brahmanic explanations (Brahmana not later than the sixth century B.C.) of the Vedic text “Samhita.” The expense of the full cermonical was not small; the Soma sacrifice involved days in performance and months in preparation. A full description of its elaborate and gorgeous ritual is to be found in the Aitareyabrahmana of the Rig Veda translated into English by Martin Haug, 1863, and its close connection with the fire-worship of the Zorastrains is there detailed.”
“cāmavētam. cāmavētattil collappaṭṭa atimēṉmaiyāṉa caṭaṅ kācāraṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilumukkiyamāṉatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl cōmalataiyiṉ racattaik kuṭippatākiya paliyē. ivviracattiṉ perumaiyaippaṟṟi rikkuvētattiṉ oṉpatāvatu atikārattil vistāramāyp pārkkalām. cāma vētattiṉ culōkañ collutalum itiliruntutāṉ eṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṟaJ. cōma eṉṟāl cantiracceṭi. cāmavētattiṟkum pitir vaṇakkattiṟkum campantam uṇṭu. ateṉṉaveṉṟāl appitirkkaḷ cantiraṉil vacikkiṟārkaḷ eṉṉum nampikkaiyē. intac culōkaṅkaḷ ātiyil eḻutap paṭṭa irākamēraiyilum tāḷattilum ippōtu collappaṭukiṟatā eṉpataippaṟṟi atika kavalaiyuḷḷavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. vētaṅkaḷaic cērnta pirāmaṇaṅkaḷil caṅkītattaippaṟṟic colliyirukkum vitikaḷiṉpaṭi inta cāmavēta culōkaṅkaḷ collappaṭāviṭṭāl avaikaḷil yātoru payaṉumillai yeṉṟu koṇṭārkaḷ. (pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ ki.mu.  āṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil uḷḷavai). caṭaṅkā cāraṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟa celavāṉatu koñcamalla. cōmaraca pali oṉṟaic celuttuvataṟku anēka nāḷ piṭittaṉa. ataṟku vēṇṭiya āyattaṅkaḷ muṭivu peṟa anēka mātaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. intap paliyiṉ atikāmpīramāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷaip paṟṟiyaṟiya vēṇṭumāṉāl rikku vētattiluḷḷa āyttariya pirāmaṇattil pārkkalām. inta nūl āyacavain ḻayarapa eṉpavarāl 1863l iṅkilīṣil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaJ. intac caṭaṅkukaḷukkum pārcikaḷāl ceyyappaṭum akkiṉi vaṇakkattiluḷḷa caṭaṅkukaḷukkum uḷḷa neruṅkiya campantam itil naṉṟāy veḷippaṭum.”
cōmaṉaip pārttup pirārttikkum kāṉattiṟkuc cōmakāṉam allatu cāmakāṉam eṉṟum cōmaracam kuṭittuc collum pāṭṭiṟkuc cāmakāṉameṉṟum peyar varalām. āṉāl rāvaṇēsvaraṉ paramacivaṉai iṉṉicaiyuṭaṉ pāṭiyapiṉpē ataṟku cāmakāṉam eṉṟu peyar vantiruppatāka nām niṉaikka vēṇṭum. rikkuvētattiluḷḷa culōkaṅkaḷē rāvaṇaṉāl kāṉam ceyyappaṭṭapiṉ cāmavētameṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu nāḷatu varaiyum ulaka vaḻakkattil collappaṭukiṟatu.
mēṟkaṇṭa vākkiyaṅkaḷil kaṇṭa carittirattai nām itaṉ muṉ kēḷvippaṭṭirukkiṟōm. iccarittirattai ñāpakappaṭuttuvataṟkāka maturai ālayattil ceyyappaṭṭirukkum corūpattaiyum pārttirukkiṟōm. itil rāvaṇēsvaraṉ oru vīṇaiyai vaittukkoṇṭu taṉ irupatu kaikaḷiṉālum vācikkiṟatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum maturaikkuc camīpattiluḷḷa āviṭaiyārkōyililum maṟṟaiya civastalaṅkaḷilum rāvaṇa vākaṉam (kailācavākaṉam) eṉṟa veḷḷi vākaṉattilum rāvaṇaṉ vīṇai vācikkiṟatākavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. cumār 2,000-3,000, varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa ālayattiṉ cittirattaiyum vākaṉattiṉ cittirattaiyum kavaṉikkaiyil rāvaṇēsvaram oru vilvāttiyam eṉṟu niṉaikka iṭamillai. āṉāl intu caṅkītattiṉ ati pūrvamāyuḷḷatum taṟkālattil illātatum āyiram tanti pūṭṭiyatumākiya pēriyāḻ eṉpatai rāvaṇaṉ ceytu taṉ 20 karaṅkaḷiṉālum avaikaḷai mīṭṭi vācittāṉeṉṟu colvatu mikavum poruttamāyirukkum eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. akkālattiṟku muṉṉamēyē caṅkītam pūraṇa tērcciyaṭaintirunta teṉṟu nām niccayikkalām. akkālattil pēriyāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, makarayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ mutaliya vīṇaikaḷiruntaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya inta akappaik kiṉṉari iruntatāka ōriṭattum collak kāṇōm. vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ utaviyaik koṇṭu oruvaṉāyt taṉittup pāṭum vaḻakkam, intiya caṅkītattiṟku mikap pūrvamākavēyuḷḷatu eṉṟu piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
H.M. Scale & Srutis, By K.B. Deval P. 46.
“The essential basis of music is melody and this is contained admittedly in the Hindu scale to its full extent. This has been the main charm of the Hindu system of music for thousands of years in the past and will continue to remain so for a number of years in the future.”
